## Endnotes / 尾注

===Original===

- 

From our experience of over a quarter of a century, writing rhymes on local subjects, we find they are preserved more carefully, and are more impressed on people’s minds than prose articles on the same subjects. This has induced us to compile this little volume. Please accept this apology. —﻿The Author ↩︎

===Chinese===

- 

根据我们二十多年来撰写关于本地题材韵文的经验，我们发现这些韵文比同类题材的散文文章保存得更为仔细，也更深入人心。这促使我们汇编了这本小书。请接受我们的歉意。——作者 ↩︎

===Original===

- 

The oration on the above interesting occasion was delivered by the late Hon. William H. Merritt, projector of the Welland Canal. He served at the battle when a young man. We witnessed the interesting ceremony and shall never forget it. —﻿The Author ↩︎

===Chinese===

- 

上述有趣场合的演讲由已故尊敬的威廉·H·梅里特先生发表，他是威兰运河的发起人。他在年轻时参加过那场战役。我们见证了那场感人的仪式，永远不会忘记。——作者 ↩︎

===Original===

- 

The nurls and birds’ eyes and curls were highly prized in furniture thirty years ago, when we used the smooth plain. ↩︎

===Chinese===

- 

三十年前，当人们使用光滑的素面时，旋钮、鸟眼纹和卷纹在家具中备受珍视。 ↩︎

===Original===

- 

James Noxon, Mayor. ↩︎

===Chinese===

- 

詹姆斯·诺克森，市长。 ↩︎

===Original===

- 

Tom Moore paddled his own canoe along the Canadian shore of Lake Erie and was enraptured with the view. He landed and remained over night at a farm house. His “Canadian Boat Song” is immortal. ↩︎

===Chinese===

- 

汤姆·摩尔划着自己的独木舟沿着伊利湖的加拿大海岸航行，被那里的景色迷住了。他上岸并在一间农舍过夜。他的《加拿大船歌》是不朽的。 ↩︎

===Original===

- 

The valley of the Thames we presume includes Stratford on the north and Woodstock and Ingersoll on the south. The Avon, on whose banks Stratford is located, joins the Thames near St. Mary’s. The middle branch flows throngh Embro and Thamesford, the south and middle branches unite and flow through Dorchester and Westminster and blend with the northern branch at London, where it deviates to Elgin in the south. ↩︎

===Chinese===

- 

我们推测泰晤士河谷包括北部的斯特拉特福以及南部的伍德斯托克和英格索尔。斯特拉特福所在的埃文河在圣玛丽附近汇入泰晤士河。中间支流流经恩布罗和泰姆斯福德，南部和中部支流汇合，流经多切斯特和威斯敏斯特，并在伦敦与北部支流汇合，随后向南转向埃尔金。 ↩︎

===Original===

- 

Some imagine the Thames is too insignificant a stream to be sung in verse. “Distance lends enchantment to the view,” and they fancy the old Scottish rivers are more worthy of song; but many of them are polluted of late years with vile odors from factories; and, as the county of Oxford is agreeably diversified with hills and dales, the clear, sparkling stream, flowing over a pebbly bottom, is indeed “a thing of beauty and a joy forever.” ↩︎

===Chinese===

- 

有些人认为泰晤士河是一条太微不足道的小溪，不值得在诗中歌颂。‘距离给景色增添了魅力’，他们幻想古老的苏格兰河流更值得歌颂；但近年来，其中许多河流都被工厂排出的恶臭污染了；而牛津郡的山峦和幽谷错落有致，景色宜人，这条清澈、波光粼粼的溪流流经布满卵石的河底，确实是‘美的事物，也是永恒的喜悦’。 ↩︎

===Original===

- 

Among the earliest champions of the Factory System of making cheese were Messrs. Chadwick, Casswell and Ballantyne. The North Oxford Company were awarded the highest honor at the Centennial Exhibition. Messrs. J. L. Grant & Co. have a fine large cold storage warehouse on the G.T.R., and the C.P.R. have erected one on their line, which is leased by Mr. Riley. Ingersoll being the great dairy centre of Ontario it was deemed requisite to have those facilities for preserving the cheese in the hot season. The following is a list of the most prominent cheese factories in this district and the salesmen thereof: ↩︎

Niagara, once the capital and business centre of Upper Canada, and also an important fortress. It is located at the mouth of the Niagara River. ↩︎

Mathew Arnold saw fit to say that Longfellow was not the National Poet of America, but we presume few believed him; one of Longfellow’s grandest pieces, the scene is laid in Canada. ↩︎

===Chinese===

Translation not found ↩︎

===Original===

- 

Alas, the reapers and mowers have displaced the Bonnie Lasses in the fields, 1884. ↩︎

===Chinese===

- 

唉，收割者和割草者已经取代了田野里的漂亮姑娘们，1884年。 ↩︎

===Original===

- 

The Royal Stuarts are owners of large estates on the banks of the Findhorn and their great rivals were near by the Royal Comyns. The Lion Hunter Gordon Comyn was of this stock. Professor J. S. Blockie has written a fine poem on the wars of the rival houses. “Mere where the dark water’d stream rushes free child of the mountain.” ↩︎

===Chinese===

- 

皇家斯图亚特家族在芬德河畔拥有大片地产，而他们伟大的对手皇家科明家族就在附近。‘猎狮人’戈登·科明就是这个家族的成员。J. S. 布洛克教授为这两大家族的战争创作了一首优美的诗。
“唯有在那里，深色的水流如大山的自由之子般奔腾。” ↩︎

===Original===

- 

Lord Rae, chief of the clan McKay. The family formerly owned large estates in Sutherland, which they lost. The present Lord Rae was born in Holland, and he married a rich lady with an estate near Edinbursh. He is one of the foremost scientific men in Britain at the present time, and he frequently presides at assemblies both in London and Edinburgh for the advancement of education and science. ↩︎

===Chinese===

- 

雷勋爵，麦凯家族的首领。该家族以前在萨瑟兰拥有大片地产，后来失去了。现任雷勋爵出生于荷兰，他娶了一位在爱丁堡附近拥有地产的富有的女士。他是目前英国最杰出的科学家之一，并经常在伦敦和爱丁堡主持旨在促进教育和科学发展的集会。 ↩︎

===Original===

- 

Mr. T. D. Millar has just secured, Sept., 1884, the first prize for cheese at the great cheese fair at Amsterdam, Holland. They weighed over 600 pounds each, and were manufactured by the burnside Factory of Dorchester. The Galloway Factory is manufacturing several cheese weighing one ton each. The mammoth cheese, alluded to in cheese ode, was manufactured by Mr. Tames Harris, Ingersoll Factory. The Dunn Cheese Factory, North Oxford, secured first prize at the great Centennial Exhibition, but where all factories produce such excellent cheese perhaps it would be making invidious distinctions to specify the honours won by any particular factory. The West Oxford Company have recently built a fine factory on the Culloden Road. ↩︎

===Chinese===

- 

T. D. 米勒先生于1884年9月在荷兰阿姆斯特丹举行的大型奶酪博览会上刚刚获得奶酪一等奖。这些奶酪每块重达600多磅，由多切斯特的伯恩赛德工厂制造。盖洛韦工厂正在制造几块重达一吨的奶酪。歌词颂歌中提到的巨大奶酪是由英格索尔工厂的詹姆斯·哈里斯先生制造的。北牛津的邓恩奶酪工厂在百年博览会上获得了一等奖，但由于所有工厂都生产如此优质的奶酪，指明任何特定工厂所获得的荣誉也许是不公平的区别对待。西牛津公司最近在卡洛登路建造了一座精美的工厂。 ↩︎

===Original===

- 

Entwining of the thistle around the maple tree, Scotia’s sons have indented their names deep in Canadian history. The names of McDonald, McKenzie, Cameron and Mowat stand conspicuous, and Brown second to none. For wealth, enterprise and benevolence those Montreal Scotsmen stand high, Sir Donald A. Smith, Sir George Stephens and Duncan McIntyre. ↩︎

===Chinese===

- 

蓟花环绕枫树，苏格兰的儿子们将他们的名字深深地刻在加拿大的历史中。麦克唐纳、麦肯齐、卡梅伦和莫瓦特的名字格外醒目，而布朗也不遑多让。在财富、进取心和仁慈方面，这些蒙特利尔苏格兰人地位崇高，如唐纳德·A·史密斯爵士、乔治·斯蒂芬斯爵士和邓肯·麦金泰尔。 ↩︎

===Original===

- 

The most celebrated lady traveller in Britain is Miss Cumming, a niece of the Lion Hunter. She has written several volumes of her travels in distant lands. ↩︎

===Chinese===

英国最著名的女性旅行家是卡明小姐，她是‘猎狮人’的侄女。她写了好几卷关于她在远方土地旅行的书。 ↩︎

===Original===

===Original===

- 

Though we have described the above cheese as mighty, yet it did not contain a single mite. The late Daniel Pheisan took a deep interest in the great cheese, and in promoting cheese factories, he became wealthy and died in Woodstock. Mr. Robert Facy was the cheesemaker at Mr. Harris, Factory the time when the big cheese was made. ↩︎

===Chinese===

- 

虽然我们将上述奶酪描述为巨大，但它并没有包含一只小虫（mite，双关语，既指‘微小’也指‘小虫’）。已故的丹尼尔·费桑对这种巨大的奶酪以及推广奶酪工厂深感兴趣，他变得富有并在伍德斯托克去世。罗伯特·费西先生是哈里斯先生工厂的奶酪制造者，当时正是制造巨型奶酪的时候。 ↩︎

===Original===

- 

The above factory is now the North Oxford Company’s. ↩︎

===Chinese===

===Original===

-

===Chinese===

- 

那位富有进取心的苏格兰人，蒙特利尔的邓肯·麦金泰尔，是上述矿山第一位热心的推动者，他与D. A. 史密斯爵士和乔治爵士（现为史蒂文勋爵）共同修建了加拿大太平洋铁路。 ↩︎

## Endnotes / 尾注

===Original===

- 

From our experience of over a quarter of a century, writing rhymes on local subjects, we find they are preserved more carefully, and are more impressed on people’s minds than prose articles on the same subjects. This has induced us to compile this little volume. Please accept this apology. —﻿The Author ↩︎

===Chinese===

- 

根据我们二十多年来撰写关于本地题材韵文的经验，我们发现这些韵文比同类题材的散文文章保存得更为仔细，也更深入人心。这促使我们汇编了这本小书。请接受我们的歉意。——作者 ↩︎

===Original===

- 

The oration on the above interesting occasion was delivered by the late Hon. William H. Merritt, projector of the Welland Canal. He served at the battle when a young man. We witnessed the interesting ceremony and shall never forget it. —﻿The Author ↩︎

===Chinese===

- 

上述有趣场合的演讲由已故尊敬的威廉·H·梅里特先生发表，他是威兰运河的发起人。他在年轻时参加过那场战役。我们见证了那场感人的仪式，永远不会忘记。——作者 ↩︎

===Original===

- 

The nurls and birds’ eyes and curls were highly prized in furniture thirty years ago, when we used the smooth plain. ↩︎

===Chinese===

- 

三十年前，当人们使用光滑的素面时，旋钮、鸟眼纹和卷纹在家具中备受珍视。 ↩︎

===Original===

- 

James Noxon, Mayor. ↩︎

===Chinese===

- 

詹姆斯·诺克森，市长。 ↩︎

===Original===

- 

Tom Moore paddled his own canoe along the Canadian shore of Lake Erie and was enraptured with the view. He landed and remained over night at a farm house. His “Canadian Boat Song” is immortal. ↩︎

===Chinese===

- 

汤姆·摩尔划着自己的独木舟沿着伊利湖的加拿大海岸航行，被那里的景色迷住了。他上岸并在一间农舍过夜。他的《加拿大船歌》是不朽的。 ↩︎

===Original===

- 

The valley of the Thames we presume includes Stratford on the north and Woodstock and Ingersoll on the south. The Avon, on whose banks Stratford is located, joins the Thames near St. Mary’s. The middle branch flows throngh Embro and Thamesford, the south and middle branches unite and flow through Dorchester and Westminster and blend with the northern branch at London, where it deviates to Elgin in the south. ↩︎

===Chinese===

-

Some imagine the Thames is too insignificant a stream to be sung in verse. “Distance lends enchantment to the view,” and they fancy the old Scottish rivers are more worthy of song; but many of them are polluted of late years with vile odors from factories; and, as the county of Oxford is agreeably diversified with hills and dales, the clear, sparkling stream, flowing over a pebbly bottom, is indeed “a thing of beauty and a joy forever.” ↩︎

===Chinese===

“唯有在那里，深色的水流如大山的自由之子般奔腾。” ↩︎

Lord Rae, chief of the clan McKay. The family formerly owned large estates in Sutherland, which they lost. The present Lord Rae was born in Holland, and he married a rich lady with an estate near Edinbursh. He is one of the foremost scientific men in Britain at the present time, and he frequently presides at assemblies both in London and Edinburgh for the advancement of education and science. ↩︎

===Chinese===

Mr. T. D. Millar has just secured, Sept., 1884, the first prize for cheese at the great cheese fair at Amsterdam, Holland. They weighed over 600 pounds each, and were manufactured by the burnside Factory of Dorchester. The Galloway Factory is manufacturing several cheese weighing one ton each. The mammoth cheese, alluded to in cheese ode, was manufactured by Mr. Tames Harris, Ingersoll Factory. The Dunn Cheese Factory, North Oxford, secured first prize at the great Centennial Exhibition, but where all factories produce such excellent cheese perhaps it would be making invidious distinctions to specify the honours won by any particular factory. The West Oxford Company have recently built a fine factory on the Culloden Road. ↩︎

-

Smith, Sir George Stephens and Duncan McIntyre. ↩︎

## Prologue to South Ontario Sketches / 南安大略速写·序曲

===Original===
The district lying South of Georgian Bay and Lake Simcoe, including Toronto.

My friends we sing Canadian themes,
For in them we proudly glory,
Her lakes and rivers and her streams,
Worthy of renown in story;
And in these leaves we hope is strewn
Some wheat among the chaff,
And maple boughs by rude axe hewn,
Where one may find a rustic staff;
To help him o'er the rugged lines
If he to weariness inclines.

===Chinese===
乔治亚湾与锡姆科湖以南地区，含多伦多。

朋友们，我们歌唱加拿大的主题，
心中满怀自豪与荣光，
她的湖泊、河流与溪流，
在传说中声名远扬；
在这些书页间我们愿撒下，
麦穗杂于糠秕之中，
粗斧砍下的枫树枝干，
可作旅人手中拐杖；
助他越过崎岖的诗句，
若他读来倦意渐生。

===Original===
Some see no beauties near to home,
But do admire the distant far,
They always love abroad to roam,
View glory in but far off star;
But let it never be forgot
That distant hills when closer seen
Are after all a barren spot
Not like your own hills clad in green;
You'll find they are but idle dreams
To seek for happiness afar.

At home there's lovely lakes and streams,
Remain content now where you are;
At us we hope you will not rage
Because we sing of local charms
In each varied town and village
As well as round our local farms,
But our address it must be brief,
So now we bid you all adieu,
But of our book pray read each leaf
Until the whole you have gone through;
Each one doth know it is not wise,
Though our songs may not be vocal,
Chants of our home for to despise,
But prize them 'cause they are local.

===Chinese===
有人视近旁无美景，
只将目光投远方，
他们总爱远游他乡，
只在遥远星辰中觅荣光；
但切莫忘记这一点——
远处山峦走近看，
终究不过是荒芜之地，
不比你家乡绿意盎然；
你会发现那不过是黄粱一梦，
幸福感不应向远方求寻。

家园有可爱湖溪在，
安守本分留此乡；
愿诸位莫要对我们发怒，
因我们歌唱家乡风物，
唱遍大小城镇与村庄，
唱遍我们田野农场；
我们的致辞须简短，
如今向诸位道别离，
但请翻开书册每一页，
直至通读全部篇章；
人人皆知这并非不明智，
纵使歌声不够嘹亮，
却也不该轻蔑家乡的歌谣，
当珍惜只因它充满乡土芬芳。

## Reminiscences / 追忆往事

===Original===
On the laying of the corner stone of the Brock monument at Queenston Heights, and the final interment of the General who had fallen at the battle of Queenston, Oct. 13th, 1812. The remains of his Aide, Col. McDonald, were also deposited under the new tower.

A wail went o'er broad Canada,
When it was known a vile outlaw
Had at midnight's awful hour,
With ruffian hand blown up the tower.

===Chinese===
昆斯顿高地布罗克（Brock）纪念碑奠基，及1812年10月13日昆斯顿战役中牺牲将军的最终安葬。其副官麦克唐纳（Col. McDonald）上校遗骸同葬于新塔之下。

哀号声响彻辽阔的加拿大，
当人们得知一个卑劣的暴徒，
竟在午夜骇人的时辰，
以流氓之手炸毁了那座高塔。

===Original===
'Neath which had slept the gallant Brock
Who bravely fell on Queenston's rock,
But graceful column soon shall rise,
Its beauteous shaft will kiss the skies.

For from Queenston's woody height
You may behold a pleasing sight,
The grim old veterans of the war,
Militiamen with many a scar.

===Chinese===
英勇的布罗克长眠其下，
他勇敢地倒在昆斯顿的岩崖上，
但一座优雅的石柱即将拔地而起，
其秀丽柱身将亲吻苍穹。

从昆斯顿林木葱郁的高处，
你能望见一幅动人的画面，
那是战争中饱经沧桑的老兵，
满身伤痕的民兵战士。

===Original===
Indian braves from each nation,
Grouped to pay their last ovation,
Round the remains of General Brock,
Who led them oft in battle's shock.

Old heroes now again do rally,
Feebly they move along the valley,
Not as they rushed in days of yore
When torrent like they onward bore.

===Chinese===
各部落的印第安勇士们，
聚在一起致以最后的敬礼，
围绕着布罗克将军的遗骸，
他曾多次率他们冲锋陷阵。

昔日的英雄们再次集结，
步履蹒跚地走过山谷，
不似当年如洪流般奔涌，
一往无前势不可挡。

===Original===
And swept away the foeman's ranks
O'er Niagara's rugged banks,
So indignant was their grief
On losing of their warrior chief.

Now with triumphant funeral car,
Adorned with implements of war,
The sad procession slow ascends,
As round the hill its way it wends.

===Chinese===
他们横扫敌军的阵营，
越过尼亚加拉崎岖的河岸，
失去他们的勇士首领，
他们的悲愤如此深重。

如今乘着凯旋的灵车，
装饰着战争的器具，
悲伤的队伍缓缓前行，
沿着山丘蜿蜒而上。

===Original===
Marching to mournful, solemn note,
While grand old flags around it float,
And now may peace be never broken
'Mong lands where Saxon tongue is spoken.

"For peace hath victories by far
More glorious than horrid war,"
England doth Longfellow revere,
And America loves Shakespeare.

===Chinese===
在哀伤庄严的乐声中行进，
古老的旗帜在四周飘扬，
愿和平永不被打破，
在说撒克逊语的土地上。

"和平之胜利远远胜过，
那可怕的战争所带来的辉煌，"
英格兰敬仰朗费罗（Longfellow），
美洲热爱莎士比亚（Shakespeare）。

## Birth of Canada as a Nation / 加拿大立国

===Original===
July First, 1867.

Written during the visit of delegates from the Lower Provinces to negotiate for confederation.

Hail Britannia's noblest daughter,
Who is surrounded by the water
Of many a lake and broad sea,
Land of beaver and of maple tree.

===Chinese===
1867年7月1日。

作于下省份代表来访商讨联邦之际。

欢呼吧，不列颠最高贵的女儿，
她被众多湖泊与辽阔大海环绕，
海狸与枫树的故乡。

===Original===
Her lofty brow is wreathed with smiles,
For from the far Atlantic isles
In pomp have come their delegates,
All seeking to unite their fates.

With Canada great northern queen,
And now throughout the land is seen,
High festival and stately dance,
Triumphant nuptials to advance.

===Chinese===
她高贵的额头环绕着微笑，
因为从遥远的大西洋诸岛，
盛装前来的代表们，
都愿将他们的命运相连。

与伟大的北方女王加拿大，
如今整片土地可见，
盛大的庆典与庄严的舞步，
奏响胜利婚礼的序曲。

===Original===
And soon shall Red River valley
And distant Vancouver rally,
To form this Empire gigantic
From Pacific to Atlantic.

===Chinese===
很快红河谷与远方的温哥华也将响应，
铸就这庞大的帝国，
从太平洋到大西洋。

## Welcome to the Prince of Wales / 欢迎威尔士亲王

===Original===
Lines written when the Prince of Wales was about embarking for Canada, May, 1860.

In his long voyage o'er the sea,
To where doth grow the maple tree,
May he be blest with pleasant gales,
The coming man, the Prince of Wales.

===Chinese===
1860年5月，威尔士亲王（Prince of Wales）即将启航前往加拿大时作。

在他远渡重洋的漫长旅途中，
前往枫树生长的地方，
愿他一路顺风顺水，
这位未来的君主，威尔士亲王。

===Original===
The maple grows but in good soil,
Where nature doth reward for toil
The farmer splitting his fence rails,
He welcome bids the Prince of Wales.

In the woods the axe is ringing
And the yeoman merry singing,
The song resounds o'er hills and dales,
Our future king the Prince of Wales.

===Chinese===
枫树只生长在肥沃的土地上，
大自然在那里酬报辛劳——
劈着篱笆栏杆的农夫，
他热情地迎接威尔士亲王。

林间传来斧头的铿锵，
农夫欢快地歌唱，
歌声回荡在山丘与谷地，
我们的未来之王，威尔士亲王。

===Original===
Round the brow of our future chief
We'll weave a wreath of maple leaf,
For o'er broad Canada prevails
Kind feelings to the Prince of Wales.

When in this land the Prince arrives,
May he have many pleasant drives,
And on our lakes have merry sails,
Great king of princes, Prince of Wales.

===Chinese===
在我们未来领袖的额头上，
我们将编织一顶枫叶花环，
因为整个加拿大洋溢着，
对威尔士亲王的深情厚谊。

当亲王踏上这片土地，
愿他享有愉快的驾车之旅，
在我们的湖上欢快扬帆，
诸亲王之王，威尔士亲王。

## Lament of the Maple Tree / 枫树之叹

===Original===
A Vision

"We had a dream which was not all a dream."
> Byron

I laid me down one day in June,
It was late long afternoon,
A very sultry summer's eve,
Such times the senses oft deceive,
The place was 'neath a maple tree,
Soon from all cares and troubles free,
By a gentle, kindly slumber,
No more our sorrows we could number,
But we heard a plaintive wail
Such as we find in fairy tale,
It was the genius of the tree
Who in sad guise appeared to me,
And then she sadly did give vent
Unto this awful grave lament:

===Chinese===
一个幻象

"我们做了一个梦，却不全是梦。"
——拜伦（Byron）

六月的一天我躺下歇息，
正是悠长的午后时分，
一个极为闷热的夏夜，
感官时常在此时迷离。
我身处一棵枫树之下，
很快摆脱了一切忧虑与烦扰，
在温柔亲切的睡意中，
不再能数清我们的哀愁。
但我们听到一声哀婉的叹息，
恰如童话中所描绘的那样，
那是树的精灵，
以悲伤的姿态出现在我面前，
然后她悲切地倾诉，
发出这庄严而沉重的哀歌：

===Original===
Though I am gay in month of June,
All decked in green, yet very soon,
Alas my beauty will be faded
And my charms be all degraded,
For is my time of glory brief,
So often flattered is my leaf.

In Canada so broad and free
All poets sing of the maple tree,
High I stand in their opinion,
Emblem of the New Dominion,
The reason I do them upbraid
Some never slept beneath my shade,
And yet they take the liberty
To chant about the maple tree,
They dare to poetise my leaf,
This is the source of all my grief,
I think their praises all so rude
And as but base ingratitude,
So often hackneyed is my name
That every fall I burn with shame,
Like maiden's cheek which blushes red
When vain rash youth asks her to wed,
Then do these foolish ones descry
In me fresh beauty and they sigh,
And then renew their songs of praise.

===Chinese===
"六月里我虽容光焕发，
满身翠绿，然而很快，
唉，我的美丽将凋零，
我的风韵将全然褪去，
我的荣耀时光何其短暂，
我的叶片被反复阿谀奉承。

在辽阔自由的加拿大，
所有的诗人都歌唱枫树，
在他们心中我地位崇高，
新自治领的象征——
我责备他们的原因在于，
有些人从未在我树荫下沉眠，
却擅自放歌咏叹枫树，
他们胆敢为我的叶子赋诗，
这便是我所有悲伤的根源，
我觉得他们的赞美何其粗鲁，
不过是卑劣的忘恩负义，
我的名字被用得太滥，
每年秋天我都羞愧难当，
如少女的脸颊泛起红晕，
当轻狂少年向她求婚时，
那些愚人们便又发现，
我焕发新的美丽而叹息，
然后重续他们的赞歌。

===Original===
But unto me how sad their lays,
For then I know my days are brief,
'Tis hectic flush upon my leaf;
True poets then should mournful sing
When the destroyer's on the wing,
For then I know my leaves of gold
Will all soon mingle with the mould,
No one does ever think to praise
The fell destroyer when he slays,
None rejoice in the flushed cheek
When the poor girl is low and weak,
Perhaps they'll say and it is true
In spring my glories I'll renew,
But 'tis poor comfort after all
To lose my offspring every fall,
Small consolation to mother
To tell her that soon another
Will replace her fond darling boy
Who has been source of all her joy,
But you know all about my wood
You know that it is strong and good,
And I have full many a curl
And pleasing eye and charming nurl,

===Chinese===
但他们的歌谣于我何其悲伤，
因为我知道我的时日无多，
那是叶片上病态的红晕；
真正的诗人应当哀婉地歌唱，
当毁灭者展翅而来，
因为我知道我金色的叶片，
很快都将与泥土混为一体。
无人想到要去赞美，
那凶残的毁灭者杀戮之时，
无人为苍白的面颊欢欣，
当可怜的姑娘病弱体衰。
也许他们会说——这确实不假——
春天我的荣光将重焕，
但这终究是可怜的安慰，
每年秋天都要失去我的孩子，
对一位母亲而言何其微薄，
告诉她很快会有另一个，
取代她心爱的宝贝儿子，
他曾是她一切欢乐的源泉。
但你深知我木材的品质，
你知它坚实而优良，
我有无数的卷曲纹理，
赏心悦目的纹路与迷人的节疤。

===Original===
Some love me as fond nature grained
And some prefer my beauty stained,
But my dear friend I hope that you
My varied shades love pure and true,
For of the woods you know the staple
Stoutest and best is good maple,
The youth my sugar eat with glee,
And old maids love me in their tea,
In me do various uses meet
In summer shade, in winter heat,
For I do make a glorious blaze
All worthy of the poet's lays,
But to their praises I'll be deaf
If more they harp about my leaf.

They call me gay when I am sober
To me 'tis gloomy month October,
But saints on earth when they die
Hope for true bliss beyond the sky,
So winter does bring no alarms
Though it strip bare my trunk and arms,
For now I know that time will bring
More glorious foliage in the spring,
Then all nature will rejoice
Triumphing with glorious voice,
And birds will in my branches sing
Hosannas to the lovely spring.

===Chinese===
有人爱我天生的纹理本色，
有人偏爱我染色后的美丽，
但我亲爱的朋友，我希望你，
对我变幻的色彩纯粹而真诚。
因为林木之中你深知，
最坚实最上等的便是好枫木，
年轻人欢快地吃我的糖浆，
老姑娘们爱在茶里加我，
我身兼多种用途——
夏日遮荫，冬日取暖，
因为我燃起熊熊的火焰，
足以配得上诗人的赞歌，
但若他们再喋喋不休歌唱我的叶子，
我对他们的赞美将充耳不闻。

他们在我清醒时称我欢快，
对我来说十月阴郁沉沉，
但世上的圣徒逝去后，
期盼着天上的真正福祉，
因此冬日并不带来惊恐，
虽它剥尽我的躯干与枝臂，
因为我知道时光将带来，
春天更加灿烂的繁叶，
那时万物欢欣鼓舞，
以荣耀的声音欢庆，
鸟儿将在我的枝头歌唱，
向可爱的春天唱起赞美诗。

## Lines on Violets / 紫罗兰吟

===Original===
Once, while digging 'neath the snow,
'Mid Canadian winter, lo!
To our joy and surprise
We saw some violets in full bloom,
Gazing at us with loving eyes,
Thanking us for opening their tomb,
Yet still they seemed so cozy and nice
Enshrined in the crystal ice,
While all else were drooping dead
Gaily they held up their head.

===Chinese===
有一次，我们在雪下挖掘，
正值加拿大严冬，看哪！
令我们欣喜而惊奇的是，
我们看见几朵盛开的紫罗兰，
以深情的目光凝望着我们，
感谢我们打开了它们的坟墓，
然而它们仍显得那么舒适美好，
安卧在晶莹的冰中，
当万物都凋零枯萎时，
它们却欢快地昂起头来。

## Patriotic Ode / 爱国颂歌

===Original===
Written at the time of the last excitement on the Niagara Frontier.

Rejoice, rejoice, we all do stand,
United in one mighty band;
No traitors in our land we find,
All one in heart, all one in mind;
Resolute in their opinion,
None shall conquer our Dominion;
For every man with dauntless mien
Will rally round our flag and Queen.

===Chinese===
作于尼亚加拉边境最近一次骚动之际。

欢欣吧，欢欣，我们屹立不倒，
团结成一支强大的队伍；
我们的国土中没有叛徒，
心心相连，意志一致；
信念坚定，矢志不移，
无人能征服我们的自治领；
因为每个人都将无所畏惧，
团结在我们的旗帜与女王周围。

## Fight of a Buffalo with Wolves / 野牛大战群狼

===Original===
We were so deeply impressed with the courage displayed by a buffalo in a prose tale that we transposed the description into verse.

A buffalo, lord of the plain,
With massive neck and mighty mane,
While from his herd he slowly strays,
He on green herbage calm doth graze,
And when at last he lifts his eyes
A savage wolf he soon espies,
But scarcely deigns to turn his head
For it inspires him with no dread,
He knows the wolf is treacherous foe
But feels he soon could lay him low,

===Chinese===
一篇散文故事中野牛展现的勇气令我们深受感动，遂将那段描写转为诗歌。

一头野牛，平原之王，
颈项粗壮，鬃毛雄壮，
当他缓缓离群漫步，
平静地啃食青草，
当他终于抬起目光，
很快发现一只凶残的野狼，
却几乎不屑于转头，
因为它不令他畏惧，
他知道野狼是阴险的敌人，
但自信很快就能将它击倒。

===Original===
A moment more and there's a pair
Whose savage eyes do on him glare,
But with contempt them both he scorns
Unworthy of his powerful horns;
Their numbers soon do multiply
But the whole pack he doth defy,
He could bound quickly o'er the plain
And his own herd could soon regain;

===Chinese===
片刻之间又多了一对，
以凶残的目光瞪着他，
但他以蔑视对待二者，
它们不配他有力的犄角；
它们的数量迅速增多，
但他藐视整群野狼，
他本可飞速奔过平原，
很快回到自己的牛群身旁。

===Original===
His foes they now are full a score
With lolling tongues pant for his gore,
He hears their teeth all loudly gnash
So eager his big bones to crash,
On every side they him infest,
The north, the south, the east, the west
Fierce rage doth now gleam from his eye,
Resolved to conquer or to die,
'Round him they yelp and howl and growl,
He glares on them with angry scowl,
They circle closer him around,
He roars and springs with mighty bound,
And of his powers gives ample proof,
Felling them with horn and hoof,

===Chinese===
他的敌人现在足有二十只，
吐着舌头渴望他的血肉，
他听见它们牙齿大声咬合，
急切地要碾碎他的粗骨。
四面八方它们围攻他，
北、南、东、西，
凶猛的怒火在他眼中燃烧，
决心不胜即死。
它们围着他吠叫、嚎叫、咆哮，
他怒目而视，愤怒狰狞，
它们越缩越近围住他，
他怒吼着纵身猛扑，
充分展示他的力量，
用犄角和铁蹄将它们击倒。

===Original===
Though some lay dead upon the plain,
Yet their attack was not in vain,
For they have tasted of his blood,
Resolved it soon shall pour a flood,
He feels that they have torn his hide
And streams gush from each limb and side,
He rushes on them in despair
And tosses them full high in air,
But others rush on him and pull
Down to the earth that glorious bull;

===Chinese===
虽然有些倒在了平原上，
但它们的攻击并非徒劳，
因为它们已尝到他的鲜血，
决意让它如洪水般倾泻。
他感到它们撕裂了他的皮毛，
血液从每条腿和身侧涌出，
他绝望地冲向它们，
将它们高高抛向空中，
但其他野狼扑上来撕扯，
将那头光荣的公牛拖倒在地。

===Original===
On the flesh of this noble beast
Their bloody jaws they soon do feast,
Full worthy of a better fate
Far from his herd and his dear mate,
Who now do look for him in vain
His bones do whiten now the plain.

===Chinese===
在这头高贵野兽的血肉上，
它们血腥的嘴巴开始饱餐，
他本配得上更好的命运，
却远离了他的牛群和爱侣，
如今她在徒然地寻找他，
他的白骨散落在平原上。

## Disaster to Steamer Victoria at London / 伦敦维多利亚号汽船灾难

===Original===
At London Thames is a broad stream,
Which was the scene of a sad theme,
A fragile steamer there did play,
O'ercrowded on a Queen's Birthday,
While all on board was bright and gay,
But soon 'neath the cold waters lay,
Naught but forms of lifeless clay,
Which made, alas! sad month of May.

===Chinese===
在伦敦，泰晤士河是一条宽阔的河流，
那里上演了一场悲惨的事件，
一艘脆弱的汽船在河上嬉游，
在女王诞辰日过度拥挤，
船上的人们曾欢声笑语，
但很快沉入冰冷的水下，
只留下一具具无生命的躯壳，
唉！让五月变得如此悲伤。

## Fight with a Bear in the Northwest / 西北大战黑熊

===Original===
The following appeared in Truth in the form of a prose tale of considerable length. We have concentrated the essence thereof into the few verses below. It is a tale of the Canadian Northwest, during the times of the Hudson Bay Company's rule.

Two youths, employed at the fur fort,
Resolved to have half-day of sport;
From Jasper House, in the far north,
For game, they joyous issued forth.

===Chinese===
以下故事曾以相当长的散文形式刊登在《真理报》（Truth）上。我们将其精华浓缩成下面的几节诗句。这是一个加拿大西北地区的故事，发生在哈德逊湾公司（Hudson Bay Company）统治时期。

两个青年受雇于皮毛堡，
决心享受半天的狩猎时光；
从遥远北方的贾斯珀堡（Jasper House），
他们欢天喜地出发猎取猎物。

===Original===
The factor of the Hudson Bay
Granted them a few hours' play,
And it was in cold winter time,
When thick on lake was glassy rime;
But beneath, o'er all their route,
They saw below big speckled trout.

With hatchet, ice they did clear
And the beauteous trout did spear,
For they were longing for a dish
Of this sweet and savory fish,
And they caught many, too, with bait,
For in the day it was not late,
But it was cloudy with snow storm;
Yet they saw the noble form,
While the hurricane did blow,
Of a fine, noble buffalo,
So they resolved to have a treat
Of the delicious buffalo meat;
But he did on his haunches rear—
To their surprise it was a bear.

===Chinese===
哈德逊湾的驻地官，
给了他们几小时的消遣，
那是在寒冷的冬日，
湖面上结着厚厚的冰霜；
但在冰下，沿着他们一路，
他们看见底下有大条斑点鳟鱼。

他们用斧头凿开冰面，
用矛刺中美丽的鳟鱼，
因为他们渴望一顿美味，
品尝这甘甜可口的鱼。
他们用鱼饵也钓到了许多，
因为天色尚未太晚，
但天色阴沉，暴风雪将至；
他们却看见一个雄伟的身影，
在飓风呼啸中出现——
一头健美的野牛。
于是他们决定享用一顿，
美味的牛肉大餐；
但它后腿直立起来——
令他们惊讶的是，那是一头黑熊。

===Original===
The lake being smooth, the youths did skate,
Resolved for to seal his fate;
A pistol shot made bruin roar,
And from him trickled drops of gore.

They round him skate, and fresh blood drew
When they at him the hatchet threw;
He chased one and then the other,
For men on skates did him bother.

But the bold Scottish lad, McBeath,
Alas! he nearly met his death,
When he so boldly did press near
To probe him with the sharp fish spear,

===Chinese===
湖面平滑，两个青年滑起了冰，
决心了结这头熊的性命；
一声手枪响，布鲁因（Bruin）怒吼，
鲜血从它身上滴落。

他们绕着它滑行，鲜血再涌，
当他们向它投掷斧头时；
它追赶这个又追赶那个，
穿冰鞋的人让它烦扰不堪。

但勇敢的苏格兰小伙子麦克比斯（McBeath），
唉！他差点丢了性命，
当他大胆地逼近，
用锋利的鱼矛刺探它时，

===Original===
For with fell swoop the paws of bruin
To him they almost did bring ruin;
But he so dexterously did skate
That he escaped such horrid fate.

And now they skated back to fort
For aid for to renew the sport,
They knew their game was no trifle
So they secured a trusty rifle.

Returning he had fled to wood,
But they tracked him with his blood,
For they did not yet despair
But that they would capture bear.

===Chinese===
因为布鲁因的利爪猛扑而下，
几乎将他毁灭；
但他身手敏捷地滑行，
逃脱了那可怕的命运。

他们现在滑回堡垒，
寻找帮手再续这场搏斗，
他们知道猎物非同小可，
于是带上了一支可靠的步枪。

返回时它已逃入林中，
但他们循着血迹追踪，
因为他们并未绝望，
相信一定能捕获这头熊。

===Original===
They saw the elder bushes sway
While he did force through them his way,
And heard the brushwood loudly snap,
Where he went through he left a gap.

They skated swiftly o'er the ice
And were near brushwood in a trice;
Full soon the savage beast is slain,
With rifle bullet in his brain.

And now these hunters do take pride
In skin as large as buffalo hide,
For Indian stripped the skin of bear
And left the carcass in his lair.

===Chinese===
他们看到接骨木丛摇曳，
它正强行穿过灌木丛，
听到灌木噼啪折断，
它经过之处留下一道缺口。

他们飞速在冰上滑行，
转眼便来到灌木丛旁；
凶猛的野兽很快被击毙，
一颗步枪子弹射入它的脑海。

猎人们为此深感自豪，
剥下一张堪比水牛皮的大熊皮，
印第安人剥下熊皮，
将尸骨留在了它的巢穴中。

## Canadian Sports and Games and Plays / 加拿大的运动、游戏与娱乐

===Original===
Burns sang of joys of Halloween
But in Canada is often seen
By far more jolly times than these
At logging raising, paring bees,
For here the youth is not afraid
To trip it with a pretty maid,
For this at night is his reward
For working at the bee so hard,
And oft times till the break of day
At forfeits they will merry play,
For he doth win e'en though he miss,
If from sweet lass he gets a kiss,
But in its place doth justly prize
His tea and cakes and pumpkin pies.

===Chinese===
彭斯（Burns）歌唱万圣节的欢乐，
但在加拿大常可见到，
远比那更为欢快的时光——
伐木聚会与削皮蜂会上，
这里的年轻人不惧怕，
与漂亮姑娘翩翩起舞，
这是他夜晚的奖赏，
因为他白天在蜂会上辛苦劳作。
他们常常欢乐嬉戏直到天明，
玩着罚物游戏，
即使输了他也有所得，
若甜美的姑娘给他一个吻，
他便觉得胜过一切，
品着茶、吃着蛋糕和南瓜派。

===Original===
When winter comes it brings no gloom
But makes fresh pleasures spring and bloom,
For when the youth longs for a bride
He gives his girl a grand sleigh ride,
Which to them both doth pleasures bring
While merry sleigh bells cheery ring,
And with the fair maid of his choice
He graceful skates with her on ice,
Charming mode of locomotion
Gliding o'er a polished ocean,
Such joys they soon do love evolve,
And they on union do resolve,
He is happy with his chosen,
For warm love gets never frozen.

===Chinese===
冬天来临不带忧郁，
却让新的欢乐涌现绽放，
当小伙子渴望娶新娘，
他便载着姑娘坐雪橇，
给他们双方带来欢乐，
欢快的雪橇铃铛叮当响。
与他中意的美丽姑娘，
他在冰上优雅地滑冰，
这迷人的行进方式，
滑过如镜的冰面海洋，
这些欢乐很快催生爱情，
他们决心共结连理，
他与心上人幸福美满，
因为温暖的爱情永不结冰。

===Original===
And young folks oft they do take pride,
How swift they down the hill can glide,
And they bravely dare the frost king
So they may enjoy the coasting,
Each striving for to lead the van
In the swift shooting toboggan.

And on the ice men love to hurl
The polished blocks to skilful curl,
And curlers all do proudly claim
Their's is a manly healthy game,
And in Canadians you trace
A generous, hardy and brave race.

===Chinese===
年轻人常以此为豪——
他们滑下山坡何其迅猛，
他们勇敢地挑战霜冻之王，
只为享受滑雪橇的乐趣，
每个人都力争领先，
在飞驰的平底雪橇上。

人们喜欢在冰上投掷，
磨光的冰壶精准地滑向圆心，
冰壶选手们自豪地宣称，
他们的运动刚健有益，
从加拿大人身上你能看出，
这是一个慷慨、坚韧而勇敢的民族。

===Original===
And brilliant as a fairy hall
Is scenes on ice at carnival,
Before the gale in an ice boat
It swiftly o'er the ice doth float,
The sensation is you fly
Like lightning shooting through the sky.

In summer time the youth do toss
The baseball and do play lacrosse,
And tradition doth for it claim
That 'tis an ancient Indian game,
And if a foe invade we can,
Drive them back with clubs Canadian.

===Chinese===
如仙宫般辉煌灿烂，
那是冰上狂欢的场景，
在大风中乘坐冰帆船，
它在冰面上飞速掠过，
那感觉犹如飞翔，
像闪电划过天际。

夏日里年轻人投掷棒球，
打起长曲棍球，
传说声称它是，
古老的印第安游戏，
若有敌人来犯我们能，
用加拿大球棒将他们击退。

## Lines on Methodist Union / 卫理公会联合颂

===Original===
Sept. 1883, whereby the whole of the churches of that denomination were united into one body.

A pleasing sight to-day we see,
Four churches joined in harmony,
There difference was but trivial,
But strove each other to outrival.

In friendship now they do unite,
And Satan only they do fight,
And they'll plant churches in North West,
Where they can serve the Lord the best.

===Chinese===
1883年9月，该教派所有教会合并为一个整体。

今天我们看到一幅喜人的景象，
四座教会和谐地联合，
它们之间的分歧微不足道，
却曾互相竞争高下。

如今它们在友谊中团结，
只与撒旦作战，
它们将在西北地区建立教堂，
在那里最好地侍奉主。

## Niagara's Charms and Death of Capt. Webb / 尼亚加拉的魅力与韦布船长之死

===Original===
Gazing on rapids mighty sea,
Struggling fiercely to be free,
But drawn downwards in its course
By gravitation's wondrous force,
O'er those perpendicular walls,
Hurled 'mong mighty rocks it falls,
Causing the earth to throb and shake
Like to the tremor of earthquake.

===Chinese===
凝望那急流的汪洋大海，
奋力挣扎着要获得自由，
但在重力的奇妙作用下，
被牵引向下奔涌，
越过那陡峭的崖壁，
坠入巨石丛中，
大地为之震颤摇晃，
如同地震的颤动。

===Original===
Thus the world's greatest wonder
Reverberates like peals of thunder,
Enshrined with mist and beauteous glow
Of varied tints of the rainbow,
Most glorious sight the human eye
Hath ever seen beneath the sky,
Along these banks none ever trod
But did feel grateful to his God,
For lavishing with bounteous hand
Glories majestic and so grand.

===Chinese===
世界最伟大的奇观就这样，
如雷鸣般轰然回响，
雾霭中笼罩着美丽的光晕，
变幻着彩虹的万千色彩，
这是人眼在天穹之下，
曾见过的最壮丽的景象，
沿这些河岸走过的人们，
无不对上帝心怀感恩，
感谢他以慷慨之手，
赐予如此庄严宏伟的荣光。

===Original===
The foaming billows soon are seen
Transformed into a beauteous green,
Plunged by whirlpools dread commotion
It becomes a seething ocean,
Where furies join in surging dance
From centre to circumference,
This is the favorite abode
Of Neptune, mightiest sea God,
He hath decreed none shall survive
Who will into this vortex dive.

===Chinese===
泡沫翻涌的波浪很快呈现，
变幻为美丽的碧绿，
被漩涡的恐怖激流搅动，
化为翻腾的海洋，
复仇女神在其中狂舞翻涌，
从中心到边缘，
这里是海神涅普顿（Neptune），
最伟大的海之神最喜欢的居所，
他已降下旨意：凡跳入此漩涡者，
无人能够生还。

===Original===
Webb swam the English channel brave,
Like seabird he did love to lave
His breast upon the mightiest wave,
Alas, found here a watery grave;
Torrent onward rushes frantic
On its course to the Atlantic,
But on its way doth gently flow
Through blue lake Ontario,
Rejoicing on its way it smiles,
Kissing the shores of Thousand Isles,
Mingling with St. Lawrence motion,
It soon is blended with the ocean.

===Chinese===
韦布（Webb）曾勇渡英吉利海峡，
如海鸟般喜爱在，
最汹涌的波涛中击水，
唉，却在此找到水中的坟墓；
急流疯狂地向前奔涌，
奔向大西洋的途中，
它在安大略湖（Lake Ontario）中，
温柔地流淌，
它欢笑着一路前行，
亲吻千岛（Thousand Isles）的海岸，
汇入圣劳伦斯河（St. Lawrence）的水流，
不久便融入了大海。

## St. Catharines / 圣凯瑟琳斯

===Original===
Lines read at the Welland House, St. Catharines, at a banquet given to the members in attendance at the Odd Fellows' Grand Lodge.

St. Catharines famed for mineral waters
And for the beauty of her daughters,
For some do worship at the shrines
Of the fair St. Catharines.

St. Catharines your greatness you inherit
From the genius of a Merritt,
You still would be a village dreary
But for this canal from lake Erie.

===Chinese===
在圣凯瑟琳斯的韦兰酒店，独立共济会（Odd Fellows）大会成员宴会上朗诵。

圣凯瑟琳斯（St. Catharines）以矿泉闻名，
也以她女儿们的美貌著称，
有些人朝拜圣殿，
为的是美丽的圣凯瑟琳斯。

圣凯瑟琳斯，你的伟大继承自，
梅里特（Merritt）的天才，
若非伊利湖（Lake Erie）的这条运河，
你仍会是沉闷的村庄。

===Original===
For on its bosom there doth float
Full many a ship and steamboat,
Brings world's commerce to your doors
And many gifts on you it pours.

Among its many great rewards
It gives you dry docks and ship yards,
To drive your mills great water power
It doth give you as a dower.

===Chinese===
因为它的胸怀上漂浮着，
众多的船舶和汽船，
将世界的贸易带到你的门前，
将丰厚的赐予倾洒在你身上。

在它诸多丰厚的回报中，
它赐予你干船坞和造船厂，
驱动你磨坊的巨量水力，
作为嫁妆赐予了你。

===Original===
Since we above lines did compose,
Through new canal vast stream it flows,
The lock gates at the hill at Thorold
Can not be equaled in the world.

===Chinese===
自从我们写下上面的诗句，
通过新运河巨流奔涌，
索罗德（Thorold）山丘上的闸门，
举世无双。

## Lines on Credit Valley Trip / 克雷迪特河谷之旅

===Original===
The Credit Valley Railway Company having placed a car at the service of the council we were kindly invited to accompany them to Toronto, a short time after the opening of the road.

Whene'er we take a tour abroad
We love to travel o'er new road,
Where scenery to us is new
And landscape pleasing to the view,
When invited for to rally
And take a trip on Credit Valley,
We resolved for to afford
A day with Council and School Board,
For to view the rural charms
Of hills and dales and fertile farms,

===Chinese===
克雷迪特河谷铁路公司（Credit Valley Railway Company）提供了一节车厢供议会使用，我们盛情获邀在铁路开通后不久随同前往多伦多。

每当我们外出旅行，
我们喜欢走新路，
那里景色对我们焕然一新，
风光令人赏心悦目，
受邀相聚共游，
沿克雷迪特河谷旅行，
我们决定抽出一天，
与议会和教育委员会同行，
去欣赏乡间的迷人风景——
山丘、谷地与肥沃的农场。

===Original===
With joy we saw the sunbeams gleam
On Grand River beauteous stream,
And those perpendicular walls
Of rock, like old baronial halls,
We saw the great lake ebb and flow,
And queen city of Ontario,
While some enjoyed the genial smile
Of Hanlon on his lake girt isle,
Returning home each one exclaims
"Happiest spot is banks of Thames."

===Chinese===
我们欣喜地看到阳光闪烁，
照在大河（Grand River）美丽的水面上，
那些陡峭的岩壁，
如同古老的贵族府邸，
我们看到大湖的潮涨潮落，
以及安大略的女王之城，
有些人则享受着，
汉隆（Hanlon）在湖中小岛上的和蔼微笑，
回家的路上每个人都感叹：
"最幸福的地方是泰晤士河畔。"

## Lines on Canadian Hunter / 加拿大猎人吟

===Original===
Of Kentucky's great hunter bold
Old Daniel Boone oft tales are told,
Of wild beasts he had no fear,
But dangers loved that pioneer.

Canada has hunters many,
Yet perhaps there is not any
For skill and boldness can compare
With our own Daniel Hebner.

In youth he was both tall and strong,
And supple as a willow thong,
He never fled from savage bear,
Though bruin on hind legs would rear.

===Chinese===
肯塔基伟大的猎人丹尼尔·布恩（Daniel Boone）的故事常被讲述，
他对野兽无所畏惧，
热爱危险的那位先驱者。

加拿大有许多猎人，
但也许无人能在，
技艺与勇气上比肩，
我们自己的丹尼尔·赫布纳（Daniel Hebner）。

他年轻时高大强壮，
柔韧如柳条一般，
他从不在凶残的熊面前逃跑，
哪怕布鲁因直立起后腿。

===Original===
In hunting mink, or fox or coon,
He was a second Daniel Boone,
His rifle oft brought down the deer,
Which to his table brought good cheer.

But through his life his highest aim
Was to kill the savage game,
To track the wild cat to its laire
And see its eyes so fiercely glare.

===Chinese===
猎水貂、狐狸或浣熊时，
他便是第二个丹尼尔·布恩，
他的步枪常常击倒鹿，
为餐桌带来丰盛佳肴。

但他一生最高的目标，
是猎杀凶猛的野兽，
追踪野猫到它的巢穴，
看它凶狠地怒目而视。

===Original===
But he oft longs for a cut ham,
Sweet as from bear near to Putnam,
For he waged his fiercest war
In big swamp of Dorchester.

Now in the winter Dan he rides
Warm 'mong his bear and coon skin hides,
He lets the younger men now snare
The beaver, muskrat and otter.

===Chinese===
但他常渴望一块切好的火腿，
像在普特南（Putnam）附近猎到的熊肉那样鲜美，
因为他曾在那里最激烈的战斗中，
在多切斯特（Dorchester）的大沼泽里搏杀。

如今冬天丹骑马出行，
裹着温暖的熊皮和浣熊皮，
他让年轻的小伙子们去设陷阱，
猎海狸、麝鼠和水獭。

## Hope for Ingersoll / 英杰赛尔的希望

===Original===
Of Ingersoll we're justly proud,
Though o'er it hath hung a cloud;
It was heavy, dark, profound,
Weighing o'er ten thousand pound.

But now the clouds do disappear,
And the sun is shining clear—
Now, with pleasure, we do behold,
Our railroad bonds are good as gold.

The people now need not despair,
But thank our Council and our Mayor.

===Chinese===
我们有充分的理由为英杰赛尔（Ingersoll）自豪，
尽管曾有阴云笼罩于其上；
它厚重、黑暗、深沉，
重逾一万英镑。

但如今阴云已消散，
阳光明媚地照耀——
如今我们欣然看见，
我们的铁路债券坚如黄金。

人们现在不必绝望，
只需感谢我们的议会和市长。

## Canadian Rivers and Lakes / 加拿大的河流与湖泊

===Original===
We have here a sight as fair
As bonnie Doon or banks of Ayr,
Like modest worth meandering slow
The quiet waters gently flow,
Rose, thistle, shamrock, all combine,
Around the maple leaf to twine,
Whose outstretched arms so gigantic
Clasp Pacific and Atlantic,
Embracing lakes like burnished gold,
With joy a Shakespeare might behold,
For either Poet Burns or Moore
Such scenery they would adore.

===Chinese===
我们这里有与多恩河（Doon）或艾尔河畔（Airs）一样美丽的景色，
如谦逊的美德缓缓蜿蜒，
宁静的水流温柔地流淌，
玫瑰、蓟草、三叶草，
都汇聚在一起，
围绕着枫叶盘旋缠绕，
它伸展的巨臂如此庞大，
环抱太平洋和大西洋，
拥抱着如抛光黄金般的湖泊，
莎士比亚见了也会欣喜，
无论是诗人彭斯还是穆尔（Moore），
都会为这景色倾倒。

## Life in the Woods / 林中岁月

===Original===
Lines on the struggles of the early settlers.

Canada hath wealthy yeomen
Whose fathers overcome the foemen,
The enemy they boldly slew
Was mighty forests they did hew,
And where they burned heaps of slain
Their sons now reap the golden grain,
But in the region of Northwest
With prairie farms they are blest.

===Chinese===
关于早期定居者奋斗的诗句。

加拿大有富裕的自耕农，
他们的父辈战胜了敌人，
他们勇敢斩杀的敌人，
是他们劈砍的茫茫林海，
在他们焚烧的战利品堆上，
如今他们的儿子收获着金色的谷物，
而在西北地区，
他们享有草原农场的福泽。

===Original===
Though this to them it may seem good
Yet many blessings come from wood,
It shelters you from the fierce storm
And in the winter keeps you warm,
For one who hath his forest trees
He builds his house and barn with ease,
And how quick he gets from thence
Timber for bridge and for his fence.

===Chinese===
虽然草原对他们似乎更好，
但森林也带来许多恩赐，
它为你遮挡猛烈的风暴，
在冬天为你保暖保暖，
对于拥有森林树木的人，
他能轻松建造房屋和谷仓，
他能多快从中获取木材，
来建造桥梁和围栏。

## The Joys of Prairie Farmers / 草原农夫的喜悦

===Original===
1884.

We let Ontario farmers sing
About the joys the woods do bring,
But we in regions of Northwest
Do think prairie farms the best,
For those poor men who swing the axe
On their strength 'tis a heavy tax,
For several years they naught can grow
While from the first we plow and sow,
And while we plow we don't get thumps
By running it against the stumps,

===Chinese===
1884年。

让安大略的农民歌唱吧，
歌唱森林带来的欢乐，
但我们身处西北地区的人认为，
草原农场才是最好的，
那些挥动斧头的可怜人，
体力上承受着沉重的负担，
好几年他们什么都种不了，
而我们从一开始就能耕种播种，
我们耕作时不会撞到树桩，
也不会被震得东倒西歪。

===Original===
And where wild Buffalo now doth feed
There very soon they'll sow the seed,
Where Indian wigwams now do stand
Will be the site of cities grand,
And where the deer and wolf doth roam
Millions will build each happy home,
So quick as if by magic wand
They will arise o'er the whole land,
But this one fact we won't deny
Ontario she can supply,
For so skilfully she doth invent
Each agricultural implement.

===Chinese===
野牛现在觅食的地方，
很快他们就会播下种子，
印第安棚屋现在矗立的地方，
将成为宏伟城市的所在地，
鹿和狼漫游的地方，
数百万人将建造各自的幸福家园，
如此迅速犹如魔法棒一挥，
它们将遍布整片土地，
但有一点我们不会否认，
安大略能为他们提供物资，
因为她如此巧妙地发明了，
各种农业器械。

## Nova Scotia / 新斯科舍

===Original===
Lines written when Nova Scotia was threatening to withdraw from the Confederation.

If you are sulky, Nova Scotia,
We'll gladly let you float away
From out our Confederation;
You sicken us with sly agitation.

If any more our patience you do tax
We'll let you go to Halifax.

===Chinese===
作于新斯科舍（Nova Scotia）威胁退出联邦之际。

如果你闹脾气，新斯科舍，
我们很乐意让你漂走，
从我们的联邦中离去；
你狡猾的煽动令我们厌烦。

若你再考验我们的耐心，
我们就让你去哈利法克斯（Halifax）。

## Port Stanley / 斯坦利港

===Original===
Lines composed on an excursion to Port Stanley, 1883.

In winter time 'tis sad and dreary
For to gaze on stormy Erie,
But here in summer time this port
It is a fashionable resort,
For then it is always cheery
For to gaze upon Lake Erie.

===Chinese===
1883年斯坦利港（Port Stanley）远足时所作。

冬日里眺望暴风雨中的伊利湖（Lake Erie），
令人悲伤而忧郁，
但夏天这里是时尚的度假胜地，
眺望伊利湖总是令人愉快。

===Original===
Or on the steamer you can sail
All independent of the gale,
Or here the youth can ply the oar
And view the fast receding shore,
And be happy with his dearie
On the bosom of Lake Erie.

No one here need ever weary
On the borders of Lake Erie,
With quadrille parties at Stanley
And games and sports all so manly,
Or bathe in waves with friends near thee,
You fear no storms of Lake Erie.

===Chinese===
或乘汽船航行，
完全不受大风的影响，
或者年轻人可以划桨，
看着岸边飞速退去，
与他的爱人在伊利湖，
宽阔的胸膛上享受甜蜜。

在伊利湖畔没有人会厌倦，
斯坦利有方阵舞会，
和各种阳刚的运动游戏，
或与朋友在浪花中嬉水，
你无惧伊利湖的风暴。

## Donald Ross / 唐纳德·罗斯

===Original===
A Scottish-Canadian tale.

By the side of a moss
Lived young Donald Ross,
Among the heathery hills
And the mountain rills,
In a snug little cot
Content with his lot
He never knew sorrow
With his wife and wee Flora.

===Chinese===
一个苏格兰-加拿大的故事。

在苔藓覆盖的路旁，
住着年轻的唐纳德·罗斯（Donald Ross），
在石楠遍野的群山间，
在山间溪流旁，
住在一间温馨的小屋里，
他满足于自己的命运，
从未尝过悲伤的滋味，
与妻子和小弗洛拉（Flora）在一起。

===Original===
But an order went forth
O'er the land of the north,
To burn many a home
So the wild deer might roam,
With grief he then did toss
Every night Donald Ross,
And sad seemed the morrow
For his wife and sma' Flora.

===Chinese===
但一道命令传遍，
北方的土地，
要焚毁许多家园，
以便野鹿自由漫游，
唐纳德·罗斯每夜辗转难眠，
满心悲苦，
为妻子和小小的弗洛拉，
明天似乎也如此悲伤。

===Original===
O it was a cruel deed
But nobles do not heed
The sorrows of the poor
Drove on a barren moor,
Where he wove a wreath
Of the blooming heath,
For to crown with glory
The brow of little Flory.

He then bade farewell
To his mountain dell,
Where his fathers appears
Had lived a thousand years,
With their few goats and sheep
Which feed on hills so steep,
O it was a sad story
For bonnie little Flory.

===Chinese===
噢，这是多么残忍的行为，
但贵族们不顾穷人的苦难，
将他们驱赶到贫瘠的荒原，
在那里他编织了一个花环，
用盛开的石楠花，
为了给小弗洛里的额头，
戴上荣耀的花冠。

然后他告别了，
他的山谷故乡，
他的先辈们似乎，
在那里生活了一千年，
带着他们稀少的山羊和绵羊，
在陡峭的山坡上放牧，
噢，这是多么悲伤的故事，
为了美丽的小弗洛拉。

===Original===
He sought a distant strand,
In Canada bought land,
To him a glorious charm
To view his own broad farm,
His horses and his cows,
Cultivators and plows,
And now his daughter Flora
She is the flower of Zorra.

===Chinese===
他远渡重洋，
在加拿大买了土地，
看到自己广阔的农场，
对他而言是多么荣耀的魅力，
他的马匹和牛群，
耕犁和犁铧，
如今他的女儿弗洛拉，
是佐拉（Zorra）的骄傲之花。

## Port Burwell / 伯韦尔港

===Original===
About a quarter of a century ago, when Port Burwell was a busy, thriving place, several friends accompanied me to the port to attend a concert, we all being specially invited. It was in the winter, and there had been a race on the ice, in which the Port Burwell horse took the lead. We gave a local piece of some length, but only retain now a few lines. The people at the concert were highly pleased with the effusion.

In winter time who here resort,
To pay a visit to your busy port,
They must be clad in fur well,
For it blows cold at Burwell;
But when you wish to trot your horse
You make Lake Erie your race course,
And we believe at every heat
All other horses you do beat.

===Chinese===
大约四分之一个世纪前，伯韦尔港（Port Burwell）还是一个繁忙繁荣的地方，几位朋友陪我前往该港参加音乐会，我们都受到了特别邀请。那是在冬天，冰上举行了一场赛跑，伯韦尔港的马匹夺了头名。我们表演了一首较长的地方作品，但如今只保留了几行。音乐会的观众对这首作品非常满意。

冬日里谁来此地，
造访你繁忙的港口，
他们必须穿好皮衣，
因为伯韦尔寒风凛冽；
但若你想策马奔跑，
就把伊利湖当赛场，
我们相信每一场竞速，
你都能胜过所有马匹。

## Lines on T. D. McGee / T. D. 麦吉颂

===Original===
Having been kindly invited as a member of the Mechanics' Institute some 20 years ago by the late Jeremiah O'Neill, Esq., to meet that gentleman in company with a number of our townsmen, when Mr. McGee was rising from the table the chair being new stuck to him, and it being near a general election he very wittily remarked that he hoped the people of Montreal would be as anxious to retain him in his seat as the people here are. We wrote the following lines at the time, the last verse was added afterwards.

DeArcy McGee,
All compliment thee,
The hope of the land
On your lecture so grand.

Though that is your forte,
Oh give us the sport
Of an hour of your chat,
Then we'll laugh and grow fat.

For none but the vile
Could 'ere cease to smile,
When near to thee
So brilliant and free.

Plant of green Erin's isle,
Long in Canadian soil,
May you take deep root
And bear much noble fruit.

Our hopes were in vain,
Alas he is slain,
By a crankish hand
The flower of the land.

===Chinese===
大约二十年前，已故的杰里迈亚·奥尼尔（Jeremiah O'Neill）先生以机械学院成员的身份盛情邀请我，与几位镇上居民一起会见麦吉先生。麦吉先生起身离席时，那把新椅子粘住了他，又恰逢大选临近，他极为机智地说："我希望蒙特利尔的人民能像这里的人们一样，渴望留住他的席位。"我们当时写下了以下诗句，最后一节是后来补加的。

德阿西·麦吉（DeArcy McGee），
人人都赞美你，
这片土地的希望，
寄托于你精彩的演讲。

虽然那是你的特长，
哦，请给我们一小时的闲聊，
让我们欢笑、开怀，
因为唯有卑劣之徒，
才会在你身边停止微笑，
你如此才华横溢、自由洒脱。

绿色艾琳岛（Erin）的幼苗，
在加拿大土壤中扎根已久，
愿你深扎根基，
结出高贵的果实。

我们的期望落空了，
唉，他被杀害了，
被一只疯狂之手，
夺去了这片土地的花魁。

## London Flood / 伦敦大洪水

===Original===
Pencil sketch of the ruin left by the London flood, July 11th, 1883.

From the long continuous rains
O'erflowing were the swamps and drains,
For each day had its heavy shower,
Torrents fell for many an hour;
At London where two branches join
It seem'd two furies did combine,
For to spread far both death and woe,
With their wild, raging overflow;

===Chinese===
1883年7月11日伦敦洪水所留废墟的素描。

连绵不断的雨水，
使沼泽和排水沟溢满，
因为每天都有一场暴雨，
大雨倾盆数小时不止；
在伦敦两条支流交汇处，
仿佛两个复仇女神联手，
要将死亡与苦难远远传播，
带着它们狂暴的泛滥。

===Original===
E'en houses did on waters float,
As though each had been built for boat,
And where was wealth and joy and bloom,
Soon naught but inmates of the tomb;
Flood o'erflowed both vale and ridges,
And swept railroads, dams and bridges,
A mother climbed in tree to save
Her infant from a watery grave,
But on the house you saw its blood
Where it was crushed 'gainst tree by flood;

===Chinese===
房屋在水面上漂浮，
仿佛每座都是为船而建，
曾经财富、欢乐与繁花似锦的地方，
很快只剩下墓穴的住客；
洪水漫过谷地和山脊，
冲毁了铁路、水坝和桥梁，
一位母亲爬上树去，
救她的婴儿免于溺亡，
但你在房屋上看到血迹——
它被洪水挤压在树上。

===Original===
Where cottages 'mong gardens stood
'Tis covered o'er with vile drift wood,
O'er flowers and bushes you may travel
For they are buried under gravel,
Or you may walk o'er barren sand,
The crops washed out and fertile land;
Two funerals we at once did see
Of one family who lost three;
No longer river's deep and wide
But gently flows to distant tide.

===Chinese===
花园中的小屋所在之处，
如今覆满了丑陋的浮木，
你可以走在花丛和灌木上方，
因为它们被埋在碎石之下，
或者你可以走在贫瘠的沙地上，
庄稼被冲走，肥沃的土地被毁；
我们同时看到两场葬礼，
一个家庭失去了三个人；
河流不再深广，
而是轻柔地流向远方的潮汐。

## London West / 西伦敦

===Original===
While the Thames meanders gently through the green pasture fields of Ingersoll, a pleasing picture to behold, how different, alas, is the feeling in London West, where the river is an object of dread and terror, neither pleasing to the eye or nostrils. As we have been living for the last quarter of a century on the edge of one of the tributary streams of the Thames and were once o'erwhelmed with ruin dire by a number of the dams giving way, we can sympathise with them. They are now built strong and substantial, and the ponds are an ornament to the town, as well as a source of wealth. The Caledonian Society, of Ingersoll, donated $50 to the flood sufferers.

The citizens of London West
Their patience oft is put to test
When they behold the various dams
Do cause the floods and the ice jams.

'Tis true that fiercer rages floods
Since country it was stript of woods,
Acid river it doth broader spread
With numerous tile drains quicker fed.

===Chinese===
泰晤士河在英杰赛尔的绿色牧场间蜿蜒流淌，是一幅赏心悦目的画面，但西伦敦的景象则截然不同，令人感叹——那里的河流是恐惧和可怕的对象，既不赏心也不悦目。由于我们在过去四分之一个世纪里一直住在泰晤士河一条支流的岸边，曾因多座水坝溃决而遭受过毁灭性的灾难，我们能感同身受。如今水坝修建得坚固而结实，池塘既是小镇的装饰，也是财富的来源。英杰赛尔的喀里多尼亚协会（Caledonian Society）向洪水受害者捐赠了五十美元。

西伦敦的市民们，
他们的耐心常受考验，
当他们看到各种水坝，
导致了洪水和冰堵。

确实洪水更加凶猛，
因为乡村的树木被砍光了，
酸性的河水蔓延更广，
无数的瓦管排水沟更快地供水。

===Original===
If they did raise embankment high
They might the raging floods defy.

Shall they with sadness gaze ever,
Or with gladness on the river?

River with dams it will not wed,
It wants no strangers in its bed,
And 'gainst them it will rage and fret
For 'tis no gentle rivulet.

===Chinese===
若他们修建高高的堤坝，
或许能抵御汹涌的洪水。

他们该永远悲伤地凝视，
还是欣然面对这条河？

河流不愿与水坝结合，
它的河床不欢迎陌生者，
它将对它们暴怒咆哮，
因为它不是温柔的小溪。

## Lines on Stratford / 斯特拉特福吟

===Original===
Our Canadian county Perth,
Commemorates great bard of earth,
Stratford and Avon both are here,
And they enshrine the name Shakespeare.

For here in Stratford every ward
Is named from dramas of great bard,
Here you may roam o'er Romeo,
Or glance on Juliet bestow.

And it is a railway centre,
Many a train doth here enter,
And railroad shops do men employ,
And gives them work and wealth and joy.

===Chinese===
我们的加拿大珀斯郡（Perth），
纪念着尘世的伟大诗人，
斯特拉特福（Stratford）与埃文河（Avon）都在此地，
它们珍藏着莎士比亚的名字。

因为在斯特拉特福每个区，
都以伟大诗人的戏剧命名，
你可以漫步罗密欧（Romeo）的地界，
或在朱丽叶（Juliet）的区域流连。

这里也是铁路枢纽，
许多列车在此汇聚，
铁路工厂雇用了工人，
给予他们工作、财富和欢乐。

## English Names on Canadian Thames / 加拿大泰晤士河上的英格兰地名

===Original===
England has given us the names
To adorn Canadian Thames,
And charms to them she has lent
In Oxford, Middlesex and Kent,
She Essex kisseth in her mouth,
And Scottish names, one north, one south,
And London now it justly claims
'Tis capital of vale of Thames,
And her strong castellated tower
Doth on the river frowning lower,
And Chatham is the river's port,
There slaves for freedom did resort,
And they did industrious toil,
And now many own the soil,
Stratford now shall be our theme,
On Avon tributary stream,

===Chinese===
英格兰赐予了我们这些名字，
装饰加拿大的泰晤牛河，
并赋予它们迷人的魅力——
牛津（Oxford）、米德尔塞克斯（Middlesex）与肯特（Kent），
她用唇吻了埃塞克斯（Essex），
还有苏格兰的名字，一南一北，
伦敦（London）如今理所当然地宣称，
它是泰晤士河谷的首府，
它那坚固的城堡塔楼，
在河上阴沉地俯视，
查塔姆（Chatham）是河上的港口，
那里曾有奴隶寻求自由，
他们辛勤地劳作，
如今许多人拥有了土地，
斯特拉特福将成为我们的主题，
在埃文河的支流上。

===Original===
And its clear waters it doth launch
Into the Thames northern branch,
Near that substantial stone town
St. Mary's with mills of renown,
Westward it winds past each town,
Growing broader as it flows down,
Onward it glides never weary,
Meandering so soft and cheery.

The sunbeam on the waters glance,
Skipping about in silvery dance,
From morn till eve the cattle feed
'Neath lofty elms along the mead.

===Chinese===
它清澈的水流奔涌而出，
汇入泰晤士河北支流，
靠近那座坚固的石头城——
圣玛丽（St. Mary's），以磨坊闻名，
它蜿蜒西行经过每座小镇，
越往下流越加宽阔，
它永不停歇地向前流淌，
蜿蜒得如此轻柔而欢快。

阳光在水面上闪耀，
如银色的舞步跳跃，
从清晨到傍晚，牛群在牧场上，
高大的榆树下觅食。

===Original===
And on its banks in warrior pride
The brave Tecumseh fought and died,
And it has now historic claims
The famous battle of the Thames.

Now soon the waters meet and pair
With the wavelets of St. Clair,
As maids when wed do lose their names,
No longer it is called the Thames.

Rejoicing on its way it smiles,
Kissing the shores of Thousand Isles,
Mingling with St. Lawrence motion,
It soon is blended with the ocean.

===Chinese===
在它的两岸，怀着战士的骄傲，
英勇的特库姆塞（Tecumseh）战斗并牺牲，
它如今拥有历史性的声名——
著名的泰晤士河战役。

如今水流很快汇合交融，
与圣克莱尔湖（St. Clair）的波浪相遇，
如同少女出嫁后改了名字，
它不再被称为泰晤士河。

它欢笑着一路前行，
亲吻千岛的海岸，
汇入圣劳伦斯河的水流，
不久便融入了大海。

## Lines on Beachville / 比奇维尔吟

===Original===
Of Beachville, village of the plain,
We now will sing a short refrain,
For here the Thames doth pleasant flow,
And charm to landscape doth bestow;
Though river here it is not deep,
Yet banks slope graceful up the steep,
And from the summit of the hills
You look down on the famed lime kilns,
And 'tis full worthy poet's rhyme
The whiteness of your pure white lime,
Your glory never shall be gone
While you have quarries of this stone,
In influence you yet will wax
With mills for flour and also flax.

===Chinese===
比奇维尔（Beachville），平原上的村庄，
我们现在将唱一首短歌，
因为泰晤士河在此愉快地流淌，
给这片风景增添了魅力；
虽然这里的河水不深，
但河岸优雅地斜向陡坡，
从山丘之巅，
你俯瞰着著名的石灰窑，
纯白石灰的洁白度，
足以配得上诗人的韵脚，
你的荣耀永不消逝，
只要你拥有这石头的采石场，
你的影响力还会增长，
有面粉厂和亚麻厂。

## Lines on Ingersoll / 英杰赛尔吟

===Original===
Ex-Mayor Thomas Brown may be considered the father of the town, he projected the first roads and bridges.

The Thames and tributary rills,
Here they do drive numerous mills,
Enabling millers to compete
To pay high price for oats and wheat.

Here streams do drive many a wheel
For to grind both flour and oatmeal,
And town will extend its boundaries
With its enterprising foundries.

For fine pianos town is famed
And highest rank for them is claimed,
And brighter days for it yet dawns
With its grand mansions and fine lawns,
And it has now the title grand
The capital of dairyland.

===Chinese===
前市长托马斯·布朗（Thomas Brown）可被视为本镇之父，他规划了第一批道路和桥梁。

泰晤士河与支流溪水，
在这里驱动着众多磨坊，
使磨坊主们能够竞争，
高价收购燕麦和小麦。

溪流在这里驱动许多轮子，
研磨面粉和燕麦片，
小镇将以其进取的铸造厂，
扩展自己的边界。

小镇以精美的钢琴闻名，
宣称拥有最高等级，
更光明的日子尚未到来——
有了宏伟的宅邸和美丽的草坪，
它如今拥有一个响亮的称号——
乳品之乡的首府。

## Lines on Woodstock / 伍德斯托克吟

===Original===
We fancy the lustre of the old town is reflected on the new. English Woodstock was a royal residence.

English Woodstock had a palace
Where the Queen in jealous malice
Slew romance's fairest flower,
Fair Rosamond in secret bower;
Our Woodstock pleasing county town,
This brings it both wealth and renown,
To your strong castle some are sent
To give them leisure to repent.

===Chinese===
我们想象古老城镇的光辉映照在新城上。英格兰的伍德斯托克曾是王室居所。

英格兰的伍德斯托克（Woodstock）有一座宫殿，
王后因嫉妒的恶意，
杀害了浪漫传说中最美的花——
美丽的罗莎蒙德（Rosamond），在秘密的闺房中；
我们的伍德斯托克是宜人的县城，
这为它带来了财富和声名，
有些被送到你坚固的城堡，
让他们有时间反省悔过。

===Original===
A charming vista you do view
Gazing on each street and avenue,
Mansions and lawn embowered 'mong trees
Where wealthy owners live at ease,
And through the air there sweetly floats
Harmonious Woodstock organ notes,
And men employment secure
In factory for furniture;

Old Oxford is a seat of knowledge,
Woodstock has a fine new college,
And farm implement work shops,
So farmers easy reap their crops;
The old court house is a disgrace,
Grand structure soon will take its place.

===Chinese===
你看到迷人的景色，
凝望每条街道和大道，
宅邸和草坪掩映在树荫中，
富有的主人悠闲地生活，
空气中飘荡着，
伍德斯托克管风琴和谐的音符，
人们在家具工厂里，
有了稳定的工作。

古老的牛津是知识的殿堂，
伍德斯托克有一所美丽的新学院，
还有农具工厂，
农民们轻松收割庄稼；
旧法院有碍观瞻，
宏伟的新建筑将很快取而代之。

## Lines on Embro and Zorra / 恩布罗与佐拉吟

===Original===
O'er various counties of the north,
When cruel order did go forth,
For to destroy many a home,
So that the wild deer free might roam;
The men of Sutherland and Ross,
The broad Atlantic they did cross,
Each seeking for a fertile farm,
These rolling lands for them had charm;
They ne'er desired again to roam,
Each happy in his woodland home,
Where middle branch of Thames doth flow
They built the village of Embro,
And it the hill tops now doth crown
Like its grand namesake Edina Town,
And good flour mills you here do find
And oats also they here do grind.

===Chinese===
在北方各郡之上，
当残酷的命令传下，
要摧毁许多家园，
以便野鹿自由漫游；
萨瑟兰（Sutherland）和罗斯（Ross）的人们，
横渡辽阔的大西洋，
各自寻找肥沃的农场，
这片起伏的土地对他们有吸引力；
他们再也不想漂泊，
各自在林中家园安乐，
在泰晤士河中支流经之处，
他们建造了恩布罗（Embro）村，
它如今冠冕般矗立在山顶，
如同它的伟大同名者——
爱丁堡（Edina Town）城，
这里有优质的面粉厂，
他们也在这里研磨燕麦。

## Lines on Salford / 索尔福德吟

===Original===
Lines Read at a Parsonage Opening at the Village where Ranney had once flourished, 1883.

Some do boast of their pedigrees,
But Salford's parent of the cheese,
Ranney, industrious and wise,
Here started this great enterprise.

He did work on the dairy plan,
While Farrington was factoryman,
Both of these men it well did please
To hear of progress making cheese.

The farmers are in cheerful mood,
For harvest all it has been good,
And all the grain was sown this spring
An abundant yield will bring.

And you can scarcely stow away
The yield of barley, oats and hay,
Such pasture it is seldom seen,
E'en now it is so fresh and green.

This beauteous colour nature decks,
While it insures you large milk cheques,
And certes you've much cause to praise,
For hogs and cattle that you raise.

===Chinese===
1883年在兰尼（Ranney）曾繁荣过的村庄，牧师住宅落成典礼上朗诵。

有些人炫耀自己的家世，
但索尔福德（Salford）是奶酪之母，
兰尼，勤劳而智慧，
在这里开创了这项伟大的事业。

他以奶业计划运营，
而法林顿（Farrington）是工厂主，
这两个人都很高兴，
听说奶酪制作取得了进展。

农民们心情愉快，
因为收获一切顺利，
今年春天播下的所有谷物，
将带来丰收。

你几乎无法存放，
大麦、燕麦和干草的收成，
这样的牧场很少见，
即使现在也如此清新翠丽。

大自然装点着这美丽的色彩，
同时为你带来丰厚的奶酪收入，
你确实有充分的理由赞美，
你饲养的猪和牛。

## Lines on Thamesford / 泰姆斯福德吟

===Original===
Delivered at Masonic concert, Thamesford—Grand Master Col. Moffat in the chair.

The middle branch of Thames doth flow
O'er pebble bed and it doth glow
And sparkle like silver in the sun,
As it through pasture lands doth run.

In dam is ample water stored,
To drive flour mills in Thamesford,
Besides the power of the stream,
Saw mills and flax are drove by steam.

Our mind it doth with pleasure fill,
To see fine brick church on each hill,
And that substantial one of stone
Owned by congregation of St. John.

===Chinese===
在泰姆斯福德（Thamesford）共济会音乐会上朗诵——莫法特（Moffat）上校总会长主持。

泰晤士河的中支流经，
在卵石河床上闪烁，
如阳光下的银光闪耀，
它穿过牧场流淌。

水坝中蓄有充足的水，
驱动泰姆斯福德的面粉厂，
除了水流的力量，
锯木厂和亚麻厂由蒸汽驱动。

我们的内心充满喜悦，
看到每座山丘上精美的砖砌教堂，
还有那座坚固的石头教堂，
属于圣约翰（St. John）教会会众。

## Farrington, 1866, Factory System / 法林顿，1866年，工厂体制

===Original===
The farmers they now all make rich
Since Farrington went to Norwich,
And the system first there began
Of making cheese on factory plan;
He came from Herkimer county,
To Canada he was a bounty;
Norwich village moved but slow,
Till railways made it quickly grow,
And industries here now take root,
The township's famous for its fruit.

===Chinese===
自从法林顿（Farrington）去了诺威奇（Norwich），
农民们如今都富裕起来，
工厂化制作奶酪的体制，
最初在那里开创；
他来自赫基默县（Herkimer county），
对加拿大而言他是天赐之福；
诺威奇村庄发展缓慢，
直到铁路让它迅速成长，
如今产业在此扎根，
这个乡镇以水果闻名。

## Lines on Tilsonburg / 蒂尔森堡吟

===Original===
After him who did the mills own,
This place was called in honor Tilson;
Bright gleaming like to a beaming star,
Is clear waters of the Otter.

And it doth form here a vast pond,
Which extends for miles beyond,
A fortune on town it will shower,
This prodigious water power.

No other spots to youth appear,
Like lovely little lakes round here,
And few small towns have fine roadway
Lined with brick blocks like your broadway.

Elsewhere, the firemen slave like Turks,
But you have got good water works;
And some do many a mile go
For to see your famous silo.

===Chinese===
以拥有磨坊的蒂尔森（Tilson）之名，
这个地方被命名为蒂尔森堡；
如闪耀的星辰般明亮的，
是奥特尔河（Otter）清澈的水流。

它在这里形成一个巨大的池塘，
向远方延伸数英里，
它将为小镇倾洒财富，
这惊人的水力。

没有其他地方像这里一样，
对年轻人有如此吸引力——
可爱的小湖泊环绕四周，
很少有小镇有如此精美的大道，
如你的百老汇般排列着砖砌街区。

别处的消防员像土耳其人般苦干，
但你这里有优良的供水系统；
有些人远行数英里，
只为看你著名的筒仓。

## Lines on Mt. Elgin / 埃尔金山吟

===Original===
Delivered at opening of Odd Fellows' Lodge.

Dereham now hath wealthy yoemen
Whose fathers overcame the foemen;
The enemy they boldly slew
Was mighty forests they did hew,
And where they burned heaps of slain
Their sons now reap the golden grain.

All will rejoice they took a trip
Onboard the craft Odd: Fellowship;
Your candidates now, in their youth,
Will soon learn friendship, love and truth.

We hope your influence will be good,
A blessing to the neighborhood.

===Chinese===
在独立共济会（Odd Fellows）分会开幕式上朗诵。

德里汉（Dereham）如今拥有富裕的自耕农，
他们的父辈战胜了敌人；
他们勇敢斩杀的敌人，
是他们劈砍的茫茫林海，
在他们焚烧的战利品堆上，
如今他们的儿子收获着金色的谷物。

所有人都将因踏上，
独立共济会这艘船而欣喜；
你们年轻的候选人们，
将很快学会友谊、爱与真理。

我们希望你们的影响力将带来好处，
成为邻里间的福泽。

## Corner Stone Laying / 奠基石典礼

===Original===
The following lines were read at the festival after the stone had been laid by Grand Master Col. Moffat, of a church on the Culloden road, with Masonic honors.

In this quiet spot this day of June,
Which will not be forgotten soon,
For when your little church on hill
You overflowingly did fill,
You then resolved there should arise
Church worthy of your enterprise,
You've laid foundation broad and deep,
And showers of blessings may you reap.

===Chinese===
以下诗句在卡洛登路（Culloden road）上一座教堂的奠基典礼后朗诵，莫法特（Moffat）上校总会长以共济会荣誉安放了基石。

在这六月的宁静之地，
这个日子不会被很快遗忘，
因为当你们小山丘上的教堂，
被挤得水泄不通时，
你们便决心建造一座，
配得上你们事业的教堂，
你们打下了宽广而深厚的基础，
愿你们收获祝福的甘霖。

===Original===
Craft of King Hiram and Saint John
Have come to lay the corner stone,
At the call of our Grand Master
Who was invited by your pastor,
With silver trowel all so fair
He laid foundation on the square,
May you be blessed with Christian love,
And we all meet in Lodge above.

===Chinese===
希兰王（King Hiram）与圣约翰（Saint John）的会众，
前来安放奠基石，
应我们总会长的号召，
他受你们牧师之邀前来，
用美丽的银泥刀，
他以正方的姿势安放了基石，
愿你们蒙受基督教之爱的祝福，
我们将在天上的会所中相聚。

## A Civic Holiday Trip / 公民假日之旅

===Original===
Lines on a civic holiday trip on the Grand Trunk, to Toronto, August, 1882. Address to Mr. Hodge, conductor of Caledonian games at Ingersoll.

From Ingersoll, on Canadian Thames,
We come to greet mine host of the St. James,
This being our town's civic holiday;
Yet we remember twenty-fourth of May,
For then at our Caledonian games
He did loud proclaim the victors' names.

===Chinese===
1882年8月，乘大干线铁路（Grand Trunk）公民假日之旅至多伦多。致英杰赛尔喀里多尼亚运动会的霍奇（Hodge）先生。

从加拿大泰晤士河畔的英杰赛尔出发，
我们前来问候圣詹姆斯（St. James）的主人，
今天是我们镇的公民假日；
然而我们记得五月二十四日，
因为在我们的喀里多尼亚运动会上，
他大声宣告了优胜者的名字。

===Original===
We came in part to contribute
To our town's mechanics' institute,
Who got up this grand excursion
Where each one has his own diversion.

Some did remain at Burlington Bay,
While we to Toronto did wend our way,
And came to see this presentation
To stalwart son of Scottish nation.

===Chinese===
我们来此部分是为了，
给我们镇的机械学院捐款，
他们组织了这次盛大的远足，
每个人都有自己的消遣。

有些人在伯灵顿湾（Burlington Bay）留步，
而我们继续前往多伦多，
前来观看这场颁奖典礼，
颁给苏格兰民族的坚毅之子。

## Robert Fleming Gourley / 罗伯特·弗莱明·古尔利

===Original===
Robert Fleming Gourley was a graduate of St. Andrews', University, Scotland. When he was crossing Niagara river, after being exiled, he asked for a brush to brush Canadian dust off his feet. Perhaps it will please some to see a little sketch of him, as he was a public man and gloried in that he had the honor of being sentenced both by the Canadian Government and the British. Exiled from Canada, he became a champion of popular rights in England. Lord Brougham opposed his schemes and he whipped His Lordship in the lobby of the House of Commons, for which breach of privilege of house he was sentenced by the House. He preserved the whip as a precious relic in his house at Mt. Elgin.

There came to Oxford Robert Gourley,
In his old age his health was poorly;
He was a relic of the past,
In his dotage sinking fast;
Yet he was erect and tall
Like noble ruined castle wall.

===Chinese===
罗伯特·弗莱明·古尔利（Robert Fleming Gourley）是苏格兰圣安德鲁斯大学（St. Andrews' University）的毕业生。被驱逐出境后，他在渡过尼亚加拉河时，要了一把刷子把加拿大尘土从脚上刷掉。也许有人会喜欢关于他的小传，因为他是一位公众人物，并以同时受到加拿大政府和英国政府的判决为荣。被加拿大驱逐后，他成为英格兰民众权利的捍卫者。布鲁厄姆勋爵（Lord Brougham）反对他的计划，他在下议院大厅中鞭打了勋爵，因此因违反议院特权被议院判刑。他将那根鞭子作为珍贵的遗物保存在埃尔金山（Mt. Elgin）的家中。

罗伯特·古尔利来到牛津，
年老体衰，健康堪忧；
他是过去的遗迹，
在暮年中迅速衰老；
但他依然挺拔高大，
如一座残破的贵族城堡的高墙。

===Original===
In early times they did him impeach
For demanding right of speech,
Now Oxford he wished to represent
In Canadian parliament,
But him the riding did not honor,
But elected Doctor Connor.

===Chinese===
早年他们弹劾他，
因为他要求言论自由，
如今他希望代表牛津，
进入加拿大的议会，
但选区没有给予他荣誉，
而是选举了康纳（Connor）博士。

## Odd Fellowship in Woodstock / 伍德斯托克的独立共济会

===Original===
Impromptu lines delivered at the opening of Maple Leaf Encampment, Woodstock, in the year 1883, in connection with Odd Fellows Lodge there. Olive Branch is the subordinate Lodge.

Well tell a tale, it shall be brief
It is of rise of Maple Leaf,
From noble Olive Branch it sprung,
And its good deeds shall yet be sung;
This Encampment, though in its youth,
Glories in friendship, love, and truth
Of all our lodges yet the chief
May be this youthful Maple Leaf;
Built of good timber, it doth launch
Well worthy of the Olive Branch,
And though it only is a shoot
We hope that it will take deep root,
And soon be vigorous Maple tree
In Canada so broad and free.

===Chinese===
1883年枫叶营地（Maple Leaf Encampment）在伍德斯托克开幕时即兴朗诵，与当地的独立共济会分会相连。橄榄枝（Olive Branch）是其下属分会。

且听一个简短的故事，
关于枫叶的崛起，
它从高贵的橄榄枝萌发，
它的善举仍将被传颂；
这个营地虽然年轻，
却以友谊、爱与真理为荣，
在我们所有分会中，
也许这棵年轻的枫叶为首；
用优质木材建造，它启航，
配得上橄榄枝的名号，
虽然它只是一株嫩芽，
我们希望它深扎根基，
很快成为一棵茁壮的枫树，
在这辽阔自由的加拿大。

===Original===
And may its usefulness ne'er cease
Sprung from the Olive Branch of peace,
But comfort brothers in distress,
Widows and orphans it will bless,
And console them in their grief,
Protected by the Maple Leaf.

Poor travellers in the wilderness
They oft do suffer great distress,
But may no pilgrim get rude shock
Sojourning here in Woodstock.

===Chinese===
愿它的功用永不衰竭，
从和平的橄榄枝萌发，
抚慰困苦中的兄弟，
祝福寡妇和孤儿，
在悲伤中安慰他们，
受枫叶的庇护。

荒野中贫穷的旅人，
他们常遭受巨大的困苦，
但愿没有朝圣者在此，
在伍德斯托克逗留时遭遇不幸。

## Odd Fellowship Lines Read at Concert / 共济会音乐会朗诵诗

===Original===
Lines delivered at Odd Fellows Concert, Town Hall, Ingersoll, December, 1883.

We look in vain for our Past Grands,
Now scattered over many lands,
Now some o'er the wide world do rove,
And some have joined Grand Lodge above,
But ever since Father Adams' fall
We are dependent creatures all,
Though man is weak yet he may join
With others strength for to combine.

===Chinese===
1883年12月在英杰赛尔市政厅独立共济会音乐会上朗诵。

我们徒然寻找我们过去的总会长，
如今散落在许多土地上，
有些人漫游在广阔的世界，
有些人已加入天上的总会，
但自从亚当祖先堕落以来，
我们都是依赖的存在，
虽然人是软弱的，但他可以，
与他人联合力量。

===Original===
The illustration it is grand,
Five Oddfellows in one hand,
And yet they all united stand,
Each finger hath a different length,
Each finger varies in its strength,
Each one is weak, but a firm fist,
You can scarcely break or twist,
'Tis same with members of a lodge,
United them you cannot budge.

===Chinese===
这个比喻何其伟大，
五个独立共济会成员如同一只手上的五指，
他们团结一致站立，
每根手指长度不同，
每根手指力量各异，
每根都很弱，但紧握成拳，
你几乎无法折断或扭曲，
分会的成员也是如此，
团结一致你便无法撼动。

===Original===
Then let us linked with pleasant chain,
Friendship, love and truth maintain,
And aid our brothers in distress,
The widows and the orphans bless,
Then let each lodge strive all it can,
Both Oxford and Samaritan
To aid distressed brother man,
Extending influence for good,
And universal brotherhood.

===Chinese===
让我们用愉悦的链条相连，
维护友谊、爱与真理，
帮助困苦中的兄弟，
祝福寡妇和孤儿，
让每个分会尽其所能，
牛津会和撒玛利亚会（Samaritan），
都来帮助苦难中的兄弟，
传播善的影响，
以及普世的兄弟情谊。

## London Children / 伦敦的孩童

===Original===
We have had the pleasure of hearing and seeing several clever children from the city of London. We commemorate them in the following.

Do you unto this world belong,
To cheer us merrily with song?
Are you mortal like ourselves,
Or are you, charming little elves,
Sent from some higher sphere
To bliss and comfort us while here?
May you be spared from earthly woes
And each blossom like the rose.

===Chinese===
我们有幸听过并见过伦敦市几位聪慧的孩童。以下诗句纪念他们。

你们是否属于这个世界，
用歌声欢快地逗我们开心？
你们和我们一样是凡人，
还是你们，可爱的小精灵，
从某个更高的天界而来，
为我们带来幸福与慰藉？
愿你们免受人间苦难，
每个人都如玫瑰般绽放。

## Caledonian Games / 喀里多尼亚运动会

===Original===
Lines on Caledonian games, May, 1884.

On grassy amphitheater
Spectators sit, to view the war
'Mong bold contestants on the plain,
Where each doth strive the prize to gain;
And when the little boys and girls
In highland dress and waving curls,
From London, danced the Highland fling;
The whole mass did their praises sing;
And at the concert did applaud
The little charmers Blanch and Maud.

===Chinese===
1884年5月喀里多尼亚运动会诗歌。

在草地圆形剧场，
观众们坐着，观看，
平原上勇士们之间的竞技，
每个人都力争夺取奖品；
当小男孩和小女孩们，
穿着高地服装、卷发飘扬，
从伦敦来跳高地舞（Highland fling）；
全场观众为他们欢呼；
在音乐会上人们鼓掌，
为小可人布兰奇（Blanch）和莫德（Maud）。

## Victoria Park and Caledonian Games / 维多利亚公园与喀里多尼亚运动会

===Original===
Lines on the naming of Victoria Park, on Queen's Birthday, 1881. The ceremony was performed by Thomas Brown, Esq., Mayor of the town.

Come one, come all, to Scottish games
On the banks of Canadian Thames;
You'll find that 'tis most pleasant way
You can enjoy the Queen's Birthday.

In future years it will be famed
The day whereon the park was named,
With its boundry great extended
And nature's charms sweetly blended.

===Chinese===
1881年女王诞辰日维多利亚公园（Victoria Park）命名仪式上的诗歌。仪式由镇长托马斯·布朗（Thomas Brown）先生主持。

来吧，大家都来吧，参加苏格兰运动会，
在加拿大泰晤士河畔；
你会发现这是最愉快的方式，
来享受女王的诞辰日。

在未来岁月里这一天将闻名，
公园被命名的那一天，
它的边界大大扩展，
自然的魅力美妙融合。

===Original===
Full worthy of the poet's theme
Is hill and dale, and wood and stream,
And glittering spires, and busy town.
Where mansions' do each mount top crown.

Come, witness the great tug-of-war,
And the great hammer thrown afar,
See running, jumping, highland fling,
At concert hear the sky lark sing.

And the bagpipes will send thrills
Like echoes from the distant hills,
And the bold sound of the pibroch
Which does resound o'er Scottish loch.

Young men and maids, and fine old dames
Will gather on the banks of Thames,
And though we have a tug-of-war
'Twill leave no wound or deadly scar.

===Chinese===
山丘与谷地、森林与溪流，
闪耀的尖塔与繁忙的城镇，
都配得上诗人的主题——
宅邸在每座山顶加冕。

来吧，见证伟大的拔河比赛，
观看大锤被远远抛出，
看跑步、跳跃、高地舞，
在音乐会上听云雀歌唱。

风笛将带来战栗，
如远山的回声，
风笛雄壮的声音，
在苏格兰湖面上回荡。

青年男女和优雅的老妇们，
将聚集在泰晤士河畔，
虽然我们有拔河比赛，
却不会留下伤口或致命的伤疤。

## The Great Fire of Ingersoll / 英杰赛尔大火

===Original===
May, 1872.

Written at the time of the disaster.

'Twas on a pleasant eve in May.
Just as the sun shed its last ray,
The bell it rang, citizens to warn,
For lo! a fire appears in barn.

An ancient barn near hotel stood,
The joining buildings all were wood;
This barn a relic of the past,
There farmers' horses were made fast.

===Chinese===
1872年5月。

作于灾难发生时。

那是一个宜人的五月傍晚，
夕阳刚刚洒下最后一缕光芒，
钟声响起，警示市民，
看哪！粮仓中出现了火光。

旅馆旁有一座古老的粮仓，
相连的建筑全是木质；
这座粮仓是过去的遗迹，
农夫们的马匹拴在那里。

===Original===
Our once fair town is now in woe,
And we have had our Chicago;
But soon a nobler town will rise,
For Ingersoll's all enterprise.

For water far town need not seek,
As there is river and the creek
Just find the means it to apply
And then all fires must quickly die.

===Chinese===
我们曾经美丽的小镇如今陷入悲痛，
我们也经历了我们的芝加哥之劫；
但一座更宏伟的城镇将很快崛起，
因为英杰赛尔充满了进取精神。

小镇不必远距离取水，
因为有河流和小溪，
只要找到使用的方法，
所有大火必将迅速熄灭。

## Our Firemen / 我们的消防员

===Original===
Lines delivered at a Firemen's Soirée in the days of the hand engines.

Our youth and beauty here arranged,
In honor of the Fire Brigade;
And now every man is ready
To obey the bugle of Brady.

And always willing for to ralley
At the trumpet sound of Walley,
To the fire they rush at once,
Led on by gallant Captain Vance.

===Chinese===
手工泵时代消防员晚会上朗诵。

我们的青春与美丽聚集在此，
向消防队致敬；
现在每个人都准备好了，
听从布雷迪（Brady）的号令。

随时愿意集结，
听从沃利（Walley）的号角声，
他们立即冲向火场，
由英勇的万斯（Vance）队长率领。

===Original===
The firemen, now, their only strife,
It seems to be a race for life,
Which engine first shall reach the fire
And cause the wild flames to expire.

===Chinese===
消防员们如今唯一的竞争，
似乎是一场生命赛跑，
看哪台泵车先到达火场，
扑灭狂暴的火焰。

## St. Thomas / 圣托马斯

===Original===
At time of Odd Fellows Grand Lodge meeting, 1884.

Oddfellows once they had to trudge
O'er rough stage road to the Grand Lodge,
But now they town of metal seek
And find it on the Kettle Creek.

For industry it here prevails
As it was built by the steel rails,
And here the wide expanded bridges
Do connect the distant ridges.

No more need to stay at home as
There's lots of railroads to St. Thomas,
You pluckily did boldly venture,
Now you are great railroad centre.

===Chinese===
1884年独立共济会大会期间。

独立共济会成员曾不得不跋涉，
走过崎岖的驿道去总会，
但现在他们寻找金属之镇，
它就在凯特尔溪（Kettle Creek）畔。

因为这里工业繁荣，
它因钢铁铁路而建，
宽阔的桥梁在这里，
连接着远方的山脊。

不必再留在家中，
因为有许多铁路通往圣托马斯，
你勇敢地大胆开拓，
如今你是伟大的铁路枢纽。

===Original===
Your city now it hath high hopes
From its great railway workshops,
And higher yet it still will rise,
This seat of so much enterprise.

When young man wants a wedded mate
He seeks Alma girl graduate,
And he loves her Alma mater
For the sake of her charming daughter.

===Chinese===
你的城市如今满怀希望，
来自它伟大的铁路工厂，
它还将继续上升，
这个充满进取精神的所在。

当年轻人想娶妻成家，
他寻找阿尔玛（Alma）的女毕业生，
他爱她的母校，
为了她迷人的女儿。

## Masonic Lines Read at a Banquet / 宴会上朗诵的共济会诗

===Original===
1884.

The prominent names connected with Masonary are Kings Solomon and Hiram and Saint John, the parent Lodge is King Hiram in Ingersoll, and Saint John is the offspring. Both masters in 1884 are legal gentlemen.

Craft of King Hiram and St. John,
They figurative work on stone,
King Hiram he is the old sire
And he was famous King of Tyre.

But great as King upon a throne
Is the good, kind, true St. John,
Cathedrals did craftsmen raise,
Fills all our minds now with amaze.

===Chinese===
1884年。

与共济会相关的重要名字是所罗门王（Solomon）、希兰王（Hiram）和圣约翰（Saint John），母会是英杰赛尔的希兰王会，圣约翰会是其分支。1884年的两位会长都是法律界人士。

希兰王与圣约翰的会众，
他们在石头上进行象征性的劳作，
希兰王是年迈的先祖，
他是著名的推罗（Tyre）国王。

但如同王座上的君王般伟大的，
是善良、仁慈、真诚的圣约翰，
工匠们建造了大教堂，
如今让我们惊叹不已。

===Original===
No modern chisel has the power
To trace such leaf, and bud, and flower,
But though our structures now are rude
Let us all make the mortar good.

And this injunction never spare,
To have the work both plumb and square,
And it must have no crack nor flaw,
So masters will lay down the law.

===Chinese===
现代的凿子没有力量，
刻出这样的叶片、花蕾和花朵，
但尽管我们现在的建筑粗糙，
让我们都把灰浆调好。

永远不要省略这训诫，
让作品既垂直又方正，
它不能有裂纹或缺陷，
如此会长们将制定法则。

===Original===
Of all our work this is the chief,
To give the needy ones relief,
And with truth and brotherly love
We sublime structure raise above.

The greatest honor has been won
By that great builder Solomon,
And craftsmen o'er the world do sing
The praises of that mighty King.

===Chinese===
在我们所有工作中这是首要的，
给予贫困者救济，
用真理和兄弟之爱，
我们在上方建造崇高的建筑。

最大的荣誉已被赢得，
由伟大的建造者所罗门，
世界各地的工匠们歌唱，
赞美那位伟大的君王。

## Oxford Cheese Ode / 牛津奶酪颂

===Original===
As cheesemaking in Canada first began in this county, and it has already become one of the chief industries of several counties, it is no insignificant theme. Mr. E. Casswell has just received this morning from Messrs. Millar, Mount Elgin, scale boards for thirty two thousand cheese; each of those cheese which they are intended to protect will weigh about 65 lbs. each, or over; about three million lbs. in all.—June 14th, 1884.

The ancient poets ne'er did dream
That Canada was land of cream,
They ne'er imagined it could flow
In this cold land of ice and snow,
Where everything did solid freeze,
They ne'er hoped or looked for cheese.

===Chinese===
由于加拿大的奶酪制作最初始于本郡，且它已成为几个郡的主要产业之一，这并非无关紧要的主题。卡斯韦尔（Casswell）先生今天早上刚从埃尔金山（Mount Elgin）的米勒（Millar）先生那里收到三万二千块奶酪的秤板；每块奶酪将重约65磅或更多；总共约三百万磅。——1884年6月14日。

古代的诗人从未梦想过，
加拿大是乳脂之国，
他们从未想象它能流淌，
在这冰雪寒冷之地，
万物都冻得坚硬，
他们从未期望或期待奶酪。

===Original===
A few years since our Oxford farms
Were nearly robbed of all their charms,
O'er cropped the weary land grew poor
And nearly barren as a moor,
But now their owners live at ease
Rejoicing in their crop of cheese.

And since they justly treat the soil,
Are well rewarded for their toil,
The land enriched by goodly cows
Yields plenty now to fill their mows,
Both wheat and barley, oats and peas,
But still their greatest boast is cheese.

===Chinese===
几年前我们的牛津农场，
几乎失去了所有的魅力，
过度耕作使疲惫的土地贫瘠，
几乎如荒原般荒芜，
但如今它们的主人安享生活，
为奶酪的收成而欢欣。

自从他们善待土地，
辛劳得到了丰厚的回报，
优良奶牛使土地肥沃，
如今产量足以装满粮仓——
小麦、大麦、燕麦和豌豆，
但他们最大的骄傲仍是奶酪。

===Original===
Cow, you must treat her as a Queen,
When grass is dry cut her feed green;
She will repay you for your toiling
For there's profit in the soiling,
Its benefits one daily sees
Who takes an interest in the cheese.

And you must careful fill your mows
With good provender for your cows,
And in the winter keep them warm,
Protect them safe all time from harm,
For cows do dearly love their ease,
Which doth insure best grade of cheese.

===Chinese===
奶牛，你必须待她如女王，
草干时给她割新鲜的青草；
她将回报你的辛劳，
因为青贮饲料有利可图，
关心奶酪的人每天都能看到，
它带来的好处。

你必须小心填满你的草仓，
为你的奶牛储备好饲料，
冬天要保持它们温暖，
时刻保护它们不受伤害，
因为奶牛深爱舒适的生活，
这能确保最上等的奶酪。

===Original===
To us it is a glorious theme
To sing of milk and curds and cream,
Were it collected it could float
On its bosom, small steam boat,
Cows numerous as swarm of bees
Are milked in Oxford to make cheese.

To prove the wealth that here abounds,
One cheese weighed eight thousand pounds,
Had it been hung in air at noon
Folks would have thought it was the moon,
It sailed with triumph o'er the seas,
'Twas hailed with welcome, queen of cheese.

===Chinese===
对我们而言这是一个光荣的主题，
歌唱牛奶、凝乳和乳脂，
如果收集起来它能浮起，
在它的怀抱上——一艘小汽船，
牛群多如蜂群，
在牛津被挤奶来制作奶酪。

为证明这里财富丰饶，
一块奶酪重达八千磅，
若在正午挂在空中，
人们会以为它是月亮，
它凯旋般航行过大海，
作为奶酪女王受到热烈欢迎。

## Lines on a Fountain / 喷泉吟

===Original===
We love cold water as it flows from the fountain,
Which nature hath brewed alone in the mountain;
In the wild woods and in the rocky dell,
Where man hath not been but the deer loves to dwell;
And away across the sea in far distant lands,
In Asia's gloomy jungles and Africa's drifting sands;
Where to the thirsty traveller a charming spot of green
Is by far the rarest gem his eyes have ever seen;
And when he has quenched his thirst at the cooling spring,
With many grateful songs he makes the air to ring;
For many nights he dreams of this scene of bliss,
And when he thinks of Heaven it is of such as this.

===Chinese===
我们爱从泉中流出的清凉泉水，
大自然独自在山中酿造；
在荒野林中和岩石谷地，
人迹罕至但鹿儿喜爱栖居；
远渡重洋到遥远的地方，
在亚洲阴暗的丛林和非洲流动的沙丘；
对饥渴的旅人而言一片迷人的绿洲，
是他眼中见过的最稀有的宝石；
当他在清凉的泉水边解渴，
他以感恩的歌声响彻空气；
许多夜晚他梦见这幸福的场景，
当他想到天堂便是如此模样。

## A Concert Invitation / 音乐会邀请

===Original===
The following invitation appeared on the programme of a concert.

If pleasant night you wish to spend,
Go and invite your lady friend;
Oddfellow's concert is the place
Where happiness beams in each face.

When you resolve to call her wife,
And to enjoy the rest of life,
All furniture you may require
You can get cheap from McIntyre.

===Chinese===
以下邀请词出现在音乐会的节目单上。

如果你希望度过一个愉快的夜晚，
去邀请你的女性朋友吧；
独立共济会音乐会是那个地方，
幸福洋溢在每张脸上。

当你决定娶她为妻，
与她共度余生，
所有你需要的家具，
你都能从麦金泰尔（McIntyre）那里便宜买到。

## Thomas Campbell / 托马斯·坎贝尔

===Original===
### Preface

As musings on Banks of Canadian Thames doth not necessarily consist of meditations in verse, but the monotony of the cogitations may be relieved by a soliloquy in prose, and as Campbell manifested a deep interest in American subjects, we will give the following anecdote related by that genial American Author Washington Irvine, to Sir Walter Scott.

Campbell wrote thus of America in the beginning of the century, and by comparing the facts as he describes them it shows the wonderous strides which the United States, especially, have taken on the Banks of Lake Erie, as Lake Ontario seems to be favorite location for Canadian cities.

### Thomas Campbell

Their industry is not in vain,
For they have bounteous crops of grain,
And you behold on every field
Of grass and roots, abundant yield;
But after all the greatest charm
Is the snug home upon the farm.

And stone walls now keep cattle warm,
The cold blast now doth them no harm.

===Chinese===
### 序言

由于对加拿大泰晤士河畔的沉思不一定以诗歌形式呈现，散文的独白可以缓解沉思的单调，而坎贝尔（Campbell）对美洲主题表现出浓厚的兴趣，因此我们将讲述和蔼的美国作家华盛顿·欧文（Washington Irving）向沃尔特·司各特爵士（Sir Walter Scott）讲述的以下轶事。

坎贝尔在世纪之初如此描写美洲，通过比较他所描述的事实，可以看出美国，特别是伊利湖沿岸取得的惊人进步，而安大略湖似乎更受加拿大城市青睐。

### 托马斯·坎贝尔

他们的勤劳不会白费，
因为他们拥有丰盛的谷物收成，
你看到每块田地上，
牧草和根茎都产量丰饶；
但最大的魅力终究是，
农场上温馨的家园。

石墙如今保护牛群温暖，
寒风再也不能伤害它们。

## Poetry / 诗歌

===Original===
Poetry to us is given,
As stars beautify the Heaven,
Or, as the sunbeams when they gleam,
Sparkling so bright upon the stream,
And the poetry of motion
Is ship sailing o'er the ocean;
Or, when the bird doth graceful fly,
Seeming to float upon the sky,
For poetry is the pure cream,
And essence of the common theme.

===Chinese===
诗歌赐予我们，
如繁星装点天空，
或如阳光闪耀时，
在溪流上闪烁得如此明亮，
运动的诗意，
是航船在大洋上航行；
或当鸟儿优雅地飞翔，
仿佛飘浮在天空，
因为诗歌是纯粹的精华，
是平凡主题的精髓。

===Original===
Poetic thoughts the mind doth fill,
When on broad plain to view a hill,
On barren heath how it doth cheer,
To see in distance herd of deer,
And poetry breathes in each flower,
Nourished by the gentle shower,
In song of birds upon the trees,
