And humming of busy bees,
'Tis solace for the ills of life,
A soothing of the jars and strife,
For poets feel 'tis a duty
To sing of both worth and beauty.

===Chinese===
诗意的思想充满心灵，
在广阔的平原上远眺山丘，
在贫瘠的荒野上多么令人振奋，
看到远处一群鹿，
诗意在每一朵花中呼吸，
被温柔的细雨滋养，
在树上鸟儿的歌唱中，
在勤劳蜜蜂的嗡鸣中，
它是人生苦难的慰藉，
是纷争与冲突的缓和，
因为诗人觉得这是一种责任，
歌唱价值与美。

## Lines on Col. Wonham / 旺纳姆上校吟

===Original===
Formerly of Ingersoll, now of the North West.

When Wonham got orders
To advance to the borders,
His boys they were ready
And fell in quite steady.

They first marched to Woodstock,
To prepare for war's shock,
And soon camped at Windsor,
Facing American shore.

===Chinese===
原为英杰赛尔人，现居西北地区。

当旺纳姆（Wonham）接到命令，
向边境进发，
他的小伙子们整装待发，
稳稳地列队。

他们首先行军至伍德斯托克，
为战争的冲击做准备，
很快在温莎（Windsor）扎营，
面向美国的海岸。

## Opening Ode / 开幕颂歌

===Original===
Delivered at an Odd Fellows' Concert.

Some have formed strange conceptions about the mysteries of our order. They believe that enshrouded 'mong the deep and mysterious surroundings of our dark recesses and caves we have chained "Gorgans and Hydras, and Chimeras dire," and that in our mazes and labyrinths,

A horrid goat we have to ride,
With long horns and shaggy hide,
And that the beast we have to stride well
Without saddle or a bridle.

Friends, with pleasure we do meet you,
And with hearty welcome greet you;
With many we have met before,
So pleasantly in days of yore.

===Chinese===
在独立共济会音乐会上朗诵。

有些人对我们的奥秘形成了奇怪的观念。他们相信，在我们黑暗洞穴深处神秘的环境中，我们锁链锁着"戈耳工、九头蛇和可怕的喀迈拉"，在我们的迷宫和曲折通道中，

我们骑着一头可怕的山羊，
它有长长的角和蓬乱的皮毛，
我们必须稳稳地骑着这头野兽，
不用马鞍也不用缰绳。

朋友们，我们高兴地与你们相聚，
衷心地欢迎你们；
我们以前见过许多人，
在过去的美好日子里。

===Original===
But some new faces we do find,
And hope they'll criticise us kind;
Abroad for talent we don't roam,
But friends and neighbors here at home
Will give us now a splendid treat,
With speeches good and songs so sweet;
And instruments whose soothing charms
Will banish cares and our alarms.

In friendly deeds they lead the van,
Both Oxford and Samaritan,
To relieve brothers in distress,
And bounteously the widows bless.

===Chinese===
但我们发现了一些新面孔，
希望他们能宽容地评判我们；
我们不在远方寻觅才华，
而是这里的朋友和邻居们，
将为我们献上精彩的演出，
有精彩演讲和甜蜜歌声；
乐器那舒缓的魅力，
将驱散我们的忧虑和惊恐。

在友善的行为中他们走在前面，
牛津会和撒玛利亚会，
帮助困苦中的兄弟，
慷慨地祝福寡妇们。

## Adventure on a Raft / 筏上奇遇

===Original===
"Truth is strange, stranger than fiction."

The following adventure happened in the experience of an Ingersoll man.

A man rafting down the river,
Time he will remember ever,
He shouted, "Pole, the raft to land,
Or we'll be wrecked upon the strand."
But captain gave him a reply
That all danger he would defy;
But, in another moment more
Part was wrecked upon the shore
Of a Nova Scotian bay;
The other half was borne away.

===Chinese===
"真相是奇特的，比虚构更奇特。"

以下冒险经历发生在一位英杰赛尔人的身上。

一个人乘筏顺流而下，
他将永远铭记这段时光，
他大喊："撑篙，把筏靠岸，
否则我们将触礁搁浅。"
但船长给了他一个回答，
说他不惧怕任何危险；
但转瞬之间，
一部分已在新斯科舍湾的岸边撞毁，
另一半则被冲走。

===Original===
Enough to make a person shiver,
Man was drifted out of river,
All alone on the broken raft,
Driven where e'er the wind did waft;
Right out on the open sea,
Where the storm did blow so free.
No shelter from the wind or wave,
He thought the gulf would be his grave.

===Chinese===
足以让人不寒而栗，
那人被冲出了河道，
独自在破碎的竹筏上，
随风漂向任何地方；
一直漂到开阔的海面，
风暴在那里肆虐，
没有躲避风浪的庇护所，
他以为海湾将成为他的坟墓。

===Original===
He had no food, life to sustain,
He laid him down, there to remain.
What happened he did know no more,
But old man on Prince Edwards shore,
While gazing through his good spy glass,
"What do I see?" he cried, "Alas!
Some poor man, and I fear he's dead,
Drifting to my humble shed."
The body acted like a sail,
And wafted raft before the gale;

===Chinese===
他没有食物维持生命，
他躺下等待命运。
后来发生了什么他已不知，
但在爱德华王子岛的岸边，
一位老人用他的望远镜眺望，
"我看到了什么？"他喊道，"唉！
一个可怜的人，我恐怕他已经死了，
漂向我简陋的小屋。"
那身体如同船帆，
在大风中推着竹筏前行。

===Original===
He called on men to man a boat,
And quickly crew had it afloat,
And in haste full soon they bore
His lifeless body to the shore.
But old man did them then desire
To place body near the fire,
And wrap it up in blankets warm—
Which did act like to a charm—
And soon the breath it did return.
With gratitude his heart did burn,
To think he was again restored
Unto his friends, whom he adored;
But worse misfortunes him befell,
Yet he bears up 'neath troubles well.

===Chinese===
他叫人去备一条船，
船员们很快让它下水，
他们匆忙将他无生命的身体，
抬到了岸上。
但老人让他们，
把身体放在火旁，
用温暖的毯子裹住——
这如同施了魔法——
他的呼吸很快恢复了。
感激之情在他心中燃烧，
想到他又回到了，
他所深爱的朋友们身边；
但更糟的不幸降临在他身上，
然而他在苦难下坚强地挺住了。

## Canada Our Home / 加拿大我们的家园

===Original===
1883.

The following response to "Canada, Our Home," was given at a banquet of the Caledonian Society, Ingersoll:

Full oft we did enraptured hark
To heavenly song of the skylark.

===Chinese===
1883年。

以下是对"加拿大，我们的家园"的回应，在英杰赛尔喀里多尼亚协会的宴会上朗诵：

我们常常欣喜地倾听，
云雀天堂般的歌声。

## Mrs. Moody / 穆迪夫人

===Original===
As we have given glances at various Canadian authors in this work, perhaps there is none more worthy than Mrs. Moody, whose vigorous pen was ever active in favor of Canada. She is a sister of the celebrated Agnes Strickland, author of "The Queens of England." Mrs. Moody is enjoying reasonably fair health in Toronto. She was born in the beginning of the century.

When this country it was woody,
Its great champion Mrs. Moody,
Showed she had both pluck and push
In her work roughing in the bush.

For there alone she did dwell
At time McKenzie did rebel,
Outbreak her husband strove to quell,
Her own grand struggles she doth tell.

===Chinese===
由于我们在本作品中提及了多位加拿大作家，也许没有人比穆迪夫人（Mrs. Moody）更值得称道，她充满活力的笔始终为加拿大的利益而活跃。她是著名的安妮·斯特里克兰德（Agnes Strickland）——《英格兰女王》（The Queens of England）作者的妹妹。穆迪夫人在多伦多享受着相当不错的健康。她出生于本世纪初。

当这个国家还是一片森林时，
伟大的捍卫者穆迪夫人，
展现出她的勇气和冲劲，
在丛林中开拓奋斗。

因为当麦肯齐（McKenzie）叛乱时，
她独自住在那片荒野，
她的丈夫努力平息叛乱，
她自己讲述了她伟大的斗争。

===Original===
Round bush life she threw a glory,
Pioneer renowned in story,
But her tale it is more cheering
When she wrote about the clearing.

Her other sister Mrs. Traill
Though eighty-seven she doth not fail,
She now is writing of wild flowers
Grown in Canada's woody bowers.

===Chinese===
她为丛林生活增添了荣耀，
在传说中声名显赫的先驱者，
但她的故事更加振奋人心，
当她写下关于开荒的经历。

她的另一个姐妹特雷尔夫人（Mrs. Traill），
虽已八十七岁仍不示弱，
她正在书写野花，
生长在加拿大林间花圃中。

## Lines Addressed to an Old Bachelor / 致一位老单身汉的诗

===Original===
Addressed to Jonathan Wingle, Esq.

In summer time we roam o'er dingle,
But winter draws us round the ingle,
Why do you remain thus single,
When love would make two hearts tingle,
Pray, tell me why my dearest wingle,
With the fair you do not mingle,
Better with love 'neath cot of shingle,
Than all your yellow gold to jingle.

===Chinese===
致乔纳森·温格尔（Jonathan Wingle）先生。

夏日里我们漫游山间谷地，
冬天则围坐在炉火旁，
你为何仍保持单身，
当爱情能让两颗心悸动？
请告诉我，我亲爱的温格尔，
你为何不与佳人交往，
在木瓦小屋下有爱相伴，
胜过你所有的黄金叮当响。

===Original===
For married life you would enjoy,
And soon a little girl and boy,
They would your leisure hours employ,
At Christmas you could buy each toy,
And fill their little hearts with joy,
For their amusements never cloy,
Business cares do men annoy,
Child's happiness knows no alloy.

===Chinese===
你会享受婚姻生活，
很快有一儿一女，
他们占据你的闲暇时光，
圣诞节你可以买每个玩具，
填满他们小小的欢乐的心，
因为他们的快乐永不厌倦，
生意的烦恼令人心烦，
孩子的幸福纯净无瑕。

## Brantford / 布兰特福德

===Original===
In these sketches of towns in Southern Ontario we are not vain enough to suppose that because we have produced some lines thereon that said rhymes are poetry. If we furnish an occasional poetic gleam like a dewdrop sparkling in the sun, it is all we dare hope for.

Brantford as thriving city's famed,
And after Indian Chief is named,
And here the sparkling Grand River
It doth flow a joy forever.

Campbell he sang a dismal tale
Of horrors of Wyoming's vale,
The tale one's mind doth ever haunt,
The cruelties of monster Brant.

===Chinese===
在这些南安大略城镇的速写中，我们并不自负地以为，因为我们为它们写了一些诗句就认为这些韵文是诗歌。如果我们偶尔提供了一丝诗意的光芒，如阳光下闪烁的露珠，那就是我们所敢期望的一切。

布兰特福德（Brantford）以繁荣城市闻名，
以印第安酋长的名字命名，
大河（Grand River）闪亮的河水，
在这里欢快地流淌。

坎贝尔曾唱过一首悲惨的歌谣，
关于怀俄明谷（Wyoming）的恐怖，
那故事萦绕在人们心头，
关于怪物布兰特（Brant）的残暴。

===Original===
But the Chief's son to England went
And Campbell to him did lament,
And all the tale he did recant
About cruel butcheries of Brant.

Now pleasant thoughts it doth awake
When Brantford thinks of her namesake,
She evermore with pride will chant
The bold heroic name of Brant.

We sing of two great Indian names,
Tecumseh on the banks of Thames,
And the Grand River it doth vaunt
O'er the historic name of Brant.

===Chinese===
但酋长的儿子去了英格兰，
坎贝尔向他表达了哀悼，
他收回了所有关于，
布兰特残暴屠杀的说法。

如今当布兰特福德想起，
她的同名者便愉快地浮想，
她将永远骄傲地吟唱，
布兰特英勇的名字。

我们歌唱两个伟大的印第安名字，
泰晤士河畔的特库姆塞（Tecumseh），
大河为布兰特，
这历史性的名字而自豪。

===Original===
The city's pride it doth find vent
In building him a monument,
And Indians will proudly stalk
Past memorial of great Mohawk.

===Chinese===
这座城市的骄傲找到了表达，
为他建造一座纪念碑，
印第安人将骄傲地走过，
伟大莫霍克（Mohawk）人的纪念物。

## The Power of Steam / 蒸汽的力量

===Original===
We now do sing a new theme,
It is prodigious power of steam;
And our little fast steam horse,
How he works with mighty force.

Instead of hay and oats, we thrust
In his mouth chips and saw dust;
Which heats the blood in his veins,
Then how he saws and bores and plains.

===Chinese===
我们现在歌唱一个新主题，
它是蒸汽的巨大威力；
我们的小快蒸汽马，
它工作时多么有力。

不给它干草和燕麦，
我们往它嘴里塞木屑和锯末；
这加热了它血管中的血液，
然后它怎样锯切、钻孔和刨平。

===Original===
He's never troubled with the botts,
But all the time he gaily trotts,
And every day he is full able
To make many a chair and table.

Work for him is only sport,
He feels so good he oft does snort,
As he trotts along his course,
Our little frolicksome steam horse.

===Chinese===
它从不患马蝇蛆病，
但总是欢快地小跑，
每天它完全有能力，
制作许多桌椅。

工作对它来说只是游戏，
它感觉太好常常嘶鸣，
当它沿着轨道小跑，
我们那匹快活的小蒸汽马。

## Lines on Thorold / 索罗德吟

===Original===
McCready, the great Irish tragedian, said that the view from Thorold was the finest in America.

Thorold is famous for its mills,
And the grand view from off its hills,
A view so charming and extended,
Nature's beauties sweetly blended.

Poetic thoughts it doth awake
To view Ontario's broad lake,
And husbandmen have their reward in
Fruits of this Provincial garden.

===Chinese===
伟大的爱尔兰悲剧演员麦克里迪（McCready）说，从索罗德（Thorold）看到的景色是美洲最美的。

索罗德以它的磨坊闻名，
以及从山丘上看到的壮丽景色，
如此迷人而广阔的视野，
大自然的美景和谐融合。

凝望安大略广阔的湖泊，
它唤醒诗意的遐想，
农夫们的收获在于，
这个省级花园的果实。

===Original===
For from the hill you see below
Gardens where choice fruits do grow,
The landscape all within your reach
Doth both produce the grape and peach.

McCready said in the New World
The finest view was from Thorold,
You see St. Catharines thriving town
And steamers sailing up and down.

And you can see on a clear day
All along Toronto Bay,
And you clearly see the haze
Where Niagara doth amaze.

===Chinese===
从山丘上你看到下方，
生长着精选水果的果园，
你伸手可及的景色中，
既有葡萄也有桃子。

麦克里迪说在新世界，
最美的景色来自索罗德，
你看到圣凯瑟琳斯繁华的城镇，
汽船来回航行。

晴朗的日子你能看到，
多伦多湾的全貌，
你能清楚地看到，
尼亚加拉令人惊叹的雾气。

===Original===
And glance where Grimsby's gardens yield
Or view Beamsville's fruitful field,
Then this thought you can advance,
This is Canada's sunny France.

You see Niagara's ancient town
Though it has lost us old renown,
And you have a splendid view
Of boats on old canal and new.

===Chinese===
眺望格里姆斯比（Grimsby）的花园出产，
或欣赏宾姆斯维尔（Beamsville）丰饶的田野，
然后你可以产生这个想法，
这是加拿大的阳光法兰西。

你看到尼亚加拉古老的城镇，
虽然它已失去了昔日的荣耀，
你还能壮丽地看到，
旧运河和新运河上的船只。

## Musings on the Northwest / 西北沉思

===Original===
Domestic cattle quiet will graze where now the Buffalos roam and in spots now covered o'er with Indian wigwams, where white men never trod cities will occupy their sites with busy trade and millions flock from eastern lands to take possession of the great Northwest. Then Winnipeg perchance may be the capital of the Dominion. In the day foretold when this indeed shall be the "Greater Britain" with Ontario's towns for workshops for this vast prairie land.

"Then poets will arise and high their lays will soar,
Worthy of the muse of a Burns or a Moore,
A Shakespeare and a Milton, the great and the wise,
Will sing of the glories of our northern skies,
Of its lakes and rivers and its mountains grand,
Of its fertile plains and great prairie land,
A fit theme for song this empire gigantic,
Whose arms stretch from Pacific to Atlantic."

===Chinese===
家畜将安静地吃草，就在如今野牛漫游的地方；如今覆盖着印第安棚屋、白人从未踏足的地方，将矗立着繁忙贸易的城市，数百万人将从东部涌来，占据这片伟大的西北地区。那时温尼伯（W Winnipeg）或许将成为自治领的首都。在预言的那一天，当这确实成为"大不列颠"，安大略的城镇将成为这片广袤草原的工坊。

"那时诗人将崛起，他们的诗篇将高飞，
配得上彭斯或穆尔的缪斯，
一位莎士比亚和一位弥尔顿，伟大而智慧，
将歌唱我们北方天空的壮丽，
歌唱它的湖泊、河流和巍峨的山脉，
歌唱它肥沃的平原和广袤的草原，
这庞大的帝国是歌唱的绝佳主题，
它的臂膀从太平洋延伸到大西洋。"

## Crimean War / 克里米亚战争

===Original===
At the announcement that Britain was to declare war Kossuth the Hungarian Patriot declared in an address in England that the British Lion was a sea dog but helpless on land.

When the British Lion offered aid to the Turk,
Round many lips a sneer of serious doubt did lurk,
They said he was at home on sea, but when on land
He would be as a ship wrecked upon the strand,
Or like some huge ungainly crocodile
Upon the marshy banks of sluggish Nile,
Who could move gaily on the deep
But on dry land could scarcely creep,

===Chinese===
当英国宣布参战时，匈牙利爱国者科苏特（Kossuth）在英格兰的演讲中宣称，英国狮是海狗，但在陆地上无能为力。

当英国狮向土耳其人提供援助时，
许多嘴唇上潜伏着嘲讽的疑虑，
他们说他在海上如鱼得水，但在陆地上，
他会像搁浅的沉船，
或像某种笨拙巨大的鳄鱼，
在迟缓的尼罗河沼泽岸边，
在深海中能欢快游动，
但在干地上几乎爬不动。

===Original===
But up the Alma heights he rushed like greyhound after hare,
And in a moment by the throat he seized the Russian bear,
Which begged so hard for mercy his life he did it spare,
And closely now it is confined within its native laire,
For its strong fortress of Sebastpol
Was forced to submit to Great Britain's rule.

===Chinese===
但他如灵缇追兔般冲上阿尔马（Alma）高地，
瞬间扼住了俄国熊的喉咙，
它乞求如此卑微的怜悯，他饶了它的命，
如今它被紧紧关在自己的巢穴中，
因为它坚固的塞瓦斯托波尔（Sebastpol）要塞，
被迫臣服于大英帝国的统治。

## A Christmas Tree / 圣诞树

===Original===
To night the children meet with glee
To view the fruits on Christmas Tree,
And when its beauties we behold
We're very sorry we are old.

The children all they have good cause
To feel grateful to old Santa Claus,
And to each teacher, and each friend,
Who did these handsome presents send.

===Chinese===
今晚孩子们欢聚一堂，
观看圣诞树上的礼物，
当我们欣赏它的美丽时，
我们很遗憾自己已老去。

孩子们都有充分的理由，
对老圣诞老人心怀感激，
对每一位老师、每一位朋友，
是他们送来了这些精美的礼物。

===Original===
To us it is a pleasant treat,
With so many friends here to meet,
You've well conducted public school
Where master doth judicious rule;
The union it doth give its strength,
Gives section breadth, as well as length.

===Chinese===
对我们来说这是一场愉快的款待，
在这里与这么多朋友相聚，
你们经营着良好的公立学校，
校长英明地管理着；
联合给予它力量，
给予区域以宽度与长度。

===Original===
Nissouri here should build a Hall,
For exhibitions in the Fall;
Its claims they cannot be ignored,
For first in township stands Thamesford.

This village keeps up with the age,
For it hath a good daily stage
And in its efforts never fails
To carry passengers and mails.

===Chinese===
尼苏里（Nissouri）应该在这里建一座大厅，
用于秋季展览；
它的要求不能被忽视，
因为泰姆斯福德在该镇中首屈一指。

这个村庄紧跟时代步伐，
因为它有良好的每日驿站马车，
在运送旅客和邮件方面，
从不懈怠。

## Dr. Springer / 斯普林格医生

===Original===
Lines on presenting Dr. Springer with a diamond ring, when he was bidding farewell to Ingersoll, and was about journeying to California.

Though we know we are no singer,
Yet we will chaunt farewell to Springer,
One of our oldest past grands,
About to visit distant lands.

And we all sincerely hope
You may enjoy Pacific slope,
And when you gaze on this ring
May it pleasant memories bring.

Of good old Lodge, Samaritan,
Where friendship makes us all as one;
'Tis hard to strike your name from roll
With us so long in Ingersoll.

Though we regret to say farewell,
May blessings rest where 'ere you dwell,
And on the partner of your joy
Happiness without alloy.

===Chinese===
在斯普林格（Springer）医生告别英杰赛尔、即将前往加利福尼亚之际，向他赠送钻戒的诗句。

虽然我们知道我们不是歌手，
但我们仍将吟唱送别斯普林格，
他是我们最资深的前任总会长之一，
即将远行探访遥远的国度。

我们真诚地希望，
你能在太平洋沿岸享受美好，
当你凝视这枚戒指时，
愿它带来愉快的回忆。

关于美好的老撒玛利亚会，
友谊让我们合而为一；
从名册上划去你的名字何其艰难，
你在英杰赛尔与我们相伴如此之久。

虽然我们遗憾地道别，
愿祝福降临你所住的每一处，
也降临你的伴侣——
愿你们幸福无缺。

===Original===
Of good old Lodge, Samaritan,

Where friendship makes us all as one;

’Tis hard to strike your name from roll

With us so long in Ingersoll.

Though we regret to say farewell,

May blessings rest where ’ere you dwell,

And on the partner of your joy

Happiness without alloy.

===Chinese===
古朴仁爱的会所，

友谊使我们亲如一家；

难以将你从名册上抹去，

你与我们同在英格索尔如此久长。

尽管我们不舍地道别，

愿祝福降临你所居之地，

也降临你欢乐伴侣的身旁，

幸福美满毫无缺憾。

===Original===
And when you gaze on this ring

May it pleasant memories bring.

===Chinese===
当你凝视这枚戒指，

愿它唤起美好回忆。

===Original===
Who was expected to attend a Banquet given by the Odd fellows in London nearly thirty years ago. Several of the London Brethern were asking for Brother Abel.

===Chinese===
此人本应出席近三十年前独立共济会（Odd Fellows）在伦敦举办的一次宴会。好几位伦敦会友都在寻找亚伯兄弟。

===Original===
When we went down to London in midst of the champaign

A brother looked at me as though I had been Cain,

And in a voice of thunder, as I sat at the table,

He fiercely asked of me "where is your Brother Abel?"

To which I did reply "I'm not my brother's keeper,

But at home you'll find him, in his bed asleep, sir."

===Chinese===
当我们来到伦敦赴宴之时，

一位兄弟看我仿佛我是该隐，

他如雷鸣般的声音响起，我坐在桌旁，

他厉声质问我"你的兄弟亚伯（Abel）在何处？"

我答道"我非我兄弟的看守人，

不过先生，你可以在他家中找到他，他正在床上酣睡。"

===Original===
When Burns did make triumphant entry

’Mong Edina's famous gentry,

A discussion did there arise

Among those solons learned and wise,

About some lines by a new poet.

The author's name none did know it,

Poem was of Canadian snow

And how o'er it the blood did flow,

For it had then been swept by war

Where armies met in deadly jar.

===Chinese===
当彭斯（Burns）凯旋般地进入

爱丁娜的名门望族之中，

一场讨论在那里兴起，

在那些博学多智的贤达之间，

关于一位新诗人的几行诗句。

无人知晓作者的姓名，

诗写的是加拿大的雪，

以及鲜血如何流淌其上，

因为那片土地曾被战火席卷，

军队在那里殊死交锋。

===Original===
But 'mong philosophers was boy

Of tender years now Scotland's joy,

He there did quickly quote each line

And author's name he did define,

Burns glanced at him with loving eyes,

Youth ever more that look did prize,

The happiest moment in his lot

Ever revered by Walter Scott.

===Chinese===
但在哲人之间有个少年，

年幼却已是苏格兰的骄傲，

他当场迅速背诵了每一行诗，

并准确说出了作者的姓名，

彭斯（Burns）以慈爱的目光瞥了他一眼，

那个青年永远珍视那目光，

那是他一生中最幸福的时刻，

后来被沃尔特·司各特（Walter Scott）永远铭记。

===Original===
Thus Scotlands greatest poets met,

And they did part with sad regret.

===Chinese===
就这样，苏格兰最伟大的两位诗人相遇了，

他们分别时满怀不舍的遗憾。

===Original===
John Galt was the manager of the Canada Company's lands, and he was a Scottish Novelist. Dunlop was at one time an eminent British Journalist, but he finally settled near Goderich. The town of Galt is named after John Galt.

===Chinese===
约翰·高尔特（John Galt）是加拿大公司地产的经理，同时他也是一位苏格兰小说家。邓洛普（Dunlop）曾是一位杰出的英国记者，但最终定居在戈德里奇附近。高尔特镇即以约翰·高尔特命名。

===Original===
John Galt and Doctor Dunlop witty

They located and planned the city

Of Guelph, and they cut the first tree down,

The stump was the centre of the town.

From thence the streets radiate like fan,

And they projected on this same plan

The towns of Stratford and Goderich,

The last it stands near broad Huron's beach,

===Chinese===
约翰·高尔特和邓洛普博士才智过人，

他们选址并规划了

圭尔夫城，砍下了第一棵树，

树桩便是这座城镇的中心。

街道从那里如扇形般辐射开来，

他们按同一蓝图还规划了

斯特拉特福和戈德里奇两座城镇，

后者矗立在广阔的休伦湖岸旁。

===Original===
Conspicuous on a bluff so grand,

'Neath which doth flow the clear Maitland,

Of glorious view you may partake,

Gazing on Huron's mighty lake.

===Chinese===
耸立在雄伟的峭壁之上，

清澈的梅特兰河在下方流淌，

你可以尽享壮丽的景色，

眺望休伦湖浩渺的湖面。

===Original===
Poor laborers they did sad bewail,

When the machine displaced the flail,

There's little work now with the hoes.

Since cultivators weed the rows.

Labor it became more fickle,

When the scythe took place of sickle,

Labor still it did sink lower,

By introduction of mower.

===Chinese===
可怜的劳工们悲伤地叹息，

当机器取代了连枷，

如今锄头的活计少了。

因为耕耘机已在田间除草。

劳动变得更加飘忽不定，

当长柄镰刀取代了短镰，

劳动的价值继续跌落，

因为割草机又随之登场。

===Original===
And the work was done much cheaper

When they added on the reaper,

Another machine to it they join,

Mower, reaper, binder, all combine.

Machines now load and stow away,

Both the barley and the hay,

And the farmers do get richer

With the loader and the pitcher.

===Chinese===
工作变得更为廉价，

当他们又加上了收割机，

再将一台机器与之相连，

割草机、收割机、捆扎机，合而为一。

如今机器装载并堆垛，

大麦和干草统统收好，

农民们因此日渐富足，

靠着装载机和投叉手。

===Original===
There's very few men now hand sows,

No more broad cast the grain it grows,

They sow and rake by the machine,

Hand labor is 'mong the things have been.

Armed with scythes the old war chariot,

Cut men down in the fierce war riot,

Round farmers' chariot fall the slain,

But 'tis the sheaves of golden grain.

===Chinese===
如今极少有人手工播种，

不再抛撒谷物任其生长，

他们用机器播种和耙地，

手工劳作已成往事。

古代战车配有镰刀武装，

在激烈的战争中砍杀敌人，

围绕农民的"战车"倒下的不是敌军，

而是成捆的金色谷物。

===Original===
A True Tale

"Can such things be and overcome us like a summer cloud, without our special wonder."

> Shakespeare

===Chinese===
真实的故事

"这样的事情竟能发生，并如夏日浮云般笼罩我们，而我们竟不觉得惊奇。"

——莎士比亚

===Original===
In a grocery store in Ingersoll our attention was called to a copper-headed snake wriggling in a glass jar. We noticed a peculiarity about its head, but soon found out it was a potato bug, which was afraid of being drowned; and the only above water being the snake's head and neck, it was fondly clinging thereto. There being "no jutty-frieze buttress or coigne of vantage, where it could make its pendant bed, elsewhere."

===Chinese===
在英格索尔（Ingersoll）一家杂货店里，我们的注意力被一条在玻璃罐中扭动的铜头蛇吸引。我们注意到它头部有个异样之处，但很快发现那是一只马铃薯甲虫，它害怕被淹死；而露出水面的只有蛇的头和脖子，它便紧紧地攀附其上，因为"没有飞檐壁柱，也没有可以悬挂的角落。"

===Original===
Some poets they abroad do roam,

But we find themes are near to home;

As we do seldom travel far,

This is a song of a glass jar.

Snake of species of the copper,

And on its head there was live hopper,

For we saw that funny sight

In a store, it was last night.

===Chinese===
有些诗人四处游荡远行，

但我们发现题材近在咫尺；

既然我们少有远行，

这便是一首玻璃罐之歌。

一条铜头蛇的品种，

头上却有只活蹦的甲虫，

因为昨晚我们亲眼目睹

那滑稽的一幕，在一家店铺之中。

===Original===
There in water was a snake,

And a bug so wide awake;

He was afraid that he would drown

So he clomb up on the snake's crown.

===Chinese===
水中有一条蛇，

和一只异常清醒的甲虫；

它害怕自己会淹死，

于是爬上了蛇的头顶。

===Original===
This snake it is near a foot long,

Which doth suffer this great wrong,

It thinks the bug wants it to throttle

This makes it wriggle in the bottle.

But fondly the kind hearted bug,

It doth its preserver hug,

For the bug when on the water

It is only but a squatter.

===Chinese===
这条蛇将近一尺来长，

忍受着如此大的冤屈，

它以为甲虫想扼住它，

这使它在瓶中不停扭动。

但那只心地善良的甲虫，

却深情地拥抱着它的救命恩虫，

因为甲虫身在水中时，

只不过是个漂泊的流浪者。

===Original===
And hath taken up homestead

On the top of the snake's head,

And on the waters it doth float

Safe and happy on this boat.

===Chinese===
它已在蛇的头顶上

安下了家园，

在水面上它安然漂浮，

在这艘小船上安全而快活。

===Original===
At a social society gathering, a quarter of a century ago, a member from a distance was called on by the gentlemen present to tell his Rabbit story. We got up in a few minutes afterward and gave it in verse as follows:﻿—

===Chinese===
大约二十五年前，在一次社交聚会上，一位远道而来的会员被在场的绅士们邀请讲述他的兔子故事。几分钟后我们站起来，以诗歌的形式将其吟诵如下：

===Original===
Our friend, Mr. Romley,

He told the tale homley,

And yet full of fun,

How the Rabbits did run,

In numbers a score,

All around his door.

They were red, black and white,

Their play folks did delight;

But they got small by degrees,

For the cats did them seise﻿—

All but one big black Rabbit,

He got into the habit

Under ground he would dive﻿—

He long time did survive,

'Till owner, wanting fat pot,

He this great beast then shot.

===Chinese===
我们的朋友罗姆利先生（Mr. Romley），

他讲述了一个质朴的故事，

却充满了欢趣，

讲兔子们如何奔跑，

数量足有二十来只，

在他家门口四处窜动。

它们有红的、黑的和白的，

它们的嬉戏令人开心；

但它们逐渐变少了，

因为猫儿们将它们逮住——

只剩一只大黑兔，

它养成了一种习惯，

爱往地底下钻——

它存活了很久，

直到主人想要一锅肥肉，

便开枪射杀了这只大块头。

===Original===
When police heard the gun,

Full quickly he did run,

In a furious rage;

But his wrath did as‑uage

When he got a quarter

Of the last martyr.

===Chinese===
当警犬听见枪声，

他飞奔而来，

怒不可遏；

但他的怒气消退了，

当他分到了一小份

最后一位殉难者的肉。

===Original===
Will you please to let me go, Ma,

To McIntyre's, to buy a Sofa.

===Chinese===
妈妈，请你让我去吧，

去麦金泰尔（McIntyre）家，买一张沙发。

===Original===
Lines written at the request of a young lady and inserted in a mental Album. We looked over the book, it was in form of questions and answers. One of the questions was "who is your favorite poet?" We were quite amused to find a number of different persons had written in answer, "McIntyre." We believe that the most of them were joking, but we hope to inspire them with a loftier idea of our poetic power in the future.

===Chinese===
应一位年轻女士之请而写的诗行，被收录于一本心灵纪念册中。我们翻阅了那本书，它以问答形式编排。其中一个问题是你最喜欢的诗人是谁？我们颇为有趣地发现许多不同的人写道："麦金泰尔。"我们相信他们大多是在开玩笑，但我们希望将来能以更高远的诗歌境界来激励他们。

===Original===
In this Album you may trace﻿—

If not the lineaments of face﻿—

Here, at least, you will find

Photographs of the mind.

Some in earnest, some in fun,

Some do lecture, some do pun;

Here the maiden and the youth,

Each proclaim some precious truth.

===Chinese===
在这本纪念册中，你或许能寻觅到——

纵然不是面容的轮廓——

至少在这里，你能看到

心灵的留影。

有些庄重，有些诙谐，

有些说教，有些打趣；

少女与青年在此留言，

各抒心中的珍贵感悟。

===Original===
And there is some fine pages﻿—

Written by maturer ages﻿—

They show that time is brief,

That soon comes sere and yellow leaf.

But we must cease, 'ere your ire

Be aroused 'gainst McIntyre,

Who doth reside in Ingersoll﻿—

Perhaps you'll think that he is droll.

===Chinese===
还有一些精彩的篇章——

由阅历更丰的岁月所书写——

它们昭示时光短暂，

枯黄的叶子转瞬即至。

但我们必须搁笔了，以免

您对麦金泰尔（McIntyre）心生不满，

他居住在英格索尔（Ingersoll）——

或许您会觉得他颇为滑稽。

===Original===
Having received a letter from a gentleman done with a typewriter, and glorying in its superiority to the pen, we replied as follows:﻿—

===Chinese===
收到一位绅士用打字机写来的信，他因打字机比手写笔更优越而自豪，我们回复如下：

===Original===
You glory in your typewriter,

And its virtues you rehearse,

But we prefer the old inditer,

For to write either prose or verse.

And let each man work his will,

But never never do abuse

The ancient and glorious quill

From the wing of a fine old goose.12

===Chinese===
你以打字机为荣，

将它的美德反复颂扬，

但我们更钟爱那古老的书写之物，

无论写散文还是写诗。

让每个人各行其是吧，

但万万不可诋毁

那古老而荣耀的鹅毛笔，

来自一只上好的老鹅翅膀。[注12]

===Original===
On a young girl showing me a scar on her cheek where a stick of wood struck her.

===Chinese===
一位年轻女孩向我展示她脸颊上的伤疤，那是被一根木棍打的。

===Original===
In its own place 'tis very good

Always to have plenty of wood;

But, striking fair maid, that is rude,

And puts me in an angry mood.

===Chinese===
在适当的地方，木材充足固然好；

但打在漂亮姑娘身上，那就无礼了，

让我不禁怒火中烧。

===Original===
Sonnet on an incident which occurred on a small Lake in Northern Ontario.

===Chinese===
十四行诗，记述安大略北部一个小湖上发生的一段往事。

===Original===
Pleasant memories it awakes,

When musing on our northern lakes;

For there I saw a charming Reed,

A friend to me in hour of need.

I wished to cross to other shore,

And deftly she did ply the oar,

And o'er the Lake me swiftly bore.

===Chinese===
它唤起愉悦的回忆，

当我沉思北方的湖泊；

因为在那里我遇见一位迷人的姑娘，

在困窘之时如朋友般相助。

我渴望渡到对岸，

她灵巧地划动双桨，

载我飞速穿越湖面。

===Original===
I was plunged in deep despair,

Before I met this charming fair;

I could not go around by land,

And I felt like shipwrecked on the strand;

Until fair vision hove in sight

Graceful as a fairy sprite.

===Chinese===
在遇见这位迷人的姑娘之前，

我陷入了深深的绝望；

我无法绕陆地而行，

感觉自己如同搁浅的破船；

直到一个美丽的身影出现在眼前，

优雅宛如仙灵。

===Original===
May she be blest, is the desire

Of her true friend James McIntyre.

===Chinese===
愿她得到祝福，这便是

她真诚的朋友詹姆斯·麦金泰尔（James McIntyre）的心愿。

===Original===
The following lines were written at the request of a little girl, who said she would recite them at a Sunday School entertainment. Prof. J. S. Blackie of Edinburgh, in a letter acknowledging the receipt of my book, said he considered this piece worthy of being committed to memory in the public schools. Sir Daniel Wilson of Toronto University also approves of them as containing good sentiments and should be impressed on the minds of the young.

===Chinese===
以下诗行应一位小女孩之请而作，她说要在主日学（Sunday School）的联欢会上朗诵。爱丁堡的J.S.布莱基教授（Prof. J. S. Blackie）在来信确认收到我的书时，认为这首诗值得在公立学校背诵。多伦多大学的丹尼尔·威尔逊爵士（Sir Daniel Wilson）也赞同这些诗句蕴含良好的情感，应当铭刻在年轻人的心中。

===Original===
Dressing in fashion will be called vain,

And they'll call you a dowdy if you are plain,

But do what is right, let that be the test,

Then proudly hold up your head with the best.

For people will talk.

===Chinese===
穿着时髦会被人说虚荣，

穿着朴素会被叫作邋遢，

但做对的事，以此为准绳，

然后昂首挺胸与最优秀的人并肩。

因为人们总会议论。

===Original===
You will never be wrong if you do what is right,

And this course pursue with all of your might,

And if you're a child going to school,

Or full grown up take this for your rule.

For people will talk.

===Chinese===
做对的事你永远不会错，

全力以赴地坚持这条道路，

无论你是上学的孩童，

还是已经长大成人，都以此为准则。

因为人们总会议论。

===Original===
The best way to do is to let them rave

And they'll think more of you if you are brave,

For no one will ever think you are rude

If you are determined for to be good.

For people will talk.

===Chinese===
最好的办法是任由他们喧哗，

你越是勇敢他们越敬重你，

只要你是真心向善，

没有人会觉得你粗鲁。

因为人们总会议论。

===Original===
Little girl on her way to Sunday School class,

Rude boys sometimes will not let her pass,

But if they see she is not afraid

They soon will respect the brave little maid.

For people will talk.

===Chinese===
小女孩走在去主日学的路上，

粗鲁的男孩有时不让她过，

但如果他们看到她毫不畏惧，

很快就会尊重这位勇敢的小姑娘。

因为人们总会议论。

===Original===
Little girls should learn to knit and to sew,

Then if to womanhood they ever grow,

Their hose they can knit and make their own dress,

And pathway of life for others they bless.

For people will talk.

===Chinese===
小女孩应该学会编织和缝纫，

日后长大成人时，

便能织袜子、做衣裳，

为他人的生命之路带来祝福。

因为人们总会议论。

===Original===
And their homes they should make tidy and neat,

Everything should be so clean and so sweet,

This line for ourselves out we will chalk

And we are determined in it to walk.

For people will talk.

===Chinese===
她们应当把家收拾得整洁有序，

一切都要干净而温馨，

我们将为自己划定这条线，

并决心沿着它走下去。

因为人们总会议论。

===Original===
Dangerous effects of seeing onesself in a crooked glass.

===Chinese===
照哈哈镜的危险效果。

===Original===
A maiden cried, "Alas!

With horror I'll expire,

Unless you bring me

That true glass

I bought of McIntyre."

===Chinese===
一位少女惊呼，"唉呀！

我要被吓死了，

除非你把我买自

麦金泰尔（McIntyre）

的那面真镜子拿来。"

===Original===
Providential escape of Ruby and Neil McLeod, children of Angus McLeod of this town. Little Neil McKay McLeod, a child of three years of age, was carried under a covered raceway, upwards of one hundred yards, the whole distance being either covered o'er with roadway, buildings or ice.

===Chinese===
鲁比（Ruby）和尼尔·麦克劳德（Neil McLeod）的天意般的脱险，他们是本镇安格斯·麦克劳德（Angus McLeod）的孩子。年仅三岁的小尼尔·麦凯·麦克劳德（Neil McKay McLeod）被冲入一条有盖的水道，被冲出一百多码远，整段距离上方都被路面、建筑物或冰层覆盖着。

===Original===
A wondrous tale we now do trace

Of little children fell in race,

The youngest of these little dears,

The boy's age is but three years.

While coasting o'er the treacherous ice,

These precious pearls of great price,

The elder Ruby, the daughter,

Was rescued from the ice cold water.

===Chinese===
我们讲述一个奇妙的故事，

关于落入水中赛道的孩子们，

最年幼的小宝贝，

男孩年仅三岁。

当在险恶的冰面上滑行时，

这两颗珍贵的珍珠——

年长的女儿鲁比（Ruby），

从冰冷的水中获救。

===Original===
But horrid death each one did feel,

Had sure befallen little Neil,

Consternation all did fill,

And they cried shut down the mill.

===Chinese===
但他们都感觉死亡如此可怖，

小尼尔（Neil）肯定已在劫难逃，

恐慌笼罩了所有人，

他们大喊关掉磨坊。

===Original===
But still no person they could tell

What had the poor child befell,

The covered race, so long and dark,

Of hopes there scarcely seemed a spark.

===Chinese===
但仍然无人能说清

那可怜的孩子遭遇了什么，

有盖的水道又长又暗，

几乎看不到一丝希望的火花。

===Original===
Was he held fast as if in vice,

Wedged 'mong the timbers and the ice,

Or was there for him ample room

For to float down the narrow flume.

===Chinese===
他是被紧紧夹住了，如同被老虎钳夹住，

卡在木梁和冰块之间，

还是有足够的空间，

让他在狭窄的水槽中漂流而下。

===Original===
Had he found there a watery grave,

Or borne along on crest of wave,

Think of the mother's agony wild,

Gazing through dark tunnel for her child.

===Chinese===
他是否在那里沉入了水底，

还是被浪尖托着漂流，

想象那位母亲疯狂的悲痛，

透过黑暗的隧道张望她的孩子。

===Original===
But soon as Partlo' started mill,

Through crowd there ran a joyous thrill,

When he was quickly borne along,

The little hero of our song.

===Chinese===
但当帕特洛（Partlo）启动磨坊时，

一阵欣喜的颤栗传遍人群，

当小英雄被迅速带出，

我们歌中这位小小的英雄。

===Original===
Alas! of life there is no trace,

And he is black all over face,

Though he then seemed as if in death,

Yet quickly they restored his breath.

===Chinese===
唉！毫无生命的迹象，

他满脸乌黑，

尽管他看起来如同死去，

但他们很快让他恢复了呼吸。

===Original===
Think now how mother13 she adored

Her sweet dear child to her restored,

And her boundless gratitude

Unto the author of all good.

===Chinese===
想想那位母亲[注13]多么珍爱

她甜美的孩子失而复得，

以及她对万善之源

无尽的感恩。

===Original===
Swept through dark passage 'neath the road,

Saved only by the hand of God,

No wonder Father now feels proud

Of little Niel McKay McLeod.

===Chinese===
穿越道路下方的黑暗通道被冲刷而出，

唯有上帝之手才能拯救，

难怪父亲如今如此自豪，

为他的小尼尔·麦凯·麦克劳德。

===Original===
Lines on Rannie, the cheese pioneer, written a quarter century ago.

===Chinese===
关于奶酪先驱兰尼（Rannie）的诗，写于二十五年前。

===Original===
Rannie began with just two cows,

Which he in winter fed on brouse,

And now he hath got mighty herds,

Numerous as flock of birds;

May he long live, our hearts to cheer,

This great and useful Pioneer.

===Chinese===
兰尼（Rannie）起步时仅有两头母牛，

冬天他以灌木枝叶喂养它们，

如今他拥有了庞大的牛群，

多如飞鸟成群；

愿他长寿，长暖我们的心怀，

这位伟大而实用的先驱。

===Original===
An old man who had charge of field,

With pride he saw two birds did build,

A broad capacious warm nest,

So full of young with speckled breast,

===Chinese===
一位老人看管着田野，

他骄傲地看到两只鸟筑巢，

一个宽敞温暖的大窝，

满巢幼鸟，胸前布满斑点。

===Original===
And when the old man there did pass,

They soon ran merry 'mong the grass,

But of the youth they were so shy,

They made strong efforts for to fly.

===Chinese===
当老人经过那里时，

它们很快在草丛中欢快地跑动，

但幼鸟们非常怕生，

它们拼命地想要飞翔。

===Original===
Youths tried with old man to prevail,

To let them blaze away at quail,

But though they longed for a fat pot,

At them they never got a shot.

===Chinese===
年轻人们试图说服老人，

让他们去猎杀鹌鹑，

但他们尽管垂涎一顿肥肉，

始终没能射中一只。

===Original===
No more the old man doth them shield,

For they have flown to broader field,

Long may they spread their wings and tail,

And may no foe them 'ere assail.

===Chinese===
老人不再庇护它们，

因为它们已飞向更广阔的天地，

愿它们展翅振尾长久翱翔，

愿没有敌人侵袭它们。

===Original===
Come, listen, while we sound the lyre,

To announce the fact, that McIntyre

Is back again to his old block,

And he has got a splendid stock.

===Chinese===
来吧，听我们弹响诗琴，

宣布一个事实，麦金泰尔（McIntyre）

已回到他原来的老店面，

而且他备有极好的存货。

===Original===
He also hath a strong desire

To see old friends, and new acquire;

His Furniture is cheap and good,

In every style and kind of wood.

===Chinese===
他还热切地渴望

见到老朋友，结交新朋友；

他的家具物美价廉，

各种款式和木材应有尽有。

===Original===
But none in health need 'ere despair,

If they buy from him an easy chair.

When you his Warehouse then do seek,

'Tis where the brick bridge spans the creek.

===Chinese===
只要身体健康，无人需要绝望，

如果从他那里买一把安乐椅。

当你寻找他的仓库时，

它就在砖桥横跨小溪之处。

===Original===
With a glance at Sir John A. Macdonald and Blake, the two living leaders, 1884.

===Chinese===
兼及约翰·A·麦克唐纳爵士（Sir John A. Macdonald）和布莱克（Blake）两位在世领袖，1884年。

===Original===
Joseph Howe, none higher stood than thou,

Thou wert a man with lofty brow;

D'Arcy McGee, so brilliant and free,

From green isle you came o'er the sea.

===Chinese===
约瑟夫·豪（Joseph Howe），无人比你更高贵，

你是一个额头高昂的人；

达西·麦吉（D'Arcy McGee），如此才华横溢、潇洒不羁，

你从绿岛漂洋过海而来。

===Original===
George Cartier to the French ever dear,

So high you stood without a peer;

John Sandfield14 for long you did build

Power under economy's shield.

===Chinese===
乔治·卡蒂埃（George Cartier），永远受法兰西人爱戴，

你卓然而立，无人能及；

约翰·桑德菲尔德（John Sandfield）[注14]，你长久地建构

经济庇护下的权力。

===Original===
George Brown, thou man of renown,

Confederation you did crown;

You now are all free from the strife

The wrangle and jangle of political life.

===Chinese===
乔治·布朗（George Brown），你声名远扬的人，

联邦的桂冠由你加冕；

如今你已远离一切纷争，

远离政治生活中无尽的争吵与喧嚣。

===Original===
But if a glance at this world you take

You will there see John A. and Blake,

But Sir John the greatest power doth wield,

Our Canadian Beaconsfield.15

===Chinese===
但如果你将目光投向此世，

你会看到约翰·A（John A.）和布莱克（Blake），

但约翰爵士掌握着最大的权力，

我们的加拿大贝肯斯菲尔德。[注15]

===Original===
The following impromptu was delivered in the Methodist Church Concert, March, 1883. It was expected that several announced in bills world take part, but from various causes they were not present. We were unexpectedly requested to fill one of the vacencies. Doctor Gardiner delivered an address, showing how he had triumphed o'er the great snow drifts during the remarkably severe winter then passing away, that we thought his courage and perseverance was worthy of being commemorated in verse; and the music of the White Brothers though simple, is natural, touching and sweet, and reaches the heart more easily than some of the highly artistic styles now in vogue, on arising, we first gave some recitations from the Poets and concluded with the following Impromptu. It was of course impossible for me to have anticipated any of the above occurrences, as the Doctors speech took the turn it did from the remarks of a previous speaker.

===Chinese===
以下即兴诗于1883年3月在卫理公会教堂音乐会（Methodist Church Concert）上发表。原本预期海报上宣布的几位参与者会出席，但由于各种原因他们未能到场。我们意外地被邀请填补其中一个空缺。加德纳博士（Doctor Gardiner）发表了演说，讲述了他如何在那个异常严寒的冬天战胜巨大的雪堆，我们认为他的勇气和毅力值得以诗歌纪念；而怀特兄弟（White Brothers）的音乐虽然朴实，却自然、感人、甜美，比当下流行的一些高度艺术化的风格更容易打动人心。起身后，我们先朗诵了几首诗人的作品，最后以这首即兴诗作结。我当然无法预见到上述所有事件，因为加德纳博士的演讲之所以转向这个话题，是因为前面一位演讲者的话。

===Original===
To us it is a pleasing sight,

To see so many here to night;

All listening with so much delight

Unto those worthy men of might

The charming songsters, Brothers White.

===Chinese===
对我们来说，这是令人愉悦的景象，

看到今夜如此多的人齐聚；

所有人都满怀欣喜地聆听

那些威武可敬之人——

迷人的歌者，怀特兄弟。

===Original===
Gardner told a sad tale of woe,

How he was oft o'erwhelmed in snow;

But was he frightened? no! no!! no!!!

He onward cheerfully did go,

And though that he did freeze his cheek

The fire side he did never seek.

But straight went onward, in his course,

So happy, driving his good horse,

And merrily along the way

The bells did ring around his sleigh.

===Chinese===
加德纳（Gardner）讲述了一个悲惨的故事，

讲他如何屡屡被大雪吞没；

但他害怕了吗？不！不！！不！！！

他欢快地继续前行，

尽管寒风冻伤了他的脸颊，

他也从未寻求炉火的温暖。

他径直向前，在他的道路上，

如此快乐地驾驭着他的骏马，

一路上欢快地前行，

雪橇周围的铃铛叮当作响。

===Original===
Lines on the death of my son, Alexander Murray McIntyre, who died on the anniversary of his mother's death, after being injured in my Furniture Factory. He died, after several days of great suffering, in July 5th, 1876.

===Chinese===
悼念我的儿子亚历山大·默里·麦金泰尔（Alexander Murray McIntyre），他在母亲忌日这一天去世，此前在我的家具工厂受伤。经过数日的极度痛苦，他于1876年7月5日离世。

===Original===
His mother from celestial bower,

In the self-same day and hour

Of her death or heavenly birth,

Gazed again upon the earth,

And saw her gentle, loving boy,

Once source of fond maternal joy,

In anguish on a couch of pain.

===Chinese===
他的母亲从天堂的花园，

在她辞世或升天的

同一个日子里，

再次凝望人间，

看到她温柔、亲爱的儿子，

曾经是母爱欢愉的源泉，

正痛苦地躺在病榻之上。

===Original===
She knew that earthly hopes were vain,

And beckoned him to realms above

To share with her the heavenly love.

===Chinese===
她知道尘世的希望已经渺茫，

便召唤他前往天上的世界，

与她共享天国的爱。

===Original===
The following sonnet on Brodie Castle Nairnshire, Scotland, was written by my venerable Uncle, Thomas McIntyre, of St. Catherines, and father of John B. McIntyre, Ex-Grand Master of the Independant Order of Odd Fellows, and now, and for many years, an Alderman of the city of St. Catherines. It was written by him as an acrostic on the words Brodie Castle. I have taken the liberty of transfusing it into common verse. The lines prove the old gentlemen to be kind hearted and as having a great affection for his native land. He has been in. St. Catherines for half a century, engaged in the same business I have been employed for about one-third of a century, in Ingersoll.

===Chinese===
以下这首关于苏格兰内恩郡（Nairnshire）布罗迪堡（Brodie Castle）的十四行诗，出自我的伯父托马斯·麦金泰尔（Thomas McIntyre），他是圣凯瑟琳斯（St. Catherines）人，也是约翰·B·麦金泰尔（John B. McIntyre）的父亲。约翰曾任独立共济会（Independant Order of Odd Fellows）大导师，如今担任圣凯瑟琳斯市议员已多年。这首诗原是他以"布罗迪堡"（Brodie Castle）各字母开头的藏头诗。我擅自将其改写为普通诗体。这些诗句证明这位老先生心地善良，对故土怀有深厚的感情。他在圣凯瑟琳斯已住了半个世纪，从事着我在英格索尔（Ingersoll）做了大约三分之一世纪的同一行当。

===Original===
Scotland has produced three great shepherds

Talford, the inventor of Suspension Bridges,

Ferguson, the Astronomer, and Hogg the

Ettrick shepherd, the great song writer.

===Chinese===
苏格兰孕育了三位伟大的牧羊人——

塔尔福德（Talford），悬索桥的发明者，

弗格森（Ferguson），天文学家，还有霍格（Hogg），

埃特里克牧羊人，伟大的歌者。

===Original===
Brodie Castle, ever dear to my heart,

For there I first played my humble part.

When only thirteen, I there had employ.

In the position of a shepherd boy;

===Chinese===
布罗迪堡（Brodie Castle），永远铭刻在我心间，

因为我在那里初次扮演了卑微的角色。

年仅十三岁，我便在那里谋生。

做一个牧羊少年；

===Original===
For I was born on that fine estate,

Famed for its trees, so lofty and great,

And its magnificent avenue grand,

Which hath been famed over the land.

===Chinese===
因为我出生在那座美好的庄园，

以高大雄伟的树木闻名，

还有那壮丽的林荫大道，

名声远播全国各地。

===Original===
And, though I did leave to learn my trade,

And in Western world a home I have made,

Yet, when I look back, my heart it still cheers,

Though it is now more than threescore years.

===Chinese===
虽然我离开去学习手艺，

在西方世界安了家，

但当我回首往事，心中依然欣慰，

尽管那已是六十多年前的岁月。

===Original===
Since first I went there as a shepherd boy,

Thoughts of the time fill my heart yet with joy,

Though, that I ne'er shall see Castle more,

Fond memory reverts to the days of yore.

===Chinese===
自从我初次以牧羊少年的身份前往那里，

那段时光的回忆至今仍让我的心充满欢喜，

虽然我再也无法重见那座城堡，

深情的记忆仍会回到往昔的岁月。

===Original===
1883.

===Chinese===
1883年。

===Original===
About one-third of a century ago, there flourished in Canada three Scottish Editors, all of whom were Poets, McQueen of the "Huron Signal," Goderich, who wrote a grand song on "Our Broad Lake," McGeorge, of the Streetsville Review, who returned to Scotland nearly a quarter of a century ago; he became a clergymen in connection with the Episcopal Church, of Oban, Argyleshire, and was dean thereof; he enjoyed the friendship of Dr. Norman McLeod, and many of the Scottish celebrities; he died this spring highly honored by all who knew him. George Menzie, Editor of a Woodstock paper, wrote, also, some fine poems. All of the gentlemen named have gone to "the land of the Leal," the following lines were suggested by finding a volume by George Menzie.

===Chinese===
大约三分之一世纪之前，加拿大有三位苏格兰编辑活跃一时，他们都是诗人。有戈德里奇（Goderich）《休伦信号报》（Huron Signal）的麦克奎恩（McQueen），他写了一首歌颂"我们辽阔湖泊"的壮丽诗歌；有《斯特里茨维尔评论》（Streetsville Review）的麦克乔治（McGeorge），他近四分之一个世纪前回到了苏格兰，成为一名圣公会牧师，担任奥本（Oban）、阿盖尔郡（Argyleshire）的教长；他与诺曼·麦克劳德博士（Dr. Norman McLeod）以及许多苏格兰名流交好；今年春天他去世了，深受所有认识他的人尊敬。伍德斯托克一家报纸的编辑乔治·门齐斯（George Menzie）也写过一些好诗。上述各位先生都已前往"忠信者之国"（the land of the Leal），以下诗行因发现乔治·门齐斯的一卷诗集而触发。

===Original===
One day while passing 'long the road

On a small book we almost trod,

Its leaves were scattered o'er the ground,

We picked them up and when we found

===Chinese===
一天，我们走在路上，

差点踩到一本小书，

它的书页散落在地上，

我们拾起它们，当我们发现

===Original===
The author's name, it did inspire

Us with a very strong desire

To read the little volume through,

For most of it to us was new.

===Chinese===
作者的名字时，它激起了

我们强烈的渴望，

要将这本小书通读一遍，

因为大部分内容对我们而言是崭新的。

===Original===
He doth sing of land of heather

And Canadian scenes together,

He did adore Niagara's roar

Where mighty flood o'er fall doth pour.

===Chinese===
他歌唱石楠花的国度

和加拿大的风景，

他倾慕尼亚加拉的轰鸣，

在那里雄浑的洪流倾泻而下。

===Original===
But poets lives are often brief

And he had his full share of grief,

Which to his life did gloom impart,

But he bore up with his brave heart.

===Chinese===
但诗人的人生往往短暂，

他承受了充分的悲伤，

这给他的生活蒙上了阴翳，

但他以坚强的心承受了一切。

===Original===
"Revenge is sweet, especially to women."

> Byron

===Chinese===
"复仇是甜蜜的，尤其对女人而言。"

——拜伦

===Original===
Remarkable strong vengeance on the part of a young lady who had always previously been considered of an amiable turn of mind; but, how sadly, alas! for the young man, her whole being, as it were, seemed to be transformed into vengeance dire, against the poor unfortunate youth who had vainly and rashly boasted of a glorious prize he intended to capture from the fair one's sweet lips.

===Chinese===
一位年轻女士爆发出了惊人的强烈报复，而她此前一直被认为性情温和；但，多么可悲啊！对那个年轻人而言，她的全部身心仿佛都化为了可怕的复仇之火，针对那个不幸的可怜青年，他曾徒劳而鲁莽地夸口说要从美人甜美的嘴唇上夺取一个辉煌的战利品。

===Original===
I heard Bill say to-day, Mary,

That you are a charming fairy,

And that to town he'd give you drive,

But just as sure as you're alive,

He does intend to have the bliss

Of stealing from your lips a kiss.

===Chinese===
我听比尔（Bill）今天说，玛丽（Mary），

你是个迷人的仙女，

他要驾车送你进城，

但只要你活着你就知道，

他确实打算窃取

从你唇上偷一个吻的幸福。

===Original===
I'll let him drive me now, Jane,

His efforts they will all be vain,

I hate him, and I him defy,

And anger flashed from her eye,

The monster's wiles I will defeat,

Peck of strong onions I will eat.

===Chinese===
我现在就让他送我，简（Jane），

他的企图注定白费，

我恨他，我藐视他，

愤怒从她眼中闪过，

我要挫败那个恶棍的诡计，

我要吃一大捧浓烈的洋葱。

===Original===
An English youth to Canada came,

A labourer, John Roe by name,

His little wealth had made him bold,

Twenty sovereigns in gold;

He was industrious and wise

And e'en small sums did not despise,

He added to his wealth each year

For independence he loved dear,

He knew a laborer he would be

Forever in the old country,

His forefathers had tilled the ground

And never one had saved a pound.

===Chinese===
一位英国青年来到加拿大，

劳工，名叫约翰·罗（John Roe），

小小的财富使他胆壮，

二十枚金币在身上；

他勤劳而明智，

连小钱也不轻视，

年复一年积攒财富，

他珍爱独立的生活，

他知道自己若留在故国

将永远做一个劳工，

他的祖先世代耕田，

从未有人攒下一英镑。

===Original===
On beds of down they did not lie

And frugally their goods did buy,

Their one luxury around their door

A few choice flowers their garden bore,

But never hoped to own the soil

But serve as hinds to sweat and toil,

To work and toil for him had charm

He hoped some day to own a farm,

So he hired with Reuben Tripp

The wealthiest man in the township.

===Chinese===
他们从未睡过羽绒床，

节俭地购买日用品，

他们唯一的奢侈

便是门口花园中几朵精选的鲜花，

但他们从不敢奢望拥有土地，

只做仆役流汗劳作，

工作和苦干对他有吸引力，

他希望有一天拥有一座农场，

于是他受雇于鲁本·特里普（Reuben Tripp），

镇上最富有的人。

===Original===
Tripp's only child, his daughter Jane,

He sought her love and not in vain,

As Jacob served for Rachel dear

So John he served year after year,

Till rich enough to buy bush farm

For to chop down with his strong arm.

===Chinese===
特里普（Tripp）的独生女简（Jane），

他追求她的爱，并非徒劳，

正如雅各为亲爱的拉结（Rachel）效力，

约翰年复一年地工作，

直到足够富裕，可以买一座荒地农场，

用他强壮的臂膀去开垦。

===Original===
The truest nobleman of all

He lives not in ancestral hall,

But sheltereth family from harm

By logs rolled up by his strong arm,

In this young glorious land so free

Where each may rear his own roof tree,

And the chief glory of old days

Broad fire place where big logs did blaze,

===Chinese===
最真正的贵族

他不住在祖传的宅邸，

而是用他强壮的臂膀滚来原木，

保护家人免受伤害，

在这片年轻而光荣的自由之地上，

人人可以竖起自己的屋梁，

昔日最辉煌的荣耀

是宽大的壁炉里巨木熊熊燃烧，

===Original===
As much as four strong men could handle;

They served alike for heat and candle;

He his young oxen did adorn

With fine gay ribbons on each horn,

And to his home with joy and pride

He did bring sweet blooming bride,

Such happiness is seldom seen,

Happier far than king or queen;

===Chinese===
四个壮汉才能搬动的大木头；

它们既供暖又照明；

他给年轻的公牛装饰上

每只角上漂亮的彩色缎带，

他满心欢喜与自豪地

将甜蜜的新娘迎娶回家，

这样的幸福极为罕见，

远胜国王与王后；

===Original===
She helped him in the fields to reap,

And spun the wool from off the sheep,

All they required they had for both,

Of her own weaving of good cloth,

And she was a good tailoress,

Did make his coat and her own dress;

===Chinese===
她帮他收割田里的庄稼，

纺羊毛为线，

他们所需的一切应有尽有，

都是她亲手织的好布料，

她还是个好裁缝，

给他做外套，给自己做衣裳；

===Original===
The golden butter that she made

Was of the very finest grade,

Each grace and virtue she possess'd,

Where'er she was, that spot was blessed,

And though they did not have stove then,

Neither did they own an oven;

===Chinese===
她做的金色黄油

品质上乘，

她拥有每一种美德与优雅，

她所在之处便是福地，

虽然他们那时没有炉灶，

也没有烤箱；

===Original===
She filled large pot with well knead dough

And baked fine bread 'mong embers glow;

He each winter the forest trees

Did quickly hew them down with ease,

For he to work had a desire

And the skill did soon acquire,

===Chinese===
她在大锅里装满揉好的面团，

在闪烁的余烬中烘烤美味的面包；

他每个冬天都砍伐森林中的树木，

轻松地将它们砍倒，

因为他渴望劳作，

很快就掌握了技巧，

===Original===
But round great giants hewed a ring

Then storms would soon them prostrate bring,

For many a time the furious breeze

Would quick o'erthrow the girdled trees,

===Chinese===
但他在大树周围砍出一圈环形，

暴风雨很快就能将它们刮倒，

因为狂暴的风不知吹倒过多少次

那些被环砍的树木，

===Original===
And sometimes they would kill the cows

When they did feed on grass or browse,

But after reckoning damage all

A benefit was each windfall;

===Chinese===
有时它们还会砸死牛，

当牛在草地上或灌木丛旁吃草，

但把所有损失算过之后，

每一次风倒都成了好事；

===Original===
Though good fortune now he sees

Might have been got from Walnut trees;

But trees were foes in his hurry,

All were slain, both oak and cherry,

And to this day he doth incline

To mourn o'er slaughter of the pine,

===Chinese===
虽然他现在发现

其实可以从胡桃树上获得财富；

但树木在匆忙中都是敌人，

全被砍伐，橡树和樱桃都不留，

直到今天他仍然倾向于

哀悼对松树的屠杀，

===Original===
And reflects how he did o'erwhelm

Many a maple, beech and elm;

And each summer day did toil

With his steers drawing logs in pile;

===Chinese===
回想他曾经摧垮了

多少枫树、山毛榉和榆树；

每个夏日都辛勤劳作，

用公牛拉起成堆的原木；

===Original===
These giants of the forest dead,

Fire did reduce to an ash bed,

And soon potatoes, wheat and corn,

They did the rugged stumps adorn,

And Jane did help him with the hoe,

And well she did keep her row:

===Chinese===
这些森林的巨人死去后，

大火将它们化为灰烬，

很快，土豆、小麦和玉米

便装点了粗犷的树桩，

简帮他锄草，

她把每一行都打理得井井有条：

===Original===
No organs then they had to play,

But she could work and sing all day;

In spring he did live maples tap

To draw from them the luscious sap,

He gathered it in big log trough,

Then boiled it down and sugared off,

===Chinese===
他们那时没有风琴弹奏，

但她可以整天干活唱歌；

春天他给枫树开口取汁，

汲取甘美的树液，

他将树液收集在原木槽里，

然后熬煮制糖，

===Original===
Enough the household for to cheer,

With all its sweets for the whole year,

And no such thing those times were seen

As the swift raising stump machine,

===Chinese===
足够让全家人开心了，

带着它全部的甜蜜度过一整年，

那时还没有

快速挖树桩的机器，

===Original===
And where main road was low and damp

With logs he built a road through swamp,

But a smooth ride could not enjoy

While it was naught but corduroy,

Each year added earth and gravel,

Now smoothly o'er they can travel,

===Chinese===
在大路低洼潮湿的地方，

他用原木在沼泽上铺路，

但木排路无法让人享受平稳的骑行，

年复一年添加泥土和碎石，

如今可以平稳地通行了，

===Original===
For it doth make an excellent road

For John and Jane to go abroad,

And it is now a great highway

Where hundreds travel every day.

===Chinese===
因为它成了一条极好的路，

供约翰和简外出之用，

它如今是一条大公路，

每天有数百人从此经过。

===Original===
There were no roads in early days

But bridle path, their guide the blaze,

And mills and marts so far away,

They never could return same day;

Log school house served as church for all,

Of various creeds, and for town hall.

===Chinese===
早期没有道路，

只有小径，以树上的刻痕为路标，

磨坊和集市如此遥远，

他们当天无法返回；

原木学校兼作所有教派的教堂，

也兼作市政厅。

===Original===
These scenes to youth do now seem strange

So wondrous quick hath been the change,

O'er paths where oxen only trod,

Cows quickly speed o'er the railroad,

And every way both up and down

There has sprung up a thriving town.

===Chinese===
这些场景如今对年轻人来说似乎陌生，

变化如此神速不可思议，

曾经只有公牛行走的小路上，

奶牛如今疾驰在铁路上，

不论上行还是下行的方向，

一座繁荣的城镇已经崛起。

===Original===
No more he fights with forest trees,

But both enjoy their wealth and ease,

Long since the old folks both are gone

And left the whole to Jane and John;

The log house now has passed away

With all its chinks filled in with clay,

And in its place fine house of stone

With lawn where choice shrubs are grown.

===Chinese===
他不再与森林搏斗，

他们两人都享受着财富与安逸，

老人们早已离去

把一切留给了简和约翰；

原木屋已经消失，

连同那些用粘土填补的缝隙，

取而代之的是精美的石头房子，

草坪上种着精选的灌木。

===Original===
With sons and daughters they are blest,

The young men say they'll move Northwest;

This gives their mother some alarm,

She wants them still on the home farm,

But father will not have them tarry

They can plow so quick on prairie,

And they find coal makes a good fire,

And build their fences of barbed wire

They would not be forever gone

As they could talk by telephone.

===Chinese===
他们有儿女的福分，

年轻人说要搬到西北去；

这让他们的母亲有些担忧，

她想让他们留在家里的农场，

但父亲不让他们久留，

他们在大草原上能更快地耕田，

他们发现煤炭可以生火，

用带刺铁丝网围起栅栏，

他们不会永远离开，

因为他们可以通过电话交谈。

===Original===
"O for a lodge in some vast wilderness"

A man cried out in his distress,

For he was tired and sick of life,

And weary of this worldly strife,

And longed for to be far away

From the continuous daily fray.

===Chinese===
"啊，在广袤的荒野中有一间小屋"

一个人在他痛苦中如此呼喊，

因为他厌倦了生活，

对这世俗的纷争感到疲惫，

渴望远离

永无休止的日常争斗。

===Original===
But the fond partner of his life,

His own dearest, loving wife,

Those sentiments did not admire,

For fiercely they did rouse her ire,

Said she, I'll never let you budge

To go and join another lodge,

Your lodges take six nights each week,

And still another lodge you seek,

For your whole time they soon will steal,

You won't get home even to a meal,

===Chinese===
但他亲爱的伴侣，

他最挚爱的妻子，

并不赞赏这些情绪，

这反而激怒了她的怒火，

她说，我绝不让你动身

去加入另一个会所，

你的会所每周占去六个夜晚，

你还要再找一个会所，

很快就会偷走你的全部时间，

你连回家吃一顿饭都做不到，

===Original===
Continuous abroad you'll roam,

And never enter your own home.

===Chinese===
你将永远在外漂泊，

再也不会踏入自己的家门。

===Original===
During the summer of 1883 we were walking along past a large field of potatoes in North Oxford, where we beheld the strange spectacle of a pair of bipeds drilling their offspring to march up one potato row and down the other, so as to annihilate the enemy, who had assembled in vast armies, dressed in yellow garments, and who were committing fearful depredations on the fruits of the husbandmen, until the valuable auxilliary forces rushed to the rescue of the farmer, o'erwhelming the enemy and with one fell swoop, bringing on them consternation and ruin dire. It appears that the foe, or their progenitors, had been citizens of Colorado in the far West. And that, having conquered all before them, they sought another World to conquer here.

===Chinese===
1883年夏天，我们走过北牛津（North Oxford）一大片土豆田时，看到一幅奇异的景象：一对两足动物正在训练它们的后代，在一排土豆中向上走，再在另一排中向下走，以便消灭那些集结成庞大军队、身着黄色盔甲的敌人，它们正对农夫的收成造成可怕的破坏，直到这支宝贵的援军冲来拯救农民，将敌人一举击溃，给他们带来恐惧和毁灭性的灾难。看来，这些敌人或它们的祖先，原本是远西部科罗拉多（Colorado）的居民。在征服了面前的一切之后，它们又来此寻找新的世界去征服。

===Original===
When we do trace out nature's laws,

And view effects, and muse on cause,

For the future there's great hope

If we our eyes do only ope.

With joy they will often glisten,

If to truth one doth but listen;

But people often turn deaf ear

And what is useful will not hear.

===Chinese===
当我们探索自然法则，

观察结果，思考原因，

对未来便有巨大的希望，

只要我们睁开双眼。

双眼常因喜悦而闪光，

只要我们倾听真理；

但人们常常充耳不闻，

对有用之物视而不见。

===Original===
Now for a minute, lend your luggs,

Our theme, it is potato bugs.

Just buy a pair of young peafowl,

Their voice may be like to screech owl,

But soon as the potato shows

You there will find the peafowl goes,

Up one row and down the other

Like loving sister with brother.

===Chinese===
现在花一分钟，竖起耳朵，

我们的主题是马铃薯甲虫。

只需买一对小孔雀，

它们的叫声可能像猫头鹰，

但只要土豆一露头，

你就会发现孔雀出动了，

在一行间上上下下，

如同相亲相爱的姐弟。

===Original===
And you will find that down their muggs

Have disappeared potato bugs,

There's no more need of Paris green

For they will keep potatoes clean.

And faithful they will work all day,

For to them 'tis gay sport and play;

No more you need their voice bewail,

But admire beauties of the tail.

===Chinese===
你会发现它们的喙下，

马铃薯甲虫已经消失，

再也不需要巴黎绿（Paris green），

因为它们会让土豆保持干净。

它们会忠实地工作一整天，

因为对它们来说这是欢乐的游戏；

你再也不必为它们的叫声叹息，

只需欣赏它们尾巴的美丽。

===Original===
Lines suggested by examining the fruit evaporator at Mt. Elgin.

===Chinese===
视察厄尔金山（Mt. Elgin）的水果蒸发器后有感而作。

===Original===
We fear to say, and yet we must,

Dried apples once were full of dust,

And you all know it is no joke.

Saturate with tobacco smoke,

And the hole where string did go through

Was nest for animalcule,

And collected the kitchen steam.

===Chinese===
我们虽不愿说，但不得不说，

苹果干曾经满是灰尘，

你们都知道这可不是玩笑。

浸透了烟草的烟味，

绳子穿过的那个小孔

是微小生物的巢穴，

还收集了厨房的蒸汽。

===Original===
But process now is sweet and clean,

Viewed with pleasure by spectator,

Work of the evaporator.

===Chinese===
但如今的工艺洁净而清爽，

令观者赏心悦目，

这就是蒸发器的功效。

===Original===
We will now sing in thoughtful strain

Of wars in Queen Victoria's reign.

===Chinese===
我们现在以深沉的旋律

歌唱维多利亚女王统治时期的战争。

===Original===
The Russian bear did ages lurk,

All ready for to spring on Turk,

For Russian statesmen did divine

That they should conquer Constantine,

But like a greyhound after hare

The Lion did drive back the Bear,

===Chinese===
俄国熊潜伏了无数岁月，

时刻准备扑向土耳其人，

因为俄国政客们预言

他们应当征服君士坦丁堡，

但雄狮如灵缇追兔般

将熊驱赶了回去，

===Original===
And made it feel the British rule

At gates of strong Sebastopol.

===Chinese===
让它在塞瓦斯托波尔（Sebastopol）的城门前

领略了英国的威严。

===Original===
Then insolent was Persia,

Till Lion had to dictate law,

And while engaged in scenes like these

He was attacked by the Chinese,

And for this outrage all so wanton

He then resolved to seize on Canton.

===Chinese===
接着波斯（Persia）傲慢无礼，

直到雄狮不得不颁布法令，

而在处理这些事务之际

他又遭到了中国人的攻击，

对于这等蛮横的暴行

他决定攻占广州。

===Original===
But soon there came a dismal cry

Of slaughter'd Britons from Delhi,

The Bengal Tiger sick with gore

Did tremble at the Lion's roar,

But Britain got a serious shock

By losing of brave Havelock,

===Chinese===
但很快从德里（Delhi）传来

惨遭屠杀的英国人的悲号，

孟加拉虎（Bengal Tiger）因血腥而战栗，

在雄狮的咆嗦面前颤抖，

但英国遭受了严重的打击，

因为失去了英勇的哈夫洛克（Havelock），

===Original===
But Campbell 'mid a numerous foe

Full quick these armed hosts did o'erthrow,

In Abyssinian dungeons vile

Lay captives of Great Britain's isle,

But soon the tyrant Theodore

Lay sadly weltering in his gore.

===Chinese===
但坎贝尔（Campbell）在众多敌人之中

迅速击溃了这些武装大军，

在阿比西尼亚（Abyssinia）的恶臭牢笼中

关押着大不列颠岛的俘虏，

但暴君西奥多（Theodore）很快

便悲惨地倒在自己的血泊中。

===Original===
The savage tribes of Ashantee

From British troops did quickly flee,

In Afghan and Zulu wars

Many did find their deadly scars;

In the land of the Pharaohs

The Christians suffered cruel woes,

===Chinese===
阿散蒂（Ashantee）的野蛮部落

在英军面前迅速溃逃，

在阿富汗和祖鲁（Zulu）战争中

许多人留下了致命的伤疤；

在法老的土地上，

基督徒遭受着残酷的苦难，

===Original===
Till in Alexandria Bay

The British iron clads did display,

The mighty power they did wield,

While their steel sides from harm did shield,

And British army on the land

Marched bravely o'er the burning sand,

===Chinese===
直到在亚历山大湾（Alexandria Bay），

英国铁甲舰展露锋芒，

它们施展着强大的威力，

钢铁船舷抵御着伤害，

而英国陆军

勇敢地越过炽热的沙地进军，

===Original===
And Arabi found 'twas useless labor,

His strong trench of Tel-el-Kebir,

Egyptians did not wish to feel

In their breasts cold British steel,

Their great power was soon laid low

And Wolseley entered Grand Cairo.

===Chinese===
阿拉比（Arabi）发现一切都是徒劳，

他坚固的泰勒凯比尔（Tel-el-Kebir）战壕，

埃及人不愿感受

冰冷的英国钢刀刺入胸膛，

他们强大的势力迅速瓦解，

沃尔斯利（Wolseley）进入了大开罗。

===Original===
Egyptians now no more revile

The Christians on the banks of Nile.

===Chinese===
埃及人如今不再诋毁

尼罗河畔的基督徒。

===Original===
We have sung three heroes' names.

Havelock from the land of Thames,

And Campbell from the banks of Clyde,

And Wolseley from Liffy's side,

When rose, thistle, shamrock unite

They do prove victors in the fight,

Now Britain once more does command

Respect alike on sea and land,

But now may wars forever cease

And mankind ever live in peace.

===Chinese===
我们歌咏了三位英雄的名字。

哈夫洛克（Havelock），来自泰晤士河之地，

坎贝尔（Campbell），来自克莱德河畔，

沃尔斯利（Wolseley），来自利菲河（Liffy）畔，

当玫瑰、蓟花和三叶草联合，

他们便是战场上的胜利者，

如今英国再次赢得了

海陆两方面的敬重，

但愿战争永远消逝，

人类永享太平。

===Original===
We had the honour of delivering in 1864 the principal address at the tercentennial anniversary of Shakespeare in the Town Hall, Ingersoll, before a large audience, and we read the following ode on the occasion:

===Chinese===
1864年，我们有幸在英格索尔（Ingersoll）市政厅的莎士比亚三百周年纪念会上，在众多听众面前发表了主题演讲，并在此场合朗诵了以下颂歌：

===Original===
"Shakespeare requires no marble monument,

He lives forever in our wonder and astonishment."

> Ben Jonson

===Chinese===
"莎士比亚不需要大理石纪念碑，

他永远活在我们的惊叹与景仰之中。"

——本·琼森（Ben Jonson）

===Original===
Three centuries have passed away

Since that most famous April day,

When the sweet, gentle Will was born,

Whose name the age will e're adorn.

That great Elizabethan age

Does not leave on history's page,

A name so bright he stands like Saul,

A head and shoulders over all.

===Chinese===
三个世纪已经逝去，

自那个最著名的四月天，

温柔甜美的威尔（Will）降生，

他的名字将永远为这个时代增光。

那个伟大的伊丽莎白时代，

在历史的书页上没有留下

一个名字如他般闪耀，他高出众人，

如同扫罗（Saul），头与肩超过所有人。

===Original===
Delineator of mankind,

Who shows the workings of the mind,

And in review in nature's glass,

Portrays the thoughts of every class.

===Chinese===
人类的描绘者，

展现心灵的运作，

在自然之镜中审视，

描绘出每个阶层的思想。

===Original===
That man is dull who will not laugh

At the drolleries of Falstaff,

And few that could not shed a tear

At sorrows of poor old King Lear.

===Chinese===
不为福斯塔夫（Falstaff）的滑稽发笑的人是愚钝的，

面对可怜的老李尔王（King Lear）的悲苦而不落泪的人少之又少。

===Original===
Or lament o'er King Duncan's death

Stabbed by the dagger of McBeth,

Or gentle Desdemona pure,

Slain by the misled jealous Moor.

===Chinese===
或为邓肯王（King Duncan）被麦克白（McBeth）的匕首刺杀而哀悼，

或为纯洁温柔的苔丝狄蒙娜（Desdemona），

被误入歧途的嫉妒摩尔人所杀害而悲伤。

===Original===
Or great Caesar mighty Roman

Who o'ercame his country's foemen,

His high deeds are all in vain,

For by his countrymen he's slain.

===Chinese===
或伟大的凯撒（Caesar），强大的罗马人，

他战胜了祖国的敌人，

他伟大的功绩都化为乌有，

因为他被自己的同胞刺杀。

===Original===
The greatest of heroic tales

Is that of Harry, Prince of Wales,

Who in combat fought so fiercely

With the brave and gallant Percy.

===Chinese===
最伟大的英雄传说

是关于威尔士亲王哈里（Harry）的，

他与勇敢英勇的珀西（Percy）

在战斗中激烈交锋。

===Original===
Imagination's grandest theme

The tempest or midsummer's dream,

And Hamlet's philosophic blaze

Of shattered reason's flickering rays.

===Chinese===
想象力最宏大的主题

是暴风雨或仲夏夜之梦，

以及哈姆雷特（Hamlet）哲学的光焰，

破碎理智的摇曳光芒。

===Original===
And now in every land on earth

They commemorate Shakespeare's birth,

And there is met on Avon's banks

Men of all nations and all ranks.

===Chinese===
如今世界各地的人们

都在纪念莎士比亚的诞辰，

艾冯河（Avon）畔齐聚一堂，

各国各阶层的人们。

===Original===
And here upon Canadian Thames

The gentle maids and comely dames

Do meet and each does bring her scroll

Of laurel leaves from Ingersoll.

===Chinese===
而在这里的加拿大泰晤士河畔，

温柔的少女与端庄的淑女

欢聚一堂，每人带来一卷

英格索尔（Ingersoll）的月桂叶。

===Original===
Like mightiest organ in full tone,

Melodious, grand, is great Milton,

He did in lofty measures tell

How Satan, great archangel, fell,

When from heaven downward hurled;

And how he ruined this our world,

So full of guile he did deceive

Our simple hearted parent Eve.

===Chinese===
如同最雄伟的管风琴奏响全音，

优美、壮丽，是伟大的弥尔顿（Milton），

他以崇高的韵律讲述

大天使撒旦如何坠落，

当被从天堂向下抛掷；

以及他如何毁坏了我们的世界，

他用无数诡计欺骗了

我们纯朴的始祖夏娃（Eve）。

===Original===
He shows how pardon is obtained

And paradise may be regained.

===Chinese===
他展示了宽恕如何获得，

天堂如何能够重新寻回。

===Original===
England had triplets at a birth,

Coleridge, Southey and Wordsworth,

And these three are widely famed,

And the "Lake Poets" they were named.

With joy they did pursue their themes,

'Mong England's lakes and hills and streams,

From there with gladness they could view

The distant Scottish mountains blue.

===Chinese===
英格兰一胎生了三兄弟，

柯勒律治（Coleridge）、骚塞（Southey）和华兹华斯（Wordsworth），

这三位声名远播，

被称为"湖畔诗人"。

他们满怀喜悦地追寻各自的题材，

在英格兰的湖泊、山丘与溪流之间，

从那里他们可以欣喜地眺望

远方苏格兰蓝色的山脉。

===Original===
We have scarcely time to tell thee

Of the strange and gifted Shelly,

Kind hearted man but ill-fated,

So youthful, drowned and cremated.

===Chinese===
我们几乎无暇向你讲述

奇异而才华横溢的雪莱（Shelley），

心地善良却命运多舛，

如此年轻，溺水而亡，身后火化。

===Original===
Poets they do pursue each theme,

Under a gentle head of steam,

Save one who needed fierce fire on,

The brilliant, passionate Byron.

His child Harold's pilgrimage,

Forever will the world engage;

He fought with glory to release

From Turkish yoke the isles of Greece,

Its glories oft by him were sung,

This wondrous bard, alas, died young.

===Chinese===
诗人们各追求自己的主题，

以温和的蒸汽推动，

唯有一人需要猛烈的火焰——

才华横溢、激情澎湃的拜伦（Byron）。

他的《恰尔德·哈洛尔德游记》（Harold's Pilgrimage），

将永远吸引世人；

他为荣耀而战，将希腊诸岛

从土耳其的枷锁中解放，

它的荣光被他反复歌颂，

这位奇妙的诗人，唉，英年早逝。

===Original===
Of our Laureate we now do sing,

His youthful muse had daring wing,

He then despised Baronhood,

And sang 'twas noble to be good.

None sang like him of knights of old,

He England's glory did uphold;

In wondrous song he hath arrayed

Glorious charge of light brigade,

And he hath the people's benison,

Greatest of living poets Tennyson.

===Chinese===
我们歌颂我们的桂冠诗人，

他年轻的缪斯有着无畏的翅膀，

他曾藐视男爵之位，

歌咏善良便是高贵。

无人能像他那样歌唱古时的骑士，

他维护了英格兰的荣耀；

在奇妙的诗篇中他描绘了

光荣的轻骑兵冲锋，

他拥有人民的祝福，

在世最伟大的诗人丁尼生（Tennyson）。

===Original===
Genius of Dryden and of Pope,

Both did take a mighty scope,

The first he Virgil did translate,

The second showed us Troys fate.

On English themes they oft did sing

And high their muses flight did wing.

===Chinese===
德莱顿（Dryden）和蒲柏（Pope）的天才，

二人都展现了宏大的视野，

前者翻译了维吉尔（Virgil），

后者向我们展示了特洛伊的命运。

他们常以英国题材吟唱，

缪斯展翅高飞。

===Original===
Moore found the ballads of Green Isle

Were oft obscured beneath the soil,

As miner digging in a mine

Finds rubbish 'mong the gold so fine,

So Moore placed dross in the waste basket

And enshrined jewels in casket,

Where all may view each charming gem

In Ireland's grand old diadem.

===Chinese===
穆尔（Moore）发现绿岛的民谣

常常埋没于尘土之下，

如同矿工在矿中挖掘

在纯净的金子中发现渣滓，

于是穆尔将渣滓丢入废篮，

将珠宝珍藏于宝匣之中，

人人可以观赏每一颗迷人的宝石，

它们镶嵌在爱尔兰壮丽的古老冠冕上。

===Original===
In eastern lands his fame prevails

In wondrous oriental tales,

So full of gems his Lala Rookh,

Hindus and Brahmins read his book,

And dark eyed Persian girls admire

The beauty of his magic lyre,

Glowing like pearls of great price

Those distant gleams of paradise.

===Chinese===
在东方大陆他的名声远播，

在奇妙的东方传说中，

他的《拉拉·鲁克》（Lala Rookh）满是珍宝，

印度教徒和婆罗门阅读他的书，

黑眼睛的波斯少女赞赏

他魔幻竖琴的美妙，

那些遥远的天堂微光

如珍贵的珍珠般闪耀。

===Original===
He sang of Bryan Borohm's glory,

Renowned in ancient Irish story,

And shows the wide expanded walls

Which once encircled Tara's Halls,

When joyous harp did there resound

And Ireland's greatest king was crowned,

All wars and tumults then did cease,

Ireland did prosper great in peace.

===Chinese===
他歌唱布赖恩·博鲁（Bryan Borohm）的荣光，

在古老的爱尔兰传说中闻名，

展示了那宽广的围墙，

曾环绕塔拉（Tara）的殿堂，

那时快乐的竖琴在那里回响，

爱尔兰最伟大的国王加冕，

一切战争和骚乱平息，

爱尔兰在和平中繁荣昌盛。

===Original===
He sung of meeting of the waters

And of Ireland's charming daughters,

Great minstrel from his harp both flows,

Ireland's triumphs and her woes,

Canada doth his fame prolong

While she doth sing his great boat song,

And his own countrymen adore

The genial, witty, bright Tom Moore.

===Chinese===
他歌唱河流的交汇

和爱尔兰迷人的女儿们，

伟大的吟游诗人从他的竖琴中流出

爱尔兰的荣耀与她的苦难，

加拿大延续着他的名声，

当她吟唱他伟大的船歌，

而他的同胞们敬爱

和蔼、机智、才华横溢的汤姆·穆尔（Tom Moore）。

===Original===
While referring to past glories of Ireland, perhaps we might refer to that great Irish Historian, the late Honourable T. D. McKee, of whom we have written a poem in the earlier portion of this work, and we will give you an anecdote of him while here, showing his ready wit while he was rising from the supper table around which was a number of guests assembled, all eyes being naturally turned on him as the great centre of attraction, but the chair, being new, stuck to him; he instantly exclaimed, I wish the Montreal people were as anxious to retain me in my seat as you are in Ingersoll. He being a member for Montreal, wrote a fine poem on the St. Lawrence, where in Cartier describes to the King, on his return to Europe, the great river.

===Chinese===
在提及爱尔兰昔日荣光之际，我们或许应该提一下那位伟大的爱尔兰历史学家，已故尊敬的T.D.麦基（T.D. McKee），我们在本书前部已为他写过一首诗，在此讲一则关于他的轶事，展现他敏捷的才智——当他从晚宴桌旁起立时，周围聚集了许多客人，众人的目光自然聚焦在他这位伟大的中心人物身上，但椅子是新的，粘住了他；他立刻脱口而出：我希望蒙特利尔人像你们英格索尔人这样急切地想留住我坐在这里。他作为蒙特利尔的议员，写了一首关于圣劳伦斯河的好诗，其中卡蒂埃（Cartier）回到欧洲后向国王描述了那条大河。

===Original===
"He told them of a river whose mighty torrent gave

A freshness for a hundred leagues to ocean's briney wave."

===Chinese===
"他告诉他们有一条河，其汹涌的激流为

咸涩的海洋之波注入了百里之遥的清新。"

===Original===
Goldsmith wrote of deserted village,

Now again reduced to tillage,

Once happiest village of the plain,

The place you look for it in vain,

There but one man he doth make rich,

"I'll fare the land to many ills a prey

Where wealth accumulates but men decay."

While hundreds struggle in the ditch,

His honest vicar of Wakefield,

Forever he will pleasure yield.

===Chinese===
哥尔斯密（Goldsmith）写过荒村的故事，

如今又变回了耕种之地，

曾是平原上最幸福的村庄，

那个地方你已无处可寻，

那里只有一个人发财，

"我哀叹这片土地沦为许多灾祸的牺牲品，

财富在那里积累，人却在那里衰亡。"

当数百人在沟壑中挣扎，

他那诚实的《威克菲尔德牧师》（Vicar of Wakefield），

将永远给予人快乐。

===Original===
Like fruit that's large and ripe and mellow,

Sweet and luscious is Longfellow,17

Melodious songs he oft did pour

And high was his Excelsior.

He shows in his Psalm of Life

The folly of our selfish strife,

With Hiawatha we bewail

His suffering in great Indian tale.

===Chinese===
如同硕大、成熟、甘美的果实，

甜蜜而醇厚是朗费罗（Longfellow）[注17]，

他常倾泻优美的歌声，

他的《人生礼赞》（Excelsior）高亢激昂。

他在《生命颂》（Psalm of Life）中揭示

我们自私争斗的愚蠢，

随着《海华沙之歌》（Hiawatha），我们哀悼

他在伟大印第安故事中的苦难。

===Original===
Indian nation was forlorn

Till great spirit planted corn;

His story of Evangeline

It is a tale of love divine.

===Chinese===
印第安民族曾孤苦无依，

直到伟大的精灵种下玉米；

他的《伊万杰琳》（Evangeline）的故事，

是一个神圣爱情的传说。

===Original===
A great enchanter too is Poe,

His bells do so harmonious flow,

Wondrous mystery of his raven

On our minds is 'ere engraven,

His weird, wonderful romances

Imagination oft entrances.

===Chinese===
另一位伟大的魔法师是爱伦·坡（Poe），

他的铃声和谐流淌，

他那神秘奇妙的乌鸦

永远铭刻在我们心中，

他那诡异、奇妙的传奇故事

常常令想象力沉醉其中。

===Original===
With pleasure we would love to dwell

On the charming themes of Lowell.

===Chinese===
我们满怀欣喜地流连于

洛威尔（Lowell）迷人的主题。

===Original===
Some in front rank will defiant,

Boldly place the poet Bryant.

===Chinese===
有人会在最前列勇敢地

将诗人布赖恩特（Bryant）安放。

===Original===
Others seek for music in the twitter

Of the sweet, charming notes of Whittier.

===Chinese===
另一些人在惠蒂尔（Whittier）

甜美的鸣啭中寻觅音乐。

===Original===
The mind that's sad it doth relax

The humor of the witty Saxe,

He puts us in a cheerful mood,

Mirthful as our own Tom Hood.

===Chinese===
忧伤的心灵会在诙谐的萨克斯（Saxe）

幽默中得到舒缓，

他使我们心情愉快，

如同我们自己的汤姆·胡德（Tom Hood）般欢乐。

===Original===
In homely apparel one

Clothes farming songs Will Carleton,

But they have a manly ring

And we his praises hearty sing.

===Chinese===
朴素的外衣之下，

威尔·卡利顿（Will Carleton）的农歌，

但它们有一种阳刚之气，

我们由衷地唱出对他的赞美。

===Original===
And Millar poet of Sierras,

For bold deeds he doth prepare us,

And now he lives by the golden gate,

Honored in California's state,

To poet 'tis position grand,

Commissioner of Forest land.

===Chinese===
还有米勒（Millar），内华达山脉的诗人，

他激励我们勇敢前行，

如今他住在金门之旁，

在加利福尼亚州备受尊敬，

对诗人而言是崇高的职位，

林地专员。

===Original===
O'er flowery fields full oft he roams,

The learned and pleasing genial Holmes.

===Chinese===
他常常漫步在鲜花盛开的田野上，

博学而令人愉悦的和蔼霍姆斯（Holmes）。

===Original===
For erratic style he leads van,

Wildly wayward Walt Whitman,

He done grand work in civil war,

For he did dress many a scar,

And kindly wet the hot parched mouth

Of Northern soldiers wounded South.

===Chinese===
在不羁的风格上他一马当先，

狂放不羁的沃尔特·惠特曼（Walt Whitman），

他在内战中做了伟大的工作，

因为他包扎了许多伤口，

并亲切地润湿了

南方负伤的北方士兵干裂的嘴唇。

===Original===
The following ode was read by the author at the Centennial Anniversary of Burns in the year 1859.

===Chinese===
以下颂歌由作者在1859年彭斯百年纪念会上朗诵。

===Original===
This night shall never be forgot

For humble life none now despise,

Since Burns was born in lowly cot

Whose muses wing soars to the skies.

'Round Scotia's brow he wove a wreath

And raised her name in classic story

A deathless fame he did bequeath,

His country's pride, his country's glory.

===Chinese===
这一夜将永远不会被忘记，

因为无人再轻视卑微的生活，

彭斯（Burns）生于简陋的茅舍，

他的缪斯之翼直冲云霄。

他在苏格兰的额前编织了花环，

在古典传说中提升了她的名字，

他留下了不朽的声名，

祖国的骄傲，祖国的荣光。

===Original===
He sang her hills, he sang her dales,

Of Bonnie Doon and Banks of Ayr,

Of death and Hornbook and such tales

As Tam O'Shanter and his mare.

===Chinese===
他歌唱她的山丘，歌唱她的谷地，

歌唱美丽的多恩河（Doon）和艾尔河岸（Ayr），

歌唱死亡与识字课本，以及诸如

《汤姆·奥桑特》（Tam O'Shanter）和他的母马之类的故事。

===Original===
He bravely taught that manly worth

More precious is than finest gold,

He reckoned not on noble birth,

But noble deeds alone extolled.

===Chinese===
他勇敢地教导人们男子的品格

比最精纯的黄金更为珍贵，

他不看重高贵的出身，

只推崇高尚的行为。

===Original===
Where will we find behind the plow

Or in the harvest field at toil

Another youth, sweet bard, like thou,

Could draw the tear or raise the smile.

===Chinese===
我们到哪里才能再找到一个

在犁后或收割田中劳作的青年，

如你这般，甜蜜的诗人，

既能催人泪下又能令人展颜微笑。

===Original===
We do not think 'twas Burns' fault,

For there were no teetotalers then,

That Willie brewed a peck of malt

And Robin preed like other men.

===Chinese===
我们并不认为这是彭斯的过错，

因为那时还没有戒酒主义者，

威利（Willie）酿了一斗麦芽，

罗宾（Robin）和别人一样痛饮。

===Original===
'Tis true he loved the lasses dear,

But who for this would loudly blame,

For Scotia's maids his heart did cheer

And love is a true heavenly flame.

===Chinese===
确实他热爱可爱的姑娘们，

但谁能因此对他横加指责，

因为苏格兰的少女让他心花怒放，

而爱是真正的天堂之火。

===Original===
So here we've met in distant land

Poor honest Robin to extol,

Though oft we differ let us stand

United now in Ingersoll.

===Chinese===
所以我们在这遥远的地方相聚

来赞美朴实的罗宾（Robin），

虽然我们常有分歧，但让我们站在一起，

如今在英格索尔（Ingersoll）团结一心。

===Original===
Burns sang so sweet behind the plow,

Daisies we'll wreath around his brow,

Musing on thee what visions throng,

Of floods you poured of Scottish song.

Scott he did write romancing rhymes

Of chivalry of ancient times;

For tender feeling none can cope

With Campbell the sweet Bard of hope.

===Chinese===
彭斯（Burns）在犁后歌唱得如此甜美，

我们将雏菊花环绕在他的额前，

沉思你时，多少幻象涌来，

你倾泻的苏格兰歌曲如洪流般汹涌。

司各特（Scott）写下浪漫的诗句

歌咏古代的骑士精神；

论温柔的情感无人能及

坎贝尔（Campbell），甜蜜的希望诗人。

===Original===
Eye with sympathetic tear in

Will shed it for Exile of Erin,

And Tannahill while at his loom

Wove flowers of song will ever bloom.

===Chinese===
含着同情泪水的双眼

会为爱尔兰的流亡者而落泪，

而坦纳希尔（Tannahill）在织布机旁

编织的歌曲之花将永远盛开。

===Original===
Hogg, Ettrick Shepherd, did gain fame

By singing when the Kie comes hame,

With good time coming Bard McKay

Still merrily doth cheer the way.

===Chinese===
霍格（Hogg），埃特里克牧羊人（Ettrick Shepherd），因歌咏

《当基回家时》（When the Kie comes hame）而赢得声名，

怀着好时光即将到来的吟游诗人麦凯（McKay）

仍然欢快地鼓舞着人们的道路。

===Original===
The South of Scotland did produce

Heroic Wallace and the Bruce,

And even time will never blot

The record of her Burns and Scott,

And Tanahill renowned bard,

And that sweet songster Ettrick Shepherd.

===Chinese===
苏格兰南部孕育了

英勇的华莱士（Wallace）和布鲁斯（Bruce），

即使时间也永远无法抹去

她彭斯（Burns）和司各特（Scott）的记录，

还有坦纳希尔（Tanahill），著名的诗人，

以及那位甜美的歌者，埃特里克牧羊人。

===Original===
Worthy of either song or story

Are the shires round Frith of Moray,

Here lies the valley of Strathspey,

Famed for its music, lively, gay,

Elgin cathedral's 'prentice aisle

Is glory of that ruined pile.

===Chinese===
莫雷湾（Frith of Moray）周围诸郡

无论歌唱还是讲述都值得，

这里是斯特拉斯佩（Strathspey）谷地，

以活泼欢快的音乐闻名，

埃尔金大教堂（Elgin cathedral）的学徒回廊

是这座废墟的荣耀。

===Original===
What modern chisel now could trace

Fine sculpture of that ancient place,

And Forres famed for Sweyn's stane

In honor of that kingly Dane,

'Graved with warriors runes and rhymes,

Long prior to historic times,

For a thousand years its been forgot

Who was victor Dane or Scot,

===Chinese===
如今哪把刻刀能描绘

那古老之地精美的雕刻，

福里斯（Forres）以斯温之石（Sweyn's stane）闻名，

为纪念那位丹麦国王而立，

上面刻着战士的符文和韵文，

远在历史纪年之前，

一千年来无人知晓

丹麦人还是苏格兰人是胜利者，

===Original===
It is the country of McBeth

Where good King Duncan met his death,

And barren heath that place of fear

Stood witches cauldron of Shakespeare,

===Chinese===
这里是麦克白（McBeth）的领地，

善良的邓肯王（King Duncan）在此丧命，

那片荒凉的荒野，令人恐惧之地，

便是莎士比亚笔中女巫的大锅所在，

===Original===
Nairn's Cawdor castle strong remains

Full worthy of the ancient Thanes,

And nestled 'neath the hills and bens

Queen of the moors, the lochs and glens,

Full proudly stands in vale of bliss

Chief Highland town of Inverness,

===Chinese===
内恩（Nairn）的考德（Cawdor）城堡坚固地屹立，

完全配得上古老的领主们，

坐落在群山和高地山丘之下的

荒原、湖泊和峡谷的女王，

骄傲地矗立在幸福的山谷中，

高地首要之镇因弗内斯（Inverness），

===Original===
Near here the famous falls of Foyers

Where Burns and others tuned their lyres,

And the fatal field of dark Culloden

Where doughty clans were once down trodden,

===Chinese===
附近便是著名的福耶尔斯瀑布（Falls of Foyers），

彭斯（Burns）和其他人在那里弹奏诗琴，

还有致命的暗色库洛登战场（Culloden），

英勇的氏族曾在那里被踩在脚下，

===Original===
Here men yet wear the tartan plaid

Ready to join the Highland Brigade,

And when the Frith you look across

The eye beholds Sutherland and Ross,

Where Duke has harnessed mighty team,

Plows hills and rocks and moors by steam,

===Chinese===
这里的人们仍然穿着格纹披肩，

随时准备加入高地旅，

当你眺望海湾对岸，

目光所及是萨瑟兰（Sutherland）和罗斯（Ross），

公爵套上了强大的犁具团队，

用蒸汽犁过山丘、岩石和荒原，

===Original===
Perhaps it may in part atone

For cruel clearings days bygone,

And Cromarty, whose wondrous mason,

First learned his geologic lesson,

Friends may rear a stately pillar,

The old red sand stone of Hugh Miller,

Ben Wyvis towers like monarch crowned,

Conspicuous o'er the hills around,

With crest 'ere white with driven snow,

===Chinese===
这或许可以部分赎偿

往昔残酷驱逐的罪过，

还有克罗马蒂（Cromarty），那位奇妙的石匠，

在那里初次学到地质学的功课，

朋友们可以竖起一座庄严的石柱，

以休·米勒（Hugh Miller）的老红砂岩，

本·怀维斯（Ben Wyvis）如加冕的君王般高耸，

在周围的群山之上格外显眼，

山顶永远被飘飞的白雪覆盖，

===Original===
Strathpeffer's water cure below.

===Chinese===
下方是斯特拉斯佩弗（Strathpeffer）的水疗之地。

===Original===
1868.

===Chinese===
1868年。

===Original===
The following is a clipping from an old Ingenoll paper on St. Andrew's Anniversary, 30th November, 1868:

===Chinese===
以下摘自1868年11月30日圣安德鲁（St. Andrew）纪念日的一份英格索尔（Ingersoll）旧报：

===Original===
Scotia's sons to-night we meet thee,

With kindly feelings we do greet thee,

In honor of the land of heather,

Around this board to-night we gather.

===Chinese===
苏格兰的儿子们，今夜我们迎接你，

以亲切的情感向你致意，

为了荣耀石楠花之国，

我们今晚围坐在这桌旁。

===Original===
Land where the fields for border edges,

Have garlands of blooming hedges,

Land of the whin and of the broom

And where the bonnie blue bells bloom.

===Chinese===
在这片田野的边界上，

环绕着盛开的篱笆花环，

金雀花和金雀花的国度，

美丽的蓝铃花在那里盛开。

===Original===
Land where you may enraptured hark

To heavenly song of the skylark,

Which soars triumphant in the skies

Above the gaze of human eyes.

===Chinese===
在这片你可以陶醉地聆听

云雀天堂般的歌声的土地上，

它在天空中凯旋般翱翔，

高过了人类目光的凝望。

===Original===
Land of bleak hills and fertile dales,

Where they tell oft their fairy tales,

Land where the folks do love the kirk

And on the Sabbath cease from work.

===Chinese===
荒凉山丘和肥沃谷地的国度，

人们常在那里讲述精灵的故事，

人们热爱教堂的国度，

安息日便停下劳作。

===Original===
Land of porridge and of brose,

Of blue bonnets and of tartan hose,

The land where all good wives do bake

The thrifty, wholesome, oaten cake.

===Chinese===
粥和麦片汤的国度，

蓝帽和格纹长袜的国度，

所有贤惠妻子烘焙

节俭、健康的燕麦饼的国度。

===Original===
We hope some day to tread the strand

Of our own dear native land,

And see the lasses shear the corn18

Near the banks of the Findhorn.

===Chinese===
我们希望有朝一日踏上

我们亲爱的故土的海滩，

看到少女们在菲德霍恩河（Findhorn）畔

收割谷物。[注18]

===Original===
Where the Jeans and the Maggies

Excel in making glorious baggies,

And o'er the sea we'll some day sail

To get a bowl of good green kail.

===Chinese===
在那里简们（Jeans）和玛吉们（Maggies）

擅长制作美味的布袋（baggies），

总有一天我们将漂洋过海，

去喝一碗上好的绿甘蓝汤。

===Original===
Lines on the North of Scotland, delivered in Embro nearly a quarter of a century ago:

===Chinese===
关于苏格兰北部的诗，大约四分之一个世纪前在恩布罗（Embro）朗诵：

===Original===
Scotsmen have wandered far and wide,

From Moray Frith to Frith of Clyde;

McDonald, from his sea girt isle,

And Campbell from his broad Argyle﻿–﻿

===Chinese===
苏格兰人已四散远游，

从莫雷湾（Moray Frith）到克莱德湾（Frith of Clyde）；

麦克唐纳（McDonald），来自他环海的岛屿，

坎贝尔（Campbell），来自他辽阔的阿盖尔（Argyle）——

===Original===
But chiefly here you have come forth

From those countries of the north;

Some oft have trod Dunrobins's Halls,

And gazed upon its stately walls.

===Chinese===
但你们主要来自

北方那些土地；

有些人常走过邓罗宾（Dunrobin）的大厅，

凝望它庄严的墙壁。

===Original===
Here to-night in this array

Is Murray, McKenzie and McKay;

And there doth around us stand

The Munroe, Ross and Sutherland.

===Chinese===
今夜在这人群之中

有默里（Murray）、麦肯齐（McKenzie）和麦凯（McKay）；

在我们周围还站立着

芒罗（Munroe）、罗斯（Ross）和萨瑟兰（Sutherland）。

===Original===
Your young men have high honour earned;

In all of the professions learned;

Your Bonnie Lasses sung in song,

And youths are famed for muscle strong.

===Chinese===
你们的年轻人赢得了崇高的荣誉；

在所有学问行业中；

你们美丽的少女被歌咏传唱，

年轻人以强壮的肌肉闻名。

===Original===
Oft' times these handsome gentlemen appeared in the garb of old Gaul.

===Chinese===
这些英俊的绅士时常身着古高卢的装束出现。

===Original===
In the year 1843 we were, though but a boy, at a fair at Cawdor Castle. Readers of Shakespeare's Macbeth will have often found Cawdor mentioned therein; the village of Cawdor is but a few miles from Culloden Moor. While we were there the old Highland people, in their broken English, were declaring that the Stuarts19 were collecting arms, and that the Clans were going to join them; those gentlemen wore the Highland garb, and were highly respected. They had lived in Cawdor for some years; one ox them built a Hermitage of wicker-work on high bank of river, which remains entire and in good repair to the present day.

===Chinese===
