1843年，虽然还是个男孩，我们就在考德堡（Cawdor Castle）参加了一个集市。莎士比亚《麦克白》（Macbeth）的读者会经常看到考德（Cawdor）这个名字；考德村离库洛登荒原（Culloden Moor）仅几英里远。当时我们在那里，高地的老人们用破碎的英语宣称斯图亚特家族[注19]正在收集武器，各氏族将加入他们；那些绅士们穿着高地服装，备受尊敬。他们在考德住了多年；其中一位在河岸高处用柳条编了一个隐士小屋，至今完好无损地保存着。

===Original===
Long 'ere Her Majesty the Queen

Had visited of Aberdeen,

'Ere she in castle did abide

'Mong glorious hills on the Dee side,

Or visited each Highland glen,

Or won the hearts of Highland men,

Here oft' was seen in celtic dress

Two Stewarts brave in Inverness,

Well worthy of the poet's lyre.

===Chinese===
远在女王陛下

造访阿伯丁（Aberdeen）之前，

远在她入住迪河（Dee）畔

壮丽山丘中的城堡之前，

或走访每一个高地峡谷，

或赢得高地人心之前，

在因弗内斯（Inverness）常可见到

两位穿着凯尔特服装的勇敢的斯图亚特（Stewarts），

完全配得上诗人的竖琴。

===Original===
They claimed Prince Charlie as grandsire,

And that they also did combine

Stuart with Royal Polish line,

Their names Sobieskie, Stuart,

They won many a Highland heart,

===Chinese===
他们声称查理王子（Prince Charlie）是他们的祖父，

而且他们还结合了

斯图亚特与波兰王室的血统，

他们的名字是索别斯基·斯图亚特（Sobieskie Stuart），

他们赢得了许多高地人的心，

===Original===
But Royal order did go forth

To build Balmoral Castle North,

Then wondrous change was quickly seen,

All hearts were captured by the Queen.

===Chinese===
但王室下令

在北方修建巴尔莫勒尔堡（Balmoral Castle），

于是神奇的变化迅速发生，

所有人的心都被女王俘获。

===Original===
We have the Murray and McKay

From the country of Lord Rae;20

McKenzie too from many a loch,

From Dingwall, Fain, and old Dornoch.

===Chinese===
我们有默里（Murray）和麦凯（McKay），

来自雷勋爵（Lord Rae）的领地；[注20]

还有麦肯齐（McKenzie），来自许多湖泊，

来自丁沃尔（Dingwall）、费恩（Fain）和古老的多诺赫（Dornoch）。

===Original===
When the price of cheese was so low a few years ago that the dairymen seriously thought of giving up the manufacture of cheese and of selling their cows, we published the following lines and distributed them by the thousands:

===Chinese===
几年前奶酪价格如此低廉，以至于乳农们认真考虑放弃奶酪制造并卖掉他们的牛，我们发表了以下诗句并印刷分发了数千份：

===Original===
Price soon will rise, though now 'tis low,

And brooks of milk will onward flow;

Were it collected in one stream

There would be floods of milk and cream.21

===Chinese===
价格很快会上涨，虽然现在很低，

牛奶的小溪将继续流淌；

如果将它们汇成一条河流，

那将是牛奶和奶油的洪流。[注21]

===Original===
Babies carried off and changed by fairies. In mid winter of last winter of 1884, in Burghed, in the North of Scotland, around great fires, incantations were pronounced to drive away the evil spirits. The custom has come down from the time of the Druids.

===Chinese===
婴儿被精灵带走并替换的故事。1884年隆冬时节，在苏格兰北部的布尔赫德（Burghed），人们围着篝火念咒驱赶邪灵。这一习俗从德鲁伊（Druids）时代流传至今。

===Original===
Where'er you find the Fisher folk

There, under superstitions yoke,

For a strong faith 'mong them prevails.

Of truth of witch and fairy tales.

===Chinese===
无论你在哪里发现渔民，

他们都在迷信的枷锁之下，

因为一种坚定的信仰在他们中间盛行，

相信女巫和精灵故事的真实性。

===Original===
They think that witch could hurl a shaft

Which would o'erwhelm their fishing craft,

For witches do with Satan truck,

They can give good or bring bad luck;

Fish women do their children teach

To bait the lines down on the beach;

===Chinese===
他们认为女巫能发射利箭

摧毁他们的渔船，

因为女巫与撒旦交易，

她们能赐福也能招祸；

渔妇们教导她们的孩子

在海滩上给鱼钩装上诱饵；

===Original===
Themselves do wade in sea for net

So husband's feet will not get wet,

For the women are barefooted,

And the men are heavy booted.

===Chinese===
她们自己赤脚下海收网

这样丈夫的脚就不会湿，

因为女人们光着脚，

而男人们穿着笨重的靴子。

===Original===
In Fisher, Town of Cromarty,

There once did meet a noisy party,

Confusion worse than Babel's Tower,

It did prevail for a whole hour;

When from sea shore wives did return

Each one did find good cause to mourn,

For each babe was left in cradle,

Had been changed, 'tis no fable;

===Chinese===
在费舍尔（Fisher），克罗马蒂镇（Cromarty），

有一次一群喧闹的人聚会，

混乱超过巴别塔，

整整持续了一个小时；

当妻子们从海边回来，

每个人都发现有充分的理由悲伤，

因为每个婴儿都被留在了摇篮里，

被替换过了，这不是寓言；

===Original===
They said 'twas fairies did them change,

And left with them but weaklings strange.

Old wife, to end confusion wild,

Said each must bring to her the child;

Soon mothers they did find their dears,

And did wipe then from eyes all tears,

While few young men across the way

They glorious did enjoy the fray,

For while the mothers were at beach

They changed all babes within their reach.

===Chinese===
他们说是精灵替换了他们，

留下的只是体弱的陌生婴儿。

老妇人为了结束这疯狂的混乱，

说每个人必须把孩子带到她那里；

很快母亲们找到了她们的宝贝，

擦干了她们眼中的泪水，

而路对面几个年轻小伙

却兴高采烈地享受着这场混乱，

因为当母亲们在海滩上时，

他们替换了一切够得着的婴儿。

===Original===
Read at Anniversary.

===Chinese===
在纪念日上朗诵。

===Original===
Our ancient custom to renew,

We meet to honor St. Andrew,

He was of the Jewish nation,

A fisherman by occupation;

No warlike knight with lance and sword

But humbly following his Lord;

And Scotia she justly claims

Her soil contains his last remains,

===Chinese===
我们延续古老的习俗，

相聚以纪念圣安德鲁（St. Andrew），

他属于犹太民族，

以捕鱼为业；

他不是手持长矛利剑的好战骑士，

而是谦卑地追随他的主；

苏格兰（Scotia）理所当然地宣称

她的土地保存着他最后的遗骸，

===Original===
In early times the Pilgrims drew

Into the shrine of St. Andrew,

For miracles it gained renown,

And thence sprang up St. Andrew's town;

Now clansmen twine round maple leaf,22

When rallying at the call of chief,

And time will come when we'll be one,

And proud of name Canadian,

===Chinese===
早年间朝圣者们纷纷前往

圣安德鲁的圣殿，

它因神迹而闻名，

圣安德鲁镇（St. Andrew's town）由此兴起；

如今族人们在枫叶[注22]周围紧密团结，

当他们响应首领的召唤集合，

将来我们会合而为一，

以加拿大的名字为荣，

===Original===
But Scotia must not be forgot

For sake of Chalmers, Burns and Scott,

But here upon Canadian soil

A man may own where he doth toil,

For here each may enjoy the charm

Of owning fine prairie farm.

===Chinese===
但苏格兰不应被遗忘，

为了查默斯（Chalmers）、彭斯（Burns）和司各特（Scott），

但在这加拿大的土地上，

一个人可以拥有他耕作的地方，

因为在这里每个人都能享受

拥有美好大草原农场的魅力。

===Original===
A tale we'll tell of what hath been

When maids and youths kept Halloween,

It is a tale of old world lore

What happened in the days of yore,

When fairies danced upon the green

So merrily on Halloween,

===Chinese===
我们讲述一个关于往事的故事，

少女和青年们如何庆祝万圣节前夜（Halloween），

这是一个古老世界的故事，

发生在久远的过去，

当精灵们在草地上跳舞，

在万圣节前夜如此欢乐，

===Original===
And witches did play many a trick

Assisted by their auld friend Nick,

And lovers meet around the fire

Near to the one their hearts desire,

For to burn nuts for to discover

The truthfulness of their lover.

===Chinese===
女巫们在她们的老朋友尼克（Nick）帮助下

施展了许多恶作剧，

恋人们围坐在炉火旁，

靠近他们心仪的人，

燃烧坚果以发现

爱人的真心。

===Original===
They first did give each nut a name,

This was Sandy, that was Jane.

If they did blaze side by side,

She knew her husband, he his bride,

But if one up the chimney flew,

One knew the other was not true.

===Chinese===
他们先给每颗坚果取了名字，

这颗是桑迪（Sandy），那颗是简（Jane）。

如果它们并排燃烧，

她知道她的丈夫，他知道他的新娘，

但如果一颗飞进了烟囱，

一方就知道另一方不忠。

===Original===
And one sure test did never fail,

Blindfold to find good stock of kale,

To pull the first comes to the hand

With heavy roots of earth and sand,

For the very weight of mould

Does denote weight of lovers gold.

===Chinese===
还有一个万无一失的测试从未失灵，

蒙着眼睛去找到一棵上好的甘蓝（kale），

拔起第一棵碰到的，

带着沉重的泥土和沙子的根，

因为泥土的重量

便代表着爱人的财富。

===Original===
In tubs children love to splatter,

Ducking for apples in the water,

For such were the delights of yore,

Which soon will cease for evermore;

At Balmoral Castle Britain's Queen

Oft' celebrated Halloween,

===Chinese===
孩子们喜欢在水盆里溅水嬉戏，

在水中叼苹果，

因为这些就是昔日的乐趣，

它们很快将永远消失；

在巴尔莫勒尔堡（Balmoral Castle），不列颠女王

常庆祝万圣节前夜，

===Original===
But Highland landlords now do clear

Land of men to make room for deer,

But here upon Canadian soil

A man may own where he doth toil.

===Chinese===
但高地地主们如今正在驱逐

居民以腾出地方养鹿，

但在这加拿大的土地上，

一个人可以拥有他耕作的地方。

===Original===
Lines written on the arrival of Governor Lorne and the Princess Louise in Canada.

===Chinese===
洛恩总督（Governor Lorne）和路易丝公主（Princess Louise）抵达加拿大时所作。

===Original===
The tidings now all hearts do please,

That she has landed safe, Louise,

Victoria's beloved daughter,

Who boldly has crossed the water,

For royal Princess doth adorn

The title of the Lord of Lorne,

For this union it doth join

Campbell with Royal Stewart line;

Lorne will be Duke of broad Argyle,

And the Lord of many an Isle.

===Chinese===
这个消息令所有人的心都欣喜，

路易丝（Louise）已安全登陆，

维多利亚心爱的女儿，

她勇敢地渡过了大海，

因为皇家公主装点着

洛恩勋爵（Lord of Lorne）的称号，

因为这桩联姻将

坎贝尔（Campbell）家族与皇家斯图亚特血脉联结；

洛恩将成为辽阔的阿盖尔（Argyle）公爵，

以及许多岛屿的主人。

===Original===
When he inherits broad domain

May he strive tenants hearts to gain.

To us it seems a brighter morn

Hath dawned on us with Governor Lorne,

And when they visited this place

True happiness beamed on each face,

The first white child who here was born

Presented was to Governor Lorne,

From Forest 'ere it was reclaimed,

Our fine town after him was named.

===Chinese===
当他继承广袤的领地时，

愿他努力赢得佃户们的心。

对我们来说，一个更明亮的黎明

随着洛恩总督的到来而破晓，

当他们访问此地时，

幸福洋溢在每个人的脸上，

这里出生的第一个白人婴儿

被献给了洛恩总督，

在森林被开垦之前，

我们美丽的城镇以他命名。

===Original===
The following appeared as a prose tale in a Scottish paper published in New York. We have endeavoured to add to the dramatic effect thereof in our rendering it into verse:

===Chinese===
以下故事以散文形式发表在纽约出版的一份苏格兰报纸上。我们努力将其改写为诗歌以增强戏剧效果：

===Original===
A lad brought up in Highland vale

Who did believe each fairy tale,

Which his grannie oft' to him told,

And of witches and of warlocks bold,

And he himself would often pour

For hours reading wizard lore.

===Chinese===
一个在高地峡谷中长大的少年，

他相信每一个精灵故事，

那是他祖母常给他讲的，

还有女巫和大胆的术士的故事，

他自己也常常花上几个小时

阅读巫师的传说。

===Original===
One night his mother to the town

In a hurry sent him down,

So o'er his pony he did stride,

And to the town did fearful ride,

He thought that demons they would rush

On him from every rock and bush,

And as he rode through the quarry

It did great increase his flurry,

He felt that fiends with fiercest hate

Would surely there seal fast his fate.

===Chinese===
一天晚上，他的母亲急匆匆地

打发他去镇上，

于是他跨上小马，

恐惧地向镇上骑去，

他以为恶魔会从

每块岩石和每丛灌木中向他扑来，

当他骑马穿过采石场时，

他的惊恐大大加剧，

他觉得满怀最凶猛仇恨的恶魔

定会在那里了结他的性命。

===Original===
But town he reached and 'neath his vest

He parcel pressed close to his breast,

The pony now he mounts once more

For to pass quarry as before,

But, alas, at that fatal spot

He heard a gun, he was elf shot,

He felt that from his breast a flood

Was pouring down of his heart's blood,

But he clung fast to pony's back,

Though loss of blood his frame did rack,

But in spite of his alarms

He resolved to die in mother's arms,

===Chinese===
但他到达了镇上，将包裹

紧紧压在马甲下贴着胸口，

他再次骑上小马，

像之前一样经过采石场，

但，唉，在那个致命之处，

他听到了枪声，他被精灵之箭射中了，

他感到一股血流

从胸中涌出心脏的鲜血，

但他紧紧抓住小马的背部，

尽管失血使他浑身剧痛，

但尽管恐惧万分，

他决心死在母亲的怀抱中，

===Original===
And when he reached his own door

He said that he was drenched in gore,

From bullet hole all in his breast.

His father opened up his vest,

And he did sadly fear the worst

But found yeast bottle had but burst.

===Chinese===
当他到了自己家门口，

他说他浑身是血，

胸口满是弹孔。

他父亲打开他的马甲，

他悲痛地担心最坏的情况

却发现不过是酵母瓶炸了。

===Original===
Some thirty years ago, in conversation with an old sea captain who had visited or voyaged to all quarters of the globe, he was denouncing fiercely the degeneracy of these costermonger times. He said there was a book in our town library which was a tissue of falsehood from beginning to end, and that there never existed such a man as Gordon Cumming, the Lion Hunter. I told the old gentleman that I had seen the Lion Hunter hundreds of times and conversed with him in the woods of Aylter, and that be was a descendant of the Royal Comyn, one of whom was killed by King Robert the Bruce, and that I had seen the magnificent person of Gordon Cumming in the garb of old Gaul, successfully punish a huge prize fighter who kept grossly insulting him during the excitement of a general election﻿—when Cumming's uncle, Major Cumming Bruce, was running for member, this Major being father-in-law to Lord Elgin, formerly Governor of Canada. I also told him that Hugh Millar was a warm friend of the Lion Hunter's mother, as she was distinguished both as a geologist and a botanist, and that Livingstone, the great traveller, was a great admirer and intimate friend of the Hunter. After his return to Britain he exhibited himself and his magnificent trophies throughout all the cities and towns of Britain and Ireland. His own noble figure in full Highland costume was perhaps no insignificant part of the exhibition. Barnum afterwards secured the noble specimens of hides and horns and monstrous tusks for his New York museum.23

===Chinese===
大约三十年前，与一位环游世界的老船长交谈时，他激烈地抨击这个堕落的市侩时代。他说我们镇图书馆里有一本书从头到尾都是谎言，从来没有戈登·卡明（Gordon Cumming）这样的猎狮者。我告诉这位老先生，我见过猎狮者数百次，在艾尔特（Aylter）的树林里和他交谈过，他是皇家科明（Royal Comyn）家族的后裔，其中一位被罗伯特·布鲁斯国王（King Robert the Bruce）所杀。我还见过戈登·卡明穿着古高卢服装的英武身躯，他成功地惩罚了一个在他竞选大选期间不断粗暴侮辱他的大块头职业拳击手——当时卡明的叔叔卡明·布鲁斯少校（Major Cumming Bruce）正在竞选议员，这位少校是加拿大前总督埃尔金勋爵（Lord Elgin）的岳父。我还告诉他，休·米勒（Hugh Millar）是猎狮者母亲的挚友，她既是一位杰出的地质学家又是一位植物学家，伟大的旅行家利文斯通（Livingstone）也是猎狮者的崇拜者和亲密朋友。回到英国后，他在英国和爱尔兰所有城镇展示了自己和他辉煌的战利品。他自己身着全套高地服装的高贵身姿或许也是展览中不可忽视的一部分。巴纳姆（Barnum）后来将那些珍贵的皮毛、鹿角和巨大的象牙标本收入了他纽约的博物馆。[注23]

===Original===
Now the youth in fertile Moray

Do in Gordon Cumming glory,

Bold lion hunter-first who made

With Africa tribes successful trade;

First in those wilds to fire a gun,

While he the mighty trophies won.

===Chinese===
如今富饶的莫雷（Moray）的年轻人

以戈登·卡明（Gordon Cumming）为荣，

勇敢的猎狮者——他是第一个

与非洲部落成功通商的人；

他是在那些荒野中第一个开枪的人，

同时他赢得了巨大的战利品。

===Original===
An American Yarn ground into rhyme in our Poetical Mill.

===Chinese===
一个美国故事，在我们的诗歌磨坊中被磨成韵文。

===Original===
As we have given several humorous Scottish stories in verse we will venture to trespass on your good nature by giving an American specimen. The scene is laid in the suburbs of New York. It was a prose tale, and we fancy we have not diminished the height, breadth or depth of the humour by grinding it in our poetical mill and having it flow out in rhyme.

===Chinese===
既然我们已经用诗歌讲了几个幽默的苏格兰故事，我们将冒昧地用一个美国故事来打扰大家的好性子。故事发生在纽约郊区。这原本是个散文故事，我们相信在诗歌磨坊中将它磨成韵文流出，并没有减损它的高度、广度或深度的幽默。

===Original===
There is a man, his name is Brown,

He lives in a suburban town

And has an office in the city,

His misfortunes you will pity.

===Chinese===
有一个人，他名叫布朗（Brown），

他住在郊区小镇上，

在城里有一间办公室，

你会同情他的不幸遭遇。

===Original===
His mind it was on stocks and change,

He cared not for things new or strange;

But agent managed him to hook

And sold to him a costly book.

===Chinese===
他满脑子都是股票和交易，

对新奇或陌生的事物毫不关心；

但书商设法把他套住

卖给他一本昂贵的书。

===Original===
Brown cared not for those glorious names﻿—

Died for religion in the flames;

Now he felt agent was a Tartar

For selling him a book of martyr.

===Chinese===
布朗不在乎那些辉煌的名字——

为宗教在火焰中殉难；

现在他觉得书商是个恶魔

竟然卖给他一本关于殉道者的书。

===Original===
The agent knew it would make strife,

But sold another to his wife;

She did not know that Brown had bought,

And agent on her easy wrought.

===Chinese===
书商知道这会引发矛盾，

但又卖了一本给他的妻子；

她不知道布朗已经买了，

书商轻而易举地搞定了她。

===Original===
Approaching her with winning smile

He poor woman did beguile.

He made her believe without a doubt

No Christian could do without

This book, which would all inspire

With spark of celestial fire,

With feelings like the first martyr

Who had died for Christian charter.

===Chinese===
他带着迷人的微笑走近她

诱骗了这位可怜的女人。

他让她毫不怀疑地相信

没有基督徒能缺少这本书，

它将以天堂之火的火花

激励所有人，

怀着如同第一位殉道者般的情感，

他为基督教的宪章而死。

===Original===
When Brown did home return at night

His wife, to add to his delight,

Resolved that she would, after tea,

Get chatting with her husband free

And tell him of fine book she bought;

Of trouble fresh she never thought,

But she noticed a gloomy frown

On the brow of her husband Brown,

But thought when I my purchase tell

Those dark clouds they will dispel;

She said, my dear, I bought martyr,

He looked as if he her could quarter.

===Chinese===
当布朗晚上回到家时，

他的妻子，为了增添他的喜悦，

决定在茶后

和丈夫畅谈一番，

告诉他她买了一本好书；

她从未想到新的麻烦，

但她注意到阴郁的皱眉

浮现在丈夫布朗的额头，

但想到当我告诉他我的购买时

那些乌云就会消散；

她说，亲爱的，我买了殉道者，

他看上去仿佛要把她撕成碎片。

===Original===
And said the scoundrel sold me book;

Out of the window then he did look

And saw the agent haste to train;

He tried to stop him, but in vain;

Smith then was passing in spring wagon,

And he had his trotting nag on;

He told him to stop book agent;

His escape for to prevent,

Smith told him Brown wanted him;

But agent-nothing daunted him;

Said he: He only wants to barter

With me for my book of Martyr.

===Chinese===
他说那个恶棍卖给了我书！

他朝窗外望去

看到书商正赶火车；

他试图拦住他，但徒劳无功；

史密斯（Smith）此时赶着弹簧马车经过，

套着他那匹快步小马；

他让他拦住书商；

阻止他逃跑，

史密斯告诉他说布朗要找他；

但书商毫不畏惧；

他说：他只是想和我交换

我的殉道者之书。

===Original===
If thats all, said Smith, with quick dash,

Give me his book, and here's your cash;

Book agent jumped aboard the car,

For he knew there would be war;

Smith met Brown with triumphant look,

Said he: I have got you the book;

Brown's feelings now no one could paint,

He there did show he was no saint;

But to big own home he now returned,

And fierce rage in his bosom burned;

He was not fit for Knight of Garter

When he brought in the third martyr.

===Chinese===
如果仅此而已，史密斯干脆地说，

把他的书给我，这是你的钱；

书商跳上了火车，

因为他知道会有冲突；

史密斯得意洋洋地见到布朗，

说：我把那本书给你弄来了；

布朗此刻的心情无人能描述，

他当场表现出他不是圣人；

但他回到了自己的家，

胸中燃烧着狂怒；

他带回了第三本殉道者时，

他可不适合做嘉德骑士。

===Original===
From roots of prose of various climes,

Each tale thus grows all clad in rhymes.

===Chinese===
从各地散文的根苗中，

每个故事就这样披上了韵文的华裳。

===Original===
Sept., 1884.

===Chinese===
1884年9月。

===Original===
In summer time it doth seem good

To seek the shade of the green wood,

For it doth banish all our care

When we gaze on scene so fair.

===Chinese===
在夏天，去寻找绿林的荫凉

似乎是一件美事，

因为当我们凝视如此美丽的景色时，

它能驱散我们所有的烦恼。

===Original===
And birds do here in branches sing

So merrily in early spring,

And lovingly they here do pair

Their mutual joys together share.

===Chinese===
鸟儿在枝头欢快地歌唱，

在早春时节如此欢快，

它们在这里恩爱地配对，

共同分享彼此的欢乐。

===Original===
Here nature's charming, never rude,

Inspiring all with happy mood,

Tables had choice fruits of season,

And we too had feast of reason.

===Chinese===
这里大自然迷人而不粗犷，

激发所有人快乐的心情，

桌上摆满了时令精选水果，

我们也有思想的盛宴。

===Original===
To dinner table all did march

Through evergreen triumphal arch,

On top the Union Jack it floats,

On each side sheaves of wheat and oats.

===Chinese===
所有人列队走向餐桌

穿过常绿的凯旋拱门，

顶部飘扬着联合旗（Union Jack），

两旁是小麦和燕麦的麦束。

===Original===
Great pumpkins and big ears of corn,

They do this rural arch adorn,

We are reminded now 'tis fall,

And boys enjoy game of baseball.

===Chinese===
大南瓜和大穗玉米，

装饰着这座田园拱门，

我们被提醒如今已是秋天，

男孩们享受着棒球游戏。

===Original===
With joy at night each one did gaze

At the mighty bonfire's blaze,

The tree leaves shone like silver bright,

The lanterns too were pleasing sight.

===Chinese===
夜晚每个人都欣喜地凝视

大火堆的火焰，

树叶闪烁着银色的光芒，

灯笼也是一道赏心悦目的风景。

===Original===
This is our earliest cheese ode. The Ingersoll factory has been removed to Thamesford.

===Chinese===
这是我们最早的奶酪颂歌。英格索尔（Ingersoll）的工厂已迁至泰姆斯福德（Thamesford）。

===Original===
When Father Ranney left the States,

In Canada to try the fates,

He settled down in Dereham,

Then no dairyman lived near him;

He was the first there to squeeze

His cows' milk into good cheese,

And at each Provincial show

His famed cheese was all the go.

===Chinese===
当兰尼老爹（Father Ranney）离开美国，

到加拿大闯荡命运时，

他在德勒姆（Dereham）定居下来，

那时附近没有其他乳农；

他是那里第一个将

牛奶制成好奶酪的人，

在每次省级展会上，

他著名的奶酪都大受追捧。

===Original===
Chorus

Then long life to Father Ranney

May he wealth and honour gain aye.

===Chinese===
副歌

祝兰尼老爹长寿

愿他永远获得财富和荣誉。

===Original===
He always took the first prize

Both for quality and size,

But many of his neighbors

Now profit by his labors,

And the ladies dress in silk

From the proceeds of the milk,

But those who buy their butter,

How dear it is, they mutter.

===Chinese===
他总是获得头奖，

品质和大小都名列前茅，

但他许多邻居

如今都得益于他的辛劳，

女士们穿着丝绸衣裳，

靠的是牛奶的收入，

但那些购买他们黄油的人，

抱怨它多么昂贵。

===Original===
Chorus

Then long life to Father Ranney,

May he his health retain aye.

===Chinese===
副歌

祝兰尼老爹长寿，

愿他永远保持健康。

===Original===
The farmers can not be beat,

They have both cheese and their wheat,

Though now their greatest care is

For to watch o'er their dairies,

They carefully fill their mows

With provender for their cows,

And they thus enrich the soil

With much profit for their toil.

===Chinese===
农民们无人能比，

他们既有奶酪又有小麦，

虽然现在他们最大的关切

是照看他们的牛舍，

他们仔细地填满牛棚

为牛备好饲料，

他们就这样丰富了土壤，

为辛劳带来丰厚的回报。

===Original===
Chorus

We will sing this refrain aye,

Long life to Father Ranney.

===Chinese===
副歌

我们将永远吟唱这个副歌，

兰尼老爹长寿。

===Original===
The motto "union is strength"

Is carried out at length,

In the most compact array

At every cheese factory,

You'll see without going far as

There is one kept by Harris,

The factory of Ingersoll,

Just out at the first toll.

===Chinese===
"团结就是力量"的格言

得到了充分的实践，

在最紧凑的布局中

每家奶酪工厂都有体现，

不远你就能看到

哈里斯（Harris）经营的一家，

英格索尔（Ingersoll）的工厂，

就在第一个收费处外面。

===Original===
Chorus

May he never suffer pain aye,

The Father of cheesemen Ranney.

===Chinese===
副歌

愿他永远不受痛苦，

奶酪之父兰尼老爹。

===Original===
Or you may go all the way

To see one kept by Galloway,

And out in the Norwiches

Dairymen are making riches,

And honor has been won

By Harvey Farrington,

The same path is trodden

By folks about Culloden.

===Chinese===
或者你可以走远一点

去看加洛韦（Galloway）经营的那家，

在诺维奇（Norwiches）一带，

乳农们正在致富，

荣誉已被

哈维·法林顿（Harvey Farrington）赢得，

在库洛登（Culloden）附近的人们

走着同样的道路。

===Original===
Chorus

May his strength never wane aye,

The great dairyman Ranney.

===Chinese===
副歌

愿他的力量永不衰减，

伟大的乳农兰尼老爹。

===Original===
And of late we saw some

Very good cheese from Lawson,

All around Mt. Elgin

Dairymen have well done,

And out in East Nissouri

They make some scores a day,

From Jarvis and Elliott

Some good cheese are bought.

===Chinese===
最近我们看到了一些

劳森（Lawson）出产的上好奶酪，

在厄尔金山（Mt. Elgin）周围

乳农们做得很好，

在东尼苏里（East Nissouri），

他们每天生产几十块，

从贾维斯（Jarvis）和埃利奥特（Elliott）

买到了一些好奶酪。

===Original===
Chorus

And we will all remain aye,

Indebted to Father Ranney.

===Chinese===
副歌

我们将永远铭记在心，

感谢兰尼老爹的恩情。

===Original===
Now we close this glorious theme,

This song of curds and rich cream,

You can buy your hoops and screws,

And all supplies for dairy use,

Milk cans and vats, all things like these,

In Ingersoll great mart for cheese,

Here buyers all do congregate

And pay for cheese the highest rate.

===Chinese===
现在我们结束这个辉煌的主题，

这首关于凝乳和浓厚奶油的歌，

你可以买到你的箍和螺丝，

以及所有乳品用途的供应品，

奶桶和大桶，所有这些物件，

在英格索尔（Ingersoll），奶酪的大市场，

买家们聚集在这里

以最高的价格购买奶酪。

===Original===
Chorus

So we call on you again aye,

To honor Father Ranney.

===Chinese===
副歌

所以我们再次呼唤你，

向兰尼老爹致敬。

===Original===
All those who quality do prize

Must study color, taste and size,

And keep their dishes clean and sweet,

And all things round their factories neat,

For dairymen insist that these

Are all important points in cheese.

===Chinese===
所有珍视品质的人

必须研究颜色、味道和大小，

保持器皿清洁而甜美，

工厂周围一切井井有条，

因为乳农们坚持认为

这些都是奶酪的重要要点。

===Original===
Grant has here a famous work

Devoted to the cure of pork,

For dairymen find it doth pay

To fatten pigs upon the whey,

For there is money raising grease

As well as in the making cheese.

===Chinese===
格兰特（Grant）在这里有一部著名的作品

专论猪肉的腌制，

因为乳农们发现用乳清

来养肥猪是划算的，

因为养肥猪和制作奶酪

一样能赚钱。

===Original===
Weighing over 7,000 pounds.

===Chinese===
重量超过七千磅。

===Original===
We have seen thee, queen of cheese,

Lying quietly at your ease,

Gently fanned by evening breeze,

Thy fair form no flies dare seize.

===Chinese===
我们见过你，奶酪女王，

安静地躺着，悠闲自在，

晚风轻柔地拂过，

你美丽的形态无蝇敢碰。

===Original===
All gaily dressed soon you'll go

To the great Provincial show,

To be admired by many a beau

In the city of Toronto.

===Chinese===
你很快将盛装出发

去参加盛大的省级展览，

在多伦多市

被众多倾慕者欣赏。

===Original===
Cows numerous as a swarm of bees,

Or as the leaves upon the trees,

It did require to make thee please,

And stand unrivalled, queen of cheese.

===Chinese===
如蜂群般众多的奶牛，

如树上的叶片般繁多，

才足以让你令人满意，

傲然独立，无与伦比，奶酪女王。

===Original===
May you not receive a scar as

We have heard that Mr. Harris

Intends to send you off as far as

The great world's show at Paris.

===Chinese===
愿你不受到任何伤痕，因为我们听说

哈里斯（Harris）先生

打算将你远送到

巴黎的世界博览会上。

===Original===
Of the youth beware of these,

For some of them might rudely squeeze

And bite your cheek, then songs or glees

We could not sing, oh! queen of cheese.

===Chinese===
要提防那些年轻人，

因为他们中有人可能粗鲁地挤压

和啃咬你的脸颊，那样我们就无法

歌唱或合唱了，啊！奶酪女王。

===Original===
We'rt thou suspended from balloon,

You'd cast a shade even at noon,

Folks would think it was the moon

About to fall and crush them soon.

===Chinese===
如果你悬挂在气球上，

即使在正午你也能投下阴影，

人们会以为那是月亮

即将坠落将他们砸碎。

===Original===
McIntyre has a few rows

Of the latest styles of Bureaus.

===Chinese===
麦金泰尔（McIntyre）有几排

最新款式的五斗柜。

===Original===
Old Version

The allusion to Woodstock in the following was a good description of the Town about the year 1860. It was then a long narrow Town with a great deal more length than breadth, but it is now one of the finest Towns of the Province, and to its many fine structures soon will be added a magnificent Court House, worthy of the County of Oxford. The eyes of the whole world were directed towards the Town where Birchall was confined, tried and executed in the year 1890, for the murder of young Benwell, to which event we refer further on.

===Chinese===
旧版本

以下对伍德斯托克（Woodstock）的描写约是1860年前后对该镇的生动描绘。那时它是一个狭长的城镇，长度远超宽度，但它如今已是该省最美丽的城镇之一，在其众多精美建筑之外，很快将增添一座宏伟的法院大楼，配得上牛津郡（Oxford County）。全世界的目光都曾聚焦在这个小镇上，1890年伯查尔（Burchall）在这里被拘押、审判并处决，罪名是谋杀年轻的本韦尔（Benwell），我们将在后文中提及此事件。

===Original===
We love to sing of classic names﻿—

Even if we choose a borrowed plume﻿—

Our theme, the Valley of the Thames,

Where man may yet find ample room.

===Chinese===
我们热爱歌咏古典之名——

纵然借用了别人的羽饰——

我们的主题是泰晤士河谷，

在那里人仍有广阔的天地。

===Original===
It is not of old England's river,

Covered o'er with many a mast;

But where Cabot did discover

The land of lakes and forests vast.

===Chinese===
这并非古老的英格兰之河，

桅杆林立遮蔽了天空；

而是卡伯特（Cabot）发现的地方，

湖泊与广袤森林的土地。

===Original===
Although we have historic claims,

Yet them we now do lay aside;

We pass the battle of the Thames,24

Where foreign foe did fiercely ride.

===Chinese===
虽然我们有历史的渊源，

但我们将它们暂时搁置；

我们略过泰晤士河之战[注24]，

外国敌人曾在那里凶猛地骑行。

===Original===
We do not sing the graceful dames,

No more than the fierce battle's shock;

We merely trace old English names,

Beginning first with the Woodstock.

===Chinese===
我们不歌咏优雅的淑女，

也不歌咏激烈的战斗冲击；

我们只是追溯古老的英国地名，

首先从伍德斯托克（Woodstock）开始。

===Original===
England's Woodstock had a palace

None was raised up grander, stronger;

Canadian Woodstock without malice,

We may say your streets by far the longer.25

===Chinese===
英格兰的伍德斯托克曾有一座宫殿，

没有比它更宏伟、更坚固的；

加拿大的伍德斯托克，恕我们直言，

可以说你的街道比它长得多。[注25]

===Original===
Yet you are Oxford's County Town,

And can boast a fine new college,

Which with old age may some day frown,

Like Old England's seat of knowledge

===Chinese===
但你是牛津郡的首府，

可以夸耀一所精美的新学院，

有朝一日它会像老英格兰的学术圣地那样

带着岁月的威严凝视。

===Original===
The next in order we enroll

Is Westminster and Middlesex,

With London for it capitol﻿—

These names a Cockney do perplex.

===Chinese===
我们接着排列的是

威斯敏斯特（Westminster）和米德尔塞克斯（Middlesex），

以伦敦（London）为首府——

这些名字让伦敦人困惑不已。

===Original===
Each familiar name doth greet thee!

Its bridges, markets, and its halls;

All things in the Forest City

Bears English names, even to St. Pauls'.

===Chinese===
每个熟悉的名字都向你致敬！

它的桥梁、市场和大厅；

森林之城中的一切

都带有英国的名字，甚至包括圣保罗（St. Pauls'）。

===Original===
The next in order we do trace,

Is Chatham﻿—once a famed resort﻿—

For there the bloodhound dared not chase,

Nor tear good colored men from sport.

===Chinese===
我们接着追溯的是

查塔姆（Chatham）——曾是一个著名的地方——

因为在那里猎犬不敢追捕，

也不敢将善良的有色人从运动中拖走。

===Original===
And now our verse draws to a close,

Because beyond the County Kent

The Thames by name no longer flows,

But in the lake it finds a vent.

===Chinese===
现在我们的诗即将结束，

因为在肯特郡（Kent）之外

泰晤士河不再以这个名字流淌，

它在湖中找到了归宿。

===Original===
The people all say and declare it is true,

The best furniture is made of McIntyre's glue.

===Chinese===
人们都说这是真的，

最好的家具是用麦金泰尔（McIntyre）的胶水做的。

===Original===
Lines on receiving an announcement by card that Mr. Gunn had assumed the control of the Courier.

===Chinese===
收到冈恩先生（Mr. Gunn）接管《信使报》（Courier）的名片通知后所作。

===Original===
My dear friend Gunn,

May you prize won,

But to us hark

When you shoot mark,

===Chinese===
我亲爱的朋友冈恩（Gunn），

愿你赢得奖赏，

但请听我们说

当你射击时要瞄准靶心，

===Original===
High you must aim

And at right game,

And truth employ

Wrong to destroy.

===Chinese===
你必须瞄准高处

对准正确的目标，

运用真理

摧毁谬误。

===Original===
Your friend the Bard

Hopes your reward

Will be furriore

For Courier.

===Chinese===
你的朋友诗人

希望你的奖赏

将因《信使报》（Courier）而更加丰厚。

===Original===
British infants who were nobly born

Were from their bleeding mother's bosom torn,

And with the bayonet dashed upon the street

There left to lie for native dogs to eat.

===Chinese===
出身高贵的英国婴儿

被从流血的母亲怀中夺走，

被刺刀摔在街道上，

躺在那里任凭野狗啃食。

===Original===
But the British Lion he quick o'erthrew,

Both the high and the low Hindu,

Now they respect the Christian laws

For fear of British Lion's paws.

===Chinese===
但英国雄狮迅速击溃了

印度教的上层和下层，

如今他们尊重基督教的律法，

因为害怕英国雄狮的利爪。

===Original===
Campbell's Block

Owing to the peculiar slant in our front glass the strange optical illusion described below was witnessed until the window was curtained:

===Chinese===
坎贝尔大楼（Campbell's Block）

由于我们前玻璃的特殊倾斜角度，在窗帘拉上之前，人们目睹了以下描述的奇特光学幻象：

===Original===
You may look but look in vain

For stores with such immense pane;

All other shops they do surpass

With their enormous lights of glass.

===Chinese===
你可以看但徒劳无功

找不到有如此巨大玻璃窗的店铺；

它们以巨大的玻璃光面

超越了所有其他商店。

===Original===
One night by chance through them we gazed

But we did quickly stand amazed;

Three moons they did beauteous glow,

And gleams so lovely they did throw.

===Chinese===
一天晚上我们偶然透过它们眺望，

但很快便惊愕地站住；

三个月亮美丽地发光，

投射出如此可爱的光芒。

===Original===
But a still more wond'rous story:

Mid day showed three suns in glory;

At the sight we scarce could gaze,

So full of brilliancy the rays.

===Chinese===
但更奇妙的故事还在后面：

正午时分显示三个辉煌的太阳；

我们几乎无法直视，

因为光芒如此璀璨。

===Original===
We had to curtain off the glory;

If you don't believe this wond'rous story

Ask Editor of the Tribune

About this tale of sun and moon.

===Chinese===
我们不得不拉上窗帘遮挡这份辉煌；

如果你不相信这个奇妙的故事，

就去问《论坛报》（Tribune）的编辑

关于这个日月的故事。

===Original===
Which did bewilder McIntyre

Gazing on mighty orbs of fire;

Eclipsing the electric light

So brilliant in their glorious might.

===Chinese===
它令麦金泰尔（McIntyre）困惑不已，

凝视着巨大的火球；

以它们辉煌的力量

使电灯黯然失色。

===Original===
Luminaries no more invade,

Excluded now by window shade;

Pray think that this is no intrusion,

This tale of optical illusion.

===Chinese===
天体不再侵入，

如今被窗帘遮挡；

请认为这不是冒犯，

这个光学幻象的故事。

===Original===
Suggested by the brilliant display at Toronto, September, 1884.

===Chinese===
1884年9月多伦多的壮丽展示有感。

===Original===
When the great Bulwer's pen did trace

The history of the coming race

His hero to us he hath unfurled

The glories of his new found world.

===Chinese===
当伟大的布尔沃（Bulwer）之笔描绘

未来种族的历史时，

他向我们展开了他的主人公

他新发现的世界的辉煌。

===Original===
This bold traveller be did venture

Far below our world's centre;

He entered through an ancient mine

And to us doth new world define.

===Chinese===
这位勇敢的旅行者冒险深入

我们地球中心之下；

他通过一座古老的矿井进入，

并向我们界定了一个新世界。

===Original===
Once all was discord and fierce jars,

Continuous engaged in wars

Till they discovered great vrill power,

Then wars did cease from that same hour.

===Chinese===
曾经一切充满了不和谐和剧烈的冲突，

不断卷入战争

直到他们发现了巨大的"弗里尔"（vrill）力量，

战争便从那一刻起停止了。

===Original===
Mighty bolts from it were hurled;

Would soon have laid waste the world;

So for world's preservation

At once they stopped desolation.

===Chinese===
巨大的能量从其中发射；

很快将使世界荒芜；

因此为了世界的存续，

他们立刻停止了毁灭。

===Original===
All wars by them are now despised

Unfit for nation civilized;

Their peaceful glories they do sing

And fly on artificial wing.

===Chinese===
他们如今鄙视一切战争，

认为它不适合文明的国度；

他们歌颂和平的荣光，

凭借人造翅膀飞翔。

===Original===
Great blazing artificial light

It ever burns both day and night,

For they have neither sun nor moon,

But radiant, always 'tis high noon.

===Chinese===
巨大而耀眼的人造光芒

日夜不停地燃烧，

因为他们既无太阳也无月亮，

但永远光芒四射，如同正午。

===Original===
When we beheld the glorious sight,

High in the air did shine so bright;

It seemed that it could banish night,

Great refulgent electric light.

===Chinese===
当我们目睹这壮丽的景象，

高悬空中如此璀璨；

它仿佛能驱散黑夜，

伟大而辉煌的电光。

===Original===
But after all, 'tis children's play,

Compared with the great orb of day,

Or when the moon it doth shine bright,

And stars do beautify the night.

===Chinese===
但归根结底，与伟大的日轮相比，

这不过是孩童的游戏，

或者当月亮明亮地照耀，

群星装点夜空的时候。

## Witch Stone / 女巫之石

===Original===
At Forres is a large round stone,
A relic of the days by gone;
For here there were two witches burned,
Underneath their ashes urned.
===
===Chinese===
福里斯镇有块大圆石，
乃是往昔岁月的遗物；
此处曾将两名女巫焚烧，
灰烬之下化为尘土。
===Original===
A man with veneration small
Broke stone and built it in his wall,
But the authorities of town
Made him full quickly pull them down.
===
===Chinese===
有个不敬之人不敬畏，
拆石砌入自家墙壁，
但镇上当权者不容此为，
命他速速将其拆除复归。
===Original===
Replace each piece, and it environ
With large bars of good Scottish iron;
May fine old town thrive and adorn
The beauteous banks of the Findhorn.
===
===Chinese===
将每块石头归位还原，
以优质苏格兰铁条围护；
愿这座美好的古镇繁盛兴旺，
装点芬德霍恩河美丽河岸。
===Original===
Great refulgent electric light.
But after all, 'tis children's play,
Compared with the great orb of day,
Or when the moon it doth shine bright,
And stars do beautify the night.
===
===Chinese===
灿烂夺目的电灯光芒。
但说到底不过是儿戏一场，
比起那伟大的日之圆轮，
抑或皓月当空明亮时，
繁星点缀夜色之美时。

## Dick and Edward / 迪克与爱德华

===Original===
The Thurso baker Robert Dick
Armed with his hammer and his pick,
Dame nature's secrets did reveal,
Which she for ages did conceal.
===
===Chinese===
瑟索面包师罗伯特·迪克（Robert Dick），
手持铁锤与尖镐为武装，
揭示了自然女神的秘密，
她将这些隐匿了无数漫长时光。
===Original===
In Banff has genius found regard
In the person of an Edward,
Who now does rank among the first
In the world as naturalist.
===
===Chinese===
在班夫，天才受到敬仰，
此人便是爱德华（Edward），
如今他在世上名列前茅，
身为博物学家声名远扬。

## Questions and Answers / 问答

===Original===
Are you a mason? No; I prefer
To work at the trade of carpenter.
Are you then an Oddfellow?
No; I married Annabella.
===
===Chinese===
你是共济会成员吗？不；我更愿
做木匠的手艺行当。
那你是共济会员吗？
不；我娶了安娜贝拉（Annabella）。
===Original===
Are you a Son of Temperance?
No; I am son of Joseph Vance.
Are you then a Forrester?
No; town life I prefer.
===
===Chinese===
你是戒酒之子吗？
不；我是约瑟夫·万斯（Joseph Vance）之子。
那你是护林员吗？
不；我更偏爱城镇生活。

## Lines Read at a Ladies' Aid / 妇女援助会上朗诵的诗

===Original===
After chatting with each friend,
We our way to the table wend,
On it we all do make a raid,
And this we call a ladies' aid.
===
===Chinese===
与每位朋友畅叙之后，
我们便向餐桌前行，
大家一齐向桌上发起"进攻"，
我们将此称作妇女援助会。
===Original===
'Tis pleasant way of taking tea,
Improvement on the old soirée,
On such a time as this I find
Food for body and for mind.
===
===Chinese===
这是品茶的愉快方式，
胜过昔日的旧式晚会，
在这样的时刻我寻得
身体与心灵的食粮。
===Original===
Gladly all obey the call,
To attend this pleasant social,
And we hope none will lament
The time and money they have spent.
===
===Chinese===
众人欣然响应召唤，
前来参加这愉快的聚会，
我们但愿无人会懊悔
所花费的时间与金钱。
===Original===
The matron and the comely maid
And youths attend the Ladies' Aid;
But now we close our short refrain,
And hope to meet you all again.
===
===Chinese===
妇人、秀丽的少女
与青年们共赴妇女援助会；
而今我们结束这段短歌，
期盼与诸位再度相会。

## Salford Parsonage / 索尔福德牧师住宅

Lines delivered at housewarming of Salford Parsonage.
索尔福德牧师住宅乔迁宴上朗诵之诗。

===Original===
Your pastor's fame first got abroad
By his success on Culloden Road;
He filled the church so that the fold
No longer it the flock would hold.
===
===Chinese===
你们牧师的名声初扬四方，
源于他在科洛登路上的成功；
他把教堂挤得满满当当，
羊圈已容不下那成群的信众。
===Original===
But soon a larger church did rise,
With fine neat tower points to the skies;
When you o'erwhelmed with ruin dire
Did lose your parsonage by fire.
===
===Chinese===
但不久一座更大的教堂拔地而起，
精美的塔楼直指苍穹；
当你们遭遇可怕的毁灭，
因大火失去了牧师住宅。
===Original===
To his call with generous bounty
You built best parsonage in county,
But some good people in the town
At this idea they might frown.
===
===Chinese===
响应他的号召，慷慨解囊，
你们建成了全县最好的牧师住宅，
但镇上一些善良的人们
对这想法或许会皱眉不满。
===Original===
Unless we made this correction:
The best in a rural section;
Your pastor he doth vigorous push,
He's not afraid to enter bush.
===
===Chinese===
除非我们做此更正：
是乡间地区最好的住宅；
你们的牧师干劲十足，
他不惧深入荒野丛林。
===Original===
And 'tis a fact there's very few
With him can stick of timber hew;
Ready for to superintend,
Or a helping hand to lend.
===
===Chinese===
事实是很少有人
能像他那样伐木砍柴；
他既可亲自监督，
也乐于伸出援手。
===Original===
When bold man's wanted at the front,
You've but to call on Reverend Hunt;
In pulpit he conviction darts,
And points the way to troubled hearts.
===
===Chinese===
当需要勇者冲锋在前，
你们只需召唤亨特牧师（Reverend Hunt）；
他在讲坛上掷地有声，
为忧愁的心灵指明方向。
===Original===
But solemn thoughts we won't employ,
As this is all a scene of joy;
Maids and matrons all charming
Do grace this pleasant housewarming.
===
===Chinese===
但我们不谈庄重之事，
因为此刻尽是欢乐的场面；
迷人的少女与妇人们
为这愉快的乔迁宴增光添彩。
===Original===
Here people come from near and far
To purchase goods at this bazar;
And all is happiness and glee
At festival of this soirée.
===
===Chinese===
人们从四面八方赶来，
在这义卖会上购买商品；
一切都洋溢着幸福与欢愉，
在这晚会的节日庆典上。

## Transformation Scene / 蜕变

A plain building was removed to a pleasant grove, and in a few weeks it was transformed into the neatest frame building in town.
一座朴素的建筑被移至宜人的小树林，数周之内便蜕变为镇上最精致的木构建筑。

===Original===
As westward we of late did rove
We beheld in the maple grove
An old church now owned by Peter;
In town there is no house looks neater,
For those bay windows do look sweeter
Than anything in prose or meter.
===
===Chinese===
我们最近向西漫步时，
在枫树林中望见
一座旧教堂现归彼得所有；
镇上没有比它更整洁的房屋，
那些凸窗看起来如此甜美，
胜过一切散文或诗句。

## Lines on London / 伦敦吟

Uniformed Encampment victorious at St. Thomas and at the great Forester's demonstration in London, August, 1884.
统一着装的露营队在圣托马斯及1884年8月伦敦大型护林员集会上大获全胜。

===Original===
They once in wilderness did ride
On beast with horn and shaggy hide—
A savage goat or unicorn,
But now parade in uniform;
As gay as ancient Knight or Lord,
With their grand plumes, and belt and swords.
===
===Chinese===
他们曾在荒野中骑行，
骑着长角披毛的野兽——
凶猛的山羊或独角兽，
但如今身着制服列队游行；
如古代骑士或贵族般华丽，
佩戴华丽的羽饰、腰带与佩剑。
===Original===
Their graceful movements at their drill
Doth all with admiration fill,
And the chief glory it was won
By the encampment of London;
For they at every tournament
For drill are the chief ornament.
===
===Chinese===
他们操练时优雅的动作，
令所有人满怀敬佩，
而那最高的荣耀
由伦敦露营队赢得；
因为每次比赛中，
他们的队列操练都是最耀眼的点缀。
===Original===
So skillfully they sword do wield,
Victorious on every field;
For victory's graven on their shield
Doth make their rivals for to yield;
Drill companies they out manoeuvre
From Newfoundland to Vancouver.
===
===Chinese===
他们舞剑如此娴熟，
在每片战场上都凯旋而归；
胜利铭刻在他们的盾牌上，
迫使对手俯首称臣；
从纽芬兰到温哥华，
他们的操练远胜所有队伍。

## Finale / 终曲

===Original===
It is folly now to aim
Or to seek for distant fame,
But rest content if we can claim
Something of a local name
Oh the pleasant banks of Thame,
Because in simple strains we sung
The glories of this country young.
===
===Chinese===
如今若还奢望
或追寻远方的名声，
实属愚蠢，
但若能获得些许地方声望，
便该心满意足——
哦，泰姆河（Thame）宜人的两岸，
因为我们以质朴的曲调
歌唱了这个年轻国度的荣光。

## Canada Before the Confederation of the Provinces / 省际联邦之前的加拿大

===Original===
Canadian provinces they lay
Divided by river and by bay,
Many a separate division,
Among them there was no cohesion.
===
===Chinese===
加拿大的各省割据一方，
被河流与海湾所分割，
各自为政互不相属，
彼此之间毫无凝聚力。
===Original===
But statesmen saw that a great nation
Could be formed by federation,
And soon they led public opinion
To favor forming this Dominion.
===
===Chinese===
但政治家们看到一个伟大的国家
可以通过联邦而诞生，
于是很快他们引导舆论，
支持建立这个自治领。
===Original===
Northwest with its streams and fountains,
With sources in the Rocky Mountains,
It was all a great mystery,
Hunting for furs its history.
===
===Chinese===
西北地区有溪流与泉源，
源头在落基山脉之中，
那里曾是一片巨大的谜，
猎取毛皮便是它的历史。
===Original===
Though Northwest is filling slow
Yet soon there will be mighty flow,
Millions to Northwest will hurry
In last decade of century.
===
===Chinese===
虽然西北人口增长缓慢，
但很快将会有大潮涌来，
数百万人将涌向西北，
在本世纪最后的十年间。
===Original===
For therein is an opening grand
In great fertile prairie land,
For there the choicest wheat it grows
Near where the Saskatchewan flows.
===
===Chinese===
因为那里有广阔的天地，
在肥沃的大草原上，
最上等的小麦在那里生长，
就在萨斯喀彻温河（Saskatchewan）流淌之处。
===Original===
And on many a river's branch
There is found great grazing ranch,
Favoured districts therein abound
Where cattle graze all the year round.
===
===Chinese===
在许多河流的支流旁，
有广袤的牧场，
其间遍布富饶的地区，
牛群四季在此放牧。
===Original===
Protected from the stormy blast
By the Rocky Mountains vast,
Through canon blows no storm terrific,
But balmy breezes from Pacific.
===
===Chinese===
有广袤的落基山脉
为其挡住狂暴的风暴，
峡谷中不闻恐怖的暴风，
唯有太平洋吹来的和煦微风。

## Canada's Future / 加拿大的未来

===Original===
Canada is a young giant,
Has not yet acquired its strength,
On the arts of peace reliant,
Throughout its vast breadth and length.
===
===Chinese===
加拿大是一个年轻的巨人，
尚未完全获得它的力量，
它依靠和平的技艺，
贯穿其辽阔的广度与长度。
===Original===
Though 'tis not famed for orange bowers
Nor for the products of its vines,
Though other lands have fairer flowers,
Yet it to nobler gifts inclines.
===
===Chinese===
虽然它不以橙树林闻名，
也不以葡萄藤的果实著称，
虽然其他土地有更美的花朵，
然而它倾向更高贵的天赋。
===Original===
It doth produce the golden grain
And few lands can with it compete,
They often try but all in vain
To produce such splendid wheat.
===
===Chinese===
它出产金色的谷物，
少有土地能与之匹敌，
他们屡屡尝试却徒劳无功，
无法产出如此出色的小麦。
===Original===
Our geologists divine,
That ere long we will behold
Many a rich glittering mine
Of copper, silver and of gold.
===
===Chinese===
我们的地质学家预言，
不久我们将看到
许多富饶闪闪发光的矿藏，
蕴藏着铜、银与黄金。
===Original===
But we sing more glorious theme,
It is our verdant pasture land,
Where cows produce a flood of cream,
Doth make cheese of the finest brand.
===
===Chinese===
但我们歌唱更辉煌的主题，
那是我们翠绿的牧场，
奶牛产出充沛的奶油，
制成顶级品牌的奶酪。
===Original===
And great thoughts oftentimes awakes
When we reflect on this wondrous land,
With vast rivers and mighty lakes,
All nature here's on scale so grand.
===
===Chinese===
当我们反思这片奇妙的土地，
常会唤醒伟大的思想，
这里有浩瀚的河流与壮阔的湖泊，
万物自然的规模如此恢宏。
===Original===
Young Dominion so gigantic,
Where rail cars run at speed terrific,
Thousands of miles from the Atlantic,
Till in the West you reach Pacific.
===
===Chinese===
年轻的自治领如此巨大，
火车以惊人的速度奔驰，
从大西洋沿岸绵延数千英里，
直到西方抵达太平洋。
===Original===
From balmy breezes of lake Erie
To the far north frozen ocean,
Where it now seems lone and dreary,
All will yet be life and motion.
===
===Chinese===
从伊利湖（Lake Erie）温暖的微风，
到遥远北方冰封的海洋，
那里如今看似寂寞荒凉，
终将充满生机与活力。
===Original===
Though nation's young its powerful fleet
Doth sail on many a distant sea,
For world's commerce to compete
Her sails in all climes flowing free.
===
===Chinese===
虽然国家年轻，她的强大舰队
已在许多遥远的海洋上航行，
为争夺世界贸易，
她的帆船在各气候带自由飘扬。

## Canada's Resources / 加拿大的资源

===Original===
Small Scotland nobly held its own
Against the might of England's throne,
And shall this land with its vast bounds
Shrink with fear ere the trumpet sounds.
===
===Chinese===
小小的苏格兰曾英勇自立，
抗衡英格兰王权的力量，
难道这片广袤的土地
会在号角吹响之前恐惧退缩？
===Original===
While British blood doth course each vein,
Proudly this heritage maintain,
With fertile acres by the billions,
Future homes for two hundred millions.
===
===Chinese===
只要英国血脉流淌在每条血管，
便当骄傲地守护这份遗产，
有数十亿英亩的肥沃土地，
可供两亿人未来的家园。
===Original===
Each son could have a fertile farm,
Brave men who ne'er will feel alarm,
And they have both the nerve and skill
To work land with a right good will.
===
===Chinese===
每个儿子都能拥有一片沃土，
勇敢的人们从不惊慌，
他们既有胆识又有技能，
满怀热忱耕耘土地。
===Original===
And she has got within her shores
Renowned mines of many ores,
While her furnaces and forges
Iron in useful shape disgorges.
===
===Chinese===
她的疆域之内拥有
多种矿石的著名矿藏，
她的熔炉与锻造厂
将铁铸成实用的形状。
===Original===
Her mighty forests they do yield
Lumber, her cities for to build,
But her wealth is not in these alone,
She has great quarries too of stone.
===
===Chinese===
她浩瀚的森林产出
木材，用以建造城市，
但她的财富不止于此，
她还有巨大的石材采石场。
===Original===
Industry it here doth bloom,
And skilful webs come from each loom,
One of great nations under sun,
A mightier race it yet will run.
===
===Chinese===
工业在此蓬勃绽放，
精巧的织品从每台织机上产出，
她是阳光下伟大的国家之一，
未来将奔向更强盛的征途。
===Original===
For with the Anglo-Saxon race
No other people can keep pace,
Here they have room for to expand
Into a nation mighty grand.
===
===Chinese===
因为与盎格鲁-撒克逊（Anglo-Saxon）种族相比，
没有其他民族能与之并驾齐驱，
他们在此有广阔的空间去扩展，
成为一个伟大辉煌的国家。
===Original===
With great railroads and canals,
And care in legislative halls,
A mighty future she will gain,
And highest rank she will obtain.
===
===Chinese===
有伟大的铁路与运河，
议政厅中勤勉治理，
她将赢得辉煌的未来，
获得至高的地位与荣耀。
===Original===
Canada hopes it will be told,
That she hath patriots brave and bold,
To guide her helm shall be extolled,
As loving country more than gold.
===
===Chinese===
加拿大希望人们传颂，
她拥有勇敢无畏的爱国者，
他们掌舵引航将受颂扬，
爱祖国胜过爱黄金。

## Northwest Rebellion / 西北叛乱

1885.

===Original===
Hail Canada our young fair land,
The world's respect it doth command;
How quick her sons at war's alarms
Sprang to her rescue with their arms.
===
===Chinese===
加拿大，我们年轻美丽的国土，向你致敬，
你赢得了全世界的尊重；
当战争的警报响起，
她的儿女们多么迅速地拿起武器前来救援。
===Original===
In Canada the English rose,
The shamrock and the thistle grows,
United garland they combine
Around the maple tree to twine.
===
===Chinese===
在加拿大，英格兰的玫瑰、
三叶草与蓟草共同生长，
它们编成统一的花环，
环绕枫树缠绕交织。
===Original===
They did march a brave gallant host
From the far East Atlantic coast,
Our Canada so proud and free,
Four thousand miles from sea to sea.
===
===Chinese===
他们组成英勇无畏的大军，
从遥远东方大西洋海岸进发，
我们骄傲而自由的加拿大，
从海到海绵延四千英里。
===Original===
Though skilful rebels did entrench,
But their deadly fires our boys did quench,
And victory it soon was won
By our General Middleton.
===
===Chinese===
尽管狡猾的叛军构筑了工事，
但我们的小伙子们浇灭了他们的炮火，
胜利很快便被
我们的米德尔顿将军（General Middleton）所赢得。
===Original===
And Colonel Williams left a name
For Canada's temple of fame,
A kind and a brave hearted man
In hour of danger led the van.
===
===Chinese===
威廉姆斯上校（Colonel Williams）留下了一个名字，
铭刻在加拿大的英名殿中，
一位善良而勇敢的人，
在危急时刻身先士卒。
===Original===
The ninetieth regiment it fought well,
And Winnipeg doth its glories tell,
London boasts of her volunteers,
For she prides in her Fusiliers.
===
===Chinese===
第九十团英勇作战，
温尼伯传颂着它的荣光，
伦敦为她的志愿军自豪，
她以她的燧发枪团（Fusiliers）为骄傲。
===Original===
Toronto troops have gained renown,
And triumph their quick march did crown,
For the relief of Battleford,
And scattering of the Indian horde.
===
===Chinese===
多伦多的部队赢得了声誉，
急行军的胜利为他们加冕，
他们解了巴特尔福德（Battleford）之围，
驱散了印第安部落的军队。
===Original===
Our volunteers took up their arms,
Each left his home and all its charms;
Though many they were tender reared,
No frost nor snow nor foe they feared.
===
===Chinese===
我们的志愿军拿起了武器，
每人离别了家园和一切温馨；
虽然许多人自幼娇生惯养，
他们不惧霜雪，不惧敌人。
===Original===
Alas that youth so true and brave,
So many now do fill a grave,
And others they are maimed for life,
While engaged in glorious strife.
===
===Chinese===
唉，那些如此忠勇的青年，
许多人如今已长眠地下，
另一些人则终身伤残，
在光荣的战斗中负伤。
===Original===
We have sprung from a good brave stock,
Rose, thistle and the shamrock,
Who all in unity agree,
'Neath the shade of the maple tree.
===
===Chinese===
我们出自勇敢优秀的血脉，
玫瑰、蓟草和三叶草，
它们在团结中和谐共生，
在枫树的荫庇之下。
===Original===
The Indians soon came to grief,
Under their great Poundmaker chief,
And Toronto troops gained fame
And Otter glory to his name.
===
===Chinese===
印第安人很快遭遇了失败，
在他们伟大的庞德梅克（Poundmaker）酋长之下，
多伦多部队赢得了名声，
奥特（Otter）也为他的名字增添了荣耀。
===Original===
We all felt proud of our gunboat
And the brave crew of the Northcote,
And of our scouts who captured Riel,
Who in vain for mercy did appeal.
===
===Chinese===
我们都为我们的炮艇感到骄傲，
还有"诺斯科特号"（Northcote）的英勇船员，
以及我们的侦察兵俘获了里尔（Riel），
他求饶却徒劳无功。
===Original===
And may all quickly come to grief
Who do not love the maple leaf,
For they spring from a noble tree,
Shades this land of the brave and free.
===
===Chinese===
愿所有不热爱枫叶之人
早日遭到报应，
因为它源于一棵高贵的树，
庇荫着这片勇敢自由的土地。

## Big Bear the Indian Chief / 印第安酋长大熊

The following impromptu was given at a banquet to one of the captives of Fort Pit after he had related his experience.
以下即兴诗作献给皮特堡（Fort Pit）一位被俘者的宴席上，在他讲述亲身经历之后。

===Original===
Sad memories it doth awake,
The death of those fell at Frog Lake,
And trials of captives of Fort Pit
When savages did capture it.
===
===Chinese===
它唤醒了悲伤的记忆，
那些在蛙湖（Frog Lake）罹难者之死，
以及皮特堡被俘者的磨难，
当野蛮人攻陷了那座堡垒。
===Original===
But soon Generals Strange and Steel
Made savage hordes their power to feel,
And they rescued women fair
From the paws of the Big Bear.
===
===Chinese===
但很快斯特兰奇将军（General Strange）和斯蒂尔将军（General Steel）
让野蛮部落尝到了他们的厉害，
他们将美丽的妇人们
从大熊（Big Bear）的爪牙下解救出来。
===Original===
Captives for days had naught to eat
But steaks of tough and lean dog meat,
In daily danger of their lives
From bullets and from scalping knives.
===
===Chinese===
被俘者连日来无物可食，
只有坚韧精瘦的狗肉排，
每日都在子弹与剥头皮刀下
冒着生命危险。
===Original===
When building big lodge for war dance,
The cry is heard, the troops advance,
To the white captives sounds so sweet,
But savages they quick retreat.
===
===Chinese===
当他们在建造大型战舞棚屋时，
喊杀声传来，军队在推进，
白人俘虏们听来如此悦耳，
而野蛮人则迅速溃退。
===Original===
A thrilling tale our guest did tell,
That close to him fell bursting shell,
This shot it was not fired in vain,
For several savages were slain.
===
===Chinese===
我们的客人讲述了一个惊心动魄的故事，
炮弹在他身旁炸响，
这一炮并非白费，
几个野蛮人被击毙。
===Original===
Our bold troops great danger braved,
So that white captives might be saved,
Who suffered hunger, cold and damp,
'Mong savage hordes in bush and swamp.
===
===Chinese===
我们勇敢的军队冒着重大的危险，
为了解救白人俘虏，
他们饱受饥寒与潮湿之苦，
在野蛮部落出没的丛林与沼泽间。
===Original===
Big Bear now they have pared his claws,
He must atone for broken laws,
Far away from his native lair,
In prison strong they put Big Bear.
===
===Chinese===
如今他们拔去了大熊的利爪，
他必须为违反法律而赎罪，
远离他故乡的巢穴，
他们将大熊关入坚固的牢狱。

## Province of Ontario / 安大略省

===Original===
In the land of woods and lakes,
Pure happiness each one partakes,
Who is sound in body and in mind,
And to industry is inclined.
===
===Chinese===
在这片林木与湖泊的土地上，
每个人都分享着纯粹的幸福，
只要身心健全，
且乐于勤勉劳作。
===Original===
Here in great lakes we do take pride,
And them with Uncle Sam divide,
Other lakes seem inferior
In size to great Superior.
===
===Chinese===
我们为这里的五大湖而骄傲，
与山姆大叔（Uncle Sam）共享它们，
其他湖泊相比之下似乎逊色，
论大小不及伟大的苏必利尔湖（Superior）。
===Original===
And Canadians do take pride,
In Huron's wide expanded tide,
But it onward flows forever,
Through St. Clair lake and river.
===
===Chinese===
加拿大人也引以为豪的，
是休伦湖（Huron）辽阔的水面，
它永不停歇地向前流淌，
穿过圣克莱尔湖（St. Clair）与圣克莱尔河。
===Original===
But soon again it doth expand,
Into Erie's lake so grand,
Then behold its wondrous charms,
When embraced in Niagara's arms.
===
===Chinese===
但不久它再度扩展开来，
涌入宏伟的伊利湖（Erie），
然后当你看到它的奇妙魅力，
当它被拥入尼亚加拉（Niagara）的怀抱。
===Original===
Then it more blessings doth bestow,
On pure bosom of Ontario,
Round it our towns and cities cluster,
O'er it Toronto sheds her lustre.
===
===Chinese===
然后它将更多的恩赐
倾洒在安大略湖（Ontario）纯洁的胸膛上，
我们的城镇环绕它聚簇，
多伦多（Toronto）在其上投射她的光辉。
===Original===
And Ontario doth awake,
The thought that 'tis our favorite lake;
Several states approach Lake Erie,
Each one claiming it for dearie.
===
===Chinese===
安大略湖令人想起，
它是我们最钟爱的湖泊；
若干州毗邻伊利湖，
每个都声称它是自己的珍宝。
===Original===
But our fires of love do glow,
Alone for Lake Ontario,
Our love for it is so unbounded,
We have almost it surrounded.
===
===Chinese===
但我们爱的火焰在燃烧，
只为安大略湖而燃烧，
我们对它的爱如此无边，
我们几乎将它环绕。
===Original===
And the lands around its beaches,
They are famed for grapes and peaches,
'Mong choicest fruits you ramble on
From Niagara to Hamilton.
===
===Chinese===
而环绕湖岸的土地，
以葡萄和蜜桃而闻名，
在最上等的果园间漫步，
从尼亚加拉到汉密尔顿（Hamilton）。
===Original===
Ontario North is land of pines,
A land of lakes and rocks and mines,
And beneath dark pine tree shade,
How happy is the youth and maid.
===
===Chinese===
安大略北部是松树之乡，
是湖泊、岩石与矿藏的国度，
在幽暗松林的荫蔽下，
少年与少女多么快乐。
===Original===
For here in summer you keep cool,
And fish for trout in sparkling pool,
For pike or salmon you can spear,
And in the season hunt the deer.
===
===Chinese===
因为这里夏日凉爽宜人，
在清澈的水潭中垂钓鳟鱼，
你可以叉到梭子鱼或鲑鱼，
在猎季还可以猎鹿。
===Original===
In great northern hunting ground,
Where both fish and game abound,
And verdant pastures here are seen,
Where cattle graze 'mong sweetest green.
===
===Chinese===
在广袤的北方猎场，
鱼类与猎物都很丰富，
这里可见翠绿的牧场，
牛群在最鲜嫩的青草间放牧。
===Original===
In the far north a land of pines,
And in the south we have the vines,
Where each year adds into the charms,
Surrounds the homesteads on the farms.
===
===Chinese===
远北是一片松林之地，
南方则有我们的葡萄藤，
每年它都增添更多魅力，
环绕着农场上的家园。
===Original===
Nature our province doth endow,
With hardy sons to guide the plow,
In south we have the fruitful soil,
Where nature's bounties on us smile.
===
===Chinese===
大自然赐予我们的省份
坚韧的儿女来驾驭犁铧，
南方有肥沃的土壤，
大自然的恩惠对我们微笑。
===Original===
We have got rich plains and highlands,
Ontario hath thousand islands,
And there is a great array
Of charming isles on Georgian Bay.
===
===Chinese===
我们拥有富饶的平原与高地，
安大略有千岛群岛，
在乔治亚湾（Georgian Bay）上
有一大片迷人的岛屿。
===Original===
And travellers all they do adore,
The lovely isles near Huron's shore,
Superior makes a grand display,
All round her shores to Thunder Bay.
===
===Chinese===
所有旅行者都对它们赞不绝口，
休伦湖岸边那些可爱的岛屿，
苏必利尔湖展现壮丽的景观，
环绕她沿岸直到桑德贝（Thunder Bay）。
===Original===
Muskoka's famed for woods and brakes,
For rocks and meadows and clear lakes,
And sportsmen for it proudly claim
That 'tis a land for fish and game.
===
===Chinese===
马斯科卡（Muskoka）以森林与密林闻名，
以岩石、草甸和清澈的湖泊著称，
运动爱好者们自豪地宣称
那里是鱼和猎物的天堂。
===Original===
There doth arise a sweet aroma
From great spruce forests of Algoma,
And from the poplar, birch and pine,
There too is wealth in many a mine.
===
===Chinese===
甜蜜的芳香飘来，
来自阿尔戈马（Algoma）广袤的云杉林，
还有白杨、桦树与松树，
那里也有许多矿藏的财富。
===Original===
It may be that of mines the best,
Will be found in Ontario West,
Stretching towards the interior,
Three hundred miles west of Superior.
===
===Chinese===
也许最优质的矿藏，
将被发现于安大略西部，
向内陆延伸，
在苏必利尔湖以西三百英里。
===Original===
Essex is our sunny south,
At the Detroit river's mouth,
There the sun doth cheerful smile
On the grape vineyards of Pelee Isle.
===
===Chinese===
埃塞克斯（Essex）是我们阳光明媚的南方，
在底特律河的入海口，
阳光在那里欢快地照耀
佩利岛（Pelee Isle）的葡萄园。
===Original===
Pioneer's axe it now doth ring,
On the shores of Nipissing,
And some do locate claims away
To distant north around James' Bay.
===
===Chinese===
拓荒者的斧头如今叮当作响，
在尼皮辛湖（Nipissing）的岸边，
有些人则将土地申领到远方，
到遥远北方的詹姆斯湾（James' Bay）周围。

## Niagara Dry / 干涸的尼亚加拉

===Original===
It happened once in early spring,
While there did float great thick ice cakes,
That then a gale did quickly bring
Them all down from the upper lakes.
===
===Chinese===
有一年早春时节，
巨大的厚冰块在漂浮，
一阵狂风迅速将它们
从上游的湖泊吹送而下。
===Original===
And from Buffalo to Lake Erie,
Across the entrance to river,
It was a scene of icebergs dreary,
Those who saw will remember ever.
===
===Chinese===
从布法罗（Buffalo）到伊利湖，
横跨河流的入口，
是一片凄凉的冰山景象，
目睹者将永生难忘。
===Original===
Then gale blew up lake and river,
And left Niagara almost dry,
This a lady did discover
As above the Falls she cast her eye.
===
===Chinese===
狂风横扫湖面与河流，
使尼亚加拉几乎干涸，
一位女士发现了这一奇景，
当她将目光投向瀑布上方时。
===Original===
Such scene it had been witnessed never,
Since Israelites crossed the Red Sea,
When they had resolved forever
From Pharaoh's bondage to flee.
===
===Chinese===
这样的景象从未有人见过，
自以色列人穿越红海以来，
当时他们决心永远
逃离法老的奴役。
===Original===
Lady she resolved to venture,
Proudly carrying British flag,
Erecting it in river's centre
In crevice of a rocky crag.
===
===Chinese===
这位女士决心冒险前行，
骄傲地举着英国国旗，
将它插在河流的中央，
立在岩石峭壁的缝隙里。
===Original===
It seems like a romance by Bulwer,
How she captured Niagara,
But it was seen by Bishop Fuller,
Who did at sight of flag hurrah.
===
===Chinese===
这好似布尔沃（Bulwer）笔下的传奇，
她如何"征服"了尼亚加拉，
但富勒主教（Bishop Fuller）亲眼目睹，
见到旗帜便欢呼雀跃。
===Original===
Ten thousand years may die away
Before another dry can tread,
In bottom of Niagara,
For she doth jealous guard her bed.
===
===Chinese===
也许万年之后才会消逝，
在此之前再无人能踏足
尼亚加拉的河床，
因为她严加守护着她的领地。
===Original===
But ice her entrance did blockade,
And wind it kept the waters back,
So that a child could almost wade
Across the brink of cataract.
===
===Chinese===
但冰封锁了她的入口，
风将水流阻挡回去，
以至于一个孩子几乎可以涉水
走过瀑布的边缘。

===Original===
Meditating o'er his sweet lays

While his fleecy flocks did graze.

===Chinese===
沉思吟咏他甜美的诗章，

绒毛羊群悠闲地牧放。

===Original===
His education it came late

After he reached to man's estate,

While his flocks were busy feeding

His favourite authors he was reading.

===Chinese===
他的学问来得迟缓，

待到成年方始开卷，

羊群忙于啃草觅食，

他却品读心仪的篇章。

===Original===
Wondrous tales he did rehearse

Of witches both in prose and verse,

And he in fairy tales did glory

Traditions of each shepherd's story.

===Chinese===
奇异故事他常常讲述，

散文与诗歌里的巫术，

他沉浸于童话世界，

传承着每位牧人的古老传说。

===Original===
The shepherd he sang late and early

Of the deeds of bold Prince Charley,

And how the charming bonnie Flora

Soothed the Prince when in his sorrow.

===Chinese===
这位牧人日夜吟唱，

勇敢的查理王子（Prince Charley）的事迹，

迷人的美丽弗洛拉（Flora），

如何在忧伤中抚慰王子。

===Original===
Few songs possess so much vigor

As his gathering of McGregor,

The lover's heart is all aflame

To meet lassie when the kye comes hame.

===Chinese===
很少有歌曲如此激昂，

像他的《麦格雷戈集结》，

恋人心中燃烧着火焰，

等候姑娘赶牛归家。

===Original===
But one who loved the shepherd's songs

And sang them unto countless throngs,

Great Kennedy has passed away

Now mournful is our Scottish lay.

===Chinese===
但有一位热爱牧人歌曲，

向无数人群高声歌唱的人，

伟大的肯尼迪（Kennedy）已经远去，

我们的苏格兰歌谣如今充满哀伤。

===Original===
## Highland Sketches / 高地素描

The Romans, Saxons and the Danes

Did oft o'er run the Scottish plains.

So daring were those mauraders

And skilful too were invaders.

===Chinese===
罗马人、撒克逊人与丹麦人，

曾频频蹂躏苏格兰平原。

那些掠夺者何等大胆，

入侵者亦技艺精湛。

===Original===
The lowland man enjoyed his farm,

But oft he was in great alarm,

When Highlanders o'er plain would sweep

And drive to hills his steers and sheep.

===Chinese===
低地农人安享他的农场，

却常陷入巨大的恐慌，

当高地人横扫平原，

将牛羊驱赶至山岗。

===Original===
For highlandmen were taught in song

The lowlands to them did belong,

Each highland chief he ruled like king

And Bards they did his praises sing.

===Chinese===
因为高地人在歌谣中被教导，

低地本就属于他们，

每位高地酋长如国王般统治，

吟游诗人为他唱起颂歌。

===Original===
In war the chief he led the van,

Marching to battle with his clan,

And when the foe attacked their chief

The clansmen rushed to his relief.

===Chinese===
战时酋长冲锋在前，

率领氏族奔赴战场，

当敌人攻击他们的酋长，

氏族成员奋勇上前救援。

===Original===
When they King William's forces mass

In Killicrankie's famous pass,

The highlanders with joy and glee

Rushed on them led by bold Dundee.

===Chinese===
当他们在基利克兰基（Killicrankie）著名的隘口，

集结威廉国王的大军，

高地人欢欣雀跃，

在勇敢的邓迪（Dundee）率领下发起冲锋。

===Original===
Lowland troops they would not tarry

But they strove to ford the Garry,

Soon many on the field lay dead,

In river floated many a head.

===Chinese===
低地军队不肯迟疑，

奋力渡过加里河（Garry），

很快许多人倒卧沙场，

河中漂浮着无数头颅。

===Original===
For clansmen with the good broad sword

Of battlefield they soon were Lord,

And historians will rank the

Chief highland victory of Killicrankie.

===Chinese===
氏族成员手持精良阔剑，

很快主宰了战场，

史家将铭记这场

基利克兰基高地大捷。

===Original===
Again there sweeps the highland clans,

Victorious at Preston Pans,

Under Prince Charlie full of hope,

They drove the troops were led by Cope.

===Chinese===
高地氏族再度横扫，

在普雷斯顿潘斯（Preston Pans）大获全胜，

在满怀希望的查理王子麾下，

他们击溃了科普（Cope）率领的军队。

===Original===
But from Culloden Charlie flies

While Highland blood the heather dyes,

For months he wandered 'mong the hills

Young Flora strove to soothe his ills.

===Chinese===
但查理从卡洛登（Culloden）仓皇逃走，

高地人的鲜血染红了石楠花，

他在山间徘徊数月，

年轻的弗洛拉竭力抚慰他的创痛。

===Original===
While he was hunted by blood hounds,

For sake of thirty thousand pounds,

But Highlanders the gold despise,

And honor only each doth prize.

===Chinese===
当他被猎犬追缉，

为那三万英镑的悬赏，

但高地人鄙弃黄金，

唯独珍视荣誉。

===Original===
At last to Charlie's great delight,

From France a vessel hove in sight,

And safely bore the gallant Prince

To the sunny shores of France.

===Chinese===
终于令查理欣喜若狂，

一艘法国船只出现在视野，

将英勇的王子安然送达

阳光明媚的法兰西海岸。

===Original===
And may once more each hill and glen

Be filled with hardy Highland men,

Who love bagpipes and bonnets blue

And give to Britain soldiers true.

===Chinese===
愿每一座山丘与峡谷，

再次充满刚毅的高地人，

他们热爱风笛与蓝色便帽，

为不列颠输送真正的战士。


===Original===
## Castle Gordon / 戈登堡

In youth we spent a pleasant day

Round Castle Gordon on the Spey,

There is no Clan can gird the sword on,

Can compare with the Clan Gordon,

In India, China and Sudan,

They manfully have led the van.

===Chinese===
少年时我们度过愉快的一天，

在斯佩河（Spey）畔的戈登堡（Castle Gordon）周围，

没有哪个氏族能佩剑比肩，

比得上戈登氏族，

在印度、中国和苏丹，

他们英勇地冲锋在前。

===Original===
## River Clyde / 克莱德河

The Glasgow people do take pride

In their river both deep and wide,

In early times the youth and maid

Did o'er its shallow waters wade.

===Chinese===
格拉斯哥人为他们

又深又宽的河流感到骄傲，

早年间少男少女

曾在它浅浅的水域中涉水而过。

===Original===
But city money did not grudge,

And dug it deep with the steam dredge,

And now proudly on its bosom floats

The mighty ships and great steamboats.

===Chinese===
但城市不惜出资，

用蒸汽挖泥船将它挖深，

如今骄傲地在它胸膛上漂浮着

巨大的船舶和宏伟的蒸汽船。

===Original===
No wonder citizens take pride

For they themselves have made the Clyde,

Great and navigable river,

Where huge fleets will float forever.

===Chinese===
难怪市民们引以为豪，

因为他们亲手缔造了克莱德河，

这条伟大而可通航的河流，

庞大的船队将永远在它之上航行。

===Original===
Dunbarton's lofty castle rock

Which oft' has stood the battle's shock,

The river it doth boldly guard,

So industry may reap reward.

===Chinese===
邓巴顿（Dunbarton）高耸的城堡岩，

屡经战火考验屹立不倒，

它勇敢地守卫着河流，

使勤劳得以收获回报。

===Original===
But more protection still they deem

Is yet required so down the stream

Strong batteries are erected,

So commerce may be safe protected.

===Chinese===
但他们认为仍需更多防护，

于是在下游建起强大的炮台，

使商贸得到安全的保障。

===Original===
Old ocean now he doth take pride

To see upon his bosom ride

The commerce of his youngest bride,

The fair and lovely charming Clyde.

===Chinese===
老海洋如今也引以为豪，

看着他最年轻的新娘之上

承载着商贸的繁荣——

那美丽迷人的克莱德河。

===Original===
## Lofty Actors Withdraw / 巨人退场

As one by one the lofty actors of the age

Withdraw from clianging play on history's page,

The act of war and peace of old and new contending

For it is long ere there's harmonious blending.

===Chinese===
当时代的巨人一个接一个，

从历史舞台上铿锵的表演中退场，

战争与和平交替上演，新旧势力相争，

要达到和谐融合尚需漫长的时光。

===Original===
And many a noble actor brave and bold

Hath perished in the fight between the new and old,

The victor and the vanquished Lincoln and Lee,

The former he four million slaves set free.

===Chinese===
许多高贵而勇敢的演员

在新旧之争中殒命，

胜利者与失败者——林肯（Lincoln）和李（Lee），

前者解放了四百万奴隶。

===Original===
The latter General fought with bravery and science,

The first he on the Lord placed strong reliance,

And in the justice of his cause he bade the North

As grand emancipators they should issue forth.

===Chinese===
后者以英勇和智谋作战，

前者则深深仰赖上帝，

他坚信自己事业的正义，号召北方

以伟大解放者的姿态奋勇出击。

===Original===
And o'er great North the conquering name of Grant

His mighty deeds of valour they 'ere more will chant,

And now doth pass from stage this last named actor,

In crushing Southern slavery potent factor.

===Chinese===
在伟大的北方之上，格兰特（Grant）的名字凯旋传扬，

他的英勇壮举将被永远传唱，

如今这位最后提及的演员也已退场，

他是粉碎南方奴隶制的重要力量。


===Original===
## United States / 美利坚合众国

The United States is wondrous land,

Within its mighty borders grand,

They are a world unto themselves,

In South the Negro plows and delves.

===Chinese===
美利坚是一片神奇的土地，

在它辽阔宏伟的疆域之内，

他们自成一个世界，

在南方，黑人耕作挖掘。

===Original===
Raising tobacco, sugar, cotton,

Grapes and rice too may be gotten,

The west for corn, cattle and swine,

On which millions o'er the world dine.

===Chinese===
种植烟草、甘蔗、棉花，

葡萄和稻米亦可收获，

西部盛产玉米、牛和猪，

供养着全世界数百万人的餐桌。

===Original===
The silver hills of Colorado,

Equaled only by Nevada,

And o'er the world you can't behold

Aught like California's mines of gold.

===Chinese===
科罗拉多（Colorado）的银山，

唯有内华达（Nevada）可与之匹敌，

走遍全世界你都无法看到

能比得上加利福尼亚金矿的景象。

===Original===
To vast extent her exports reaches,

Of grapes and oranges and peaches,

And Florida the land of flowers

Is famous for its orange bowers.

===Chinese===
她的出口远达四方，

葡萄、柑橘和桃子，

而佛罗里达这鲜花之邦，

以柑橘林闻名遐迩。

===Original===
Pennsylvania's hills environ

Wondrous mines of coal and iron,

Great marvel of these modern days,

All you require yourselves could raise.

===Chinese===
宾夕法尼亚（Pennsylvania）群山环绕，

蕴藏着神奇的煤铁矿藏，

近代伟大的奇迹，

你所需的一切都能自行生产。

===Original===
But still Canadians are content

With their own half of continent,

Though Uncle Sam is most wealthy,

Canada's content and healthy.

===Chinese===
但加拿大人依然满足于

自己拥有的半壁大陆，

尽管山姆大叔（Uncle Sam）最为富有，

加拿大却安乐而健康。

===Original===
Sam and her are blood relation,

Sprung alike from British nation,

May peaceful odes alone be sung

'Tween people of one race and tongue.

===Chinese===
山姆与她是血脉至亲，

同出不列颠一族，

但愿同宗同语的民族之间，

只吟唱和平的颂歌。

===Original===
When Canada's ten million strong,

Then none will dare to do her wrong,

Either as a youthful nation

Or in Imperial Federation.

===Chinese===
当加拿大人口达到千万之众，

便无人胆敢欺凌她，

无论是作为年轻的国度，

还是在帝国联邦之中。

===Original===
## Caledonia Springs, New York / 纽约卡里多尼亚泉

The water spouts up from the ground

And there doth form a pond around,

'Tis fed from no apparent rills,

And it near by drives several mills.

===Chinese===
泉水从地面喷涌而出，

在周围形成一个池塘，

不见任何明显的溪流汇入，

它却在附近驱动着几座磨坊。

===Original===
And here the little ponds are seen

Where fish are propagate by Green,

Fish hatcheries here had their birth,

And now they're spread o'er all the earth.

===Chinese===
这里可以看见若干小池塘，

格林（Green）在此繁殖鱼类，

鱼类孵化场诞生于此，

如今已遍布全世界。

===Original===
Here in each pond thousands of trout

Rush around their circular route,

Of various breeds, age and size,

Speckled o'er with various dyes.

===Chinese===
每个池塘里成千上万的鳟鱼，

沿着环形水道游弋，

品种、年龄和大小各异，

身上点缀着斑驳的色彩。

===Original===
Geologists do gaze with pride

On specimens all petrified,

Here church is built of those rare stone,

Fish, reptiles, birds, in days bygone.

===Chinese===
地质学家骄傲地凝视着，

那些完全石化的标本，

一座教堂就用这些珍贵的石头建成——

远古时代的鱼、爬行动物和飞鸟。

===Original===
Strange thoughts in mind it doth awake,

As it is far from sea and lake,

Even the hill tops do abound

With proofs here flowed the sea profound.

===Chinese===
它在心中唤起奇妙的思绪，

此地远离大海和湖泊，

就连山顶上也遍布证据，

证明这里曾是深邃的海洋。

===Original===
With reverence we gaze around

On an ancient burial ground,

Those who once did tread the heather

Now rest quietly here together.

===Chinese===
我们怀着崇敬环顾四周，

这是一片古老的墓地，

那些曾经踏过石楠花的人，

如今在这里静静安息在一起。


===Original===
## Local Sketches / 乡土素描

On grassy amphitheatre,

Spectators sit to view the war,

'Mong bold contestants on the plain

Where each doth strive the prize to gain.

===Chinese===
在绿草如茵的圆形剧场，

观众端坐观看竞技，

勇士们在平原上较量，

各自奋力争夺奖赏。

===Original===
Come witness the great tug of war,

And see great hammer thrown afar,

See running, jumping, highland fling,

At concert hear the skylark sing.

===Chinese===
来看壮观的拔河比赛，

看大铁锤被远远抛出，

看赛跑、跳远、高地掷木棒，

在音乐会上听云雀歌唱。

===Original===
And the bagpipes will send thrills,

Like echoes from the distant hills,

And the bold sound of the pibroch,

Which does resound o'er highland loch.

===Chinese===
风笛送来阵阵颤栗，

如远处山峦的回声，

还有皮布鲁赫（Pibroch）雄壮的声响，

在高地上空的湖泊上回荡。

===Original===
Young men and maids and fine old dames

Will gather on the banks of Thames,

And though we have a tug of war

'Twill leave no wound or deadly scar.

===Chinese===
年轻人、少女和优雅的老太太们，

将聚集在泰晤士河（Thames）畔，

虽然我们也有拔河之争，

却不会留下伤口或致命的疤痕。

===Original===
## Brain Engravings / 大脑的铭文

Great wonder is the human brain,

How it impressions doth retain,

Inscribed on it are autographs,

And there is also photographs.

===Chinese===
人脑是多么伟大的奇迹，

它如何铭记所受的印象，

上面铭刻着亲笔签名，

还有照片般的影像。

===Original===
And every hill and plain and nook,

It is deep graven in this book,

A great variety here belongs,

Snatches of sermons and of songs.

===Chinese===
每一座山丘、平原和角落，

都深深地刻在这本书中，

这里包罗万象，

有布道的片段和歌曲的片段。

===Original===
Here you'll find are numerous themes,

Both mighty thoughts and foolish dreams,

Here love and hope so bright and fair,

There hate and doubt and dark despair.

===Chinese===
你会发现无数的主题，

有伟大的思想也有愚蠢的梦，

这里有爱与希望，明亮而美好，

那里有恨与怀疑，还有黑暗的绝望。

===Original===
And here is too the bower of bliss,

Where youthful lovers first did kiss,

Here are memories of childhood

And of old ages thoughtful mood.

===Chinese===
这里还有幸福的凉亭，

年轻恋人初次亲吻的地方，

这里有童年的记忆，

也有暮年深沉的思绪。

===Original===
View well the whole, 'tis a strange sight,

Both of suffering and delight,

You see the parting with old friend,

And where new hearts with yours do blend.

===Chinese===
仔细审视这全部，真是奇异的景象，

既有苦难也有欢欣，

你看到与旧友的离别，

也看到新的心灵与你交融。

===Original===
Greatest blessing, hope of heaven,

For our comfort it is given,

Indented deep in mind of wise

Are glories of the heavenly prize.

===Chinese===
最伟大的祝福，天堂的希望，

为我们的慰藉而赐予，

深深地铭刻在智者的心中，

是那天国奖赏的荣耀。

===Original===
## Survival of the Fittest / 适者生存

Now let the hero of our song,

Be he who gentle treats the throng,

And would not cruel treat another,

But to each be as to a brother.

===Chinese===
现在让我们歌中的英雄，

是善待众生的人，

不会残忍地对待他人，

而对每个人都如兄弟一般。

===Original===
And he must have both sense and wit,

And be possessed of strength and grit,

Then strong as proof of holy writ,

For to survive he is most fit.

===Chinese===
他必须兼具理智与机智，

拥有力量与毅力，

如此方能如圣典所示般坚强，

最适于在这世上生存。

===Original===
And according to our test,

The fittest only is the best,

These have a right for to survive,

And well they do deserve to thrive.

===Chinese===
按照我们的标准，

唯有适者才是最好的，

他们有权存活于世，

也理应蓬勃发展。

===Original===
And this kind of evolution

It will bring no revolution,

But revolve in Christian sphere,

Where scripture truths are prized and dear.

===Chinese===
这种类型的进化，

不会引发革命，

而是在基督教的领域中运转，

在那里圣经真理被珍视和看重。

===Original===
Give us the man doth persevere,

And presses on in his career,

Undaunted struggling for the right,

Though all mankind 'gainst him unite.

===Chinese===
给我们那个百折不挠的人，

他在事业上不断奋进，

无畏地为正义而斗争，

纵使全人类联合起来反对他。

===Original===
Though now on top of highest mount,

Where he has found true honour's fount,

Yet those below he don't despise,

But strives to aid them for to rise.

===Chinese===
虽然他已站在最高峰顶，

在那里找到了真正荣耀的源泉，

但他不蔑视下面的人，

而是努力帮助他们向上攀登。


===Original===
## Motto / 座右铭

Politeness, perseverance and pluck,

To their possessor will bring good luck.

===Chinese===
礼貌、毅力与勇气，

将为它们的拥有者带来好运。

===Original===
## Things Should Be Judged by Merit / 事物当以价值论

A picture hung in a public hall,

And it was much admired by all,

Painted by a true artist's hand,

The subject it was truly grand.

===Chinese===
一幅画悬挂在公共大厅，

深受所有人的赞赏，

出自真正艺术家之手，

题材确实宏伟壮丽。

===Original===
Its fame o'er the whole world resounds,

Valued at ten thousand pounds,

Beauteous lady none 'ere passed her,

She was the work of an old master.

===Chinese===
它的名声传遍全世界，

估值一万英镑，

这位美丽的女士无人能出其右，

它是古老大师的作品。

===Original===
At last a critic keen did gaze

And saw 'twas work of modern days,

Then quick it was pronounced a daub,

And artist but a money grab.

===Chinese===
最后一位敏锐的批评家凝视审视，

看出这是近代的作品，

于是很快便被贬为拙劣之作，

画家不过是个敛财之徒。

===Original===
The true, the noble and the grand,

Will lend to struggling helping hand,

Then let no man of dues be shorn,

If he a subject doth adorn.

===Chinese===
真正的、高贵的、伟大的事物，

会向挣扎者伸出援手，

那么不要亏待任何人，

只要他装点了题材。

===Original===
## The Gates Ajar / 半掩的门

A good kind man who knew no malice,

Happy with wife and daughter Alice,

More precious far to him than gold,

His little darling six years old.

===Chinese===
一位善良的好人，从无恶意，

与妻子和女儿爱丽丝（Alice）幸福生活，

女儿对他远比黄金更珍贵，

他那六岁的小宝贝。

===Original===
True nobleman with many friends,

His career too soon it ends,

The casket friends enshrined with flowers,

While soul had fled to heavenly bowers.

===Chinese===
一位真正的贵族，朋友众多，

他的生涯过早地结束了，

朋友们用鲜花装殓了灵柩，

而他的灵魂已飞向天堂的花园。

===Original===
The wreaths were lovely, but the star,

Admired by all was gates ajar,

The widow led her little girl

To where death his dart did hurl.

===Chinese===
花圈很美，但那颗星——

令所有人赞叹的是半掩的门，

寡妇领着她的小女儿，

来到死亡投射利箭的地方。

===Original===
And stricken her poor father down,

But child exclaimed he's won the crown,

And he will watch for me afar,

And keep for me the gates ajar.

===Chinese===
它击倒了她可怜的父亲，

但孩子欢呼他已经赢得了冠冕，

他会在远方为我守望，

为我保持那半掩的门。

===Original===
And when we cross the crystal fount,

He will point out the heavenly mount,

Here neither sun nor moon doth shine,

Lighted with radiance all divine.

===Chinese===
当我们跨过水晶的泉源，

他会指给我看天堂的山，

这里既无日也无月，

全靠神圣的光辉照耀。

===Original===
For I know well for me he'll wait

Anxious at the pearly gate,

For I would fear to view alone

The glories of the heavenly throne.

===Chinese===
因为我知道他会为我等待，

在珍珠门前焦急守候，

因为我害怕独自面对

天堂宝座的辉煌。

===Original===
Pa will admit his little Alice

Safe into the heavenly palace,

And glories to me will unfold

As we tread the streets of gold.

===Chinese===
爸爸会迎接他的小爱丽丝，

安全进入天堂的宫殿，

为我展开荣耀的画卷，

当我们踏上黄金铺就的街道。


===Original===
## Child Made Happy / 获得快乐的孩子

In a great city hospital

There lay poor Mary Crosby small,

She had no friends her heart to cheer,

So time with her passed sad and drear.

===Chinese===
在一家大城市的医院里，

躺着可怜的小玛丽·克罗斯比（Mary Crosby），

她没有朋友来宽慰心灵，

日子在悲伤凄凉中度过。

===Original===
She sought for ease but all in vain,

Month after month she passed in pain,

She had no relative nor friend

Who aid or comfort could her lend.

===Chinese===
她寻求安适却徒然无果，

月复一月在痛苦中度过，

她没有亲人也没有朋友，

无人能给她援助或安慰。

===Original===
A surgeon saw her cheerless state,

And deplored the poor child's fate,

She tried to make doll of her finger,

And sang to it poor little singer.

===Chinese===
一位外科医生看到她凄惨的状况，

怜悯这可怜孩子的命运，

她试着把手指当作娃娃，

对着它唱歌，可怜的小歌手。

===Original===
Hers indeed was an awful lot,

The weary days she spent in cot,

For the poor child she could not walk,

And it soon exhausted her to talk.

===Chinese===
她的命运确实可怖，

在小床上度过疲惫的日子，

这可怜的孩子不能走路，

说几句话便很快精疲力竭。

===Original===
But surgeon bought her ribbon gay,

And with it she all day did play,

The giver often she did bless,

And thought sometimes she was princess.

===Chinese===
但外科医生给她买了鲜艳的丝带，

她整天拿着它玩耍，

她常常祝福赠送者，

有时幻想自己是公主。

===Original===
For in it she did take such pride,

She fancied she was beauteous bride,

And was possessed of great riches,

Or thought herself a wealthy Duchess.

===Chinese===
她对此如此引以为豪，

幻想自己是美丽的新娘，

拥有巨大的财富，

或觉得自己是一位富有的公爵夫人。

===Original===
And she would bind it round her hair,

Imagining that she was fair.

But poor child feels that she must die,

She asks the surgeon to come nigh.

===Chinese===
她把丝带系在发间，

想象自己有多么美丽。

但可怜的孩子觉得自己要死了，

她请医生走近身旁。

===Original===
And kindly o'er her he doth stand,

She asked him for to take her hand,

Thanked him for ribbon green and blue,

Then evermore bade him adieu.

===Chinese===
他慈祥地站在她身旁，

她请他握住她的手，

感谢他送的蓝绿丝带，

然后永远地与他告别。

===Original===
## Power of Love / 爱的力量

Love it is the precious loom,

Whose shuttle weaves each tangled thread,

And works flowers of exquisite bloom,

Shedding their perfume where we tread.

===Chinese===
爱是那珍贵的织机，

它的梭子穿梭编织每根缠绕的丝线，

织出精致绽放的花朵，

在我们行走之处洒落芬芳。

===Original===
## Her Lover's Step / 恋人的脚步

Step, step, step, 'tis her lover's walk,

She knows his step as well's his talk;

He is the favorite of her choice,

So his step's familiar as his voice.

===Chinese===
脚步、脚步、脚步，那是恋人的步履，

她熟悉他的脚步如同熟悉他的言语；

他是她心仪的人，

所以他的脚步和声音一样熟悉。

===Original===
Step, step, step, she now is wed,

And it is now her husband's tread;

His homeward step it cheers her life,

For she is a kind faithful wife.

===Chinese===
脚步、脚步、脚步，她如今已婚，

那已成为她丈夫的脚步；

他归家的脚步鼓舞她的生命，

因为她是一位贤惠忠诚的妻子。

===Original===
But he the husband and yet lover,

His steps at last do cease forever;

And she doth soon hear the tread

Of men who do bear out the dead.

===Chinese===
但他既是丈夫也是恋人，

他的脚步终于永远停歇；

她很快听到那样的步伐——

那是人们抬着死者走过。

===Original===
Her heart it now doth throb with pain,

Though she knows sorrow is but vain;

For him she never can recall,

And no more hear his footsteps fall.

===Chinese===
她的心因痛苦而阵阵悸动，

虽然她知道悲伤无济于事；

她永远无法将他唤回，

再也不会听到他的脚步声。

===Original===
But still she hopes he yet will come

And visit her in their old home;

But time approaches, she must die,

Her husband's footsteps she hears nigh.

===Chinese===
但她仍然希望他会回来，

回到他们的老屋探望她；

但大限将至，她必须死去，

她听到丈夫的脚步声渐渐靠近。

===Original===
Step, step, step, we ne'er shall part,

I hear the echo in my heart;

Now happiness dispels the gloom,

Radiant with joy my face doth bloom.

===Chinese===
脚步、脚步、脚步，我们永不分离，

我听到回声在我心中回响；

如今幸福驱散了阴霾，

我的脸庞绽放出喜悦的光彩。

===Original===
Pain and suffering all are past,

She joyous cried he's come at last;

And soon she breathes out her last breath,

He guides her through the vale of death.

===Chinese===
痛苦与磨难都已过去，

她欢喜地叫道他终于来了；

她很快呼出最后一口气，

他引领她走过死亡的幽谷。


===Original===
## The Useful Weed / 有用的杂草

Do not despise the humble weed,

For the Lord He first sowed the seed,

Perhaps it bears most precious fruit,

And useful leaves and potent root.

===Chinese===
不要鄙视卑微的杂草，

因为是主先播下了种子，

也许它结出最珍贵的果实，

还有有用的叶子和强效的根。

===Original===
Though it seems now a useless weed,

Countless millions it yet may feed,

Or future ages it may prize,

Finding in it beauteous dyes.

===Chinese===
虽然它现在看似无用的杂草，

将来或许能养活无数的人，

或许未来的世代会珍视它，

从中找到美丽的染料。

===Original===
Or a valued healing balm,

Will make the heated pulse beat calm,

And the future men of science,

May place on it strong reliance.

===Chinese===
或是一种珍贵的疗愈药膏，

让发热的脉搏平缓跳动，

未来的科学家们，

或许会对它寄予厚望。

===Original===
And it may play important part,

In advancing skill and art,

And no person now doth know

How useful are the weeds that grow.

===Chinese===
它可能在推动技艺和艺术方面，

发挥重要的作用，

如今没有人知道，

生长的杂草究竟有多少用处。

===Original===
Weeds we now look on with loathing,

They may yet be used for clothing,

Producing silken glossy coat,

Or paper fine for the bank note.

===Chinese===
我们如今厌恶地看待杂草，

它们将来或许可以用来制衣，

产出丝般光泽的外衣，

或是印制钞票的精美纸张。

===Original===
But you at present must take heed,

And do not grow the cumbrous weed,

Either in garden or in field,

Where plants with profit now do yield.

===Chinese===
但你现在必须留心，

不要让累赘的杂草生长，

无论在花园还是田地，

那里如今长着有益的作物。

===Original===
## Golden Egg / 金蛋

In ancient times we have been told

A goose did lay an egg of gold,

She did produce one every day,

So regular this goose did lay.

===Chinese===
古人告诉我们，

有只鹅下了一枚金蛋，

她每天下一枚，

下蛋如此准时有规律。

===Original===
But her stupid foolish master

Wanted her to lay them faster,

And he at last the goose did kill,

Gold grist no more came to his mill.

===Chinese===
但她愚蠢的主人，

想让她下蛋更快些，

最终他把鹅杀了，

金子再也不会流入他的磨坊。

===Original===
But a strange tale we now unfold,

In California's mines of gold,

There they keep both hens and chickens,

'Mong the gravel scratching pickings.

===Chinese===
但我们现在讲一个奇闻，

在加利福尼亚的金矿里，

他们养着母鸡和小鸡，

在砾石中刨食觅食。

===Original===
But hens do find the golden shiner,

Is too heavy for their dinner,

For it they cannot well digest,

As it lies solid in their breast.

===Chinese===
但母鸡们发现那闪亮的金粒，

太重不适合当晚餐，

它们无法很好地消化，

因为金粒沉甸甸地留在胸腔里。

===Original===
Then they are slain and you behold

In their craw the shining gold,

Made up of particles so fine,

The purest gold in all the mine.

===Chinese===
于是它们被宰杀，你会看到

在它们的嗉囊里闪亮的黄金，

由极其细微的颗粒组成，

是矿中最纯净的金子。

===Original===
Then how happy is the miner,

When he has sweet fowl for dinner,

And he doth find within its craw,

A little golden bonanza.

===Chinese===
矿工是多么快乐啊，

当他有美味的禽肉当晚餐，

还在它的嗉囊中发现，

一小笔金色的财富。

===Original===
And in Ontario the hen

Is worthy of the poet's pen,

For she doth well deserve the praise

Bestowed on her for her fine lays.

===Chinese===
而在安大略，母鸡

值得诗人挥毫书写，

她确实配得上人们

因她优秀的产蛋而给予的赞美。


===Original===
## Little Dora / 小朵拉

I tell you what my little Dora,

You do cause my heart to sorrow,

Tell me now you little misses

What you do with all your kisses.

===Chinese===
我告诉你啊小朵拉，

你真让我心里难过，

现在告诉我你这小丫头，

你把那些吻都用在了哪里。

===Original===
I see you get them by the dozen

From each aunt and little cousin,

Said she I do intend dear pa

To give them all to you and ma.

===Chinese===
我看你从每个阿姨和小表亲那里，

一下子就能得到一打吻，

她说亲爱的爸爸我打算，

把它们全部送给你和妈妈。

===Original===
And not a single one I'll miss,

But I will give you back each kiss,

And both of you will feel you're blest

When I pay you interest.

===Chinese===
一个吻我也不会少，

我会把每个吻都还给你们，

当我还你们利息的时候，

你们俩都会感到幸福。

===Original===
You'll better in my bank invest

And put my principle to test,

And you can dividend partake,

For my bank will never break.

===Chinese===
你们最好在我的银行投资，

考验我的本金吧，

你们可以分享红利，

因为我的银行永远不会倒闭。

===Original===
## Lines on the Death of a Farmer's Wife / 悼农夫之妻

This good woman when in this life,

She was kind mother and good wife,

And managed her household with care,

She and her husband happy pair.

===Chinese===
这位善良的女人在世时，

是慈祥的母亲和贤惠的妻子，

用心管理着她的家，

她和丈夫是一对幸福的伴侣。

===Original===
And her name it will long be praised

By the large family she has raised,

She laid up treasures in the skies,

And now enjoys the Heavenly prize.

===Chinese===
她的名字将长久被赞美，

被她养育的大家庭，

她在天上积攒了财宝，

如今享受着天国的奖赏。

===Original===
She rose each morn with happy smile,

And ardent all the day did toil,

For work it to her had a charm,

And busy was each hand and arm.

===Chinese===
她每天清晨带着愉快的微笑起身，

热情地劳作一整天，

工作对她有一种魅力，

双手和双臂总是忙碌不停。

===Original===
## Fighting for Home / 为家而战
