A hawk while soaring on the wing,

O'er a tiny sparkling spring,

Beheld a sleek and beauteous mink,

Was enjoying a bath and drink.

===Chinese===
一只鹰在空中翱翔，

飞过一处闪烁的小泉，

看见一只光滑美丽的水貂，

正在沐浴畅饮。

===Original===
And though the hawk was bent on slaughter

The mink was more at home on water,

And it is strange this curious quarrel

All occurred in a sunk barrel.

===Chinese===
虽然鹰一心要杀戮，

水貂在水中却更为自在，

而这场奇特的争端

竟发生在一口沉没的桶中，实在奇怪。

===Original===
In the Township of Nissouri,

There the hawk it came to sorrow,

But it strove often for to sink,

In vain it strove to drown the mink,

===Chinese===
在尼索里（Nissouri）镇，

鹰在那里遭遇了不幸，

它不断试图把水貂按入水中，

却徒劳无功无法淹死水貂。

===Original===
But mink it did successful balk,

All the attacks were made by hawk,

The bird was drenched, it could not fly,

And ne'er again it soared on high.

===Chinese===
但水貂成功地挫败了

鹰的所有攻击，

鸟被淋得透湿，无法飞翔，

再也不能高高翱翔。

===Original===
## Every Rose Hath Its Thorn / 每朵玫瑰都有刺

There was a maiden all forlorn,

She loved a youth, his name was Thorn,

But he was shy for to disclose

How he loved dear the sweet May Rose.

===Chinese===
有一位姑娘孤苦伶仃，

她爱上一个青年，名叫索恩（Thorn），

但他羞于表白，

他多么深爱甜美的五月玫瑰。

===Original===
Lustre sweet it would give to Thorn,

If this fair flower would it adorn,

Said he all other names above

Your charming name alone I love.

===Chinese===
如果这朵美丽的花能为索恩增光，

他说超越所有其他名字，

我只爱你迷人的名字。

===Original===
Said she of beauty 'tis soon shorn,

Unless that it is joined to Thorn,

It very soon doth droop and die,

And she heaved a gentle sigh.

===Chinese===
她说美丽很快就会凋零，

除非与索恩结合在一起，

它很快就会枯萎死去，

她轻轻叹了口气。

===Original===
Said he we'll wed to-morrow morn,

No more from me you shall be torn,

For you will banish all my woes,

And near my heart I'll wear the rose.

===Chinese===
他说我们明天早上就结婚，

你再也不会与我分离，

因为你会驱散我所有的忧愁，

我会把玫瑰戴在心口旁。

===Original===
Now little rose buds they are born,

All clinging to the parent Thorn,

In grace and beauty each one grows,

Full worthy of the sweet May Rose.

===Chinese===
如今小玫瑰花蕾诞生了，

依偎着它们的母株索恩，

每一朵都优雅而美丽地成长，

配得上甜美的五月玫瑰。

===Original===
Some flowers they only shed their bloom

In the sweet month of leafy June,

But May doth bloom each month in year

A fragrant Rose forever dear.

===Chinese===
有些花只在

绿叶繁茂的六月绽放，

但五月的花每月都在盛开，

一朵芬芳的玫瑰永远珍贵。


===Original===
## Danger of Fire Arms / 火器之险

For to save life one great solver

Would be to prohibit the revolver,

Weapon of coward and of bully,

Who slaughter friends in their folly.

===Chinese===
要挽救生命，一个绝佳的解决办法

就是禁止左轮手枪，

懦夫和恶霸的武器，

他们在愚蠢中屠杀朋友。

===Original===
Let now no man or any boy,

With loaded arms ever toy,

Showing off their manly vigor,

Pointing to friend and pulling trigger.

===Chinese===
从今以后不要任何人或男孩，

拿装了弹药的枪当玩具，

炫耀他们的男子气概，

对着朋友瞄准然后扣动扳机。

===Original===
And sending bullet through their brain,

And then exclaim in mournful strain,

When friends with grief they are goaded,

I did not know that it was loaded.

===Chinese===
子弹穿过他们的大脑，

然后哀伤地惊呼，

当朋友被悲伤刺痛时，

我不知道枪是上了膛的。

===Original===
Fire arms oft' times do bring woes,

And they kill more friends than foes,

Hunting now o'er fertile fields,

'Tis seldom that it profit yields.

===Chinese===
火器常常带来灾祸，

杀死的朋友比敌人更多，

如今在肥沃的田野上狩猎，

很少能带来什么收益。

===Original===
## Bird Sent by Providence / 天意遣来的鸟

A poor man stood beside his door,

His sad fate for to deplore,

For landlord's heart would not relent,

And seized his furniture for rent.

===Chinese===
一个穷人站在自家门前，

哀叹他悲惨的命运，

因为房东铁石心肠，

为租金扣押了他的家具。

===Original===
He hears song sweet as from fairy,

And soon he sees a canary,

Into his cage it did alight

And poured forth notes sweet and bright.

===Chinese===
他听到甜美的歌声宛如仙境，

很快看到一只金丝雀，

飞进他的笼子，

发出甜脆明亮的鸣叫。

===Original===
But owner of the bird did mourn,

And sadly longed for its return,

Without it she found no delight,

So she did landlord's bill requite.

===Chinese===
但鸟的主人悲伤哀叹，

苦苦期盼它的归来，

没有它她找不到快乐，

于是她替房东付清了账单。

===Original===
The poor man thinks the bird was sent

By the Lord to pay up his rent,

And he now stout maintains from thence

That there is a kind Providence.

===Chinese===
穷人认为这只鸟是

主派来替他付房租的，

从此他坚定地主张，

有一个仁慈的天意存在。

===Original===
## Help in Need / 困境中的帮助

A poor man's horse it ran away,

Soon man upon the roadside lay,

With his leg all badly broken,

Of sympathy some gave token.

===Chinese===
一个穷人的马跑了，

很快人躺在路边，

腿严重骨折，

有人表示了同情。

===Original===
One said your trouble grieves my heart,

But with his money would not part,

Another said, while heaving sighs,

It brings the tears into mine eyes.

===Chinese===
一个说你的痛苦让我心碎，

但不愿掏出他的钱，

另一个一边叹气一边说，

这让我流泪了。

===Original===
But a good true hearted man,

His heart with kindness it o'er ran,

The poorest man among the three,

A pound he did contribute free.

===Chinese===
但一个善良真诚的人，

他的心充满了善意，

他是三人中最穷的那个，

却慷慨地捐出了一英镑。

===Original===
Others gave in empty feeling,

But this poor man he did bring healing,

The giver only Lord doth prize,

Who helps afflicted for to rise.

===Chinese===
其他人只是空洞地表达同情，

但这个穷人却带来了真正的治愈，

主只珍视那些帮助受苦者崛起的施予者。


===Original===
## Lines on the Finding of a Young Man's Body in Toronto Bay / 多伦多湾发现年轻男子遗体有感

His identity was discovered by finding the maker's name on the suit he wore and by sending a strip of the cloth to the maker in Montreal.

===Chinese===
通过在他所穿衣服上找到制造商的名字，并将一块布料寄给蒙特利尔的制造商，确认了他的身份。

===Original===
A young man's body long it lay

In bottom of Toronto Bay,

But at last the waters bore,

And raised him up near to the shore.

===Chinese===
一具年轻人的遗体长久地躺在

多伦多湾的底部，

但最终海水将他托起，

将他推到了靠近岸边的地方。

===Original===
But no one knew his rank or station,

No one knew his home or nation,

But his form and dress were genteel,

And sorrow many they did feel.

===Chinese===
但无人知道他的身份地位，

无人知道他的家乡或国籍，

但他的体态和衣着都很讲究，

许多人都感到悲痛。

===Original===
Kind man took charge of the remains,

And was well rewarded for his pains,

So skilful he did him embalm,

Restored the features sweet and calm.

===Chinese===
一位善心人负责处理遗体，

他的辛劳得到了丰厚的回报，

他技艺高超地为遗体做了防腐处理，

恢复了安详甜美的面容。

===Original===
The father came and he did bless

The man who did restore the face,

And saved for him his son's remains,

And thus he fame and honor gains.

===Chinese===
父亲前来祝福了

那位修复面容的人，

为他保全了儿子的遗体，

此人因此赢得了名誉和荣誉。

===Original===
## Lines on a Lawn Party / 草地派对有感

Where it was announced ten days previous that the cereus would bloom, August, 1888.

===Chinese===
十天前已宣布昙花将于1888年8月开放。

===Original===
We own we felt a little curious,

To see the rare night blooming cereus,

And as if 'twas divine anointed,

It came in bloom at the time appointed,

And gorgeous too their oleander,

None 'ere saw shrub blossom grander.

===Chinese===
我们承认心中有几分好奇，

想看看稀有的夜开昙花，

仿佛受到神的恩膏，

它在预定的时刻绽放，

夹竹桃也华丽夺目，

从未有人见过更壮观的灌木花朵。

===Original===
## All Men Are Brothers / 人人皆兄弟

We are in ancient stories told,

All were brothers in days of old,

But these with facts they do not chime

For all mankind do love the dime,

===Chinese===
古代的故事告诉我们，

在远古时代人人都是兄弟，

但这些说法与事实并不吻合，

因为世人都爱金钱。

===Original===
And worship the mighty dollar,

And admire the golden collar,

The rich man's washed with whitest lime,

The poor man's cover'd o'er with slime,

===Chinese===
崇拜那强大的美元，

羡慕那金色的项圈，

富人被白色的石灰洗净，

穷人却浑身沾满污泥。

===Original===
But we should try to love each other

And treat each man as our brother.

===Chinese===
但我们应该努力彼此相爱，

把每个人都当作兄弟对待。

===Original===
## The Good Man / 好人

Cheerful and happy was his mood,

He to the poor was kind and good,

And he oft' times did find them food,

Also supplies of coal and wood,

===Chinese===
他总是心情开朗愉快，

对穷人和善仁慈，

他常常为他们找到食物，

还有煤炭和木柴的供应。

===Original===
He never spake a word was rude,

And cheer'd those did o'er sorrows brood,

He passed away not understood,

Because no poet in his lays

===Chinese===
他从未说过一句粗鲁的话，

安慰那些忧愁沉思的人，

他默默离世不被理解，

因为没有诗人在他的诗篇中。

===Original===
Had penned a sonnet in his praise,

'Tis sad, but such is world's ways.

===Chinese===
为他写下一首赞美的十四行诗，

这很可悲，但世界就是这样。


===Original===
## Lay of the Spring / 春之歌

Let others sing their favourite lay,

From early morn till close of day,

More useful themes engage our pen,

We sing the lay of our good hen.

===Chinese===
让别人唱他们心爱的歌吧，

从清晨直到日暮，

更有益的主题激发我们的笔，

我们来唱我们好母鸡的歌。

===Original===
For she doth lay each morn an egg,

And it is full and large and big,

Abroad she doth never travel,

Happy she when scratching gravel.

===Chinese===
因为她每天早晨下一个蛋，

又大又圆又饱满，

她从不远行，

在碎石中刨食她就快乐。

===Original===
And she loud cackles songs of praise

Every morn when e'er she lays,

Proud she is when she finds pickings

For to feed her brood of chickens.

===Chinese===
每天早晨她下蛋时，

都会大声咯咯唱起赞歌，

当她找到食物喂养

她的小鸡仔时，她很自豪。

===Original===
It greatly puzzled her one day

When she found white nest egg of clay,

She knew some one did trick play her,

For she was no brick layer.

===Chinese===
一天她大惑不解，

发现窝里有个白泥做的假蛋，

她知道有人在戏弄她，

因为她可不是泥瓦匠。

===Original===
Vain and stately male bird stalks,

Leading his hens along the walks,

Proudly each feather in his tail

Makes rival roosters for to quail.

===Chinese===
自负而威严的公鸡昂首阔步，

领着他的母鸡沿小路行走，

他尾巴上每一根骄傲的羽毛，

都让竞争的公鸡畏缩。

===Original===
Our muse now soars on feathery wing,

And cheerful it doth hail the spring,

Bringing the sunshine and showers,

Green grass and buds and leafy bowers.

===Chinese===
我们的缪斯展翅高飞，

欢快地迎接春天的到来，

带来阳光和春雨，

绿草、嫩芽和绿叶成荫。

===Original===
So pleasant is the month of May,

When bushes shoot out blooming spray,

'Ere spring we're tired of winter's white,

Spring's varied colours do delight.

===Chinese===
五月是多么美好，

灌木抽出繁花的枝条，

春天来临前我们已厌倦了冬的苍白，

春天缤纷的色彩令人欣喜。

===Original===
## High in Masonry / 共济会高处

Give me a board so I can trace on

How high I have gone as a mason,

Said worker on true square cut stone

Unto some knights of good St. John.

===Chinese===
给我一块板子好让我画出，

我作为石匠爬到了多高，

一位精于方正切割石材的工人

对圣约翰骑士团的骑士们说。

===Original===
Each one of them could trace with ease

The highest Masonic degrees,

Tell us how high then you have gone

They said to worker on cut stone.

===Chinese===
他们每个人都能轻松画出，

共济会的最高级别，

告诉我们你爬到了多高，

他们对那位石匠说。

===Original===
Who said to their astonishment

To top of Washington monument,

I solemnly to you declare

And will swear to fact upon the square.

===Chinese===
他令他们大为惊讶地说，

到了华盛顿纪念碑的顶端，

我庄严地向你们声明，

并愿以方矩起誓此言属实。

===Original===
I there stood thirty-three degrees,

While the mercury did freeze,

And none of you will have desire

In masonry for to go higher.

===Chinese===
我在那里站了三十三度，

水银都凝固成冰，

你们当中没有人会再想，

在共济会中爬得更高了。


===Original===
## Tramp and Fish / 流浪汉与鱼

A hungry tramp did long for dish,

And he stole a big bunch of fish,

But he full soon did come to grief,

He was quick captured as a thief.

===Chinese===
一个饥饿的流浪汉渴望一顿饭，

于是偷了一大串鱼，

但他很快就遭遇了不幸，

很快被当作小偷抓获。

===Original===
And brought before the magistrate,

So judge he would pronounce his fate,

Judge asked him how he came by fish,

The thief said for them he did wish,

===Chinese===
被带到治安法官面前，

法官要宣判他的命运，

法官问他鱼是怎么得来的，

小偷说他只是想吃鱼。

===Original===
The best fish ever came from brook,

I own, my Lord, I did them hook,

Said constable, he speaks what's true,

And you must give the devil his due.

===Chinese===
最好的鱼来自溪流，

大人，我承认是我钓的，

警官说他说的是实话，

你必须给魔鬼应有的评价。

===Original===
Then judge did constable rebuke,

He owns fish if he did them hook,

He has not broken any clause

I know of in our fishery laws.

===Chinese===
然后法官斥责了警官，

他承认鱼是他钓的，

据我所知他没有触犯

我们渔业法的任何条款。

===Original===
Unless you can show this reason,

He has hooked them out of season,

Your duty you have thus mistook,

For man had right the fish to hook.

===Chinese===
除非你能证明这个理由，

他在禁渔期钓了鱼，

你搞错了你的职责，

因为人有权钓鱼。

===Original===
Fishing here it is not treason,

Him you had no right to seize on,

And when the poor man did fish hook,

He had a right the same to cook.

===Chinese===
在这里钓鱼不是叛国罪，

你无权逮捕他，

当穷人钓到了鱼，

他有权将鱼煮了吃。

===Original===
## Seal Fishing and Hunting / 海豹捕猎

Where frosts doth northern bays congeal,

There you will find the finest seal,

They do pursue them o'er each bay

From early dawn till close of day.

===Chinese===
在冰霜冻结北方海湾之处，

你能找到最好的海豹，

他们追逐它们穿越每个海湾，

从清晨直到日暮。

===Original===
The Indians display great zeal,

Engaged in hunting of the seal,

And ladies love for to display

Their seal furs from this famous bay.

===Chinese===
印第安人展现出极大的热情，

投身于猎捕海豹，

女士们喜欢炫耀

来自这著名海湾的海豹皮毛。

===Original===
Most precious of all furs is seal,

Their flesh is good as finest veal,

And the seal it is a squatter,

Lives on either land or water.

===Chinese===
海豹皮是所有毛皮中最珍贵的，

海豹肉如最上等的小牛肉般美味，

海豹是两栖动物，

陆地或水中都能生活。

===Original===
The Indian boys happy feel,

When they capture the young seal,

They do seem so happy ever,

Sailing o'er each bay and river.

===Chinese===
印第安男孩们快乐极了，

当他们捕捉到幼海豹，

他们看起来总是那么快乐，

在每个海湾和河流上航行。

===Original===
They train them their canoes to tow,

And o'er the waters merry go,

Full quick to right or left they wheel,

Guided by reins the docile seal.

===Chinese===
他们训练海豹拖拽独木舟，

在水面上欢快前行，

驯服的海豹拉着缰绳，

敏捷地左右转向。

===Original===
Like lad on colt without a saddle,

These youths use no oar nor paddle,

But swift o'er water rushes keel,

For 'tis propelled by the fast seal.

===Chinese===
像骑着无鞍小马的少年，

这些青年不用桨也不用橹，

船身在水面飞速滑行，

因为它由飞快的海豹推动。

===Original===
To Uncle Sam we do appeal,

How can you own the whole sea's seal,

And at the same time claim our cod,

If from the shore a mile abroad.

===Chinese===
我们向山姆大叔提出疑问，

你怎么能拥有整个海洋的海豹，

同时又宣称我们的鳕鱼，

只要离岸一英里便是你的。


===Original===
## Snake and Its Young / 蛇与幼蛇

There is a peculiar snake,

You might almost call it squatter,

It loves to dive in pond or lake,

At home on either land or water.

===Chinese===
有一种奇特的蛇，

你几乎可以称它为两栖动物，

它喜欢潜入池塘或湖泊，

在陆地或水中都能安家。

===Original===
But it excited my good dog

To see small snakes bask in the sun,

Enjoying themselves on a big log,

Near into where the water run.

===Chinese===
但这激起了我的好狗的好奇，

它看到小蛇在阳光下晒太阳，

在一根靠近水流的大木头上享受时光。

===Original===
But their mother she was watching

Her numerous brood on the log,

She thought to them was danger hatching,

When she beheld myself and dog.

===Chinese===
但它们的母亲正在守护着

她那一大窝幼蛇在木头上，

当她看到我和狗时，

以为危险正在酝酿。

===Original===
For she gave a hissing sound,

All her offspring to awake,

She ope'd her mouth and at a bound,

Down her throat did rush each snake.

===Chinese===
于是她发出嘶嘶声，

唤醒她所有的后代，

她张开嘴一跃之间，

每条蛇都冲进了她的喉咙。

===Original===
I scarcely my own eyes could trust,

To see those small snakes disappear,

I really thought that she would burst,

For the sake of her offspring dear.

===Chinese===
我几乎不敢相信自己的眼睛，

看着那些小蛇消失不见，

我真的以为她会撑破，

为了她心爱的孩子们。

===Original===
But I soon hid among the brakes,

To view the young ones leave their prison,

Will you believe this tale of snakes,

If I did count right just four dozen.

===Chinese===
但我很快藏在蕨丛中，

看幼蛇们离开它们的牢笼，

你会相信这个蛇的故事吗？

如果我没数错，刚好四打。

===Original===
## Whale and Its Foes / 鲸鱼与它的敌人

Six hundred miles north of Cape Flattery,

On sea there seemed a floating battery,

And stream of blood did dye the water,

Sailors wondered what was the matter.

===Chinese===
在弗拉特里角（Cape Flattery）以北六百英里，

海面上仿佛漂浮着一座炮台，

一股鲜血染红了海水，

水手们不知发生了什么事。

===Original===
But they soon saw a great sword fish

With its bayonet make a quick rush,

Into which proved to be large whale,

And thrasher too did it assail.

===Chinese===
但他们很快看到一条巨大的剑鱼，

用它的刺刀猛冲向前，

刺入的竟是一头巨大的鲸鱼，

而长尾鲨（Thrasher）也在攻击它。

===Original===
The whale dived deep to save its life,

But thrasher eager for the strife,

Knew whale must rise for to get breath,

Then it would thrash the whale to death.

===Chinese===
鲸鱼深潜以求自保，

但长尾鲨渴望战斗，

知道鲸鱼必须浮上来换气，

然后就会把鲸鱼拍死。

===Original===
At last the great whale it arose,

And in distress it spouts and blows,

In anger sweeps its mighty tail,

Defensive weapon of the whale.

===Chinese===
最终巨鲸浮出水面，

痛苦地喷水和喷气，

愤怒地挥动它巨大的尾巴——

鲸鱼的防御武器。

===Original===
But thrasher high in air did leap,

And fell on monster of the deep,

The sword fish then it did advance,

And assaulted it with its lance.

===Chinese===
但长尾鲨高高跃向空中，

落在深海巨兽身上，

剑鱼随后挺进，

用它的长矛发起攻击。

===Original===
Thus united foes soon do prevail,

And quick there floated a dead whale,

The thrasher now victorious winner,

From off the whale expected dinner.

===Chinese===
就这样联合的敌人很快占了上风，

一具死鲸很快浮在水面，

长尾鲨成了胜利者，

正要从鲸鱼身上享用晚餐。

===Original===
But ship's crew they secured the prize,

Whale bone and blubber monster size,

Others fought but they only reap,

The spoils of mammoth of the deep.

===Chinese===
但船员们抢走了战利品，

鲸骨和鲸脂巨如怪物，

其他参战者只能收获，

这深海巨兽的残余。


===Original===
## Tiger and Elephant / 老虎与大象

On Ganges banks roams the tiger,

And lion rules by the Niger,

Hunter heard shrill cry of peacocks,

In Indian jungles go in flocks.

===Chinese===
老虎在恒河岸边游荡，

狮子统治着尼日尔河畔，

猎人听到孔雀尖锐的叫声，

在印度丛林中成群结队。

===Original===
And he saw tiger crouch and spring,

To crush a bird with beauteous wing,

But the tiger missed his aim,

And he hung his head with shame.

===Chinese===
他看到老虎伏身猛扑，

要扑碎一只翅膀美丽的鸟，

但老虎扑空了，

他羞愧地低下头。

===Original===
Then there came a mighty crush,

Of elephants rush through the bush,

The tiger cat-like crouched on ground,

And elephants rushed in with bound.

===Chinese===
接着传来一阵巨大的轰响，

大象们冲过灌木丛，

老虎像猫一样伏在地上，

大象们跳跃着冲来。

===Original===
In front was baby elephant,

To crush its bones did tiger want,

But mother saw fierce forest ranger,

And she gave a cry of danger.

===Chinese===
前面是一头小象，

老虎想碾碎它的骨头，

但母象看到了凶猛的丛林猎手，

发出了危险的叫声。

===Original===
Leader of herd he madly rushed,

Resolved the tiger should be crushed,

But tiger strove to run away,

Willing to relinquish prey.

===Chinese===
象群的首领疯狂地冲上前去，

下定决心要碾碎老虎，

但老虎试图逃跑，

愿意放弃猎物。

===Original===
But when he found that he must fight,

On elephant's back he strove to light,

But elephant struck him with his foot,

And then with tusks he did him root.

===Chinese===
但当他发现必须一战时，

他试图跳上大象的背，

但大象用脚踢他，

然后用象牙将他掀翻。

===Original===
So now once more must praise be sung,

To beasts who nobly fight for young,

And grateful feelings were now stirred,

Towards the leader of the herd.

===Chinese===
现在要再次唱起赞歌，

赞美那些为幼崽英勇战斗的野兽，

感激之情在心中涌动，

向着象群的首领致敬。

===Original===
## Strange Life Preserver / 奇特的救生员

A sailor he was swept from deck,

In minute he seem'd as a speck,

Tossing on each briny wave,

They feared the sea would be his grave.

===Chinese===
一个水手被从甲板上卷入海中，

转眼间他像一个小点，

在咸涩的浪涛中颠簸，

他们担心大海将成为他的坟墓。

===Original===
Though they full quickly launched the boat,

They could not see where he did float,

He now was a long ways astern,

His whereabouts they could not learn.

===Chinese===
虽然他们很快放下了小船，

却看不到他漂浮在哪里，

他已经被冲到很远的后方，

他们无法得知他的下落。

===Original===
But while he on the waves did toss,

He was seized by great Albatross,

Who had been looking round in quest

Of something whereon it could rest.

===Chinese===
但当他在波浪中颠簸时，

他被一只巨大的信天翁抓住了，

它一直在四处寻觅

可以栖息的地方。

===Original===
It hover'd o'er him with its wings,

And its great webb feet on him clings,

And it tore him with its sharp beak,

For it was longing for some steak.

===Chinese===
它用翅膀在他上方盘旋，

用它巨大的蹼脚抓住他，

用尖锐的喙啄他，

因为它渴望吃一块肉排。

===Original===
But sailor seized it by the throat,

And found with ease he then could float,

So sailor's life was saved by loss

Of the life of the Albatross.

===Chinese===
但水手抓住了它的喉咙，

发现自己可以轻松漂浮了，

水手的生命因

信天翁的死而获救。

===Original===
The boat's crew found him none too soon,

For he had fallen into a swoon,

Him they would not have come across

Had they not seen the Albatross.

===Chinese===
船员们找到他时还算及时，

因为他已经陷入昏迷，

如果不是看到了信天翁，

他们不会发现他。

===Original===
And thus poor Jack his life was saved,

For fierce rage of bird he braved,

Though in a faint he still did cling,

One arm round neck and one round wing.

===Chinese===
就这样可怜的杰克活了下来，

他冒着鸟的猛烈攻击，

虽然昏迷中仍然紧抓不放，

一只手臂搂着脖子，一只搂着翅膀。

===Original===
For as a friend he did hug close

That fine large bird the Albatross,

Sailors row comrade back to ship,

Where he relates his wondrous trip.

===Chinese===
他像拥抱朋友一样紧紧抱住

那只美丽的大鸟信天翁，

水手们将同伴划回船上，

他讲述着他奇妙的经历。

===Original===
Sailors fear that many a cross

Will fall on crew kill Albatross,

This was slain in self defence,

And so no harm it came from thence.

===Chinese===
水手们担心许多灾祸

会降临到杀死信天翁的船员身上，

但这是出于自卫而杀，

所以不会有什么灾祸降临。

===Original===
"The lone Albatross incumbent on the air."

===Chinese===
"孤独的信天翁倚靠在天空中。"


===Original===
## Virtues of a Diamond Ring / 钻戒的神奇

Of the strange virtues of a ring,

In simple strains we now will sing,

Brave warrior of ancient France,

Where danger was he did advance.

===Chinese===
关于一枚戒指的奇妙力量，

我们用质朴的曲调来歌唱，

古法兰西的勇敢战士，

哪里有危险他就冲向哪里。

===Original===
But he at last was struck by ball

And on the battle field did fall,

They dug for him a shallow grave

And slightly cover'd warrior brave.

===Chinese===
但他最终被子弹击中，

倒在了战场上，

他们为他挖了一个浅浅的墓穴，

草草掩埋了这位勇敢的战士。

===Original===
But his servant man with warm heart,

Loathed with his master thus to part,

So he moved soil from where he lay

And found a lump of lifeless clay.

===Chinese===
但他忠实的仆人有着一颗热心，

不忍与主人就此分离，

于是他移开他躺的地方的泥土，

发现了一团无生命的躯体。

===Original===
He turned away in sad despair,

That could not be his master fair,

That famed brave youth of noble birth,

Now all stained o'er with blood and earth.

===Chinese===
他悲伤绝望地转过身去，

那不可能是他英俊的主人，

那位出身高贵的英勇青年，

如今浑身沾满血迹和泥土。

===Original===
As he begins to move away

The moon reflects on brilliant ray,

From diamond ring on dead man's finger

The servant now doth fondly linger.

===Chinese===
正当他转身离去时，

月光映射出一道璀璨的光芒，

来自死者手指上的钻戒，

仆人不禁驻足凝望。

===Original===
For he knows it's his master's ring,

And hopes to life he may him bring,

In finger he discovers heat

And hopes his heart it still may beat.

===Chinese===
因为他认出那是主人的戒指，

希望它能将主人带回生命，

他在手指上发现了温度，

希望他的心脏仍在跳动。

===Original===
Though surgeons they pronounce him dead,

For long he bathes his breast and head,

And slowly master did restore

To fight more brave than he had before.

===Chinese===
虽然外科医生已经宣布他死亡，

他久久地擦拭他的胸膛和头部，

主人慢慢地恢复了生机，

比之前更加英勇地重返战场。

===Original===
And now this tale to close we bring,

Of warrior saved by a ring,

Full oft again to fight for king,

His praise his countrymen they sing.

===Chinese===
现在这个故事讲完了，

一位被戒指救活的战士，

他又多次为国王征战，

他的同胞传唱着他的颂歌。

===Original===
## Child Saved by Dog / 狗救孩子

Johnston he is an engineer,

He always looks if track is clear,

For he hath a keen eagle eye,

Danger afar he doth espy.

===Chinese===
约翰斯顿（Johnston）是一位工程师，

他总要查看轨道是否通畅，

因为他有一双锐利的鹰眼，

远处的危险也逃不过他的视线。

===Original===
And he hath too a warm true heart,

Of others woes he shares a part;

One day he gazed far down the line,

And a large dog he could define.

===Chinese===
他还有一颗温暖真诚的心，

分担他人的苦难；

有一天他沿着线路远望，

看到一只大狗。

===Original===
So eager busy on the track,

In mouth it seemed to lift a pack,

But it oftentimes did fail

For to raise it o'er the rail.

===Chinese===
它在轨道上急切而忙碌，

嘴里似乎叼着一包东西，

但它好几次都没能

把它叼过铁轨。

===Original===
The engineer put on his steam

And he loud made his whistle scream,

So that the dog would take alarm

And thus preserve his life from harm.

===Chinese===
工程师加大了蒸汽动力，

拉响了响亮的汽笛，

好让狗受惊而警觉，

从而保全它的生命。

===Original===
This noble dog, it feared not danger,

Fear to him it was a stranger,

His mistress child he wished to save,

And all the danger he would brave.

===Chinese===
这只高贵的狗不惧危险，

对它来说恐惧是陌生的，

它想救主人的孩子，

愿意冒一切危险。

===Original===
His last great effort did prevail,

He raised it safe beyond the rail,

Into a snug and hollow spot,

A place seem'd formed for a child's cot.

===Chinese===
它最后的巨大努力奏效了，

它把孩子安全地叼过了铁轨，

放进一个舒适低洼的地方，

一个仿佛专为孩子准备的摇篮。

===Original===
This dog of noble mastiff breed,

For his own safety took no heed,

But at approaching train did bark,

To make them to his troubles hark.

===Chinese===
这只高贵的獒犬品种的狗，

不顾自身的安危，

对着驶来的火车吠叫，

让人们注意它的困境。

===Original===
The engineer did sad bewail,

To see the dog still on the rail,

A moment more the beast is slain,

Cut in two by the cruel train.

===Chinese===
工程师悲伤地哀叹，

看到狗还在铁轨上，

再过一瞬间它就被残忍的火车

拦腰切断了。

===Original===
The engineer now shuts off steam

For to investigate the theme,

That caused the dog to die at post,

Finds to save child its life it lost.

===Chinese===
工程师现在关闭了蒸汽，

去调查原因，

是什么让狗死在岗位上，

发现它为了救孩子失去了生命。

===Original===
Faithful in the cause of duty,

Saving life of little beauty,

A little darling three year old,

More precious than her weight in gold.

===Chinese===
在职责的召唤下忠诚无比，

救了一个小美人的生命，

一个三岁的小宝贝，

比她体重等量的黄金还珍贵。

===Original===
On track she wandered for to play,

But soon she in quiet slumber lay,

And all the efforts of old Towser,

Were not able to arouse her.

===Chinese===
她跑到铁轨上玩耍，

但很快就安静地睡着了，

老陶泽（Towser）的所有努力，

都无法把她唤醒。

===Original===
The mother now in agony wild,

Rushed down to train to find her child,

There she found it sweetly sleeping,

While some for faithful dog were weeping.

===Chinese===
母亲此时心如刀绞，

冲下火车去找她的孩子，

在那里发现孩子甜甜地睡着，

而有些人在为忠犬流泪。

===Original===
And a brave man was engineer,

For he himself knew not of fear,

But his heart was filled with pain,

Because the noble dog was slain.

===Chinese===
工程师是个勇敢的人，

他自己从不知恐惧，

但他的心充满了悲痛，

因为那只高贵的狗被杀死了。


===Original===
## Leach the Engineer / 工程师利奇

The engineer who drives the train,

From scalds he oft doth suffer pain,

And they are more noble scars,

Than hirelings gain in foreign wars.

===Chinese===
驾驶火车的工程师，

常常因烫伤而忍受痛苦，

这些是比雇佣兵在外国战争中

获得的伤疤更为高贵的勋章。

===Original===
Our hero's name should live forever,

Train wrecked in Columbia River,

And fatal scalded was Frank Leach,

In mid the river far from beach.

===Chinese===
我们英雄的名字应永垂不朽，

火车在哥伦比亚河（Columbia River）上失事，

弗兰克·利奇（Frank Leach）被严重烫伤，

在远离河岸的河中央。

===Original===
Heroic deed it should be sung,

Though his flesh in ribbons hung,

He saw a man who could not swim,

And he resolved to rescue him.

===Chinese===
英雄的壮举应当被传唱，

虽然他的皮肉已绽裂成条，

他看到一个不会游泳的人，

下定决心要救他。

===Original===
And success his efforts crowned,

He saved the man from being drowned,

For him upon his back he bore,

And boldly swam with him to shore.

===Chinese===
他的努力获得了成功，

他把那人从溺水中救起，

把他背在背上，

勇敢地带着他游到岸上。

===Original===
Heroic deed we now enroll,

And grave it deep on fame's scroll,

For this great truth we wish to teach,

High 'mong our heroes ranks Frank Leach.

===Chinese===
我们如今将这英雄事迹记录，

深深地刻在荣耀的卷轴上，

我们要传授这个伟大的真理，

弗兰克·利奇在我们的英雄行列中地位崇高。

===Original===
When he had but short time to live,

His strength and skill he both did give,

His poor fellow man to save,

From laying in a watery grave.

===Chinese===
当他余下的时间不多时，

他献出了自己的力量和技能，

拯救他可怜的同胞，

不让他沉入水底的坟墓。

===Original===
The heroes oft in days of old,

Were those who hired to fight for gold,

But the true heroes we revere

Doth fight for home and country dear.

===Chinese===
古代的英雄，

往往是被雇来为金钱而战的人，

但我们所崇敬的真正英雄，

是为家园和亲爱的祖国而战的人。

===Original===
## Jackal and Child / 豺与孩子

In the great Province of Bengal,

The scavenger is the Jackal,

For it doth love each night to feast,

On the carrion of some beast.

===Chinese===
在辽阔的孟加拉（Bengal）省，

豺是清道夫，

因为它喜欢每晚饱餐

某头野兽的腐肉。

===Original===
The stench of which pollutes the air,

But to this beast 'tis sweet and fair,

Carcass to it is source of wealth,

Jackals promote the public health.

===Chinese===
腐肉的恶臭污染空气，

但对这野兽来说却甜美芬芳，

腐尸是它的财富之源，

豺促进了公共卫生。

===Original===
When the "Seapoys" did rebel,

A strange adventure child befell,

An English Colonel and his wife,

They thought still distant was the strife.

===Chinese===
当"西波伊"（Seapoys）叛变时，

一个孩子遭遇了奇异的经历，

一位英国上校和他的妻子，

他们以为战事还很遥远。

===Original===
And left their little girl at home,

While they to distant village roam;

And thus saved their lives from slaughter,

But rebels carried off their daughter.

===Chinese===
把小女儿留在家中，

自己去了远方的村庄；

这样保住了他们的性命，

但叛军掳走了他们的女儿。

===Original===
Their servant woman, a Hindu,

They knew her to be kind and true,

It almost drove her crazy wild,

To see them dragging off the child.

===Chinese===
他们的仆人是一位印度教徒（Hindu），

他们知道她善良忠诚，

看到他们拖走孩子，

几乎让她发疯。

===Original===
The Colonel soon he doth return,

And in his breast fierce rage doth burn,

He knows the child is doomed to die,

But he the rebels will defy.

===Chinese===
上校很快返回，

胸中燃烧着猛烈的怒火，

他知道孩子注定要死，

但他要反抗叛军。

===Original===
So quick he doth gird on his sword,

And asks for blessings from the Lord,

He puts his pistols in their case,

And carries with him trusty brace.

===Chinese===
他迅速佩上宝剑，

祈求主的祝福，

他把手枪装进枪套，

带着可靠的一对手枪。

===Original===
Seapoys by the river side

Left child to drown in rising tide,

But Jackal went there for water,

And spied the Colonel's daughter.

===Chinese===
西波伊在河边，

把孩子丢在那里让涨潮淹死，

但豺去那里找水喝，

发现了上校的女儿。

===Original===
He knew the river soon would rise,

So quick he carried off the prize,

Though love for child he does not feel,

He only wants her for a meal.

===Chinese===
他知道河水很快会涨，

于是迅速叼走了这个"战利品"，

虽然它对孩子并无感情，

它只想要她当一顿饭。

===Original===
He ran with her towards the south,

Carrying her in his broad mouth,

The Colonel had a strong desire

On this savage beast for to fire.

===Chinese===
它叼着她朝南方跑去，

用宽大的嘴叼着她，

上校很想

朝这只野兽开枪。

===Original===
But from it he refrained for fear

The ball might strike his little dear,

He saw that brute was now weary

Running with his little dearie.

===Chinese===
但他忍住了，怕子弹

会打中他心爱的小宝贝，

他看到那畜生已经跑累了，

叼着他的小宝贝在跑。

===Original===
So he then quickly gave it chase,

And full soon then he gained the race,

The coward beast then dropped the child

And fled away in terror wild.

===Chinese===
于是他迅速追赶，

很快便追上了，

那懦弱的畜生丢下了孩子，

惊恐万分地逃跑了。

===Original===
With joy Colonel he doth weep

When he finds babe is still asleep,

And on it neither scar nor trace

Can disfigure its fair face.

===Chinese===
上校喜悦地流下眼泪，

发现孩子仍然安睡，

脸上既没有伤疤也没有痕迹，

没有毁损她美丽的面容。

===Original===
And now he takes it in his arms,

With joy and pride surveys its charms,

We fear that kisses did it smother

When he handed it to mother.

===Chinese===
他现在把她抱在怀里，

满怀喜悦和骄傲地端详她的可爱，

我们担心当他把她交给母亲时，

太多的亲吻差点让她窒息。


===Original===
## Little Hero / 小英雄

'Mong silver hills of Nevada

There is many a wild bravado,

Who oft indulge in lawless vice,

And there are pearls of great price.

===Chinese===
在内华达（Nevada）的银山之中，

有许多狂野的冒险者，

他们常常沉溺于无法无天的恶行，

但那里也有珍贵的珍珠。

===Original===
Rough hearts, but true at the core,

There is the genuine silver ore,

But it needs skill of the refiner

To find pure gems in the miner.

===Chinese===
粗犷的心灵，但内核真实，

那里有真正的银矿石，

但需要提炼者的技艺，

才能在矿工身上发现纯净的宝石。

===Original===
Far from their home two children stray,

Among the mountains far away,

The eldest of these travellers bold,

Jack Smith he was but six years old.

===Chinese===
两个孩子远离家园迷了路，

在遥远的山间游荡，

这些旅行者中最大的勇敢孩子，

杰克·史密斯（Jack Smith）才六岁。

===Original===
So far poor children went abroad,

That both at last they lost their road,

But their good dog the trusty Rover,

By scent and search doth them discover.

===Chinese===
可怜的孩子们走得太远，

最终两人都迷了路，

但他们忠实的好狗罗孚（Rover），

靠嗅觉和搜索找到了他们。

===Original===
Their friends they search for them in vain,

Dark night comes on and heavy rain;

And savage wolves around them howl,

But they fear Rover's bark and growl.

===Chinese===
他们的朋友们徒劳地寻找他们，

黑夜降临，大雨倾盆；

凶猛的狼在周围嚎叫，

但它们害怕罗孚的吠叫和咆哮。

===Original===
On the third day the searchers hark

For sounds and they hear Rover's bark,

Joyous that boys were alive,

And that though feeble they survive.

===Chinese===
第三天搜索者倾听声响，

听到了罗孚的吠叫，

欣喜若狂两个男孩还活着，

虽然虚弱却幸存了下来。

===Original===
Miners they left their silver ore,

And for more precious pearls explore,

And when the children they discover,

'Tis unbounded then their pleasure.

===Chinese===
矿工们放下他们的银矿石，

去寻找更珍贵的珍珠，

当他们找到孩子们时，

喜悦之情无以言表。

===Original===
The eldest little hero bold

Had stripped his coat to keep the cold

From little brother three years old,

A worthy deed should be extolled.

===Chinese===
大一点的小英雄，

脱下自己的外套为三岁的小弟弟

抵御寒冷，

这高尚的义举值得颂扬。

===Original===
From home they were many a league,

And weak with hunger and fatigue,

Each clung upon a miner's back,

On their way home down mountain track.

===Chinese===
他们离家许多里路，

又饿又累虚弱不堪，

每个孩子都趴在矿工的背上，

沿着山路回家。

===Original===
## Gray Hairs / 白发

Once on a time a lady quarrelled

With the witty Douglass Jerrold,

Because that he had been so bold,

To hint that she was growing old.

===Chinese===
从前有一位女士与

机智的道格拉斯·杰罗尔德（Douglass Jerrold）争吵，

因为他竟敢暗示她日渐老去。

===Original===
She said her hair was dark 'till one day

She used an essence turned it gray,

O, yes, said he, tincture of time

Affects the hair in this our clime.

===Chinese===
她说她的头发本来是黑的，直到有一天

她用了一种染发液把它变灰了，

哦是的，他说，时间的酊剂

在我们的气候里影响着头发。

===Original===
## Good Shot / 精准射击

At great reunion of the South,

Of those had faced the cannon's mouth,

Where each could show a glorious scar

He had received in the war.

===Chinese===
在南方盛大的聚会上，

那些曾面对炮口的人们，

每个人都展示在战争中

获得的光荣伤疤。

===Original===
And there was an artillery major,

Said he, I am free to wager

I fired the best shot in the war,

And fame of it was heard afar.

===Chinese===
其中有一位炮兵少校，

他说我敢打赌，

我打出了战争中最精彩的一枪，

它的名声远近皆闻。

===Original===
The Federals fired at us a shell,

And near our battery it fell,

It gave us momentary alarm,

But it caused us little harm.

===Chinese===
联邦军向我们发射了一枚炮弹，

落在我们炮台附近，

它引起了一时的恐慌，

但我们损失甚微。

===Original===
For the fuse it ceased to burn,

And I resolved shell to return

To the same spot from whence it came,

I put in fuse was sure to flame.

===Chinese===
因为引信停止了燃烧，

我决心把炮弹送回

它飞来的同一个地方，

我装上了一根肯定会燃烧的引信。

===Original===
From mortar's mouth it went forth

Seeking for vengeance on the North,

And this swift messenger from South

It entered their great cannon's mouth.

===Chinese===
它从迫击炮口飞出，

向北方寻求复仇，

这来自南方的迅捷信使

钻进了他们大炮的炮口。

===Original===
And blew the gun all into atoms,

Which left desolate many homes,

But North had justice on her side.

In truth and virtue was her pride.

===Chinese===
把那门大炮炸成了碎片，

使许多家园荒芜，

但北方有正义站在她一边，

以真理和美德为荣。

===Original===
And high Heaven had well decreed

That the poor slave he should be freed,

And even the South doth prosper more

With freedom than she did before.

===Chinese===
至高的上天早已 decreed，

可怜的奴隶应当获得自由，

甚至南方如今也比从前

在自由中更加繁荣。


===Original===
## Lost Son Found / 失散之子归来

An English ship when homeward bound,

Near to its port was shipwrecked found,

For it had struck a sunken rock,

And was slowly sinking from the shock.

===Chinese===
一艘英国船在归航途中，

在港口附近遭遇海难，

因为它撞上了暗礁，

被撞击后慢慢沉没。

===Original===
In port they quick did man life boat,

Which o'er tempestuous sea did float,

They rescued all the crew, save one,

And were content with what they done.

===Chinese===
在港口他们迅速派出救生艇，

在波涛汹涌的大海上漂浮，

他们救出了所有船员，除了一人，

对所做的事感到满意。

===Original===
But they had not their captain, Harry,

Who on the shore was forced to tarry,

And knew not of the disaster,

So crew had worked without a master.

===Chinese===
但他们没有救出船长哈里（Harry），

他被迫在岸上停留，

不知道这场灾难，

所以船员们是在没有船长的情况下工作的。

===Original===
But when he heard of the shipwreck,

And that a man was left on deck,

He quickly hurried the boat's crew

For to again attempt his rescue.

===Chinese===
但当他听说了海难，

还有一个人被困在甲板上，

他急忙催促船员们

再次尝试营救。

===Original===
But earnestly his old mother,

Reminded him of his lost brother,

Perhaps drowned in foreign sea,

She cried, son, stay and comfort me.

===Chinese===
但他年迈的母亲恳切地提醒他，

他那个失散的兄弟，

也许已溺死在异国的海洋，

她哭着说，儿子，留下来安慰我吧。

===Original===
But wreck they reach and rescue man,

And thrill of joy o'er city ran,

When it was found 'twas Harry's brother,

Had returned to comfort mother.

===Chinese===
但他们到达失事处救起了那人，

一阵喜悦的颤栗传遍全城，

当人们发现那是哈里的兄弟，

他回来安慰母亲了。

===Original===
Thus providence rewards the brave

Who strive their fellow men to save,

The mother's griefs it did assuage,

And happy now is her old age.

===Chinese===
就这样天意奖赏了勇敢的人，

那些努力拯救同胞的人，

它抚慰了母亲的悲伤，

如今她的晚年幸福安康。

===Original===
## Shoe Black / 擦鞋童

Gent on sidewalk held out his foot

While boy in gutter brushed his boot,

But at this time, how sad, alas,

An unruly horse did o'er him pass.

===Chinese===
绅士在人行道上伸出脚，

男孩在水沟里擦他的靴子，

但就在这时，多么可悲，唉，

一匹失控的马从他身上踩过。

===Original===
The child for friends he sad did lack,

They said he was but a shoeblack,

Kind hearted man the poor child bore,

To a soft cot in back of store.

===Chinese===
这孩子可悲地缺少朋友，

他们说他不过是个擦鞋童，

一位好心人抱起了可怜的孩子，

送到商店后面柔软的小床上。

===Original===
And brought from hospital ward

A skilful nurse the lad to guard,

She often listened for his breath,

As he was passing the vale of death.

===Chinese===
从医院请来一位

熟练的护士照看这少年，

她时常倾听他的呼吸，

因为他正走过死亡的幽谷。

===Original===
But, poor child, once he ope'd his eyes,

And he looked round in great surprise,

Feebly he asked, heaving a sigh,

Where in the world now am I.

===Chinese===
但可怜的孩子一度睁开了眼睛，

惊讶地环顾四周，

他虚弱地叹了一口气问道，

我现在到底在什么地方。

===Original===
The tender nurse bent o'er his face,

And said, dear boy, you're in good place,

She asked his name, he said it was Tom,

And that for long he had no home.

===Chinese===
温柔的护士俯身看着他的脸，

说，亲爱的孩子，你在一个好地方，

她问他叫什么，他说叫汤姆（Tom），

而且他已经很久没有家了。

===Original===
And since his mother was stricken dead,

He had not once reposed in bed,

And while suffering child did rack,

He eagerly asked will mother come back.

===Chinese===
自从母亲去世后，

他从未在床上安睡过，

当痛苦折磨着孩子时，

他急切地问妈妈会回来吗。

===Original===
The nurse she gently answered, no,

But, to your dear mother you can go,

In his last sleep he had a dream,

Shining up boots it was his theme.

===Chinese===
护士温柔地回答说不会，

但你可以到你亲爱的妈妈那里去，

在他最后的睡眠中他做了一个梦，

擦亮靴子是他的主题。

===Original===
He soon awoke and called out, mother,

I see you and little brother,

Christ, I know, has me forgiven,

For they are beckoning me to Heaven.

===Chinese===
他很快醒来喊道，妈妈，

我看到你和小弟弟了，

基督，我知道，已经宽恕了我，

因为他们在召唤我去天堂。


===Original===
## Duck and Oyster / 鸭与牡蛎

Once on a time there lived a duck,

And by its fishing it did thrive;

So one day it longed to suck

An oyster and for one did dive.

===Chinese===
从前有一只鸭子，

靠捕鱼过得很滋润；

有一天它渴望吮吸

一只牡蛎，于是潜入水中。

===Original===
The oyster near shore lay open,

The duck, its gizzard for to fill,

Resolved that he would pop in

Between the shells his sharp bill.

===Chinese===
牡蛎在岸边敞开着，

鸭子想填饱自己的嗉囊，

决定把尖嘴

伸进两片壳之间。

===Original===
And then he would have glorious treat,

But oyster closed on it its shell,

And duck it got no oyster meat,

But worse than that the duck befell.

===Chinese===
那样它就能大饱口福，

但牡蛎合上了壳，

鸭子没有吃到牡蛎肉，

更糟糕的事情发生了。

===Original===
This oyster was so very large,

It held the duck's head under water,

And towed small oysters like a barge,

Each one clung to it like squatter.

===Chinese===
这只牡蛎非常大，

把鸭子的头按在水下，

像驳船一样拖着小牡蛎，

每只都像寄居者一样紧贴着。

===Original===
Duck it died of too much moisture,

And it floated towards the beach,

And to its bill clung big oyster,

Sticking to it like a horse leach.

===Chinese===
鸭子因过多的水分而死，

漂向了海滩，

大牡蛎还粘在它的嘴上，

像马蛭一样紧贴不放。

===Original===
On the shore there stood a shyster,

Watching fat duck floating to him,

And the wondrous big oyster,

How fine it would be to stew them.

===Chinese===
岸上站着一个狡猾的家伙，

看着肥鸭漂向他，

还有那只奇大的牡蛎，

把它们炖了该多好。

===Original===
Alas the duck lost its dinner,

And at the same time lost its life,

But on it dined a hungry sinner,

Who did reap all the spoils of strife.

===Chinese===
唉鸭子失去了它的晚餐，

同时也失去了生命，

但一个饥饿的罪人却饱餐了一顿，

收获了这场争斗的全部战利品。

===Original===
For when the duck to land did float,

It towed small oysters not a few,

For it sailed like to a boat,

With fat duck pie and oyster stew.

===Chinese===
当鸭子漂到岸上时，

它拖着不少小牡蛎，

它像一艘船一样航行而来，

带来了肥鸭派和牡蛎炖菜。

===Original===
None can the poor duck's fate bewail,

For it reckoned without its host,

It strove for conquest and did fail,

Fighting for spoil it battle lost.

===Chinese===
没人能为可怜鸭子的命运哀叹，

因为它没有考虑到对手，

它努力征服却失败了，

为争夺战利品输掉了这场战斗。

===Original===
You see the wild duck did not know,

When it attacked the big oyster,

It had to fight a numerous foe,

Of these shell fish a whole cluster.

===Chinese===
你看这只野鸭不知道，

当它攻击大牡蛎时，

它必须对抗一大群敌人，

整整一簇贝类。

===Original===
Borne feathers on briny billow,

They were full of springy down,

And they made a glorious pillow,

Where fellow he could rest his crown.

===Chinese===
在咸涩的波涛上漂浮的羽毛，

充满了蓬松柔软的绒毛，

它们做了一个舒适的枕头，

这位伙计可以在上面安歇。

===Original===
This chap he did not plow nor sow,

But from sea and land he did reap

The wealth that others caused to grow,

And thus he treasures up did heap.

===Chinese===
这个家伙不耕也不种，

但他从海洋和陆地上收获了

别人种出的财富，

就这样积聚起了他的宝藏。


===Original===
## Baby Darling / 宝贝心肝

A miner in California mine,

For his distant home he did repine,

In a far off Eastern state,

Where did live his own dear mate.

===Chinese===
一个在加利福尼亚矿上的矿工，

思念他远方的家，

在遥远的东方某州，

住着他亲爱的伴侣。

===Original===
And one great source of all his joy,

His little darling baby boy,

One night to drive his cares away

To concert hall his footsteps stray.

===Chinese===
他所有欢乐的一大源泉，

是他心爱的小男孩，

一天晚上为了驱散忧愁，

他的脚步徘徊到了音乐厅。

===Original===
And loud resounding o'er the hall,

He heard a little boy squall,

The sweetest music he e'er heard,

Sweeter far than singing bird.

===Chinese===
响亮的声音回荡在大厅，

他听到一个小男孩在啼哭，

这是他听过的最甜美的音乐，

比歌唱的鸟儿还要甜美。

===Original===
For his thoughts it caused to roam,

To his distant eastern home,

Near to the mine there were no trace

Of baby dear or woman's face.

===Chinese===
因为这让他思绪飘飞，

飘向他遥远的东方家园，

矿场附近没有一丝痕迹

宝贝或女人的面容。

===Original===
When violinist with his bow

Did make exquisite music flow,

The miner he did loudly bawl

Stop fiddle and let baby squall.

===Chinese===
当小提琴手运弓

奏出精美的旋律时，

矿工大声喊道

别拉了让小宝贝哭吧。

===Original===
My sad heart his cries doth cheer,

Reminding me of my own dear,

For sooner I would him behold,

Than if I found a mine of gold.

===Chinese===
他悲伤的心因那哭声而振作，

想起了他自己的孩子，

因为与其找到一座金矿，

他更渴望见到他的孩子。

===Original===
For what are all the joys of life,

So far away from child and wife,

But few more months I will stay here

Then join my wife and baby dear.

===Chinese===
远离妻儿，

生命中所有的快乐又算什么，

再待几个月我就

去和我的妻子和宝贝团聚。

===Original===
## Riding an Avalanche / 骑雪崩

With our Canadian snow shoes,

O'er snow you walk where'er you choose,

But on long shoes Norwegian

They are like narrow toboggan.

===Chinese===
穿着我们的加拿大雪鞋，

你可以随意在雪上行走，

但挪威的长雪鞋，

就像窄窄的平底雪橇。

===Original===
And all your movements you control

By the aid of a stout long pole,

With it you balance or propel

But we show now what once befell.

===Chinese===
你所有的动作都靠

一根结实的长杆来控制，

你用它保持平衡或推进，

现在我们来讲讲曾经发生的事。

===Original===
Two miners full of pluck and game,

Wished to locate a mining claim,

On a high steep mountain crest

In Colorado of the West.

===Chinese===
两位充满胆识的矿工，

想在西部科罗拉多

一座高耸陡峭的山脊上

划定一个采矿权。

===Original===
Though snow was deep they would attempt

Their good mine for to pre-empt,

So up the mountain they do climb,

Covered o'er with snow and rime.

===Chinese===
尽管积雪很深他们仍要尝试

先行占有他们的好矿，于是他们

攀登山峰，全身覆盖着

白雪和冰霜。

===Original===
Norwegian shoes slide over the snow,

High and higher still they go,

One was two hundred yards ahead,

Till snow gives way where he doth tread.

===Chinese===
挪威雪鞋在雪上滑行，

越爬越高，

一人领先了两百码，

直到他脚下的雪突然塌陷。

===Original===
Which quickly starts an avalanche,

He seizes on a stout tree branch,

But all in vain he rushes fast,

His snow shoes in the avalanche.

===Chinese===
迅速引发了一场雪崩，

他抓住一根结实的树枝，

但一切徒劳他飞速滑下，

和他的雪鞋一起陷入雪崩。

===Original===
His friend on shoes Norwegian,

Like lightning down the hill he ran,

Or rather o'er the ice did glide

Down the long steep and glassy slide.

===Chinese===
他的朋友穿着挪威雪鞋，

像闪电一样冲下山坡，

不如说是在冰面上滑行，

沿着陡长光滑的滑道而下。

===Original===
And after him the Avalanche,

Tearing up trees both root and branch,

The man on Avalanche doth yell

To his friend you are doing well.

===Chinese===
雪崩在他身后追赶，

连根拔起树木，

在雪崩上的人大喊

朋友你做得好。

===Original===
With your shoes Norwegian,

Swifter than a toboggan,

Go on, go on, you'll win the race,

For we are slack'ning in our pace.

===Chinese===
穿着你的挪威雪鞋，

比平底雪橇还快，

继续，继续，你会赢得比赛，

因为我们的速度正在减慢。

===Original===
But, alas! poor runner feels

The Avalanche doth touch his heels,

Shall he be buried in the mass,

Or will the vast pile o'er him pass.

===Chinese===
但，唉！可怜的奔跑者感觉

雪崩已经碰到了他的脚后跟，

他会被埋在雪堆里吗，

还是巨大的雪堆会从他身上掠过。

===Original===
He stops quite sudden 'neath a rock,

It passed o'er him with mighty shock,

Though it did cause him great alarm,

Yet still he was all safe from harm.

===Chinese===
他在一块岩石下突然停下，

雪崩带着巨大的冲击力从他身上掠过，

虽然让他惊恐万分，

但他最终安然无恙。

===Original===
The Avalanche yet downward slides,

And his friend on it he rides,

Until it safely him doth launch

On outer edge of his own ranch.

===Chinese===
雪崩仍然向下滑去，

他的朋友骑在上面，

直到它安全地将他

送到了他自己牧场的外缘。

===Original===
After three miles down the gulch,

They both might have been crushed to mulch,

The one he seemed to run a race,

While one on Avalanche did chase.

===Chinese===
沿着峡谷下滑三英里后，

他们两人都可能被碾成碎片，

一个像是在赛跑，

另一个在雪崩上被追赶。

===Original===
But it was not for bravado,

One rode, one run in Colorado,

For it was desperate the strife

Each had for to secure his life.

===Chinese===
但这不是逞英雄，

一个骑，一个跑在科罗拉多，

这是你死我活的争斗，

每个人都为保全自己的生命而拼尽全力。

===Original===
This tale of shoes Norwegian

Is not for the collegian,

But for such youths as do take pride

In reading of a wondrous slide.

===Chinese===
这个关于挪威雪鞋的故事，

不是给大学生看的，

而是给那些以阅读

奇妙滑行为荣的年轻人看的。


===Original===
## Final Address to Our Patrons / 致赞助者的最后致辞

Now to our friends who proved so true,

We bid you for short time adieu.

===Chinese===
现在对我们这些真诚的朋友们，

我们暂时向你们道别。

===Original===
## Card of Thanks to the Press / 感谢新闻界

It is folly now to aim

Or to seek for distant fame,

But rest content if we can claim

Something of a local name.

===Chinese===
如今再去追求或寻觅

遥远的名声是愚蠢的，

但如果我们能获得

一点地方性的名声，便该知足了。

===Original===
On the pleasant banks of Thame,

Because in simple strains we sung

The glories of this country young.

===Chinese===
在泰晤士河（Thame）宜人的岸边，

因为我们用质朴的曲调

歌唱了这个年轻国家的荣耀。

===Original===
Of cows, and milk, and cream and cheese

And of fat steers sent o'er the seas,

Of horses plowing land with ease,

Of hogs and sheep and hens and bees,

Themes worthy of our songs and glees.

===Chinese===
关于牛、牛奶、奶油和奶酪，

关于运过大海的肥壮阉牛，

关于轻松犁地的马匹，

关于猪、羊、母鸡和蜜蜂——

这些都是值得我们歌唱的主题。

===Original===
## Lines on Canada / 加拿大颂

A land o'er flowing with milk and honey

Where the industrious may gather money,

Here men do not rank for wealth they inherit,

The stamp of nobility is one of merit.

===Chinese===
一片流淌着奶与蜜的土地，

勤劳的人们可以聚敛财富，

这里的人不以继承的财富论高低，

高贵的标志在于品德与功绩。

===Original===
Though we cannot boast of antiquities old

We truly can boast of mines of pure gold,

And the discoveries at Fraser River

Hudson Bay monopoly to pieces will shiver.

===Chinese===
虽然我们不能夸耀古老的遗迹，

但我们确实拥有纯金的矿脉，

弗雷泽河（Fraser River）上的发现

将粉碎哈德逊湾（Hudson Bay）的垄断。

===Original===
For Britain at present little would move her

To present to Canada Hudson Bay and Vancouver,

Then she would be a yoang giant empire

As great as a Briton could well desire.

===Chinese===
目前英国不太愿意

将哈德逊湾和温哥华赠予加拿大，

否则她将成为一个年轻的巨人帝国，

伟大得连英国人也梦寐以求。

===Original===
Then with the whole of British America

United by railroad as well as by law,

Proudly we might with banners unfurled

Proclaim her the great highway of the world.

===Chinese===
那么整个英属美洲，

通过铁路和法律联合起来，

我们可以骄傲地展开旗帜，

宣告她是世界伟大的通途。

===Original===
## The Glorious Land of Maple Leaf / 光荣的枫叶之邦

Come let us join the tuneful throng

And sing a new Canadian song,

As this great lands of woods and lakes

Sweet melody in us awakes,

===Chinese===
来吧让我们加入这和谐的合唱，

唱一首新的加拿大之歌，

这片森林与湖泊的广袤大地

唤醒了我们心中甜美的旋律。

===Original===
For of all land this is the chief

This glorious land of maple leaf.

===Chinese===
因为万邦之中她为首，

这光荣的枫叶之邦。

===Original===
It is land of streams and rivers

And the land of toiling beavers,

More noble emblems we rely on

Than preying eagle, bear or lion,

===Chinese===
这是溪流与河流之邦，

是勤劳的海狸之邦，

我们依靠比掠食的鹰、熊或狮子

更为高贵的象征。

===Original===
True, worthy honours are not brief,

But flourish shall the maple leaf.

===Chinese===
真正值得尊敬的荣誉不会短暂，

枫叶将永远繁荣兴盛。

===Original===
For the grand maple wood is strong

And labour ranks high in our song

So let us glory in our soil

Where nature doth reward for toil,

===Chinese===
因为雄伟的枫木坚强，

劳动在我们的歌中地位崇高，

让我们为我们的土地而骄傲，

在那里大自然奖赏勤劳。

===Original===
And ever banish all our grief

Rejoicing in our maple leaf.

===Chinese===
永远驱散我们所有的悲伤，

在枫叶的光荣中欢欣鼓舞。

===Original===
With gloom we should not be o'ercast

For we have a young empire vast,

Awaiting only for the plough

When all with wealth it will endow,

===Chinese===
我们不应被阴霾笼罩，

因为我们拥有一个辽阔的年轻帝国，

只待犁铧翻动，

便会赋予一切财富。

===Original===
Then of all lands 'twill be the chief

This glorious land of maple leaf.

===Chinese===
届时万邦之中她为首，

这光荣的枫叶之邦。


===Original===

And life enjoy like to a queen.

===Chinese===

过得如同王后一般惬意。

===Original===
But very oft the farmer’s wife

Has to work hard through all her life;

If husband has to drive the ploughs,

The wife perhaps must milk the cows.

===Chinese===
然而农夫之妻往往

终其一生劳作不歇；

丈夫若需驱犁耕田，

妻子或许得去挤奶。

===Original===
And she too may churn the butter,

And no grumble she doth utter;

Though she doth work as hard as slave,

All trials she doth nobly brave.

===Chinese===
她还得搅制黄油，

却从不发出一句怨言；

纵然她如奴隶般辛劳，

一切磨难她都高贵地直面。

===Original===
Oft times large family she doth raise,

Her virtues they have cause to praise;

A guardian angel o’er her young,

When they were feeble round her clung.

===Chinese===
她常常养育一大家子，

她的美德值得赞颂；

一位守护天使守护着她的儿女，

当他们孱弱时依偎在她身旁。

===Original===
## Tile Drains / 瓷砖排水管

'Tis sad to see the richest land,

Barren where water it doth stand;

You seek for crop but all in vain,

For land requires the under drain.

===Chinese===
看到最肥沃的土地因积水而荒芜，令人悲伤；

你寻觅庄稼却徒劳无功，

因为土地需要地下排水。

===Original===
But you cause mother earth to smile,

When ventilated by the tile;

Before, she felt sour and old,

Drains warm her heart and expel cold.

===Chinese===
但你让大地母亲展露笑颜，

当她经由瓷砖管道通风透气；

从前，她感觉酸涩而苍老，

排水管温暖她的心房并驱散寒气。

===Original===
Porous now are all her veins,

From filtration of the drains,

And each tiny sparkling rill,

Sends through her heart a pleasing thrill.

===Chinese===
如今她所有的脉络都变得多孔，

得益于排水管的过滤，

每一条闪闪发亮的细流，

都穿过她的心房送来愉悦的震颤。

===Original===
Before, it was cold and crusty,

And it was both sour and musty;

But now it doth beat high with hopes,

Rejoicing in her mighty crops.

===Chinese===
从前，它冰冷而板结，

又酸又霉；

但如今它满怀希望地热烈跳动，

为她的丰收而欢欣。

===Original===
Tile must be laid straight and level,

But of course with a slight bevel;

Sloping towards ditch or creek,

Where way to ocean it doth seek.

===Chinese===
瓷砖管道须铺设得笔直而水平，

但当然要有轻微的倾斜；

朝向沟渠或小溪倾斜，

在那里它寻求通往大海之路。

===Original===
'Tis true that fiercer rages floods,

Since country it was stripp'd of woods,

And rivers they do broader spread,

With numerous tile drains quicker fed.

===Chinese===
确实洪水比以往更加凶猛，

自从这片土地上的森林被砍伐殆尽，

河流变得更加宽阔，

无数的排水管使洪水更快地汇入。

===Original===
## Nickel Mines / 镍矿

Prospectors from all nations hurry

To locate mines in Sudbury;

To them the highest worldly fame

Is to secure good mining claim.

===Chinese===
来自各国的探矿者匆匆赶往

萨德伯里寻找矿藏；

对他们来说，世间最高的荣耀

莫过于获得一处好的矿权。

===Original===
No hardships daunt them in attempt,

A good mining claim for to pre-empt;

And the old miners here from Wales,

Of these mines tell glowing tales.

===Chinese===
没有什么艰难险阻能阻止他们

去抢占一处好的矿权；

那些来自威尔士的老矿工们，

对这些矿藏赞不绝口。

===Original===
Mining fortune oft is fickle,

But 'tis not so with mines of nickel;

And Uncle Sam thinks he would feel

His navy safe 'neath nickel steel.

===Chinese===
采矿的财运往往变幻无常，

但镍矿却并非如此；

山姆大叔认为他会感到

他的海军在镍钢的护佑下安然无恙。

===Original===
Railroads perform important function,

In opening mines at Sudbury Junction;

There if the rocks you do but tickle,

They smile and you behold the nickel.

===Chinese===
铁路在开发萨德伯里交汇处的矿山中

发挥着重要作用；

在那里，你只要轻轻挠一挠岩石，

它们就会微笑，让你看到闪亮的镍。

===Original===
And where the landscape late has been

Covered o'er with forest green;

The fire has left charred stumps of pine,

And there too is the glittering mine.34

===Chinese===
而那片曾经覆盖着

苍翠森林的风景之地；

大火留下了烧焦的松树桩，

在那里也有闪闪发光的矿藏。34

===Original===
## Mild Christmas / 温和的圣诞

On Christmas Day of eighty-nine,

The sun all day did pleasant shine;

The cows they would not eat their hay,

But o'er the pasture lands did stray.

===Chinese===
在八九年圣诞那天，

阳光整日和煦地照耀；

母牛不肯吃干草，

却在牧场上四处游荡。

===Original===
Such winter day is seldom seen,

Instead of white the fields were green;

Colts and young cattle they did play,

Happy as in the month of May.

===Chinese===
这样的冬日实属罕见，

田野不是白色而是绿色；

小马驹和小牛犊欢快嬉戏，

快乐得如同五月一般。

===Original===
But Canadians do delight,

To see the landscape robed in white;

To them the sweetest music dwells

In merry tinkle of sleigh bells.

===Chinese===
但加拿大人乐于看到

大地披上洁白的衣裳；

对他们而言，最悦耳的音乐

来自雪橇铃铛欢快的叮当声。

===Original===
And land doth more abundant yield,

When the snow mantle covers field;

And farmer quick can load his sleigh,

And cheerful drive o'er icy way.

===Chinese===
当白雪覆盖大地，

土地会更加丰产；

农夫可以迅速装好雪橇，

欢快地驱车驶过冰封之路。

===Original===
For true Canadians love the snow,

And like to hear old Boreas blow;

For with just pride they all do boast,

They love the winter's cold and frost.

===Chinese===
因为真正的加拿大人热爱冰雪，

喜欢听老北风呼啸；

他们都自豪地夸耀，

他们爱冬天的寒冷与霜冻。

===Original===
## The Steamboat / 蒸汽船

In early times the swift steamboat

O'er the broad ocean did not float,

But ships were wafted o'er the seas,

With sails inflated by the breeze.

===Chinese===
在早年间，快捷的蒸汽船

并未在广阔的大海上航行，

船只借助风力漂洋过海，

船帆被微风吹得鼓胀。

===Original===
In progress they could not prevail,

If 'gainst ship's haad did blow the gale;

And oft times for many a day

Becalmed on ocean's breast they lay.

===Chinese===
若逆风航行，它们便寸步难行；

常常一连许多日子，

它们在大洋的胸膛上静止不动。

===Original===
But now the mighty monarch, steam,

O'er land and sea doth rule supreme;

Steamship proudly ploughs the ocean,

Independent of wind's motion.

===Chinese===
但如今，强大的君王——蒸汽，

在陆地和海洋上至高无上地统治；

蒸汽船骄傲地犁过大海，

无需依赖风的运动。

===Original===
From Scottish hill when I was boy,

I cried out in my childish joy;

The thought which filled my heart with glee,

America, I plainly see.

===Chinese===
当我还是苏格兰山丘上的男孩，

我以童真的喜悦欢呼；

一个念头充满我心令我欣喜，

美洲，我清清楚楚地看到了。

===Original===
Then I was told to my amaze,

That it was far beyond my gaze;

The chart to me was then unfurled,

To prove I could not see whole world.

===Chinese===
然后我惊异地被告知，

它远在我视线之外；

地图在我面前展开，

证明我无法看到整个世界。

===Original===
The truth to me was great surprise then,

That world was greater than horizon;

Our views through life they thus expand,

Beholding God's creation grand.

===Chinese===
那真相曾令我大为惊讶，

世界竟比地平线更加辽阔；

我们的人生视野因此不断拓展，

目睹上帝宏伟的造化。

===Original===
## Duties of Life / 生活的责任

Life is greatly what we make it,

Through good and ill we must take it;

And we should not troubles borrow,

But strive to banish all our sorrow.

===Chinese===
生活很大程度上取决于我们如何度过，

好坏我们都必须承受；

我们不该自寻烦恼，

而应努力驱散一切悲伤。

===Original===
Each man for life should choose a mate,

And true love should cultivate;

For women unto us are given,

To transform the earth to Heaven.

===Chinese===
每个人都应为人生选择一位伴侣，

并培育真挚的爱情；

因为女性被赐予我们，

是为了将人间化为天堂。

===Original===
Abroad the men should never roam,

But make a Paradise of home;

Domestic bliss is truest joy,

With wife and little girl and boy.

===Chinese===
男人绝不应浪迹天涯，

而要把家庭变成乐园；

家庭的幸福是最真实的喜悦，

有妻子和儿女相伴。

===Original===
But all need to be forgiven,

And to put their trust in Heaven;

The truest joys are above,

Obtained alone but through Christ's Love.

===Chinese===
但所有人都需要被宽恕，

并将信任托付于天；

最真实的喜乐在天上，

唯有通过基督的爱才能获得。

===Original===
While doing right if you seem to fail,

You must not for a moment quail;

But at once banish all your fear,

Press nobly on in your career.

===Chinese===
当你行善似乎遭遇失败时，

你一刻也不应退缩；

而要立即驱散一切恐惧，

在你的人生道路上高贵地前行。

===Original===
More honor far then you will gain.

Because you strove right to obtain;

And as you have been dutiful,

You've won good, true and beautiful.

===Chinese===
你将赢得更多的荣耀，

因为你努力追求正义；

既然你已尽忠职守，

你便赢得了真善美。

===Original===
## Great Fire in Michigan / 密歇根大火

Farmer's Loss

Many a homestead it was burned,

And many a family sadly mourned,

The death of those were near and dear,

But fire the tangled bush did clear.

===Chinese===
农夫的损失

许多家园付之一炬，

许多家庭悲伤地哀悼

亲近之人的离世，

但大火清除了丛生的灌木。

===Original===
And soon our country was more wealthy,

Now already 'tis more healthy;

'Twas cheap way matted brush to clear,

But for the loss of friends so near.

===Chinese===
很快我们的家乡变得更加富裕，

如今也更加宜居；

清除纠结的灌丛虽然代价低廉，

却痛失了亲近的朋友。

===Original===
We lost our houso and barn and corn,

But for long we did not mourn;

For there came quick to our relief,

The nation's aid to soothe our grief.

===Chinese===
我们失去了房屋、谷仓和谷物，

但我们没有长久地悲伤；

因为国家的救助迅速到来，

抚慰我们的悲痛。

===Original===
It seems to me now but a dream,

How our lives were saved in a broad stream;

Our cattle and horse there we drove,

And bears and wolves were there, by jove.

===Chinese===
如今回想仿佛一场梦，

我们在宽阔的溪流中保住了性命；

我们将牛马赶往那里，

熊和狼也在那里，天哪。

===Original===
Though all were huddled in a jam,

Each wild beast was as quiet as lamb;

And there we were for three whole days,

Surrounded by the furious blaze.

===Chinese===
尽管所有人挤作一团，

每只野兽都温顺如羔羊；

我们在那里待了整整三天，

被凶猛的大火包围。

===Original===
Worse than the fire the awful smoke,

To death by hundreds it did choke;

Many thought it was judgment day,

When Heaven and earth would pass away.

===Chinese===
比大火更可怕的是那可怖的浓烟，

成百上千的人被窒息而死；

许多人以为那是审判日，

天地即将毁灭。

===Original===
## Horace Greely and Mrs. Staunton / 霍勒斯·格里利与斯汤顿夫人

During the late great civil war,

Which did the Grand Republic jar

From its circumference to its centre,

The grand old mnn he did venture.

===Chinese===
在那场伟大的内战期间，

它震动了伟大的共和国

从边疆到腹地，

这位了不起的老人挺身而出。

===Original===
While chatting with a lady freely,

I demand, said Horace Greely,

And do not think my question wanton,

But answer me, Mrs. Staunton,

===Chinese===
当与一位女士自由交谈时，

"我请问，"霍勒斯·格里利（Horace Greely）说道，

"请别以为我的问题轻佻，

请回答我，斯汤顿（Staunton）夫人，

===Original===
Would you go South and fight to quell,

Those bold men who do rebel;

If you got woman's suffrage,

Would you in deadly war engage.

===Chinese===
你愿意南下去战斗平息，

那些胆大妄为的叛乱分子吗；

如果你获得了女性参政权，

你愿意投身于致命的战争吗？"

===Original===
Our country now wants every man,

And of women too all we can;

Each to shoulder trusty rifle,

So South with us no more may trifle.

===Chinese===
我们国家现在需要每一个男人，

也需要我们能动员的所有女性；

每个人都扛起可靠的步枪，

这样南方再也不敢轻视我们。

===Original===
You're champion of woman's rights,

And should take part like men in flights;

Said she, I never could delight,

To slay a man in bloody fight.

===Chinese===
"你是女性权利的拥护者，

应当像男人一样参战；"

她说："我绝不会乐意

在血腥的战斗中杀死一个人。

===Original===
So my fair hands I'll not imbue,

But I will stay at home like you,

And urge the others on to war,

And keep from battle field afar.

===Chinese===
所以我不会用我洁白的双手

沾染鲜血，但我会像你一样留在家中，

催促别人上战场，

远离战场。

===Original===
But Mistress Staunton she was really

More robust than Horace Greely;

And she possessed a noble form,

And even now would face the storm.

===Chinese===
但斯汤顿夫人实际上

比霍勒斯·格里利更为强壮；

她有着高贵的体魄，

即使现在也能直面风暴。

===Original===
## Reflections After Reading "The Coming Race" and "Looking Backward" / 读《未来种族》与《回顾》有感

When the great Bulwer's pen did trace

The history of the coming race,

His hero to us hath unfurled

The glories of his new-found world.

===Chinese===
当伟大的布尔沃（Bulwer）执笔

描绘未来种族的历史，

他的主人公向我们展开了

他新发现世界的辉煌。

===Original===
This bold traveller he did venture

Far below our world's centre;

He entered through an ancient mine,

And to us doth new world define.

===Chinese===
这位勇敢的旅人冒险深入

远低于我们世界中心的地方；

他通过一座古老的矿井进入，

为我们描绘了一个全新的世界。

===Original===
Once all was discord and fierce wars,

Continuous engaged in jars,

Till they discovered great Vril power,

Then wars did cease from the same hour.

===Chinese===
曾经到处是纷争与激烈的战争，

无休止地争斗不休，

直到他们发现了伟大的弗里尔（Vril）之力，

战争便从那一刻起停止了。

===Original===
Mighty bolts from it were hurled,

Would soon have laid waste the world;

So for world's preservation,

At once they stopped desolation.

===Chinese===
强大的雷电从其中发射，

很快就会毁灭整个世界；

因此为了保全世界，

他们立刻停止了破坏。

===Original===
All wars by them ar now despised,

Unfit for nations civilized;

And peaceful glories they do sing,

And are not slaves to warlike king.

===Chinese===
所有的战争如今被他们所鄙弃，

文明的民族不应再有战争；

他们歌唱和平的荣耀，

不再做好战君王的奴隶。

===Original===
Bellamy describes a poor rose,

Where in cold swamp it feebly grows;

Transplanted to the heat and light,

Bush blossoms are a charming sight.

===Chinese===
贝拉米（Bellamy）描述了一朵可怜的玫瑰，

在寒冷的沼泽中无力地生长；

移植到温暖和光明之处，

灌丛花开是一道迷人的风景。

===Original===
## Raise High the Banner / 高举旗帜

July 1st, 1891.

The school children were trained by Messrs. McDiarmid and Underwood, the former being Principal of the Public School.

Raise high up the banner

O'er both school-house and manor,

For this glorious standard

Doth lead on in the vanguard.

===Chinese===
1891年7月1日。

学童们由麦克迪尔米德（McDiarmid）先生和安德伍德（Underwood）先生训练，前者是公立学校的校长。

高高举起旗帜，

飘扬在学校和庄园之上，

因为这面光荣的旗帜

引领着先锋的队伍。

===Original===
Teach our children to honor

This grand old famous banner;

Triumphant in many a field,

Our freedom it doth ever shield.

===Chinese===
教导我们的孩子尊崇

这面伟大而古老的旗帜；

它在许多战场上凯旋，

永远守护着我们的自由。

===Original===
Children sang in happy manner

At the raising of the banner;

They followed leader all so good,

For they were led by Underwood.

===Chinese===
孩子们快乐地歌唱

在旗帜升起之时；

他们追随优秀的领袖，

因为引领他们的是安德伍德。

===Original===
And our brave youths will man her,

This pure, unspotted banner;

Where the Maple Leaf entwines

And with Union Jack combines.

===Chinese===
我们勇敢的青年将守护它，

这面纯洁无瑕的旗帜；

在那里枫叶交织缠绕，

与联合杰克旗合而为一。

===Original===
## A Pleasing Illusion / 愉悦的幻觉

I saw a pleasing sight this spring,

Seem'd like a hundred ducks on wing;

Fluttering o'er the crystal water,

I felt like rippling o'er with laughter.

===Chinese===
今春我看到一幅悦目的景象，

仿佛百只鸭子展翅飞翔；

在清澈的水面上翩翩起舞，

我不禁笑得浑身颤动。

===Original===
When the truth it did awaken,

In silver ducks I was mistaken;

It was the sunbeam's merry dancing,

That on the water I saw glancing.

===Chinese===
当真相苏醒时，

我误以为是银色的鸭子；

那是阳光欢快的舞蹈，

我在水面上看到它闪烁。

===Original===
Rejoicing that from bonds of ice

They were free these pearls of price;

Though banks were bound with fringe of snow,

Which gave more lustre to the glow.

===Chinese===
它们为从冰封的束缚中获得自由

而欢欣——这些珍贵的珍珠；

虽然河岸仍镶着雪的花边，

却为光辉增添了更多的光彩。

===Original===
So 'tis no wonder that we sing

About the glories of the spring;

Each day fresh beauties will be seen,

When fields are cover'd o'er with green.

===Chinese===
所以我们歌唱春的辉煌

不足为奇；

当田野覆盖上绿色，

每天都会有新的美景呈现。

===Original===
Adieu to Winter brings no grief,

For we do long for bud and leaf;

Which are brought out by sunny showers,

And covers landscape with the flowers.

===Chinese===
与冬天告别并不悲伤，

因为我们渴望萌芽和新叶；

阳光和雨露将它们催生，

用鲜花覆盖大地的风景。

===Original===
The sun is glorious orb of day,

Refulgent is each sparkling ray;

The moon she is the Queen of Night,

Enthroned among the stars so bright.

===Chinese===
太阳是光辉灿烂的天体，

每一道闪耀的光线都璀璨夺目；

月亮是夜晚的女王，

端坐在璀璨的群星之中。

===Original===
## A Ghost Story / 一个鬼故事

There was a bonnie Scottish lass,

She had two lovers green as grass;

This fair maiden's name was Mary,

And she was playful as a fairy.

===Chinese===
有一位美丽的苏格兰姑娘，

她有两个嫩绿如草的追求者；

这位美丽少女名叫玛丽（Mary），

她活泼得如同仙女。

===Original===
Lovers haunted her night and day,

She could not make thera stay away;

Two then sought favor in her eyes,

But both alike she did despise.

===Chinese===
追求者们日夜缠着她，

她无法让他们远离；

两人都在她眼中寻求青睐，

但她对两人同样不屑一顾。

===Original===
She promised each one to reward,

If ho would go to the church yard,

Which was close by, the first dark night

So that the other he would fright.

===Chinese===
她许诺给每人一个奖赏，

如果他能在第一个漆黑的夜晚

去附近的教堂墓地，

好吓唬另一个人。

===Original===
No other road it could be found

To Mary's, save by burial ground;

She knew that each was coward loon,

When nights were dark without a moon.

===Chinese===
去玛丽家没有别的路可走，

只能经过墓地；

她知道两人都是懦弱的傻瓜，

在漆黑无月的夜晚。

===Original===
When each for to secure his bride,

He in the grave yard then did hide,

Each thinking 'twas a glorious lark

To frighten other in the dark.

===Chinese===
当每个人都想赢得新娘时，

他便躲在墓地里，

每个人都觉得在黑暗中

吓唬对方是个绝妙的玩笑。

===Original===
But both wore cowards--far from brave,

Each trembled alongside of grave

Expecting to see ghost arise,

Strange sights they float before their eyes.

===Chinese===
但两人都是懦夫——远非勇士，

每个人都颤抖着站在墓旁，

期待看到幽灵升起，

奇怪的景象在他们眼前飘浮。

===Original===
Both had around them sheets so white

Each wished the other for to fright,

While rival on the road did pass,

They both quick rose, but sad, alas.

===Chinese===
两人都披着洁白的床单，

都希望吓唬对方，

当对手从路上经过时，

两人迅速起身，但唉，可悲啊。

===Original===
Each saw a ghost; the one did faint,

The other's horror who can paint;

He there severelyy bruised his bones,

Madly rushing o'er grave stones.

===Chinese===
每个人都看到了一个鬼魂；一个晕倒了，

另一个的恐惧谁能描绘；

他在疯狂地冲过墓碑时

严重地摔伤了骨头。

===Original===
And he did get a fearful fall,

Jumping o'er the churchyard wall;

They both fell sick and lost their pride,

And neither went to claim his bride.

===Chinese===
他在翻越教堂墓地的围墙时

摔得不轻；

两人都病倒了，失去了尊严，

谁也没有去迎娶新娘。

===Original===
When they arose from their sick bed,

They heard the news that Mary wed

A brave and handsome farmer's son,

Who never from a ghost had run.

===Chinese===
当他们从病床上起来时，

他们听说玛丽嫁给了

一位勇敢英俊的农夫之子，

他从不因鬼魂而逃跑。

===Original===
## Sir Walter Scott / 沃尔特·司各特爵士

After long and mature reflection, I have no hesitation in saying that I think Sir Walter Scott is entitled to the second place in British Literature, immediately next to Shakespeare. After poring over the ballads and tales of James Mogg, which from their nature resemble Scott's, I made the mistake of attributing this couplet by Scott to James Hogg:

===Chinese===
经过长期而成熟的思考，我毫不犹豫地说，我认为沃尔特·司各特（Sir Walter Scott）爵士在英国文学中应位居第二，仅次于莎士比亚。在仔细研读了詹姆斯·莫格（James Mogg）的民谣和故事后——它们在性质上与司各特的相似——我犯了一个错误，将司各特的这副对句归于了詹姆斯·霍格（James Hogg）：

===Original===
### MacGregor's Gathering / 麦格雷戈的集结

While there's leaves on the forest or foam on the river,

MacGregor despite them, shall flourish forever.

===Chinese===
只要森林里还有树叶或河上还有泡沫，

麦格雷戈（MacGregor）便藐视一切，永世繁荣。

===Original===
## Big Stag / 大牡鹿

Each Highland forester doth brag

That he hath seen a mighty stag,

But sportsmen they do think it strange,

He never comes within their range.

===Chinese===
每个高地护林员都吹嘘

他见过一只巨大的牡鹿，

但猎手们觉得奇怪的是，

它从不进入他们的射程。

===Original===
But the keeper, Donald McKay,

Says he saw it just the other day,

And though gents can't it, dihcover,

It is there the same, whatever.

===Chinese===
但护林员唐纳德·麦凯（Donald McKay）说，

他前几天还见过它，

虽然先生们无法发现它，

它确实在那里，毫无疑问。

===Original===
The last I saw of wondrous stag,

He was grazing near yon crag,

In company with hia dear doe,

They seemed to love each other so.

===Chinese===
我最后一次看到那只奇妙的牡鹿，

它在那边的悬崖附近吃草，

与它亲爱的母鹿相伴，

它们看起来如此相爱。

===Original===
And playful sporting on the lawn,

'Long side of them their bonnie fawn;

I loved to see the creatures play,

From them I scarce could turn away.

===Chinese===
它们在草地上嬉戏玩耍，

旁边是它们可爱的小鹿；

我喜欢看这些生灵嬉戏，

几乎无法将目光移开。

===Original===
My heart grew tender, I did lag

So long I could not shoot the stag,

And forest still he yet adorns,

With magnificent head of horns.

===Chinese===
我的心变得柔软，我犹豫了太久，

无法射杀那只牡鹿，

它依然装点着森林，

顶着一头壮丽的鹿角。

===Original===
But I do fear some fatal day,

That some pot-hunter will him slay,

Who would be heedless of the woe

And sorrows of the fawn and doe.

===Chinese===
但我担心终有一天，

某个偷猎者会将它杀害，

那个人不会在意小鹿和母鹿

的悲伤与哀愁。

===Original===
## Eagle and Stag / 鹰与牡鹿

In lonely distant Highland glen,

Far away from abode of men,

A herd of deer they quietly graze,

No foe appears where e'er they gaze.

===Chinese===
在偏远孤寂的高地幽谷中，

远离人烟之处，

一群鹿安静地吃草，

在它们目光所及之处没有敌人出现。

===Original===
But there was one with flashing eyes

Was glaring on them from the skies,

Dooming the one whose antlered horns,

The monarch of the glen adorns.

===Chinese===
但有一双闪烁的眼睛

从天空俯视着它们，

瞄准了那个鹿角高耸的——

山谷之王。

===Original===
The king of birds while high in air,

Resolves that he will boldly dare

To attack this fine, noble beast.

And from its heart's blood have a feast.

===Chinese===
鸟中之王在高空盘旋，

决心大胆地

攻击这头美丽而高贵的野兽，

饮它心脏的血以飨盛宴。

===Original===
He circles downwards in his flight,

Floats calm, takes aim, then with his might,

Like bullet strikes the mighty deer,

Whose frame doth tremble all with fear.

===Chinese===
他盘旋着向下俯冲，

平稳地漂浮，瞄准目标，然后全力出击，

像子弹一样击中那头雄鹿，

它的全身因恐惧而颤抖。

===Original===
He doth not fear without a cause,

For eagle's buried deep his claws

In the neck of this noble buck,

And with sharp beak its blood doth suck.

===Chinese===
它并非无端恐惧，

因为鹰已将利爪深深扎入

这头高贵雄鹿的颈项，

用尖喙吮吸它的鲜血。

===Original===
For fierce bird with powerful swing,

Lashes the deer with each big wing;

And with strong eflibrts too it trys

For to pick out Voth of its eyes.

===Chinese===
凶猛的鸟用有力的翅膀

抽打着鹿；

它还用尽全力

试图啄出它的双眼。

===Original===
But now the deer doth know its foe,

And with its antlers strikes it blow;

Makes it sprawl among the heather,

And doth ruffle up each feather.

===Chinese===
但此刻鹿已认出了敌人，

用鹿角猛击它；

使它在石楠丛中跌跌撞撞，

弄乱了它的每一根羽毛。

===Original===
But bird quick takes aerial flight,

And descends with power and might

Further back on the deer's haunches,

Out of reach of antler branches.

===Chinese===
但鸟迅速升入空中，

然后带着力量俯冲而下

落在鹿的后腿上，

超出了鹿角能及的范围。

===Original===
But deer it was both wise and bold,

He down the hill with eagle rolled;

But bird he closer to it clung,

And from deer's side the blood it wrung.

===Chinese===
但鹿既聪明又勇敢，

它带着鹰滚下山坡；

但鸟紧紧抓住它不放，

从鹿的身侧榨取鲜血。

===Original===
The stag, though suffering cruel harm,

Yet not o'erwhelm'd with alarm;

He threw himself heels over head,

Until at last the eagle fled.

===Chinese===
牡鹿虽然遭受了残酷的伤害，

却没有被惊恐所淹没；

它翻了个跟头，

直到最后鹰飞走了。

===Original===
And high again in air he flew,

Once more the contest to renew,

But deer made his escape full good

In the shade of a neighboring wood.

===Chinese===
鹰再次高飞入空，

准备重新开始较量，

但鹿成功地逃脱了，

躲进了邻近树林的荫蔽中。

===Original===
And bird descending for its prey,

Finds it hath swiftly fled away;

Eagle then like a hungry sinner

Had to seek elsewhere for its dinner.

===Chinese===
鹰俯冲下来寻找猎物，

发现它已迅速逃走；

于是鹰像个饥饿的罪人

不得不另寻晚餐。

===Original===
And now it homeward takes its flight,

Its golden plumes in a sad plight,

To a high rock where its throbbing breast

May there find rest in its own nest.

===Chinese===
现在它振翅归巢，

金色的羽毛凌乱不堪，

飞向一块高耸的岩石，

在那里它悸动的胸脯

可以在自己的巢中安息。

===Original===
And soon around the antler'd deer,

The does do crowd his heart to cheer,

Rejoicing he was so brave

As to drive off the winged knave.

===Chinese===
很快，围绕着这头有角的牡鹿，

母鹿们挤在一起为它鼓舞，

欣喜它如此勇敢，

赶走了那个长翅膀的无赖。

===Original===
## Fairy Tale / 童话

Among the hills lives John McCrae,

An honest man so all do say;

John and his wife live together

'Mong the hills and blooming heather.

===Chinese===
在群山之间住着约翰·麦凯（John McCrae），

一个公认的老实人；

约翰和他的妻子一起生活，

在群山与盛开的石楠之间。

===Original===
On their small faim they do keep

A cow and a few goats and sheep;

They own a little Highland horse,

Which ploughs and draws the peats from moss.

===Chinese===
在他们的小农场里他们养着

一头奶牛和几只山羊和绵羊；

他们拥有一匹小小的高地马，

用来犁地和从沼泽中拖取泥炭。

===Original===
For they never saw a coal fire,

And peats give heat all they require;

Peat fire makes best Highland whisky,

Which doth make a man so frisky.

===Chinese===
因为他们从未见过煤火，

泥炭提供了他们所需的一切热量；

泥炭火能酿出最好的高地威士忌，

让人精神焕发。

===Original===
John is a crofter in Skye,

May better days on him draw nigh;

Yet John he did not inherit

Any discontented spirit.

===Chinese===
约翰是斯凯岛上的一名佃农，

愿美好的日子向他靠近；

然而约翰并没有继承

任何不满的精神。

===Original===
But happy with his humble lot,

His little croft and poor turf cot;

He always to the Lord gives praise,

Though but a poor crop he doth raise.

===Chinese===
但他安于卑微的命运，

他的小块佃地和简陋的草皮小屋；

他总是赞美主，

尽管他只种出了微薄的收成。

===Original===
He never travelled far abroad,

And worships still his father's God;

From modern thoughts he is quite free,

And newspapers doth seldom see.

===Chinese===
他从未远行，

仍然敬拜他祖先的上帝；

他完全不受现代思想的影响，

也几乎不看报纸。

===Original===
He believes the tales his Granny told

To him long since in days of old,

And his wife, kind-hearted Mary,

Believes in both witch and fairy.

===Chinese===
他相信很久以前他奶奶

在往昔岁月中讲给他听的故事，

而他善良的妻子玛丽，

既相信女巫也相信仙子。

===Original===
She sweeps her hearth so clean each night,

For fairies in bright fire delight;

And they love to see all things neat,

Those pretty little creatures sweet.

===Chinese===
她每晚把壁炉打扫得干干净净，

因为仙子喜欢明亮的炉火；

她们喜欢看到一切整洁有序，

那些可爱的小精灵们。

===Original===
So to the cot of John McCrae

They every night do wend their way

For to view the peat fire burn,

And to help his wife to churn.

===Chinese===
所以她们每晚都前往

约翰·麦凯的小屋，

去看泥炭火燃烧，

并帮助他的妻子搅制黄油。

===Original===
Neighbors great jealousy display,

They can't make butter like McCrae,

For the fairies have the power

Of making all the milk turn sour.

===Chinese===
邻居们极为嫉妒，

他们做不出像麦凯家那样好的黄油，

因为仙子有力量

让所有的牛奶变酸。

===Original===
One moonlight night old John McCrae,

He in the glen saw fairies play;

The prettiest sight he ever seen,

While they did dance upon the green.

===Chinese===
一个月光皎洁的夜晚，老约翰·麦凯，

他在山谷中看到仙子们嬉戏；

那是他见过的最美的景象，

她们在草地上翩翩起舞。

===Original===
And John doth solemn pass his word

They were as small as humming bird;

When he these charmers did behold,

They were clad in green and gold.

===Chinese===
约翰庄严地保证

它们小如蜂鸟；

当他注视这些迷人的小精灵时，

她们身着绿色和金色的衣裳。

===Original===
The most charming one upon the green,

She was just crowned the fairy queen,

She told John she loved his wife

Because her home it knew no strife.

===Chinese===
草地上最迷人的那一位，

刚刚被加冕为仙子女王，

她告诉约翰她喜欢他的妻子，

因为她的家中从无争吵。

===Original===
But she asked John for a reward,

She said Mary's bannocks were too hard,

And that the faries loved to eat

Little nibs of softer meat.

===Chinese===
但她要求约翰给她一个奖赏，

她说玛丽的烤饼太硬了，

仙子们喜欢吃

柔软的小点心。

===Original===
So fully John he told Mary

Of the strange request of fairy,

So now each day she doth bake

A little tiny griddle cake,

===Chinese===
于是约翰把仙子的

奇怪请求完完全全告诉了玛丽，

所以现在她每天都烘烤

一块小小的煎饼，

===Original===
In morning fairies they have flown,

And the little cake too is gone;

But wicked people full of vice

Say that the cake is eat by mice.

===Chinese===
到了早晨仙子们已经飞走，

小煎饼也不见了；

但邪恶而堕落的人们

说煎饼是被老鼠吃掉的。

===Original===
But this John's heart it snd doth grieve

That people should themselves deceive;

It hath been so since Adam's fall,

Some believe much, some not at all.

===Chinese===
但这件事让约翰的心悲伤，

人们竟然如此自欺欺人；

自亚当堕落以来便是如此，

有些人深信不疑，有些人全然不信。

===Original===
So now farewell to John McCrae,

May we meet him some other day,

For to our heart it is relief

To find a man with old belief.

===Chinese===
现在向约翰·麦凯道别吧，

愿我们改日再与他相见，

因为发现一个坚守古老信仰的人，

是我们心中的慰藉。

===Original===
Some folks to beauty they are blind,

So full of selfishness the mind;

And others happy to catch gleam

Of the green field or hill or stream.

===Chinese===
有些人对美视而不见，

心中满是自私自利；

而另一些人却为捕捉到

绿地、山丘或溪流的光芒而欣喜。

===Original===
## Address to the Scottish Clans at Woodstock / 在伍德斯托克对苏格兰氏族的致辞

June 19th, 1890.

Scotia's Sons! We love to meet thee,

And with hearty feelings greet thee;

You have come in a right good flock

To this progressive town, Woodstock.

===Chinese===
1890年6月19日。

苏格兰的儿女们！我们乐于与你们相聚，

满怀热忱地迎接你们；

你们成群结队来到

这座进步的城镇——伍德斯托克。

===Original===
And Scottish clans, noble order,

Here they meet from o'er the border;

From greatest of Republics, south,

That vast land of wondrous growth.

===Chinese===
苏格兰氏族，高贵的秩序，

他们从边境的那边汇聚于此；

从南方最伟大的共和国来，

那片辽阔而奇迹般成长的土地。

===Original===
We will not call them foreign nation,

For they are our own blood relation,

And this gathering all so grand

Is welcomed by Clan Sutherland.

===Chinese===
我们不会称他们为异邦人，

因为他们是我们血浓于水的亲人，

这场盛大的聚会

受到了萨瑟兰氏族的欢迎。

===Original===
The Highland men of Illinois,

Tn our old age increased our joys,

Making us an honorary member,

Their kindness we will e'er remember.

===Chinese===
伊利诺伊的高地人，

在我们晚年增添了我们的欢乐，

推选我们为荣誉会员，

他们的善意我们将永远铭记。

===Original===
And may you often meet together,

From land of whin and broom and heather;

Wallace, and Bruce, and Burns, and Scott,

Are names should never be forgot.

===Chinese===
愿你们常常相聚，

从金雀花和石楠丛生的土地而来；

华莱士、布鲁斯、彭斯和司各特，

这些名字永远不应被遗忘。

===Original===
And Zorra's fame is heard afar,

Victorious in each tug of war;

All loved the little boys and girls

In Highland dress and waving curls.

===Chinese===
佐拉（Zorra）的美名远扬，

在每次拔河比赛中获胜；

所有人都喜爱穿着高地服饰、

卷发飘飘的小男孩和小女孩们。

===Original===
And you have chose Canadian

For the chief of your Royal Clan,

And you will find that Sutherland,

Is one who ably will command.35

===Chinese===
你们选择了一位加拿大人

担任你们皇家氏族的首领，

你们会发现萨瑟兰

是一位能干的指挥官。35

===Original===
## Lines on Colonel Wonham / 咏沃纳姆上校

Formerly of Ingersoll. He spent the last years of his life in Winnipeg. These lines were published at the time of the Fenian Raid:

===Chinese===
原英格索尔人。他在温尼伯度过了人生的最后岁月。这些诗句发表于芬尼亚突袭时期：

===Original===
When Wonham got orders

To march to the borders,

His boys they were ready,

And fell in quite steady.

===Chinese===
当沃纳姆（Wonham）接到命令

向边境进军时，

他的弟兄们已整装待发，

整齐地列队站好。

===Original===
They first march'd to Woodstock

To prepare for war's shock,

And soon camped at Windsor,

Facing American shore.

===Chinese===
他们首先进军伍德斯托克

为战争的冲击做准备，

很快驻扎在温莎，

面向美国海岸。

===Original===
## Murder of a Young Englishman / 谋杀年轻英国人

By one of his own countrymen, a few miles east of Woodstock, February, 1890.

Death of Benwell and execution of Birchall.36 Mr. Perry was Sheriff at that time; now Mr. Brady holds this position, and a son of the late sheriff is Deputy.

===Chinese===
1890年2月，在伍德斯托克以东几英里处，被其同胞所杀害。

本韦尔之死和伯查尔之死。36 佩里先生当时是警长；现在由布雷迪先生担任此职，已故警长的儿子是副手。

===Original===
Now a sad tale we hiive to tell:

A few miles east, in Blenheim swamp,

So cruel slain was young Benwell,

Whose body lay 'mong snow and damp.

===Chinese===
现在我们要讲述一个悲伤的故事：

在东边几英里处的布伦海姆沼泽，

年轻的本韦尔被残忍杀害，

他的尸体躺在冰雪与潮湿之中。

===Original===
He was a well-bred English youth,

Hoping to own Canadian farm,

But his countryman devoid of truth,

He did plot his deadly harm.

===Chinese===
他是一个有教养的英国青年，

希望能拥有一个加拿大农场，

但他的同胞背信弃义，

密谋对他下了毒手。

===Original===
Sad fate, the world did appal,

That he by bullet thus should die;

Fired by protended friend, Birchall,

Who swung for it on gallows high.

===Chinese===
悲惨的命运令世人震惊，

他竟这样死于子弹之下；

被假朋友伯查尔（Birchall）射杀，

他为此被绞死在高高的绞刑架上。

===Original===
## Fair Day at Embro / 恩布罗集市日

On the Middle Branch of the Thames.

Of Scottish names a great array

We meet at Erabro on Fair Day;

McDonald, McKenzie and McKay,

And Murray, Ross and Sutherland,

And Munroes round you stand.

===Chinese===
在泰晤士河的中支流上。

我们在恩布罗集市日齐聚一堂，

阵容强大的苏格兰姓氏；

麦克唐纳、麦肯齐和麦凯，

还有默里、罗斯和萨瑟兰，

门罗斯家族围绕在你身旁。

===Original===
Your lassies worthy of our song,

And youth are famed for muscle strong

For they can pull both hard and long;

And their fame is heard afar.

Victorious in each tug of war.

===Chinese===
你们的姑娘们值得我们歌唱，

小伙子们以强健的肌肉闻名，

他们可以又用力又持久地拔河；

他们的美名远扬，

在每次拔河中所向披靡。

===Original===
But these battles bring no sorrow

To the hardy youth of Zorra,

And no tears to eyes of Flora;

They are not deadly tugs of war,

And they leave no wounds nor scar.

===Chinese===
但这些较量不会给

佐拉坚毅的青年带来悲伤，

也不会让弗洛拉（Flora）流泪；

这不是致命的战争角逐，

不会留下伤口或伤疤。

===Original===
## Decoration Day / 阵亡将士纪念日

The following lines were delivered by me at ihe first Decoration of Graves in Ingersoll. I took part in a ceremony of the same kind in Woodstock in 1890, and such was the superabundance of flowers there that I reserved a boquet for the grave of young McKenzie, who fell nobly fighting for his country at the Battle of Ridgeway. The County of Oxford has erected a fine monument, with a substantial iron railing around the hero's grave, on the banks of the Thames.

===Chinese===
以下诗行由我在英格索尔首次扫墓仪式上朗诵。1890年我参加了伍德斯托克同样性质的仪式，那里的鲜花如此充裕，我特意留了一束献给年轻的麦肯齐之墓——他在里奇韦战役中为国英勇捐躯。牛津郡在泰晤士河畔为这位英雄的墓竖立了一座精美的纪念碑，并围上了坚固的铁栏杆。

===Original===
This is the opening dedication

Of annual day of decoration,

And thus each year will brothers meet

To shed the bloom and perfume sweet;

For they will have a noble aim

In meeting on the banks of Thame.

===Chinese===
这是年度装饰日的

开幕致辞，

兄弟们每年都会在此相聚，

献上花朵和芬芳的花束；

因为他们将在泰晤士河畔

有着崇高的目标。

===Original===
In the States they decorate grave

Of each departed soldier brave,

But here in Ontario West

We decorate where brothers rest,

Though some are unknown to fame

They peaceful rest on banks of Thame.

===Chinese===
在美国，他们为每一位

勇敢的退伍军人扫墓，

但在西部安大略，

我们为长眠于此的兄弟们装饰，

虽然有些人的名字不为人知，

他们安息在泰晤士河畔。

===Original===
No more their grave is place of gloom,

But scene of fragrance and bloom;

No more the tomb is dismal cave

For flowers each year will o'er it wave;

In sweet remembrance each name

Will live beside the silver Thame.

===Chinese===
他们的墓地不再是阴郁之所，

而是芬芳与花团锦簇的景象；

坟墓不再是阴暗的洞穴，

因为鲜花每年都会在上面飘扬；

每个名字都将在银色的泰晤士河畔

因甜蜜的怀念而永存。

===Original===
Flowers here will shed their rich perfume

And thus dispel the dismal gloom;

Departed's memory we cherish,

And their names shall never perish,

While doth flow the pleasant stream
