O'er pebbly bed, the crystal Thame.

===Chinese===
鲜花将在这里散发浓郁的芬芳，

驱散阴郁的忧伤；

我们珍藏逝者的记忆，

他们的名字永远不会消亡，

只要清澈的泰晤士河

在卵石河床上流淌。

===Original===
## Strange Water Wheels at Beachville / 比奇维尔的奇异水车

Invented by John Cross, a laborer, while working there.

It is the cause of a good deal of curiosity on the part of travellers passing along, and observing wheels revolving in the river, evidently driving some machinery which they cannot discover conveniently, as they are driving pumps low down in the quarry beds. Good building stone as well as stone for the perpetual kilns are obtained convenient to two lines of railway depots and sidings.

===Chinese===
由工人约翰·克罗斯（John Cross）在那里工作时发明。

经过的旅人看到河中旋转的轮子时感到十分好奇，这些轮子显然在驱动某些他们不便看到的机械，因为它们在采石场底部驱动水泵。优质的建筑石材和用于永久性窑炉的石材在两条铁路的车站和侧线附近即可获得。

===Original===
Here in the river you descry

Wheels revolve to keep quarries dry;

Elsewhere such pumps are drove by steam,

But here by current of the stream.

===Chinese===
在这里的河中你可以看到

轮子旋转以保持采石场干燥；

在别处，这样的泵由蒸汽驱动，

但在这里，由溪流驱动。

===Original===
Easy it the water carries,

And leaves dry the beds of quarries;

Continuous at perpetual kiln,

You withdraw lime and with stone fill.

===Chinese===
它轻松地将水排走，

让采石场的坑底保持干燥；

在永久窑炉中持续运作，

你取出石灰并装满石料。

===Original===
## Fight in a Cave / 洞穴搏斗

This is a tale of a hunter brave,

He sought for refuge in a cave

To escape a furious storm,

And he built fire to keep him warm.

===Chinese===
这是一个勇敢猎人的故事，

他寻找洞穴避难

以逃脱猛烈的风暴，

他生火取暖。

===Original===
Dry leaves and twigs made fire and smoke,

Which wild beasts' fury did provoke;

By its growl he knew 'twas bear,

But bruin's fury he would dare.

===Chinese===
干树叶和树枝生出火和烟，

激怒了野兽；

从咆哮声他知道那是熊，

但他敢于直面棕熊的怒火。

===Original===
For he determined not to run,

And he gave it a shot from gun,

But this the bear it did enrage,

Resolved in war for to engage.

===Chinese===
因为他决心不逃跑，

他开了一枪打中了它，

但这激怒了那头熊，

它决心投入战斗。

===Original===
For it came shambling along,

To have revenge for suflering wrong;

Hunter with the butt of rifle

Struck and stunned him a trifle.

===Chinese===
它蹒跚地走过来，

要为受到的伤害复仇；

猎人用步枪的枪托

打了它一下，让它有点眩晕。

===Original===
Once more he aimed, it glanced on rock,

And he was left with broken stock;

Now the bear it doth hunter seize,

And gives him many a hug and squeeze.

===Chinese===
他再次瞄准，子弹弹到岩石上，

枪托断裂了；

现在熊抓住了猎人，

给了他许多拥抱和挤压。

===Original===
The struggle is so close for life,

The hunter cannot use his knife;

But the nimble hunter bold

Soon made the bear relax his hold.

===Chinese===
生死搏斗如此激烈，

猎人无法拔出刀来；

但敏捷勇敢的猎人

很快迫使熊松开了爪子。

===Original===
By a sudden spring bear he rolls,

With his nose in burning coals;

His nostrils full of fire and smoke,

Poor Brvin it doth almost choke.

===Chinese===
他猛一用力将熊翻倒，

让它的鼻子埋进燃烧的煤中；

它的鼻孔里满是烟火，

可怜的棕熊差点窒息。

===Original===
The hunter now doth use his knife,

Stabs bear in heart and ends the strife;

So hunter he did victory win,

And spoils of battle in grand skin.

===Chinese===
猎人现在拔出了刀，

刺入熊的心脏结束了争斗；

猎人赢得了胜利，

战利品是一张漂亮的大熊皮。

===Original===
## The Present Time / 当代

1890.

This is no age for mystery,

But full of eventful history;

Not a time for stupid dreaming,

When the lightning it is gleaming.

===Chinese===
1890年。

这不是一个神秘的时代，

而是充满重大历史事件的时代；

不是痴人说梦的时候，

当闪电正在闪耀。

===Original===
A brighter light doth now surpass,

And throws in shade once favored gas;

Now it is not an idle dream,

Predicting greater power than steam.

===Chinese===
更明亮的光芒如今已经超越，

将一度受宠的煤气灯抛入阴影；

如今这不再是空想的梦境，

预示着比蒸汽更强大的力量。

===Original===
But perhaps it is not wise

For us to philosophise,

As we might get in water deep,

Or find a hill for us too steep.

===Chinese===
但也许对我们来说

高谈阔论并不明智，

因为可能会陷入深水，

或发现山峰太过陡峭。

===Original===
Some think it is almost a crime

To write a simple kind of rhyme

That every one can understand;

They love a style is lofty, grand.

===Chinese===
有些人认为写一首

人人都能理解的简单韵文

几乎是一种罪过；

他们喜欢高远而宏伟的风格。

===Original===
Which no one can comprehend;

Without beginning or an end,

And if it only is obscure,

To them it is a proof full sure.

===Chinese===
没有人能够理解的那种；

没有开头也没有结尾，

如果它足够晦涩，

对他们来说就是确凿的证明。

===Original===
The authors have a mighty mind,

And to philosophy inclined;

They adore the metaphysical,

And humble thoughts do them appal.

===Chinese===
这些作者有着强大的头脑，

倾向于哲学思考；

他们崇拜形而上学，

朴素的想法令他们恐惧。

===Original===
But though our style it may seem rude,

We love for to be understood;

And though we have no skill nor art,

We hope to reach some honest heart.

===Chinese===
但尽管我们的风格可能显得粗糙，

我们渴望被理解；

虽然我们没有技巧也没有才华，

我们希望能触动某些真诚的心灵。

===Original===
Our old style walk will soon he gone,

In place of wood wo will have stone;

And water works from lofty tower

Blessings on the town will shower.

===Chinese===
我们老旧的木板人行道很快将消失，

取而代之的将是石材；

从高塔而来的自来水工程

将给城镇洒下祝福。

===Original===
## The Rescue / 营救

In a deep cleft among the hills

There flows one of those mountain rills;

In fine weather a scene of charms,

But in a storm 'tis all alarms.

===Chinese===
在群山深处的一道裂谷中，

流淌着一条山间小溪；

晴天时风景如画，

暴风雨中却处处险象环生。

===Original===
And woe befalls those seek to trudge,

And strive to cross its rustic bridge;

The roadway it becomes deep rut,

And spot is named the babies' cut.

===Chinese===
那些试图跋涉前行、

奋力跨越简陋桥梁的人将遭殃；

道路变得深陷泥泞，

那个地方被称为"婴儿峡"。

===Original===
Minister's only child had died,

And the poor father mourned and sighed,

And found no rest when child was buried,

Grave robbers then mourners worried.

===Chinese===
牧师的独子去世了，

可怜的父亲悲痛叹息，

孩子下葬后他仍不得安宁，

盗墓者让丧亲之人更加忧心。

===Original===
About the middle of first night

The preacher woke in a great fright,

For there raged a furious storm,

And he thought he saw the form

===Chinese===
在第一个夜晚的午夜时分，

牧师在巨大的恐惧中惊醒，

外面狂风暴雨肆虐，

他觉得看到了一个身影

===Original===
Of his sweet child alive in grave.

Shall he the rain aod lightning brave

Or humble submit and kiss the rod,

Obedient to the will of God?

===Chinese===
是他亲爱的孩子在坟墓中活着。

他应该冒着风雨和闪电冲出去，

还是谦卑地屈服，亲吻惩罚之杖，

顺从上帝的旨意？

===Original===
Shall he who others tries to fill

With resignation to God's will,

Forget the precepts he has taught,

And at once set them all at naught?

===Chinese===
他这个努力劝导他人

顺从上帝旨意的人，

会忘记他所教导的训诫，

立刻将它们全部抛弃吗？

===Original===
But he rushed to burial ground,

And to his wonder there he found

Men with a light who quick as dart,

Did throw a bundle in their cart.

===Chinese===
但他冲向墓地，

令他惊异的是，他发现

有人拿着灯，动作快如飞镖，

将一个包袱扔进了他们的推车。

===Original===
Then at a furious race they drove,

But clergyman impelled by love

For his dear child, did quickly follow,

And o'ertook them in the hollow,

===Chinese===
他们疯狂地驾车飞奔，

但牧师出于对他亲爱的孩子的爱，

迅速追赶上去，

在山谷中追上了他们，

===Original===
Where lightning flashed and thunder roared,

And the stream with fury poured;

Here the preacher shouted wild,

"Give me back my darling child."

===Chinese===
在那里闪电划过，雷声轰鸣，

溪水汹涌奔流；

牧师疯狂地喊叫：

"把我亲爱的孩子还给我。"

===Original===
The horse affrighted plunged in river,

And the men were lost forever;

The minister he heard the crash

As 'gainst the broken bridge they dash.

===Chinese===
受惊的马冲入河中，

那些人永远消失了；

牧师听到一声巨响，

那是他们撞上了断裂的桥。

===Original===
But though a great storm did prevail,

Yet he heard a sad human wail,

Sound coming from the bridge's rack,

He climbed o'er timber and found sack.

===Chinese===
尽管暴风雨肆虐，

他还是听到了一阵悲伤的哀号，

声音从桥的残骸中传来，

他爬过木料，发现了一个麻袋。

===Original===
Then his heart got reconciled,

Sack proved to hold his darling child;

The parents' joy who can tell,

To find their child alive and well.

===Chinese===
他的心终于安定了，

麻袋里装着的正是他亲爱的孩子；

谁能描述父母的喜悦，

发现他们的孩子安然无恙。

===Original===
Child had been buried in a swoon,

Next danger was that it might drown;

But Providence on parents smiled,

And they were blessed with their child.

===Chinese===
孩子被埋时只是昏厥了，

接下来的危险是可能会被淹死；

但天意眷顾了这对父母，

他们得到了上天赐予的孩子。

===Original===
## Lady Ann / 安妮夫人

A Highland clan were in despair

Because their chief he had no heir,

And even the women of the clan,

Enchantments tried 'gainst Lady Ann.

===Chinese===
一个高地氏族陷入了绝望，

因为他们的首领没有继承人，

甚至氏族中的女性们

也对安妮夫人（Lady Ann）施了咒语。

===Original===
They consulted prophets and seers,

And they at last did quell their fears;

If the clan would pilgrimage pay

To Saint Botham's Shrine on Christmas Day.

===Chinese===
他们请教了先知和预言者，

最终平息了他们的恐惧；

如果氏族在圣诞日

前往圣博瑟姆圣殿朝圣。

===Original===
If they but visited this shrine,

Then would a miracle divine

Make Chief and Lady happy pair,

And they would be bless'd with an heir.

===Chinese===
如果他们只是造访这座圣殿，

就会有神圣的奇迹

使首领和夫人成为幸福的一对，

他们将得到一个继承人的祝福。

===Original===
The day arriv'd, it chill'd their blood,

For river was a mighty flood,

And through the storm they must trudge,

Then cross o'er a dangerous bridge.

===Chinese===
那一天到来时，它让他们血液凝固，

因为河水暴涨成了洪流，

他们必须在暴风雨中跋涉，

然后跨越一座危险的桥。

===Original===
Though great dimage the flood had made,

They reached the shrine, devotions paid;

And homeward now they wend their way

To cross again the flooded Spey.

===Chinese===
尽管洪水造成了巨大破坏，

他们还是到达了圣殿，完成了祈祷；

现在他们踏上归途

再次穿越洪水泛滥的斯佩河（Spey）。

===Original===
Bridge was built of rough long plank,

Which halfway reached from bank to bank;

But while crossing this flooded stream

Lady Ann gave a sudden scream.

===Chinese===
桥是由粗糙的长木板搭建的，

只架到河中间；

但在穿越这条泛滥的溪流时，

安妮夫人突然尖叫起来。

===Original===
For Carlock push'd her in the river,

And she was borne away forever;

So at least old ruffian thought,

Though wicked schemes oft come to naught.

===Chinese===
因为卡洛克（Carlock）将她推入河中，

她被永远冲走了；

至少这个老恶棍是这么想的，

尽管邪恶的阴谋往往化为泡影。

===Original===
Superstitious was old Carlock;

He believed in witch and warlock,

And that oft times departed spirit

Would its home again inherit.

===Chinese===
老卡洛克迷信巫术；

他相信女巫和男巫，

相信逝去的灵魂

常常会重返故里。

===Original===
One night he sees a horrid sight,

It was Lady Ann dress'd in white;

To ruffian a deadly shock,

He flung himself from castle rock.

===Chinese===
一天晚上他看到一个恐怖的景象，

是穿着白衣的安妮夫人；

这对恶棍是致命的一击，

他从城堡的悬崖上跳了下去。

===Original===
The chief at first o'ercome with fear,

Then to his heart press'd Lady dear,

Whom he had nivnn up for lost,

And thought at first she was a ghost.

===Chinese===
首领起初被恐惧征服，

然后将亲爱的夫人拥入怀中，

他曾以为她已经失去，

起初还以为她是个幽灵。

===Original===
But the kind miller of Clack Clam,

With the great flood had lost his dam;

Himself, and son, and wife and daughter,

All were working near the water.

===Chinese===
但克拉克·克拉姆（Clack Clam）的善良磨坊主，

在大洪水中失去了水坝；

他自己、儿子、妻子和女儿

都在水边劳作。

===Original===
Quite suddenly the miller's son

Ran to the house to get his gun;

He saw what he methought was swan,

Which prov'd to be the Lady Ann.

===Chinese===
突然间，磨坊主的儿子

跑回家去拿枪；

他看到一只他以为是天鹅的东西，

结果证明是安妮夫人。

===Original===
At first they thought that she was dead,

And carried her to a warm bed;

There full soon she did recover,

And was happier than ever.

===Chinese===
起初他们以为她已经死了，

将她抬到一张温暖的床上；

她很快就苏醒了，

比以往任何时候都更幸福。

===Original===
After her floating on the waters,

She gave birth to sons and daughters,

And ne'er had cause for to repine

Her pilgrimage into the shrine.

===Chinese===
在她漂浮在水上之后，

她生下了儿女们，

再也没有理由后悔

她的朝圣之旅。

===Original===
## Tom Norton / 汤姆·诺顿

A Tale of Shipwreck

That staunch good ship, *The Albatross*,

Dismantled in a storm did toss,

For the wind it blew terrific,

In the Southern Pacific.

===Chinese===
一个海难的故事

那艘坚固的好船"信天翁号"，

在风暴中被拆散颠簸，

因为风刮得猛烈可怕，

在南太平洋上。

===Original===
The ship went down and of the crew

Those that were saved of them were few;

In various ways they strove to float

On chicken coop and spar and boat.

===Chinese===
船沉没了，船员中

获救的人寥寥无几；

他们以各种方式试图

在鸡笼、圆材和小船上漂浮。

===Original===
And Tom Norton, the second mate,

Met with a romantic fate;

Most of his poor comrades sank,

But safe he floated on his plank.

===Chinese===
汤姆·诺顿（Tom Norton），二副，

遭遇了传奇般的命运；

他大多数可怜的同伴都沉没了，

但他安全地漂浮在木板上。

===Original===
Although he was in a sad plight,

Tossed about by day and night,

For he had little food or drink.

Yet brave Tom's courage did not sink.

===Chinese===
尽管他处境悲惨，

日夜被海浪颠簸，

食物和饮水都所剩无几。

但勇敢的汤姆没有气馁。

===Original===
The seventh day with joy he sees

An island cover'd with palm trees,

And as the sea was now quite calm,

He floated slow to isle of palm.

===Chinese===
第七天他欣喜地看到

一座长满棕榈树的岛屿，

海面已相当平静，

他缓缓漂向棕榈之岛。

===Original===
Here nature pleasantly doth smile

On this dear little lonely isle;

The natives soon do him espy,

And cast on him a longing eye.

===Chinese===
大自然在这座可爱的小孤岛上

愉快地微笑；

土著人很快发现了他，

用渴望的目光注视着他。

===Original===
They thought his flesh they would like raw,

The first white man they ever saw;

His skin to them seem'd sweet and clean,

But they found he was far too lean.

===Chinese===
他们想尝尝他的生肉，

这是他们见过的第一个白人；

他的皮肤在他们看来白净可爱，

但他们发现他太瘦了。

===Original===
So after talking o'er the matter,

They resolved to make him fatter,

And they put him in a bamboo cage,

And the king's daughter did engage

===Chinese===
经过一番商议，

他们决定把他养胖，

他们把他关进一个竹笼，

国王的女儿被指派

===Original===
The beautiful and youthful Wambo

To feed captive in the cage of bamboo;

She fed her captive all so well,

That soon in love with him she fell.

===Chinese===
美丽的少女万博（Wambo）

来喂养竹笼中的囚徒；

她把她的囚徒喂得那么好，

很快她就爱上了他。

===Original===
For each day he seem'd to sweeten,

Too good by far for to be eaten,

So she resolved him to save,

And her father's wrath to brave.

===Chinese===
因为他每天都似乎变得更可爱，

好得远远不能被吃掉，

所以她决定救他，

并敢于面对父亲的怒火。

===Original===
King Bomba wished to make great feast,

And slaughter poor Tom like a beast;

For to the nation King did boast,

Tom would make a glorious roast.

===Chinese===
邦巴国王（King Bomba）想要举办盛大宴会，

像宰杀牲畜一样宰杀可怜的汤姆；

因为他向全国吹嘘，

汤姆将成为一道绝妙的烤肉。

===Original===
But those who wished for slices raw,

They would get a chance to crunch their jaw,

On some pieces of long Tom pork

They could set their teeth to work.

===Chinese===
但那些想尝生肉片的人，

将有机会大嚼特嚼，

在一些长长的汤姆猪肉上

他们可以让牙齿好好工作。

===Original===
Poor Tom they all resolved to slay,

To grace the feast on King's birthday;

But the daughter of King Bomba

Resolved they should not use Tom so.

===Chinese===
他们所有人都决定杀死可怜的汤姆，

为国王的生日宴会助兴；

但邦巴国王的女儿

决定他们不该这样对待汤姆。

===Original===
So she selected one dark night,

And her footsteps were so light;

She knew the guards she could slip by

Before they her could e'er espy.

===Chinese===
于是她选了一个漆黑的夜晚，

她的脚步如此轻盈；

她知道可以在守卫发现她之前

悄悄溜过去。

===Original===
She full determined him to save

By hiding him in a small cave

That she discovered o'er the lake,

Well hid among the rocks and brake.

===Chinese===
她下定决心要救他，

把他藏在一个小山洞里，

那是她在湖对岸发现的，

隐藏在岩石和灌丛之间。

===Original===
Her steps so light she did not rouse the

Sleeping guards, they were so drowsy;

She whispered in the ear of Tom

That she would find him better home.

===Chinese===
她的脚步如此轻盈，没有惊醒

那些昏昏欲睡的守卫；

她在汤姆耳边低语

说她会为他找到更好的归宿。

===Original===
She knew the cannibals would rage,

When they would find an empty cage;

But innocence she could slam so,

They would not suspect Miss Wambo.

===Chinese===
她知道食人族会暴怒，

当他们发现笼子空了时；

但她可以表现得如此无辜，

他们不会怀疑万博小姐。

===Original===
The waters of this lake doth lave

The mouth of this hidden cave;

In her canoe Bomba's daughter

Quickly rowed him o'er the water.

===Chinese===
这个湖的水拍打着

这个隐秘山洞的洞口；

邦巴的女儿划着独木舟

迅速将他渡过水面。

===Original===
This little cave doth bushes screen,

Save her none else had ever seen

This cosy, safe and snug retreat,

Where her dear lover she could meet.

===Chinese===
这个小山洞被灌丛遮蔽，

除了她没有人见过

这个舒适、安全而温馨的避风港，

她可以在这里与她的爱人相会。

===Original===
For she so dearly loved poor Tom,

He was so far away from home;

To him a helping hand she'd lend,

And she would be his warmest friend.

===Chinese===
因为她如此深爱着可怜的汤姆，

他离家那么遥远；

她会向他伸出援手，

做他最温暖的朋友。

===Original===
She promised Tom to bring him roots,

And coconuts and banyan fruits;

She was so skilful with the oar,

Each night she would replace the store.

===Chinese===
她答应给汤姆带来树根，

椰子和榕树果；

她划桨如此熟练，

每晚都会补给食物。

===Original===
But a great tumult did arise

When savages lost their fat prize

For King's birthday it now was near,

And Tom's flesh they prized so dear.

===Chinese===
但当野蛮人丢失了他们

丰腴的猎物时引起了一场大骚动，

因为国王的生日临近了，

他们如此珍视汤姆的肉。

===Original===
They searched for him both day and night,

And it was a romantic sight

To see these naked barbarous men

Search each hill and nook and glen.

===Chinese===
他们日夜搜索他，

看着这些赤身裸体的野蛮人

搜索每一座山丘、角落和山谷，

景象颇为壮观。

===Original===
In dark nights with burning flambeau

They search'd, led on by King Bomba;

But Tom his trials he stood so brave,

He felt secure in hidden cave.

===Chinese===
在漆黑的夜晚，他们举着燃烧的火把

在邦巴国王的带领下搜索；

但汤姆勇敢地经受住了考验，

他在隐蔽的洞穴中感到安全。

===Original===
But each night he longed for Wambo,

She brought him turtle and clam so

Large and fat and delicious,

And he fend they were nutritious.

===Chinese===
但每个夜晚他都渴望万博，

她给他带来海龟和蛤蜊，

又大又肥又美味，

他觉得它们营养丰富。

===Original===
He told his love that story good,

Of little Red Riding Hood,

And how the wolf poor child deceives,

This cruel tale her sadly grieves.

===Chinese===
他给他心爱的人讲了那个故事，

小红帽的故事，

狼如何欺骗可怜的孩子，

这个残酷的故事让她悲伤不已。

===Original===
First time she ever heard a story,

It added greatly to Tom's glory;

The wolf so treacherous she did hate,

And pitied poor Riding Hood's fate.

===Chinese===
这是她第一次听故事，

这大大增加了汤姆的声望；

她憎恨那只如此狡诈的狼，

同情小红帽的悲惨命运。

===Original===
This sad tale so cruel, gory,

It saved Tom's life this little story;

Tom would been found soon or late,

And would have met a horrid fate.

===Chinese===
这个如此残酷血腥的悲伤故事，

这个小故事救了汤姆的命；

汤姆迟早会被找到，

并遭遇可怕的命运。

===Original===
And she to cave did father bring,

So Tom could tell tale to the King;

The King and daughter both they cried,

Because little Riding Hood died.

===Chinese===
她将父亲带到洞穴，

这样汤姆就能给国王讲故事；

国王和女儿都哭了，

因为小红帽死了。

===Original===
Bomba gave to Tom his daughter,

And soon other tales he taught her;

With wonder they oft times would fill her;

"Jack in Bean Stalk" and "Giant Killer."

===Chinese===
邦巴把女儿嫁给了汤姆，

他很快又教给她其他故事；

它们经常让她惊叹不已；

《杰克与魔豆》和《巨人杀手》。

===Original===
The first so quickly he could walk

To the top of a tall bean stalk;

It was high as lofty steeple,

There Jack looked down upon the people.

===Chinese===
第一个故事中他能迅速攀爬到

一棵高大豆茎的顶端；

它像高耸的教堂尖塔一样高，

杰克在那里俯瞰着人们。

===Original===
And more wondrous tale of giant,

To the world so long defiant;

Carrying off oxen on his back,

'Till he was slain by little Jack.

===Chinese===
还有关于巨人的更奇妙的故事，

他长期与世界为敌；

将公牛扛在背上带走，

直到被小杰克杀死。

===Original===
And when e'er she was full of gloom,

He told her tale of "Hop, O' My Thumb";

Natives wou'd come from far and near,

These wondrous stories for to hear.

===Chinese===
每当她满心忧郁时，

他就给她讲《大拇指》的故事；

土著人会从四面八方赶来，

听这些奇妙的故事。

===Original===
They brought him coconuts so free,

And the bread from the fruit tree;

On fat turtles he could feast,

When he told of "Beauty and the Beast."

===Chinese===
他们自由地给他带来椰子，

和果树上的面包果；

当他讲《美女与野兽》时，

他可以享用肥美的海龟。

===Original===
And Tom thought that it was no crime

To change tales of prose into rhyme,

As we do here in hopes that tingle

To the ear will be sweet jingle.

===Chinese===
汤姆认为将散文故事

改编成韵文并非罪过，

正如我们在此所做，希望那悦耳的

叮当声能传入耳朵。

===Original===
But at last they all refused to

Listen to anything but "Crusoe,"

How weary time he did beguile,

Living so long on lonely isle.

===Chinese===
但最终他们拒绝听

任何故事，除了《鲁滨逊漂流记》，

他如何消磨无聊的时光，

在孤岛上住了那么久。

===Original===
After all with greatest pride, they

Heard about his black man Friday,

Until they got stories off by heart,

Then with their goods they would not part.

===Chinese===
最后他们最为自豪地

听说了他忠实的黑人星期五，

直到他们把故事都背熟了，

然后他们就不愿与汤姆分开了。

===Original===
For they were teaching one another.

The King to stop all this bother;

Gave son-in-law a copyright,

He only should the folks delight.

===Chinese===
因为他们彼此相互传授。

国王为了停止这一切纷扰；

给了女婿版权，

只有他才能给人们带来欢乐。

===Original===
But fortune now on Tom doth smile,

An English ship anchored on isle,

For they required interpreter

To bring to them supply of water.

===Chinese===
但命运如今向汤姆微笑，

一艘英国船停泊在岛上，

因为他们需要一个翻译

去为他们取水。

===Original===
Water on ship was old and stale,

There was a stench from water pail,

But Tom he quickly did procure

Water from the spring so pure.

===Chinese===
船上的水陈旧变质，

水桶里散发着臭味，

但汤姆迅速地弄来了

清澈的泉水。

===Original===
The captain tried Tom to beguile,

And take him off from savage isle,

But Tom eujoyed this kind of life,

And loved his children and his wife.

===Chinese===
船长试图引诱汤姆离开，

将他从野蛮人的岛上带走，

但汤姆享受这种生活，

爱他的孩子和妻子。

===Original===
From ship Tom got a book of stories,

And in "Arabian Nights" soon glories.

Then each savage he delights,

Listening to tales about those Nights.

===Chinese===
汤姆从船上得到了一本故事书，

很快就沉醉在《一千零一夜》中。

他让每个野蛮人都很高兴，

听他讲那些夜晚的故事。

===Original===
Tom's influence no more it fails,

He reads to them those charming tales;

While with awe on him they look,

As he reads stories from the book.

===Chinese===
汤姆的影响力不再减弱，

他给他们读那些迷人的故事；

他们敬畏地看着他，

听他从书中读故事。

===Original===
Bomba Tomba, his eldest son,

He fame and fortune will soon won,

For he can tell them many a whopper

Of "Cinderella and Glass Slipper."

===Chinese===
邦巴·通巴（Bomba Tomba），他的长子，

很快就会赢得名望和财富，

因为他能给他们讲很多

《灰姑娘与水晶鞋》的大故事。

===Original===
And he is learning now to read,

So natives generous will him feed;

Thus he will retain their hearts,

When his father this life departs.

===Chinese===
他现在正在学习阅读，

这样土著人就会慷慨地供养他；

这样当他父亲去世时，

他将赢得他们的心。

===Original===
This tale a proof it doth afford

That pen is mightier than the sword;

In warriors no more they glory,

But in the man who tells the story.

===Chinese===
这个故事提供了一个证明，

笔比剑更有力量；

他们不再以武士为荣，

而是以讲故事的人为荣。

===Original===
No more they now each other drub,

Flourishing the warlike club;

Spears no more are used for stabbing,

Nor their javelins for jabbing.

===Chinese===
他们不再互相殴打，

挥舞着战棍；

长矛不再用于刺杀，

标枪也不再用于投掷。

===Original===
Except when they do wish to feast

On the Hesh of some fat beast,

They reserve their bows and arrows,

To shoot the pigeons and sparrows.

===Chinese===
除非他们想要享用

某种肥美野兽的肉食，

他们才会保留弓箭，

用来射鸽子和麻雀。

===Original===
All warlike arts they have forsook,

And reverence the man with book;

Peaceful arts they all now prize,

For Christian truths hath made them wise.

===Chinese===
他们已经放弃了所有好战的技艺，

尊敬手持书本的人；

他们现在珍视和平的艺术，

因为基督教的真理使他们变得明智。

===Original===
For Tom impressed them with Christ's love,

And they all hope for Heaven above;

Bomba Tomba is now crowned King,

Of him will future poets sing.

===Chinese===
因为汤姆用基督的爱感动了他们，

他们全都期盼着天国；

邦巴·通巴如今被加冕为国王，

未来的诗人们将为他歌唱。

===Original===
## Fox Hunt Yarn / 猎狐趣谈

This my adventure is no hoax;

I mounted horse to hunt a fox,

The cunning beast got into cover,

Hunting him we rode all over.

===Chinese===
我的这次冒险绝非虚构；

我骑上马去猎狐，

狡猾的畜生钻进了灌木丛，

我们骑马四处追猎。

===Original===
But my fine horse he stumbling fell

Down to the bottom of the well,

And thus the noble beast was slain.

My comrades scattered o'er the plain,

===Chinese===
但我那匹好马失足跌落

到井底，

就这样这匹高贵的马死了。

我的同伴们散布在平原上，

===Original===
Knew not that in the depths profound

I was in deep hole underground;

While o'er broad fields they proudly course,

I was laying on my dead horse.

===Chinese===
不知道我在深邃的地底

掉进了深坑；

当他们在广阔的田野上骄傲地驰骋时，

我正躺在我的死马旁边。

===Original===
And from thence did often yell,

But no one came near to that well;

The carcass soon it did ferment,

And from it there went a strong scent,

===Chinese===
我不时从那里呼喊，

但没有人靠近那口井；

尸体很快开始腐烂，

散发出强烈的气味，

===Original===
Which attracted flock of buzzards,

And down they came to fill their gizzards;

Around four of them I did cling,

And made them fly aloft on wing.

===Chinese===
吸引了一群秃鹫，

它们飞下来填饱肚子；

我紧紧抓住四只，

让它们带着我飞向高空。

===Original===
But they flew higher than I wanted,

Though T was still quite undaunted;

I first let one buzzard slip,

Then another on downward trip,

===Chinese===
但它们飞得比我想要的还高，

虽然我仍然毫不畏惧；

我先放掉一只秃鹫，

然后另一只开始下降，

===Original===
'Till I arrived all safe and sound,

Without a scratch on solid ground,

But now no man can ever coax

Me to go hunting of a fox.

===Chinese===
直到我安然无恙地到达坚实的大地上，

毫发无伤，

但现在再也没有人能哄骗我

去猎狐了。

===Original===
## The King and Peasant / 国王与农夫

A pleasant tale we now will sing

Of an old peasant and the King;

The kind Italian King, Humbert,

With the gun is a great expert.

===Chinese===
我们现在要唱一个愉快的故事，

关于一个老农夫和国王；

善良的意大利国王翁贝托（Humbert），

是一位用枪的行家。

===Original===
Peasant with wonder saw the King

Bring down the partridges on wing;

The peasant he doth sportsman praise,

Each shot it filled him with amaze.

===Chinese===
农夫惊奇地看到国王

打下飞翔的鹧鸪；

农夫赞美这位猎手，

每一枪都让他惊叹不已。

===Original===
The King he dearly loves to shoot

In plain garb without his suite;

He casts aside his Kingly fetters,

All his escort are two setters.

===Chinese===
国王酷爱射击，

穿着朴素便服，不带随从；

他抛却了国王的束缚，

他的全部护卫就是两只塞特犬。

===Original===
To farmer he doth chat pleasant

About the partridge and the pheasant;

He tells the King his chicken flocks

Are oft invaded by the fox.

===Chinese===
他愉快地与农夫闲聊

关于鹧鸪和雉鸡的事；

他告诉国王他的鸡群

常被狐狸侵扰。

===Original===
And proposal he advances

For to give the King two francs,

If he the enemy will slay,

He cheerfully the sum will pay.

===Chinese===
他提出了一个建议，

给国王两个法郎，

如果他愿意消灭敌人，

他会乐意支付这笔钱。

===Original===
But he must come at early morn,

For then the fowls from coop are torn;

The King he came and shot reynard,

And peasant then gave him reward.

===Chinese===
但他必须在清晨来，

因为那时家禽从鸡笼中被叼走；

国王来了，打死了狐狸，

农夫然后给了他报酬。

===Original===
And he gave the King his breakfast,

His majesty enjoyed repast,

And kindly chatted to the dame,

Forgetting all his rank and fame.

===Chinese===
他请国王吃早餐，

陛下享用了一顿美餐，

亲切地与女主人闲聊，

忘记了他所有的地位和名声。

===Original===
Next day a carriage drove to door,

With gilt it was all covered o'er;

And liveried servant brought present,

Both for the wife and the peasant.

===Chinese===
第二天一辆马车驶到门口，

全身覆盖着金漆；

穿着制服的仆人送来礼物，

给妻子和农夫的。

===Original===
And now their thoughts do fondly cling

Around their kind-hearted King,

Who won their hearts while in disguise:

The fox he slew they greatly prize.

===Chinese===
现在他们的思绪深情地萦绕

在他们善良的国王身边，

他在乔装时赢得了他们的心：

他们极为珍视他射杀的那只狐狸。

===Original===
No more lost fowl they do bewail,

But glory in the fox fine tail;

They love to view him in glass case,

So they may cunning features trace.

===Chinese===
他们不再为丢失的家禽悲伤，

而是以狐狸漂亮的尾巴为荣；

他们喜欢透过玻璃柜观赏它，

好描摹它狡猾的面容。

===Original===
It to them doth pleasure bring,

As it reminds them of their King,

Who did one morn at break of day.

Enemy of their chickens slay.

===Chinese===
它给他们带来欢乐，

因为它让他们想起他们的国王，

在一天清晨破晓时分，

杀死了他们鸡群的敌人。

===Original===
## Spanish Donkey / 西班牙驴

In Spain full oft the little donkey

Is misehievous as a monkey,

But Spanish peasant owned a prize,

A donkey was both strong and wise.

===Chinese===
在西班牙，小毛驴常常

调皮得像猴子一样，

但一位西班牙农夫拥有一头宝贝驴，

它既强壮又聪明。

===Original===
Peasant drove him each day to town,

And sold his milk both up and down;

And where his customers did dwell,

The peasant he did ring door bell.

===Chinese===
农夫每天赶它进城，

到处兜售牛奶；

在顾客住的地方，

农夫就去按门铃。

===Original===
But he was struck down by fever,

And he was a strong believer

That the donkey milk could sell,

And at each house would pull the bell.

===Chinese===
但他染上了重病，

他坚信

那头驴能够卖掉牛奶，

会在每户人家拉响门铃。

===Original===
It really did turn out that way,

House without bell for them he'd bray;

And allowed the Spanishers

To unload his panniers.

===Chinese===
事情果然如此发展，

在没有门铃的人家他会嘶鸣；

并允许西班牙人们

卸下它的驮篓。

===Original===
When the town's Mayor did hear the news,

He said no one must beast abuse;

And each measure out milk honest,

And truly they all acted just.

===Chinese===
当镇长听到这个消息时，

他说没有人可以虐待这头牲畜；

每家都老老实实地量取牛奶，

他们确实都做得公正。

===Original===
So thus the donkey milk did sell

Until the peasant he got well,

And we are happy for to say,

Each for his milk did honest pay.

===Chinese===
就这样驴子的牛奶一直卖着，

直到农夫康复，

我们高兴地说，

每个人都为牛奶付了诚实的钱。

===Original===
## Child and Horse / 孩子与马

A mother driving out with child,

When the storm it blew so wild;

So keen the cold the mother felt,

She went to house to get a quilt.

===Chinese===
一位母亲带着孩子驾车外出，

暴风雨猛烈地刮着；

母亲感到寒风刺骨，

她进屋去拿一条被子。

===Original===
And her little girl she snug lay

In the bottom of the buggy,

But as the horse it was not tied,

At a dog it got terrified.

===Chinese===
她的小女儿舒适地躺在

马车的底部，

但因为马没有被拴住，

一条狗把它吓着了。

===Original===
And off it ran with the dear child,

Leaving mother in anguish wild;

She tried to follow up the horse,

But soon she got on the wrong course.

===Chinese===
它载着孩子跑了出去，

留下母亲陷入疯狂的痛苦；

她试图追赶马，

但很快就走错了方向。

===Original===
She searched all day, but found no trace,

'Till faint and weary with her pace;

And as the day draws to a close,

She fears her child it will be froze,

===Chinese===
她找了一天，但毫无踪迹，

直到她精疲力竭；

随着天色渐暗，

她担心孩子会被冻死，

===Original===
If she is not dashed to pieces

By the runaway, so vicious:

A horrid night the mother spent,

And many a prayer to Heaven sent,

===Chinese===
如果不被那匹失控的马

凶猛地摔成碎片的话：

母亲度过了一个恐怖的夜晚，

向天发出了许多祈祷，

===Original===
That the Lord would save her dear child

From her numerous dangers wild;

It plainly shows the Lord did hear,

And spared the life of infant dear.

===Chinese===
祈求上帝拯救她亲爱的孩子

免受众多凶险；

这清楚地表明上帝听到了，

保全了珍贵的幼儿的性命。

===Original===
For it so happened that next day

Some boys who in the woods did play,

In place well sheltered by the trees

Where but slightly it did freeze,

===Chinese===
因为碰巧第二天

有几个在树林里玩耍的男孩，

在一个被树木遮蔽得很好的地方，

那里只是微微结冰，

===Original===
These youths there found the buggy,

And horse it there beside it lay

With the child beside its breast,

It seemed as if it child caressed.

===Chinese===
这些少年在那里发现了马车，

马就躺在旁边，

孩子依偎在它的胸前，

它似乎在抚慰着孩子。

===Original===
So lovingly within its arm,

As if protecting it from harm.

The mother came and thanked the Lord

That her lost child was now restored.

===Chinese===
如此深情地将孩子拥在怀中，

仿佛在保护她免受伤害。

母亲赶来感谢上帝

她失散的孩子现在安然归来。

===Original===
And even the runaway horse,

From his race was none the worse;

The beast the family highly prize

For its care of child so wise.

===Chinese===
甚至那匹失控的马，

从它的奔跑中并无大碍；

家人极为珍视这头牲畜，

因为它如此智慧地照看孩子。

===Original===
It seem'd as if little deary,

Of the buggy had grown weary;

And when the horse lay down to rest

She went and lay upon its breast.

===Chinese===
好像小宝贝

厌倦了马车；

当马躺下休息时，

她就走过去躺在它的胸前。

===Original===
While mother she was in alarm,

Her child was sleeping snug and warm;

The lesson we must learn from thence,

Is for to trust in Providence.

===Chinese===
当母亲惊慌失措时，

她的孩子正舒适温暖地睡着；

我们必须从中汲取的教训，

是信赖天意。

===Original===
## Mule and Bear Fight / 骡与熊的搏斗

We have a favorite old mule,

A big load he can easy pull;

He is a quiet and gentle beast,

No trouble with him in the least.

===Chinese===
我们有一匹心爱的老骡子，

它能轻松拉动重物；

它是一头安静温顺的牲畜，

一点麻烦都没有。

===Original===
Once we left him in the wood,

And he got into surly mood,

For a big bear did round him fool

'Till kicked by hind feet of our mule.

===Chinese===
有一次我们把它留在树林里，

它变得暴躁起来，

因为一头大熊在它周围戏弄它，

直到被我们骡子的后腿踢了一脚。

===Original===
One caulk it struck in its neck

Enough the joint of it to break;

The bear was quite stunned with the blow,

And soon lay helpless in the snow.

===Chinese===
一蹄子踢中了熊的脖子，

力道足以踢断关节；

熊被这一击打得晕头转向，

很快就无助地躺在雪地里。

===Original===
I unhitched mule from the sleigh.

So as to give the beast fair play;

I knew the bear would soon come round,

And that our mule would stand his ground.

===Chinese===
我把骡子从雪橇上解下来，

好让这头牲畜公平地打一场；

我知道熊很快就会恢复过来，

我们的骡子也会坚守阵地。

===Original===
The bear now raised upon his haunches,

But mule a blow at him he launches;

With both hind feet to lay him low,

But the bear he dodged the blow.

===Chinese===
熊现在用后腿站立起来，

但骡子朝它踢了一脚；

用后腿想把它打倒，

但熊躲过了这一踢。

===Original===
The bear then threw his arms round mule,

And he strove him down to pull,

But all in vain, the bear with feet,

He could not with the mule compete.

===Chinese===
熊然后用双臂抱住骡子，

试图将它拉倒，

但全是徒劳，熊用脚

无法与骡子匹敌。

===Original===
For he gave bear no time to pause,

But with his hoofs he broke his jaws,

And the great bear he soon laid low,

His blood all scatter'd o'er the snow.

===Chinese===
因为它不给熊喘息的时间，

用蹄子踢碎了它的下巴，

很快就把大熊打倒在地，

它的鲜血洒满了雪地。

===Original===
I truly think the mule takes pride,

Each winter o'er the large bear's hide;

He looks at it often in the sleigh,

Then merry he doth trot away.

===Chinese===
我真心觉得骡子引以为豪，

每个冬天都看着那张大熊皮；

它常常在雪橇上看看它，

然后欢快地小跑而去。

===Original===
## Bear and Buzzsaw / 熊与电锯

A Saw Mill Yarn

I owned sawmill in Michigan,

Which by water power ran;

Buzzsaw possessed mighty power,

Thousands of feet it cut each hour.

===Chinese===
锯木厂的故事

我在密歇根拥有一座锯木厂，

由水力驱动；

电锯拥有强大的力量，

每小时能锯数千英尺的木材。

===Original===
Mills could compete with it were few,

It cut plank that were four feet through;

Most of the mills were drove by steam,

But mine was drove by a big stream.

===Chinese===
能与之竞争的锯木厂寥寥无几，

它能锯开四英尺厚的木板；

大多数锯木厂由蒸汽驱动，

但我的由一条大溪流驱动。

===Original===
One morning when I started mill,

With wonder soon my mind doth fill,

For I was there then all alone,

With fear I was forced to groan,

===Chinese===
一天早上当我启动磨坊时，

惊奇很快充满了我的脑海，

因为我独自一人在那里，

我吓得呻吟起来，

===Original===
When I saw big bear touch its paw

Against the swift running buzzsaw;

Its foot in moment it did tear,

And this it did enrage the bear.

===Chinese===
当我看到大熊将它的爪子

触碰到飞速运转的电锯上；

它的脚瞬间就被撕裂了，

这激怒了那头熊。

===Original===
The bear was now roused to fury,

It would be both judge and jury;

Rushing on it with open jaws,

And for to crush it with its paws.

===Chinese===
熊现在被激怒到狂暴，

它要充当法官和陪审团；

张开大嘴冲向电锯，

要用爪子把它碾碎。

===Original===
Most wondrous sight I ever saw,

It cut through centre of its jaw;

Saw's speed for moment did not fail,

Cutting through its back bone and tail.

===Chinese===
这是我见过的最奇异的景象，

电锯切穿了它的下颚中央；

锯的速度一瞬间都没有减弱，

切断了它的脊骨和尾巴。

===Original===
In two the bear it did divide,

One half did lay upon each side;

Carcass we froze and fresh bear steak

We enjoyed many a week.

===Chinese===
熊被一分为二，

一半倒向一边，另一半倒向另一边；

我们将尸体冷冻，新鲜的熊肉排

我们享用了很多个星期。

===Original===
We sewed together the bear's hide

Where the saw did skin divide;

In winter warmly now I ride,

'Neath bear skin famous far and wide.

===Chinese===
我们将熊皮缝合在一起，

在电锯割开皮的地方；

冬天我现在温暖地骑行，

在远近闻名的熊皮下面。

===Original===
Mill cogs and shafts they ran with ease

When lubricated with bear's grease;

I never shall forget strange freak,

Brought nie bear's skin and grease and steak.

===Chinese===
磨坊的齿轮和轴在熊油的润滑下

轻松运转；

我永远不会忘记这奇怪的怪事，

带来了熊皮、熊油和熊肉排。

===Original===
## Wooden Leg / 木腿

Misfortune sometimes is a prize,

And is a blessing in disguise;

A man with a stout wooden leg,

Through town and country he can beg.

===Chinese===
不幸有时也是一种奖赏，

是伪装的祝福；

一个装着粗壮木腿的人，

可以在城乡各地乞讨。

===Original===
And the people in the city,

On poor man they do take pity;

He points them to his timber leg

And tells them of his poor wife, Meg.

===Chinese===
城里的人们对穷人心生怜悯；

他指着自己的木腿，

告诉他们他可怜的妻子梅格（Meg）。

===Original===
And if a dog tries him to bite,

With his stiff leg he doth him smite,

Or sometimes he will let him dig

His teeth into the wooden leg.

===Chinese===
如果有狗试图咬他，

他就用僵硬的腿打它，

或者有时他会让它把牙

咬进木腿里。

===Original===
Then never more will dog delight

This poor cripple man for to bite;

Rheumatic pains they never twig,

Nor corns annoy foot of leg.

===Chinese===
那狗再也不会高兴地咬

这个可怜的跛子；

风湿痛永远找不到他，

鸡眼也不会折磨那只脚。

===Original===
So cripple if he's man of sense,

Finds for ills some recompense;

And though he cannot dance a jig,

He merry moves on wooden leg.

===Chinese===
所以如果跛子是个聪明人，

会为不幸找到补偿；

虽然他不能跳吉格舞，

但他用木腿欢快地行走。

===Original===
And when he only has one foot,

He needs to brush only one boot;

Through world he does jolly peg,

So cheerful with his wooden leg.

===Chinese===
当他只有一只脚时，

他只需要刷一只靴子；

他快乐地一瘸一拐走遍世界，

如此开朗地带着他的木腿。

===Original===
In mud or water he can stand

With his foot on the firm dry land,

For wet he doth not care a fig,

It never hurts his wooden leg.

===Chinese===
他可以在泥泞或水中站立，

脚踏坚实干燥的陆地，

他毫不在意潮湿，

因为它永远不会伤害他的木腿。

===Original===
No aches he has but on the toes

Of one foot, and but one gets froze;

He has many a jolly rig,

And oft enjoys his wooden leg.

===Chinese===
他只有一只脚的脚趾会痛，

而且只有一只会冻伤；

他有许多有趣的把戏，

经常享受他的木腿带来的乐趣。

===Original===
## Alligator Rider / 鳄鱼骑手

I was born in State of Florida,

On the banks of a mighty river,

Where each big alligator's jaw

Makes the northern folks to shiver.

===Chinese===
我出生在佛罗里达州，

在一条大河的河岸上，

在那里每条大鳄鱼的下巴

都让北方人不寒而栗。

===Original===
I truly was possessed with vim,

And some droll antics I would play,

While in the river I did swim,

And rode alligators in the bay.

===Chinese===
我确实精力充沛，

会做一些滑稽的恶作剧，

当我在河里游泳时，

在海湾里骑鳄鱼。

===Original===
It oft times amused spectators

Who came down south to gain their health,

To see me ride those alligators;

My sports to hotel brought great wealth.

===Chinese===
它常常让南方来疗养的

观众们感到有趣，

看我骑那些鳄鱼；

我的娱乐活动给酒店带来了丰厚的收入。

===Original===
In hotel were many waiters

To tend the guests, enjoyed my rides;

They loved to see the alligators

When with my spurs I pierced their hides.

===Chinese===
酒店里有许多服务员

照料客人，欣赏我的骑行；

他们喜欢看鳄鱼

当我用马刺刺穿它们的皮肤时。

===Original===
My bridle great did them surprise,

I put no bit into their mouth,

But put my fingers in their eyes,

And thus amused the folks down south.

===Chinese===
我的缰绳让他们大为惊讶，

我不在它们嘴里放马嚼子，

而是把手指放进它们的眼睛里，

就这样逗乐了南方的人们。

===Original===
'Tis strange I still am a survivor,

Living 'mong earth's sons and daughters,

For I was a wondrous diver,

And could boldly swim the waters.

===Chinese===
奇怪的是我至今仍然活着，

生活在地球的儿女们中间，

因为我曾是一个了不起的潜水员，

能大胆地在水中游泳。

===Original===
Swifter far than any skaters

Do o'er north frozen waters glide,

I could stride my alligators,

And o'er the waters proudly ride.

===Chinese===
比任何滑冰者都快得多，

他们滑过北方冰封的水面，

我能跨骑我的鳄鱼，

骄傲地在水面上驰骋。

===Original===
## Young Swell and Tramp / 纨绔青年与流浪汉

One day a youth full of conceit,

With witty tramp he chanced to meet,

As he was taking walk abroad

So leisurely along the road.

===Chinese===
一天，一个自负的青年，

碰巧遇到了一个机智的流浪汉，

当时他正悠闲地

在路上散步。

===Original===
The tramp well knew the stylish gent

Wouid have hard work to raise a cent,

But a sad tale the tramp did tell

Of sorrows that had him befell.

===Chinese===
流浪汉很清楚这位时髦的绅士

连一分钱都很难拿出来，

但流浪汉讲述了一个悲伤的故事，

讲述降临在他身上的苦难。

===Original===
While on the road he thus did stand

And humbly held his hat in hand;

Great eloquence he did employ,

He said he was poor orphan boy.

===Chinese===
他站在路上，

谦卑地把帽子拿在手里；

他运用了极好的口才，

说自己是个可怜的孤儿。

===Original===
From labor he would never shirk,

But would be happy to get work,

For his family the bread winner,

But none of them had their dinner.

===Chinese===
他从不逃避劳动，

很高兴能有工作做，

他是家里的顶梁柱，

但他们都没有吃上晚饭。

===Original===
And some whole days would go past

Wherein they never broke their fast,

So pray now give me some relief

And help for to assuage my grief.

===Chinese===
有些日子整天过去

他们都没有吃过东西，

所以请给我一些救济，

帮我减轻悲伤。

===Original===
Said swell, "I never take a load

Of money with me when abroad,

For to dispense to tramps on street,

When I by chance with them do meet.

===Chinese===
纨绔青年说："我出门时从不

带一大笔钱在身上，

在街上分发给流浪汉，

在我偶然遇到他们的时候。

===Original===
This speech for moment did not damp

The ardor of the witty tramp;

Said he, "Give me your card to call,

And visit your manorial hall."

===Chinese===
这番话一时没有挫伤

机智流浪汉的热情；

他说："给我一张名片让我登门拜访，

去参观你的庄园大厅。"

===Original===
## Lightning Rod Agent / 避雷针推销员

An agent with both smiles and nods,

Asked Jones to buy his lightning rods;

Said Jones, "You have made a blunder,

I fear not lightning but the thunder.

===Chinese===
一个满面笑容、连连点头的推销员，

请琼斯（Jones）买他的避雷针；

琼斯说："你弄错了，

我不怕闪电，怕的是雷声。

===Original===
"Others you may go a frightening,

I tremble not at the lightning;

Those that are scared at them, I wonder,

But I own I dread the thunder."

===Chinese===
"别人你可以去吓唬，

我不怕闪电；

那些被闪电吓到的人，我奇怪，

但我承认我怕雷声。"

===Original===
"I know," said agent. "It is true,

Thunder's most dangerous of the two";

Said Jones, "Set me up thunder rods,

And from my mind it will take loads."

===Chinese===
"我知道，"推销员说。"确实如此，

雷声是两者中更危险的"；

琼斯说："给我装上防雷杆，

它会减轻我心里的负担。"

===Original===
Said agent, "Lightning easy glides,

O'er these brass tips it smoothly slides,

But when the thunder loudly rolls,

These nickel tips it soon controls.

===Chinese===
推销员说："闪电容易滑过，

在这铜尖上平滑地滑过，

但当雷声轰隆作响时，

这些镍尖能很快控制住它。

===Original===
But Jones, you are a man of sense,

You see thunder rods are more expense";

Said Jones, "I care not for the cost,

Safety is what I prize the most."

===Chinese===
但是琼斯，你是个明白人，

你知道防雷杆更贵"；

琼斯说："我不在乎费用，

安全才是我最看重的。"

===Original===
Said agent, "Biggest bolt of thunder

Will be harmless as coal cinder;

You then can smile while each explodes,

When we get up your thunder rods."

===Chinese===
推销员说："最大的雷击

会像煤渣一样无害；

到那时你就可以在每一次雷鸣中微笑，

等我们给你装好防雷杆。"

===Original===
## Conquered by a Child / 被孩子征服

A gent and lady take a trip

Along Pacific coast in ship,

And with them is little daughter,

For she doth enjoy the water.

===Chinese===
一对绅士和女士带着小女儿

乘船沿太平洋海岸旅行，

因为她喜欢大海。

===Original===
The little maiden is adored

By all the sailors are aboard;

The wheelsman often child did coax

To go up with him to his box.

===Chinese===
这位小姑娘受到

船上所有水手的喜爱；

舵手常常哄着孩子

跟他一起到驾驶台去。

===Original===
And she is a great favorite too

Among the whole of the deck crew;

On lower deck was strange cargo,

Three men chained on way to Fargo.

===Chinese===
她在全体甲板船员中

也很受欢迎；

下层甲板上有奇怪的货物，

三个被铁链锁着的人正被押往法戈（Fargo）。

===Original===
They are ruffians desperate,

And law and order both they hate,

But conquered by this little child,

Who talked to them and on them smiled.

===Chinese===
他们是亡命之徒，

憎恨法律和秩序，

但被这个小女孩征服了，

她跟他们说话，对他们微笑。

===Original===
These men, though they were bound in chains,

They soon forgot their rage and pains,

And felt she was an angel, sent

For to request them to repent.

===Chinese===
这些人虽然被铁链锁着，

很快就忘记了愤怒和痛苦，

觉得她是天使，被派来

请求他们忏悔的。

===Original===
Sheriff with pistols in his hand,

O'er those men lie held command;

But vessel struck 'gainst sunken rock

And child went o'everboard with the shock.

===Chinese===
警长手持手枪，

对那些人发号施令；

但船撞上了暗礁，

孩子因撞击被甩出船外。

===Original===
Bill Jones, though chained hand and foot,

Cried, "Sheriff please now do not shoot,

And little darling I will save

From sinking in a watery grave."

===Chinese===
比尔·琼斯（Bill Jones），虽然手脚被锁，

喊道："警长请不要开枪，

我要把小宝贝从

水底坟墓中救出来。"

===Original===
His hands and feet they both were tied,

Yet danger all he bold defied;

It seems the Lord doth efforts bless,

With teeth he seized the child by dress,

===Chinese===
他的手脚都被绑着，

但他勇敢地藐视一切危险；

似乎上帝祝福了他的努力，

他用牙齿咬住孩子的衣服，

===Original===
And held her head above the wave

'Till rescue comes them both to save;

All on board were glad to see

The sheriff set brave Bill Jones free.

===Chinese===
把她的头保持在水面之上，

直到救援到来将他们两人都救起；

船上所有人都高兴地看到

警长释放了勇敢的比尔·琼斯。

===Original===
## Joined His Church / 加入教会

They tell us, May, that love is blind,

But dear, there's something on my mind;

And since we now are engaged,

I hope you will not be enraged.

===Chinese===
人们告诉我们，梅（May），爱情是盲目的，

但亲爱的，有些事在我心里；

既然我们现在订婚了，

我希望你不要生气。

===Original===
Can you, dear May, now bear the news

From the poor husband vou would choose;

I fear to tell you, but I must,

That I am a somnambulist.

===Chinese===
亲爱的梅，你能承受这个消息吗？

从你将要选择的这个可怜丈夫这里；

我害怕告诉你，但我必须说，

我是一个梦游者。

===Original===
Said she, "Dear Dick, I will not lurch,

But stick to you if that's your church;

Though I own, as a tender maid

Of your belief I was afraid.

===Chinese===
她说："亲爱的迪克（Dick），我不会退缩，

如果那是你的教会，我会追随你；

虽然我承认，作为一个温柔的姑娘，

我曾对你的信仰感到害怕。

===Original===
But now the name I do not heed,

And will adopt it as my creed;

Though, alas, she soon is weeping

To see her husband walking sleeping.

===Chinese===
但现在我不在意这个名字，

并会将它作为我的信条；

虽然，唉，她很快就哭泣起来，

看着她丈夫梦游走路。"

===Original===
## Soldier and Gander / 士兵与公鹅

When Sherman marched through Georgia,

Hanger in soldiers oft did knaw;

A soldier longing for to dine

He soon procured a hook and line,

===Chinese===
当谢尔曼（Sherman）行军穿过佐治亚时，

饥饿常常折磨着士兵；

一个渴望用餐的士兵

很快弄到了一根鱼钩和鱼线，

===Original===
And put on hook a tempting bait.

And old wife stood beside her gate,

And round her a flock of geese;

The soldier he resolved to fleece

===Chinese===
在钩上放了诱饵。

一位老妇人站在她的门口，

周围是一群鹅；

士兵决心薅她的鹅毛

===Original===
The old Irtdy of her gander,

And make it from flock to wander;

Quick as the soldier threw the bait

The gander then it left each mate.

===Chinese===
从老太太那里弄走她的公鹅，

让它离开鹅群；

士兵刚抛出诱饵，

公鹅就离开了它的同伴。

===Original===
And it then seem'd to the beholder

As if the gander chased the soldier;

Old lady told soldier not to run,

For gander only was in fun,

===Chinese===
在旁观者看来，

好像是公鹅在追赶士兵；

老太太告诉士兵不要跑，

公鹅只是在闹着玩，

===Original===
And she would pledge to him her word,

The gander was kind-hearted bird;

She never knew him yet to fight,

For in good deeds he took delight.

===Chinese===
她向他保证，

公鹅是只善良的鸟；

她从未见它打架，

因为它以行善为乐。

===Original===
The soldier cried, "I will not stay,

But from fierce fowl will run away";

Old woman she could not divine,

Gander was pulled by hook and line.

===Chinese===
士兵喊道："我不会留下，

要从这只凶猛的鹅身边跑开"；

老太太不明白，

公鹅是被鱼钩和鱼线拖着走的。

===Original===
When soldier got across the hill,

He took the hook from out its bill,

Twisted its neck, and goose did roast,

Oft of that meal he yet doth boast.

===Chinese===
当士兵翻过山丘，

他从鹅嘴里取出鱼钩，

拧断了它的脖子，烤了鹅肉，

他至今仍常夸耀那顿饭。

===Original===
## Indian Wars / 印第安战争

We started to march o'er the plain,

And looked for shelter long in vain,

At last a scout brought in the news,

He found a house to rendezvous.

===Chinese===
我们开始在平原上行军，

长时间寻找庇护所却徒劳无功，

最后一名侦察兵带来了消息，

他找到一所房子可以集合。

===Original===
A large block house in the valley

Where the company could rally;

And soon they start a cheerful fire

With all the comfort they desire.

===Chinese===
山谷中有一座大型木屋，

连队可以在那里集合；

他们很快生起欢快的篝火，

享受他们渴望的所有舒适。

===Original===
But when the wind went howling by,

It bore along an infant's cry;

Men rushed out on the prairie

For to find the little fairy.

===Chinese===
但当风呼啸而过时，

它带来了一个婴儿的哭声；

士兵们冲向草原

去寻找那个小天使。

===Original===
They found child in arms of mother,

And met its father and its brother,

And a young sister who was quite small,

With kitten wrapped up in a shawl.

===Chinese===
他们发现孩子在母亲的怀抱里，

遇到了它的父亲和哥哥，

还有一个很小的妹妹，

用披肩裹着一只小猫。

===Original===
These folks were drawn by an ox team,

And of such storm they did not dream;

O'er wagon canvas erection,

It gave them but slight protection.

===Chinese===
这些人是用牛车拉来的，

他们没有想到会有这样的暴风雨；

篷布的马车顶篷

几乎没有给他们什么保护。

===Original===
But though this family was in gloom,

The soldiers gave them warm room,

Where they slept till dawn of day,

Then emigrants went on their way.

===Chinese===
但尽管这家人陷入困境，

士兵们给了他们温暖的房间，

他们在那里睡到黎明，

然后移民们继续上路了。

===Original===
But soon a soldier gave alarm,

Which to us seem'd to bring us harm;

He thought, or was it but a dream,

That he heard the baby scream.

===Chinese===
但很快一个士兵发出了警报，

对我们来说似乎带来了危险；

他以为——或者只是一个梦——

他听到了婴儿的尖叫。

===Original===
Horror on all it did hefal,

When soldier said 'twas wrapped in shawl;

We knew not how it should be fed,

And feared that soon it would be dead.

===Chinese===
当士兵说婴儿是裹在披肩里的，

所有人都惊恐万分；

我们不知道该怎么喂它，

担心它很快就会死去。

===Original===
The mess we fixed is historic,

For to feed it milk and paregoric;

Just then we heard Indians yell,

And thick 'mong us their arrows fell.

===Chinese===
我们调配的混合饲料成了传奇，

用牛奶和止痛剂喂它；

就在这时我们听到印第安人的呐喊，

箭如雨下落在我们中间。

===Original===
But they found they could not trifle

With us, when each seized his rifle;

But so quick they us surrounded,

Several of our men were wounded.

===Chinese===
但他们发现他们无法

戏弄我们，当每个人都拿起步枪时；

但他们如此迅速地包围了我们，

我们好几个人受了伤。

===Original===
But soon quiet did reign supreme,

Then infant once more was the theme,

Though no more the babe doth squall,

For arrows have transfixed the shawl,

===Chinese===
但很快安静占据了上风，

然后婴儿又成了话题，

虽然婴儿不再啼哭，

因为箭射穿了披肩，

===Original===
Each soldier's heart was grieved sore,

For blood it trickled to the floor;

So much witl, grief they were smitten,

They opened shawl and found dead kitten.

===Chinese===
每个士兵的心都悲伤不已，

因为鲜血滴到了地上；

他们如此悲痛，

他们打开披肩，发现是一只死小猫。

===Original===
## Love in a Snowdrift / 雪堆中的爱情

One winter day in snow-bound train,

In drift we stuck in State of Maine;

It happened near a village small,

And near the track was public hall.

===Chinese===
一个冬日，一列被大雪困住的火车，

在缅因州陷入了雪堆；

事情发生在一座小村庄附近，

铁轨旁有一座公共礼堂。

===Original===
The passengers did all complain

Of these fierce blizzards blow in Maine;

With snow plough they had striven all day,

But found they could not make headway.

===Chinese===
乘客们都在抱怨

缅因州这些猛烈的暴风雪；

他们用铲雪机努力了一整天，

但发现无法前进。

===Original===
The weary hours brought grief and pain

To many on that trip in Maine;

On me the passengers they call

To lecture to them in the hall.

===Chinese===
疲惫的时光给缅因州

这次旅途中的许多人带来了悲伤和痛苦；

乘客们请我

到礼堂给他们做讲座。

===Original===
And the next morning I felt vain,

When a young man, belonged to Maine,

Warmly thanked me for my lecture,

"Well pleased with it," was my conjecture.

===Chinese===
第二天早上我感到虚荣满足，

因为一个缅因州的年轻人

热情地感谢我的讲座，

"他很满意"，我猜想。

===Original===
He said it gave him chance to gain

The heart of finest girl in Maine;

When to the hall you all resorted,

The sweet girl in car I courted.

===Chinese===
他说这给了他赢得

缅因州最美姑娘芳心的机会；

当你们都去礼堂的时候，

我在车厢里追求那个可爱的姑娘。

===Original===
My vanity at first was slain.

But when the young man there in Maine

Asked me to wed him to his Jane,

I felt the snow drift was a gain.

===Chinese===
我的虚荣心一开始被打击了。

但当那个缅因州的年轻人

请我主持他和简（Jane）的婚礼时，

我觉得这场雪堆倒是一件好事。

===Original===
## Blanket Shield / 毯子盾牌

When blanket round us we do fold,

It doth keep out the wind and cold;

And when house is near to great fire,

Wet blankets save from ruin dire.

===Chinese===
当我们裹上毯子时，

它能挡住风和寒冷；

当房屋靠近大火时，

湿毯子能挽救它免于可怕的毁灭。

===Original===
A General in Mexico

Advanced against Indian foe,

But Indians did hold the field,

Protected by wet blanket shield.

===Chinese===
墨西哥的一位将军

向印第安敌人进军，

但印第安人坚守阵地，

靠湿毯子盾牌的保护。

===Original===
While Mexicans by hundreds fall,

Each pierced by an Indian ball;

Wet blanket it must hang quite loose,

Or for protection 'tis no use.

===Chinese===
墨西哥人成百上千地倒下，

每人被印第安人的子弹射穿；

湿毯子必须挂得相当松，

否则起不到保护作用。

===Original===
Thus Indians rushed o'er the field,

And caused the Mexicans to yield;

The savages they were more wise

Than Christian foe did them despise.

===Chinese===
就这样印第安人冲过战场，

迫使墨西哥人投降；

野蛮人比轻视他们的

基督教敌人更加聪明。

===Original===
As a shield we high must rank it,

So light you cannot outflank it;

The bullets glance off from the blanket,

For its uses we must thank it.

===Chinese===
作为盾牌我们必须高度评价它，

如此轻便你无法包抄它；

子弹从毯子上弹开，

我们必须感谢它的用途。

===Original===
## Dime Story / 一角钱的故事

We tell a tale, 'tis all in rhyme,

To show how boy earned a dime:

A parson who his flock did feed,

On written sermons he did read.

===Chinese===
我们讲一个故事，全是韵文，

讲述一个男孩如何赚到一角钱：

一位牧师向他的教众布道，

读着写好的讲道稿。

===Original===
One day at top of pulpit stair

He was thrown into despair,

For he had forgot his paper,

And his thoughts were but as vapor.

===Chinese===
一天在讲坛台阶顶端，

他陷入了绝望，

因为他忘记了他的讲稿，

他的思绪如蒸汽般飘散。

===Original===
But fortunate his own dear wife,

Who often soothed his ills in life.

She found sermon on the table,

And found a boy who was able

===Chinese===
但幸运的是他亲爱的妻子，

常常抚慰他生活中的不幸。

她在桌上找到了讲道稿，

并找到了一个能够

===Original===
To deliver it in good time,

By promising to give him dime.

The boy to church did quickly run,

And he the dime then fairly won.

===Chinese===
按时送到的男孩，

许诺给他一角钱。

男孩迅速跑向教堂，

他公平地赢得了那一角钱。

===Original===
So he went back to parsonage

And demanded then his wage;

The wife she asked the little vermin

If he had delivered sermon.

===Chinese===
于是他回到牧师住宅，

要求领取报酬；

妻子问这个小家伙

他是否送到了讲道稿。

===Original===
Said he, "My contract I did cover,

I gave it to parson to deliver,

And he delivers it with feeling.

And the poor folks' hearts he's healing."

===Chinese===
他说："我履行了我的合同，

我把它交给牧师去宣读，

他深情地宣讲着。

他在治愈穷苦人的心灵。"

===Original===
## Alligator and Boy / 鳄鱼与男孩

This is a true tale and no yarn,

Our boys bathed in Hoxa Tarn;

But for long they had spectator,

Eager, watching alligator.

===Chinese===
这是一个真实的故事而非编造，

我们的男孩们在霍克萨塘（Hoxa Tarn）游泳；

但很久以来他们有一个旁观者，

热切地注视着——一条鳄鱼。

===Original===
He wished to make an example

With fattest boy for a sample,

So that the lads no more would fool,

'Nor play around his favorite pool.

===Chinese===
它想杀鸡儆猴，

以最胖的男孩做样本，

这样男孩们就不敢再胡闹，

不在他心爱的水塘边玩耍了。

===Original===
His fierce assaults the boys did warn,

But they resolved to hold the Tarn,

As they expertly all could swim;

For time, they out-manoeuvred him.

===Chinese===
它的凶猛袭击警告了男孩们，

但他们决心坚守水塘，

因为他们全都是游泳好手；

暂时他们智胜了它。

===Original===
But they knew sooner or later,

They would be caught by alligator;

So strategy they did employ,

And they rigged up a seeming boy.

===Chinese===
但他们知道迟早

会被鳄鱼抓住；

所以他们施展了计谋，

制作了一个假男孩。

===Original===
He thought, "I here bear sovereign rule,

No boy shall trespass in my pool;

So now I will enforce my laws,

And crunch him quick between my jaws."

===Chinese===
它想："我在这里拥有至高无上的统治权，

没有男孩可以在我的水塘里擅入；

所以现在我要执行我的法律，

迅速将他咬碎在我的大嘴之间。"

===Original===
He ope'd his mouth, the earth did shake,

Was it thunder bolt or earthquake?

For there was scattered far and wide

Grease and alligator's hide.

===Chinese===
它张开大嘴，大地为之震动，

那是雷击还是地震？

因为油脂和鳄鱼皮

散落得四处都是。

===Original===
## Lines / 赋诗

On Oxford County rosolving to have a Refuge for the honest poor. June, 1891.

===Chinese===
关于牛津郡决定为诚实的穷人设立庇护所。1891年6月。

===Original===
Disgrace no more we will bewail,

That honest poor are sent to jail

Or allowed to beg from door to door,

When they are old, sick and footsore;

They will have retuge on the farm,

Where cold and hunger will not harm.

===Chinese===
我们不再为这样的耻辱哀叹，

诚实的穷人被送进监狱

或者被允许挨家挨户乞讨，

当他们年老、生病、脚痛时；

他们将在农场上得到庇护，

寒冷和饥饿不会伤害他们。

===Original===
## Lines / 赋诗

On Dr. McKay being appointed whip of the Ontario Legislature.

===Chinese===
关于麦凯博士被任命为安大略省立法机关党鞭。

===Original===
McKay he is a clever chap,

Will make good whip for he has snap.

===Chinese===
麦凯（McKay）是个聪明的家伙，

会做个好党鞭，因为他有冲劲。

===Original===
## Political / 政治

Canada was found by Cabot,

And now the Premier is Abbot.

Cheap sugar to all is a boon,

Folks love it in their tea and spoon,

Cartwright thinks lie could build state coach,

Laurier could drive without reproach.

===Chinese===
加拿大由卡伯特（Cabot）发现，

如今总理是阿博特（Abbot）。

便宜的糖对所有人都是恩惠，

人们爱在茶里和勺子里加糖，

卡特赖特（Cartwright）认为他能打造国车，

洛里埃（Laurier）能毫无瑕疵地驾驭。

===Original===
## The Flood on the Creek / 小溪洪水

April 1891.

Almost broken was the lyre

In the hands of bard McIntyre,

Who long had mused beside the stream,

Till rudely wakened from his dream.

===Chinese===
1891年4月。

诗人麦金泰尔（McIntyre）手中的

竖琴几乎断裂，

他长久地在溪流旁沉思，

直到被粗暴地从梦中惊醒。

===Original===
The waters high in each dam pent,

Rushed furious when they found vent;

Through the flood gates opened wide

Madly raged the foaming tide.

===Chinese===
高高蓄积在每个水坝中的水，

找到出口后狂暴地奔涌；

通过大开的水闸，

泡沫翻涌的潮水疯狂地咆哮。

===Original===
He heard the waters awful dash,

And he heard his warehouse crash,

And saw the waves in wild commotion

Bearing his stock to the ocean.

===Chinese===
他听到水可怕的撞击声，

他听到他的仓库崩塌，

看到波浪疯狂翻滚，

将他的存货冲向大海。

===Original===
Now thanks he gives unto each friend,

Who a helping hand did lend;

With gratitude they did inspire

The heart-felt thanks of McIntyre.

===Chinese===
现在他感谢每一位

伸出援手的朋友；

他们激发了麦金泰尔

发自内心的感激之情。

===Original===
Old friends and new he'll gladly meet

On the west side of Thames street,

Where he has a foundation sure,

And a good stock of furniture.

===Chinese===
他将乐于在泰晤士街的西边

会见新老朋友，

在那里他有了稳固的根基，

和一批上好的家具。

===Original===
## Big Crops of 1891 / 1891年大丰收

Eighteen hundred and ninety-one

For good crops it leads the van;

As land had blessed showers of rain,

Which brought abundant crops of grain.

===Chinese===
一千八百九十一年

在好收成中名列前茅；

因为土地受到了甘霖的恩赐，

带来了丰盛的谷物收成。

===Original===
Not scorched with heat, the air was cool,

And the ears were large and full;

Forty bushel to the acre

Makes flour that delights the baker.

===Chinese===
没有被炎热烤焦，空气凉爽，

麦穗又大又饱满；

每英亩四十蒲式耳的产量

制造出让面包师高兴的面粉。

===Original===
None ever saw such crops as these,

So great the yield of oats and peas;

Fifty bushels to the acre

Makes us grateful to our maker.

===Chinese===
没有人见过这样的庄稼，

燕麦和豌豆的产量如此之高；

每英亩五十蒲式耳的产量

让我们对造物主心怀感激。

===Original===
## Death of Parnell / 帕内尔之死

Oct., 1891.

Ireland's great champion, Parnell,

To worldly strife has bade farewell.

===Chinese===
1891年10月。

爱尔兰的伟大斗士帕内尔（Parnell），

已向尘世的纷争告别。

===Original===
## Short Route to the Orient / 通往东方的捷径

Having predicted the building of the Canadian Pacific Railroad, in a poem I published in August, 1858, I felt proud to see the wonderful quick passage of the mails from Japan to Liverpool, via this road.

Jules Verne, that brilliant son of France,

Astonished all with wild romance;

Around the world in eighty days,

This tale it doth no more amaze.

===Chinese===
我曾在1858年8月发表的一首诗中预言了加拿大太平洋铁路的修建，看到邮件通过这条铁路从日本快速运抵利物浦，我感到自豪。

儒勒·凡尔纳（Jules Verne），法国的才华横溢之子，

以其狂野的传奇让所有人惊叹；

八十天环游地球，

这个故事不再令人惊奇了。

===Original===
For the Canadian Pacific

Runs her cars at speed terrific;

Her steamers swiftly plow the seas,

Which gives to us our cheap, fine teas.

===Chinese===
因为加拿大太平洋铁路

以惊人的速度运行着列车；

她的轮船迅速犁过大海，

为我们带来了便宜的好茶。

===Original===
For she is bound to lead the van

With her short, quick route to Japan;

And proudly she may glory vent

In highway to the Orient.

===Chinese===
因为她注定要引领潮流，

以她通往日本的快捷短途路线；

她可以骄傲地在

通往东方的高速路上荣光焕发。

===Original===
From a small sprout these leaves they grew,

And bard now bids you kind adieu.

===Chinese===
这些诗页从小小的嫩芽长成，

诗人现在向你们亲切地道别。
